||bhagavānuvāca||
ahaṃ tṛṣṭastu yuvayoḥ pārvatīṃ dayitāṃ mama|
maddattaṃ kāmamicchantāvārādhayatamīśvarīm || 51.212 ||
tāmṛte tu na śaknomi dātumiṣṭaṃ sanātanam|
sevitiṃ ca sutau nityaṃ śaraṇaṃ vrajataṃ śivām|| 51.213 ||
acirād yena bhāvena prītiṃ devī gamiṣyati|
atra vā tatra vā gatvā bhāvena cāryyatām|| 51.214 ||
iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe vetālabhairavotpattikathane ekapañcāśo’dhyāyaḥ||

<52>
||aurvva uvāca||
evaṃ vadati bhūteśe tadā vetālabhairavau|
prāhaturvyomakeśaṃ tau harṣotphullavilocanau|| 52.1 ||
||vetālabhairavāvūcatuḥ||
pārvatyā na hi jānīvo dhyānaṃ mantraṃ vidhiṃ tathā|
kathamārādhayiṣyāvo bhagavan samyagucyatām|| 52.2 ||
||bhīgavānuvāca||
mahāmāyāvidhiṃ mantraṃ ca bhavatoḥ sutau|
upadekṣyāmi tattvena yena sarvaṃ bhaviṣyati|| 52.3 ||
||aurvva uvāca||
ityuktvā sa mahāmāyādhyānaṃ mantraṃ vidhiṃ tathā|
kathayāmāsa giriśastayoḥ samyaṅ nṛpottama|| 52.4 ||
yadaṣṭādaśabhiḥ paścātpaṭalaiśca sa bhairavaḥ|
sa nirṇayavidhiṃ kalpaṃ nibabandha śivāmṛte|| 52.5 ||
||sagara uvāca||
kīdṛṅ mantraṃ purā śambhuravocadubhayostayoḥ|
yenārādhya mahāmāyāṃ tau gaṇeśatvamāpatuḥ|| 52.6 ||
sakalpaṃ sarahasyaṃ ca sāṅgaṃ tacchrītumutsahe|
daśāṣṭapaṭalairyat tu nibabandha sa bhairavaḥ|| 52.7 ||
||aurvva uvāca||
bahutvād vadituṃ tasya cireṇaiva tu śakyate|
tasmāt sadyaḥ samuddhṛtya yanmahādevabhāṣitam|
saṃkṣepāt kathaye tattvaṃ tacchṛṇuṣva nṛpottama|| 52.8 ||
pṛcchantau pārvatī mantraṃ tadā vetālabhairavau|
jagāda sa mahādevaḥ śṛṇuta mantrakalpakau|| 52.9 ||
||śrībhagavānuvāca||
śṛṇu mantraṃ pravakṣyāmi guhyād guhyatamaṃ param|
aṣṭākṣaraṃ tu vaiṣṇavyā mahāmāyāmahotsavam|| 52.10 ||
asya śrīvaiṣṇavīmantrasya nāraṛṣiḥ śambhurdevatā|
anuṣṭup chandaḥ sarvārthasādhane viniyogaḥ|| 52.11 ||
hāntāntayurvo rāntaśca nānto ṇāntastathaiva ca|
kaikādaśāṣṭādiṣaṣṭhaḥ khānto viṣṇupuraḥsaraḥ|| 52.12 ||
ebhiraṣṭākṣarairmantraṃ śoṇapatrasamaprabham|
oṃkāraṃ pūrvataḥ kṛtvā japyaṃ sarvaistu sādhakaiḥ|| 52.13 ||
mahāmantramidaṃ guhyaṃ vaiṣṇavīmantrasaṃjñakam|
mantraṃ kalevaragataṃ tasmādaṅgaṃ prakīrtitam|| 52.14 ||
mahādevasyordhvamukhaṃ bījametat prakīrtitam|
oṃkārākṣarabījaṃ ca yakāraḥ śaktirucyate|| 52.15 ||
sabījaṃ kathitaṃ mantraṃ kalpaṃ ca śṛṇu bhairava|
tīrthe nadyāṃ devakhāte gartaprasravaṇādike|| 52.16 ||
parakīyetare toye snānaṃ pūrvaṃ samācaret|
ācāntaḥ śucitāṃ prāptaḥ kṛtāsanaparigrahaḥ|| 52.17 ||
uttarābhimukho bhūtvā sthaṇḍilaṃ mārjayet tataḥ|
kareṇānena mantreṇa yūṃ sahaḥ kṣityā iti svayam|| 52.18 ||
oṃ hrīṃ sa iti mantreṇa āśāpūraṇakena ca|
toyairabhyukṣayet sthānaṃ bhūtānāmapasāraṇe|| 52.19 ||
tataḥ savyena hastena gṛhītvā sthaṇḍilaṃ śuciḥ|
mantraṃ likhet suvarṇena yājñikena kuśena vā|| 52.20 ||
oṃ vaiṣṇavyai namaḥ iti mantrarājamathāpi vā|
tatastrimaṇḍalaṃ kuryāt tenaiva samarekhayā|| 52.21 ||
nityāsu na hi pūjāsu rajobhirmaṇḍalaṃ likhet|
puraścaraṇakāryeṣu tatkāmyeṣu prayojayet|| 52.22 ||
rekhāmudīcyāṃ prathamaṃ paścime tadanantaram|
dakṣiṇe tu tataḥ paścāt pūrvabhāge tu śeṣataḥ|| 52.23 ||
varṇānāṃ ca sahadvārairevameva kramo bhavet|
oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ sa iti mantreṇa maṇḍalaṃ pūjayet tataḥ|| 52.24 ||
hastena maṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā kuryād digbandhanaṃ tataḥ|
āśābandhanamantreṇa pūrvoktena yathākramam|| 52.25 ||
phaḍantenātmanāpyatra kareṇaiva nibandhayet|
yavānāṃ maṇḍalairekamaṅgulaṃ cāṣṭabhirbhavet|| 52.26 ||
adīrghayojitairhastaiscaturviṃśatiraṅgulaiḥ|
tatpramāṇena hastena hastaikaṃ tasya maṇḍalam|| 52.27 ||
padmaṃ vitastimātraṃ syāt karṇikāraṃ tadardhakam|
dalānyanyonyasaktāni hyāyatāni niyojayet|| 52.28 ||
na nyūnādhikabhāgāni sabahirveṣṭitāni ca|
madhyabhāge nyased dvāraṃ nyūne nādhike tathā|
subaddhaṃ maṇḍalaṃ tacca raktavarṇaṃ vicintayet|| 52.29 ||
ito’nyathā maṇḍalamugramasyāḥ karoti yo lakṣaṇabhāgahīnam|
phalaṃ na cāpnoti na kāmamiṣṭaṃ tasmādidaṃ maṇḍalamatra lekhyam|| 52.30 ||
iti śīkālikāpurāṇe mahāmāyākalpe’ṣṭādaśapaṭale dvipañcāśattamo’dhyāyaḥ||

<53>
||śrībhagavānuvāca||
tato lamiti mantreṇa arghapātrasya maṇḍalam|
catuṣkoṇaṃ vidhāyāśu dvārapadmavivarjitam|| 53.1 ||
oṃ hrīṃ śrīmitimantreṇa arghapātraṃ tu maṇḍale|
vinyaset prathamaṃ tatra pūjayitvā samidhyati || 53.2 ||
oṃ hrī hraumitimantreṇa gandhapuṣpe tathā jalam|
arghapātre kṣipet tatra maṇḍalaṃ vinyaset tataḥ|| 53.3 ||
pūrvavanmaṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā arghapātre tato jalaiḥ|
tribhāgaiḥ pūrayet pātraṃ puṣpaṃ tatra viniḥkṣipet 53.4 ||
tato hrīmiti mantreṇa āsanaṃ pūjayet svakam|
tataḥ kṣaumitimantreṇa ātmānaṃ pūjayed budhaḥ|| 53.5 ||
gandhaiḥ puṣpaiḥ śirodeśe tataḥ pūjāṃ samācaret |
oṃ hrīṃ sa iti mantreṇa puṣpaṃ hastatalasthitam|| 53.6 ||
saṃmṛjya savyahastena ghrātvā vāmakareṇa tu|
aiśānyāṃ nikṣipedetat pūrvamantreṇa kovidaḥ|| 53.7 ||
raktaṃ puṣpaṃ gṛhītvā tu karābhyāṃ pāṇikacchapam|
baddhvā kuryāt tataḥ paścād dahanaplavanādikam|| 53.8 ||
vāmahastasya tarjanyāṃ dakṣiṇasya kaniṣṭhikām |
tatā dakṣiṇatarjanyāṃ vāmāṅgaṣṭhaṃ niyojayet|| 53.9 ||
unnataṃ dakṣiṇāṅguṣṭhaṃ vāmasya madhyamādikāḥ|
aṅgulīryojayet pṛṣṭhe dakṣiṇasya karasya ca|| 53.10 ||
vāmasya pitṛtīrthena madhyamānāmike tathā|
adhomukhe tu te kuryād dakṣiṇasya karasya ca|| 53.11 ||
kūrmapṛṣṭhasamaṃ pṛṣṭhaṃ kuryād dakṣiṇahastataḥ |
evaṃ baddhaḥ sarvasiddhiṃ dadāti pāṇikacchapaḥ|| 53.12 ||
kuryāt taddhṛdayāsannaṃ nimīlya nayanadvayam|
samaṃ kāyaśirogrīvaṃ kṛtvā sthiramanā budhaḥ|| 53.13 ||
dhyānaṃ samārabhed devyā dāhaplavanapūrvakam|
agniṃ vāyau vinikṣipya vāyuṃ toye jalaṃ hṛdi|| 53.14 ||
hṛdayaṃ niścale dattvā ākāśe nikṣipetsvanam svakam |
oṃ hūṃ phaḍiti mantreṇa bhittvā randhraṃ tu mastake|| 53.15 ||
śabdena sahitaṃ jīvamākāśe sthāpayet tataḥ|
vāyvagniyamaśakrāṇāṃ bījena varuṇasya ca|| 53.16 ||
parāsthānaparāścaitaiḥ sārdhacandraiḥ savindukaiḥ|
śoṣaṃ dāhaṃ tathocchādaṃ pīyūṣāsevanaṃ param|| 53.17 ||
yathākrameṇa kartavyaṃ cintāmātraṃ viśuddhaye|
tatastu devībījena aṇuṃ jāmbūnadākṛtim|| 53.18 ||
tatrāsādya dvidhā kuryāt um hrīṃ śrīmiti mantrakāḥ|
tadūrdhvabhāgeṣu hṛdlokaṃ svargaṃ ca khaṃ tathā|| 53.19 ||
niṣpādya śeṣabhāgena bhuvaṃ pātālavāriṇi dhāriṇīm |
cintayettatra sarvāṇi saptadvīpāṃ ca medinīm|| 53.20 ||
tatteṣu sāgarāṃstāṃstu svarṇadvīpaṃ vicintayet|
tanmadhye ratnapayaṅka ratnamaṇḍapasaṃsthitam|| 53.21 ||
ākāśagaṅgātoyodhaiḥ sadaiva sevitaṃ śubham|
tatparyaṅke raktapadmaṃ prasannaṃ sarvadāśivam|| 53.22 ||
cintayet svarṇamānāṅkaṃ saptapātālanālakam|
ābrahmabhuvanasparśi suvarṇācalakarṇikam|| 53.23 ||
tatrasthitāṃ mahāmāyāṃ dhyāyedekāgramānasaḥ|
śoṇapadmapratīkāśāṃ muktamūrdhajalambinīṃ|| 53.24 ||
calatkāñcanāmāruhya kuṇḍalojjvalaśālinīm|
suvarṇaratnasampanna dvayadhāriṇīm|| 53.25 ||
śuklakṛṣṇāruṇairnetrai stribhiścāruvibhūṣitām|
sandhyācandrasamaprakhyakapolāṃ lolalocanām|| 53.26 ||
vipakva dāḍimībījadantān subhrū yogojjvalām|
bandhūkadantavasanāṃ śirīṣaprabhanāsikām|| 53.27 ||
kambugrīvāṃ viśālākṣīṃ sūryakoṭisamaprabhām|
caturbhujāṃ vivasanāṃ pīnonnatapayodharām|| 53.28 ||
dakṣiṇordhvena nistriṃśatpareṇa siddhasūtrakam|
bibhratīṃ vāmahastābhyāmabhīti varadāyinīm|| 53.29 ||
nimnanābhi kramāyātāṃ kṣīṇamadhyāṃ manoharām|
ānamannāgapāśorūṃ guptagulphāṃ supārṣṇikām|| 53.30 ||
baddhaparyaṅkasaṅkalpāṃ nivīrāsanarājitām|
gātreṇa ratnasaṃstambhaṃ samyagālambya saṃsthitām|| 53.31 ||
kimicchasīti vacanaṃ vyāharantīṃ muhurmuhuḥ|
pañcānanaṃ puraḥsaṃsthaṃ nirīkṣantīṃ suvāhanam || 53.32 ||
muktāvalī svarṇaratnahā rakaṅkaṇādibhiḥ|
sarvairalaṅkāragaṇairujjvalāṃ sasmitānanām|| 53.33 ||
sūryakoṭipratīkāśāṃ sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutām|
navayauvanasampannāṃ tathā sarvāṅgasundarīm|| 53.34 ||
īdṛśīmambikāṃ dhyātvā namaḥ phaḍiti mastake|
svakīye prathamaṃ sā’hameva vicintya ca|| 53.35 ||
aṅganyāsakaranyāsau tataḥ kuryāt krameṇa ca |
ebhirmantraiḥ svaraiḥ saha sṛmīsūmaiḥ kramānvitaiḥ|| 53.36 ||
om kṣaum caite sapraṇavāṃ raktavarṇāṃ manoharām|
aṅguṣṭhādikaniṣṭhāntamantrasaṃbeṣṭanaṃ phaṭ|| 53.37 ||
prāntena kuryād vinyāsaṃ pūrvaṃ karataladvaye |
hṛcchiraḥ śikhākavacanetreṣu kramato nyaset|| 53.38 ||
tatastu mūlamantrasya vaktre pṛṣṭhe tathodare|
bāhvorguhye pādayośca jaṅghayorjaghane kramāt|| 53.39 ||
vinyasedakṣarāṇyaṣṭau oṃkāraṃ ca tathā smaran|
ebhiḥ prakārairati śuddhadehaḥ pūjāṃ sadaivārhati nānyathā hi|
śarīraśuddhiṃ manaso niveśaṃ bhūtaprasāraṃ kurute nṛṇāṃ tat|| 53.40 ||
iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe mahāmāyākalpe tripañcāśo’dhyāyaḥ||

<54>
||śrībhagavānuvāca||
tato’rghapātre tanmantramaṣṭadhākṛtya sañjajapet|
tena toyāni puṣpāṇi svaṃ maṇḍalamathāsanam|| 2.54.1 ||
āśodhayet tataḥ paścāt pūjopakaraṇaṃ samam|
oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ hrau miṃti mantreṇa śabdaprāṃśuvivarjitam|| 2.54.2 ||
dvārapālaṃ tato devyā āsanāni ca pūjayet|
nandibhṛṅgimahākālagaṇeśā dvārapālakāḥ|
uttarādikramāt pūjyā āsanāni ca madhyataḥ|| 2.54.3 ||
ādhāraśaktiprabhṛti hemādyantāt prapūjayet|
prasiddhāt sarvatantreṣu pūjākalpeṣu bhairava|| 2.54.4 ||
daśadikpālasahitān dharmādharmādikāṃstathā|
maṇḍalāgnyādikoṇeṣu pūjayet pārśvadeśataḥ|| 2.54.5 ||
sūryāgnisomamarutāṃ maṇḍalāni ca padmakam|
rajastathā tamaḥ sattvaṃ yogapīṭhaṃ guroḥ padam|| 2.54.6 ||
sārādīn bhadrapīṭhāntān sāṅgopāṅgān prapūjayet|
brahmāṇḍaṃ svarṇaḍimbaṃ ca brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarān|| 2.54.7 ||
sasāgarān saptadvīpān svarṇadvīpaṃ samaṇḍapam|
ratnapadmaṃ saparyaṅkaṃ ratnastambhaṃ tathaiva ca|| 2.54.7 ||
pañcānanaṃ maṇḍalasya madhye’vaśyaṃ prapūjayet|
hrīṃ mantreṇa tataḥ kūrmapṛṣṭhaṃ pāṇyornibadhya ca|| 2.54.9 ||
dhyāyecca pūrvavad devīmāsādyāsanamuttamam|
hṛnmadhye cintayet svarṇadvīpaṃ paryaṅkasambhṛtam || 2.54.10 ||
paśyanniva tato devīmekāgramanasā smaret|
pratyakṣīkṛtya hṛdaye mānasairupacārakaiḥ|| 2.54.11 ||
ṣoḍaśānāṃ prakāraistu hṛdisthāṃ pūjayecchivām|
tatastu vāyubījena dakṣiṇe ca puṭena ca|| 2.54.12 ||
nāsikāyā viniḥsāryaṃ krīṃ mantreṇa ca bhairava|
sthāpayet padmamadhye tu taddhastaṃ na viyojayet|| 2.54.13 ||
kṛte viyoge hastasya puṣpāt tasmācca bhairava|
gandharvaiḥ pūjyate devī pūjakairnāpyate phalam|| 2.54.14 ||
āvāhanaṃ tataḥ kuryād gāyatryā śirasā saha |
mahāmāyāyai vidmahe tvāṃ caṇḍikākhyāṃ dhīmahi || 2.54.15 ||
etaduktvā tataḥ paścād dhiyo yo naḥ pracodayāt|
snānīyaṃ devi te tubhyaṃ oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ nama ityataḥ || 2.54.16 ||
snānīyaṃ ca tato daivyai dadyāllakṣaṇalakṣitam|
tatastu mūlamantreṇa gandhapuṣpaṃ sadīpakam|| 2.54.17 ||
dhūpādikaṃ pradadyāt tu modakaṃ pāyasaṃ tathā|
sitāṃ guḍaṃ dadhikṣīraṃ sarpirnānāvidhaiḥ phalaiḥ|| 2.54.17 ||
raktapuṣpaṃ puṣpamālāṃ suvarṇarajatādikam|
naivedyamuttamaṃ devyā lāṅgalaṃ modakaṃ sitām|| 2.54.19 ||
śāṇḍilyakaratāmrākhyakūṣmāṇḍānāṃ phalāni ca|
harītakīphalaṃ cāpi nāgaraṅgakamelakam|| 2.54.20 ||
bālapriyaṃ ca yad dravyaṃ kaserukabisādikam|
toyaṃ ca nārikelasya devyai deyaṃ prayatnataḥ|| 2.54.21 ||
raktaṃ kauśeyavastraṃ ca deyaṃ nīlaṃ kadāpi na|
devyāḥ priyāṇi puṣpāṇi bakulaṃ keśaraṃ tathā|| 2.54.22 ||
mādhyaṃ kahlāravajrāṇi karavīrakuruṇṭakān|
arkapuṣpaṃ śālmalakaṃ dūrvāṅkuraṃ sukomalam || 2.54.23 ||
kuśamañjarikā darbhā bandhūkakamale tathā|
mālūrapatraṃ puṣpaṃ ca trisandhyāraktaparṇake|| 2.54.24 ||
sumanāṃsi priyāṇyetānyambikāyāśca bhairava|
bandhūkaṃ bakulaṃ mādhyaṃ bilvapatrāṇi sandhyakam|| 2.54.25 ||
uttamaṃ sarvapuṣpeṣu dravye pāyasamodakau|
mālyaṃ bandhūkapuṣpasya śivāyai bakulasya vā|| 2.54.26 ||
karavīrasya mādhyasya sahasrāṇāṃ dadāti yaḥ|
sa kāmān prāpya cābhīṣṭān mama loke pramodate|| 2.54.27 ||
candanaṃ śītalaṃ caiva kālīyakasamanvitam|
anulepanamukhyaṃ tu devyai dadyāt prayatnataḥ|| 2.54.27 ||
karpūraṃ kuṅkumaṃ kūrcaṃ mṛganābhiṃ sugandhikam |
kālīyakaṃ sugandheṣu devyāḥ prītikaraṃ param|| 2.54.29 ||
yakṣadhūpaḥ pratīvāhaḥ piṇḍadhūpaḥ sagolakaḥ |
aguruḥ sindhuvāraśca dhūpāḥ prītikarā matāḥ|| 2.54.30 ||
aṅgarāgeṣu sindūraṃ devyāḥ prītikaraṃ param|
sugandhi śālijaṃ cānnaṃ madhumāṃsasamanvitam|| 2.54.31 ||
apūpaṃ pāyasaṃ kṣīramannaṃ devyāḥ praśasyate|
ratnodakaṃ sakarpūraṃ piṇḍītakakumārakau || 2.54.32 ||
rocanaṃ puṣpakaṃ devyāḥ snānīyaṃ parikīrtitam|
ghṛtapradīpo dīpeṣu praśastaḥ parikīrtitaḥ|| 2.54.33 ||
puṣpāñjalitrayaṃ dadyād mūlamantreṇa śobhanam|
dattvopacārānakhilānmadhye caitāḥ prapūjayet|| 2.54. 34 ||
kāmeśvarīṃ guptadurgāṃ vindhyakandaravāsinīm|
koṭeśvarī dīrghikākhyāṃ prakaṭīṃ bhavaneśvarīm || 2.54. 35 ||
ākāśagaṅgāṃ kāmākhyāṃ yadā dikkaravāsinīm |
mātaṅgī lalitāṃ durgāṃ bhairavī siddhidāṃ tathā|| 2.54.36 ||
balapramathinīṃ caṇḍīṃ caṇḍogrāṃ caṇḍanāyikām|
ugrāṃ bhīmāṃ śivāṃ śāntāṃ jayantīṃ kālikāṃ tathā|| 2.54.37 ||
maṅgalāṃ bhadrakālīṃ ca ca śivāṃ dhātrīṃ kapālinīm |
svāhāṃ svadhāmaparṇāṃ ca pañcapuṣkariṇīṃ tathā|| 2.54.37 ||
madanīṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ manaḥ protsāhakāriṇīm |
damanīṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ catuḥṣaṣṭiṃ ca yoginīḥ|| 2.54.39 ||
etāḥ sampūjya madhye tu mantreṇāṅgāni pūjayet|
hṛcchirastu śikhāvarmanetrabāhupadāni ca|| 2.54.40 ||
mūlamantrādyakṣaraistu tribhirādyaṅgapūjanam|
ekaikaṃ varddhayet paścānmantrāṇyaṅgaughapūjane|| 2.54.41 ||
siddhasūtraṃ ca khaḍgaṃ ca khaḍgamantreṇa pūjayet|
tato’ṣṭapatramadhye tu pūjayedaṣṭayoginīḥ|| 2.54.42 ||
śailaputrīṃ caṇḍaghaṇṭāṃ skandamātarameva ca|
kālarātriṃ ca pūrvādicaturdikṣu prapūjayet|| 2.54.43 ||
caṇḍikāmatha kūṣmāṇḍīṃ tatā kātyāyanīṃ śubhām|
mahāgaurīṃ cāgnikoṇe taiṛtyādiṣu pūjayet|| 2.54.44 ||
mahāmāyāṃ kṣamasveti mūlamantreṇa cāṣṭadhā|
pūjayet padmamadhye tu balidānaṃ tataḥ param|| 2.54.45 ||
evaṃ yadā kalpavidhānamānaiḥ sampūjyate bhairava kāmadevī|
tadā svayaṃ maṇḍalametya deyaṃ gṛhṇāti kāmaṃ ca dadāti samyak|| 2.54.46 ||
iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe aṣṭādaśapaṭaloddhāre mahāmāyākalpacatuḥpañcāśo’dhyāyaḥ||

<55>
||śrībhagavānuvāca||
balidānaṃ tataḥ paścāt kuryād devyāḥ pramodakam |
modakairgajavaktraṃ ca haviṣā toṣayedravim || 55.1 ||
tauryatrikaiśca niyamaiḥ śaṅkaraṃ toṣayeddharim |
caṇḍikāṃ balidānena toṣayet sādhakaḥ sadā|| 55.2 ||
pakṣiṇaḥ kacchapā grāhāśchāgalāśca varāhakāḥ|
mahiṣo godhikāśoṣā tatā navavidhā mṛgāḥ|| 55.3 ||
cāmaraḥ kṛṣṇasāraśca śaśaḥ pañcānanastathā|
matsyāḥ svagātrarudhiraiścāṣṭadhā balayo matāḥ || 55.4 ||
abhāve ca tathaivaiṣāṃ kadāciddhayahastinau|
chāgalāḥ śarabhāścaiva naraścaiva yathākramāt|| 55.5 ||
balirmahābaliriti balayaḥ parikīrtitatāḥ|
snāpayitvā baliṃ tatra puṣpacandanadhūpakaiḥ || 55.6 ||
pūjayet sādhako devīṃ balimantrairmuhurmmuhuḥ|
uttarābhimukho bhūtvā baliṃ pūrvamukhaṃ tathā|| 55.7 ||
nirīkṣya sādhakaḥ paścādimaṃ mantramudīrayet|
varastvaṃ balirūpeṇa mama bhāgyādupasthitaḥ|| 55.8 ||
praṇamāmi tataḥ sarvarūpiṇaṃ balirūpiṇam|
caṇḍikā prītidānena dāturāpadvināśanaḥ || 55.9||
vaiṣṇavībalirūpāya bale tubhyaṃ namo namaḥ |
yajñārthe paśavaḥ sṛṣṭāḥ svayameva svayambhuvā|| 55.10 ||
atastvāṃ ghātayāmyadya tasmād yajñe vadho’vadhaḥ|
aiṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ iti mantreṇa taṃ baliṃ kāmarūpiṇam || 55.11 ||
cintayitvā nyaset puṣpaṃ mūrdhni tasya ca bhairava|
tato devīṃ samuddiśya kāmamuddiśya cātmanaḥ|| 55.12 ||
abhiṣicca baliṃ paścāt karavālaṃ prapūjyet|
rasanā tvaṃ caṇḍikāyāḥ suralokaprasādhaka || 55.13 ||
aiṃ hrīṃ śrīmiti mantreṇa dhyātvā khaḍgaṃ prapūjayet|
kṛṣṇaṃ pinākapāṇiṃ ca kālarātrisvarūpiṇam|| 55.14 ||
ugraṃ raktāsyanayanaṃ raktamālyānulepanam|
raktāmbaradharaṃ caikaṃ pāśahastaṃ kuṭumbinam|| 55.15 ||
pīyamānaṃ ca rudhiraṃ bhuñjānaṃ kravyasaṃhatim |
asirviśasanaḥ khaḍgastīkṣṇadhāro durāsadaḥ|| 55.16 ||
śrīgarvo vijayaścaiva dharmapāla namo’stu te|
pūjayitvā tataḥ khaḍgaṃ oṃ āṃ hrīṃ phaḍitimantrakaiḥ|| 55.17 ||
gṛhītvā vimalaṃ kaḍgaṃ chedayed balimuttamam|
tato balīnāṃ rudhiraṃ toyasaindhavasatphalaiḥ|| 55.18 ||
madhubhirgandhapuṣpaiśca adhivāsya prayatnataḥ|
oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ kauśikīti rudhiraṃ dāpayāmi te || 55.19 ||
sthāne niyojayedraktaṃ śiraśca sapradīpakam|
evaṃ dattvā baliṃ pūrṇaṃ phalaṃ prāpnoti sādhakaḥ|| 55.20 ||
hīnaṃ syāddhīnatāmūlaṃ niṣphalaṃ syād viparyayāt|
balidāne tu durgāyā anyatrāpi vidhiḥ sadā|| 55.21 ||
ayameva prayoktavyaḥ sadbhirvetālabhairavau|
japaṃ samārabhet paścāt pūrvavaddhyānamānamāsthitaḥ || 55.22 ||
hastena srajamādāya cintayenmanasā śivām|
cintayitvā guruṃ mūrdhni yathā varṇādikaṃ bhavet|| 55.23 ||
mantraṃ ca kaṇṭhato dhyātvā sitavarṇaṃ hiraṇmayam|
mahāmāyāṃ ca hṛdaye ātmānaṃ gurupādayoḥ|| 55.24 ||
ācakṣeta tataḥ paścād gurormantrasya cātmanaḥ|
devyāścāpyekatāṃ dhyātvā suṣumnāvartmanā tataḥ|| 55.25 ||
tattvasvarūpamekaṃ tu ṣaṭcakraṃ prati lambayet|
ṣaṭcakre’pi mahāmāyāṃ kṣaṇaṃ dhyātvā prayatnataḥ|| 55.26 ||
lambayenmūlamātreṇa vādiṣoḍaśacakrakam|
ādiṣoḍaśacakrasthāṃ sādhakānandakāriṇīm|| 55.27 ||
cintayan sādhako devīṃ japakarma samārabhet|
bhravorupari nāḍīnāṃ trayāṇāṃ prānta ucyate|| 55.28 ||
tatprāntaṃ tripathasthānaṃ ṣaṭkoṇaṃ caturaṅgulam|
raktavarṇaṃ tu yogajñairājñācakramitīryate|| 55.29 ||
kaṇṭhe trayāṇāṃ nāḍīnāṃ veṣṭhanaṃ vidyate nṛṇām|
suṣumneḍāpiṅgalānāṃ ṣaṭkoṇaṃ tatṣaḍaṅgulam|| 55.30 ||
tat ṣaṭcakramiti proktaṃ śuklaṃ kaṇṭhasya madyagam |
trayāṇāmatha nīḍīnāṃ hṛdaye caikatā bhavet|| 55.31 ||
tatsthānaṃ ṣoḍaśāraṃ syāt saptāṅgulapramāṇataḥ|
tatprayuktaṃ tu yogajñairādiṣoḍaśacakrakam|| 55.32 ||
dhyānānāmatha mantrāṇāṃ cintanasya japasya ca|
yasmādādyaṃ tu hṛdayaṃ tasmādādīti gadyate|| 55.33 ||
japādau pūjayenmālāṃ toyairabhyukṣya yatnataḥ|
nidhāya maṇḍalasyāntaḥ savyahastagatāṃ ca vā|| 55.34 ||
oṃ māle māle mahāmāye sarvaśaktisvarūpiṇi|
caturvargastvayi nyastastasmānme siddhidā bhava|| 55.35 ||
pūjayitvā tato mālāṃ gṛhṇīyād dakṣiṇe kare|
madhyamāyā madhyabhāge varjayitvātha tarjanīm|| 55.36 ||
anāmikākaniṣṭhābhyāṃ yutāyā namrabhāgataḥ|
sthāpayitvā tatra mālāmaṅguṣṭhāgreṇa tadgatam|| 55.37 ||
pratyekaṃ bījamādāya japyādardhena bhairava|
prativāraṃ paṭhenmantraṃ śanairoṣṭhaṃ ca cālayet || 55.38 ||
mālābījaṃ tu japtavyaṃ spṛśennahi parasparam|
pūrvajāpaprayuktena naivāṅguṣṭhena bhairava|| 55.39 ||
pūrvabījaṃ japan yastu parabījaṃ ca saṃspṛśet|
aṅguṣṭhena bhavet tasya niṣphalastasya tajjapaḥ|| 55.40 ||
mālāṃ svahṛdayāsanne dhṛtvā dakṣiṇapāṇinā|
devīṃ vicintayan japyaṃ kuryād vāmena na spṛśet|| 55.41 ||
sphaṭikendrākṣarudrākṣaiḥ putrañjīvasamudbhavaiḥ|
suvarṇamaṇibhiḥ samyak pravālairathavābjajaiḥ || 55.42 ||
akṣamālā tu kartavyā devīprītikarī parā|
japedupāṃśu satataṃ kuśagranthyātha pāṇinā|| 55.43 ||
mālābījeṣu sarveṣu rudrākṣo matpriyāpriyaḥ|
rudraprītikarī yasmāt tena rudrākṣarocanī|| 55.44 ||
pravālairathavā kuryādaṣṭāviṃśatibījakaiḥ|
pañcapañcāśatā vāpi na nyūnairadhikaiśca vā || 55.45 ||
rudrākṣaryadi japyeta indrākṣaiḥ sphaṭikaistathā|
nānyaṃ madhye prayoktavyaṃ putrañjīvādikaṃ ca yat|| 55.46 ||
yadyanyat tu prayujyeta mālāyāṃ japakarmaṇi|
tasya kāmaṃ ca mokṣaṃ ca dadāti na priyaṅkarī|| 55.47 ||
miśrībhāvaṃ tato yāti cāṇḍālaiḥ pāpakarmabhiḥ|
janmāntare jāyate sa vedavedāṅgapāragaḥ|| 55.48 ||

eko merustatra deyaḥ sarvebhyaḥ sthūlasambhavaḥ|
ādyaṃ sthūlaṃ tatastasmād nyūnaṃ nyūnataraṃ tathā|| 55.49 ||
vinyaset kramatastasmāt sarpākārā hi sā yataḥ|
brahmagranthiyutaṃ kuryāt pratibījaṃ yathāsthitam|| 55.50 ||
athavā granthirahitaṃ dṛḍharajjusamanvitam|
dvirāvṛtyātha madhyena cārdhavṛtyāntadeśataḥ|| 55.51 ||
granthiḥ pradakṣiṇāvartaḥ sa brahmagranthisaṃjñakaḥ|
ātmanā yojayenmālāṃ nāmantro yojayennaraḥ || 55.52 ||
dṛḍhaṃ sūtraṃ niyuñjīta jape truṭyati no yathā|
yathā hastānna cyaveta japataḥ srak tamācaret || 55.53 ||
hastacyutāyāṃ vighnaṃ syācchinnāyāṃ maraṇaṃ bhavet|
evaṃ yaḥ kurute mālāṃ japaṃ ca japakovidaḥ || 55.54 ||
sa prāpnotīpsitaṃ kāmaṃ hīne syāt tu viparyayaḥ|
anyatrāpi japenmālāṃ japyaṃ devamanoharam|| 55.55 ||
tādṛśaḥ sādhakaḥ kuryānnānyathā tu kadācana|
yathāśakti japaṃ kuryāt saṅkhyayaiva prayatnataḥ|| 55.56 ||
asaṅkhyātaṃ ca yajjaptaṃ tasya tanniṣphalaṃ bhavet|
japtvā mālāṃ śirodeśe prāṃśusthāne’tha vā nyaset|| 55.57 ||
stutipāṭhaṃ tataḥ kuryādiṣṭaṃ kāmaṃ nivedya ca|
statiścāpi mahāmantraṃ sādhanaṃ sarvakarmaṇām|| 55.58 ||
vakṣye yuvāṃ mahābhāgau sarvasiddhipradāyakam|
sarvamaṅgalamaṅgalye śive sarvārthasādhike|| 55.59 ||
śaraṇye tryambake gauri nārāyaṇi namo’stu te|
saptadāvartanaṃ kṛtvā stutimenāṃ ca sādhakaḥ|| 55.60 ||
pañcapraṇāmān kṛtvātha aiṃ hrīṃ śrīmitimantrakaiḥ|
anyeṣāṃ purataścaiva adhikaṃ vā yathecchayā|| 55.61 ||
yonimudrāṃ tataḥ paścād darśayitvā visarjayet|
dvau pāṇi prasṛtīkṛtya kṛtvā cottānjalim|| 55.62 ||
aṅguṣṭhāgradvaṃya nyasya kaniṣṭhāgradvayostataḥ|
anāmikāyāṃ vāmasya tatkaniṣṭhāṃ puro nyaset || 55.63 ||
dakṣiṇasyānāmikāyāṃ kaniṣṭhāṃ dakṣiṇasya ca|
anāmikāyāḥ pṛṣṭhe tu madhyame dve niveśayet|| 55.64 ||
dve tarjanyau kaniṣṭhāgre tadagreṇaiva yojayet|
yonimudrā samākhyātā devyāḥ prītikarī matā|| 55.65 ||
trivāraṃ darśayet tāṃ tu mūlamantreṇa sādhakaḥ|
tāṃ mudrāṃ śirasi nyasya maṇḍalaṃ vinyaset tataḥ|| 55.66 ||
aiśānyāmagrahastena dvārapadmavivarjitam|
tatra natvā raktacaṇḍāṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ mantreṇa sādhakaḥ|| 55.67 ||
raktacaṇḍāyai nama iti nirmālyaṃ tatra nikṣipet |
udake tarumūle vā nirmālyaṃ tatra saṃtyajet|| 55.68 ||
evaṃ yaḥ pūjayed devīṃ vidhānena śivāṃ naraḥ|
so’cireṇa labhetkāmān sarvāneva manogatān|| 55.69 ||
ardhalakṣajapaṃ japtvā prathamaṃ caiva sādhakaḥ |
puraścared viśeṣeṇa nānānaivedyavedanaiḥ|| 55.70 ||
kuṇḍaṃ maṇḍalavat kṛtvā cāṣṭamyāṃ samupoṣitaḥ|
navamyāṃ śuklapakṣasya rajobhiḥ pañcabhirnaraḥ || 55.71 ||
pūrvavanmaṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā gurupitrośca sannidhau|
anenaiva vidhānena pūjayitvā tu caṇḍikām|| 55.72 ||
sahitairbilvapatraiśca aṣṭottaraśatatrayam|
tilairhomaṃ caret tasyāṃ sahasratritayaṃ japet|| 55.73 ||
naivedyaṃ gandhapuṣpe ca vastraṃ dadyāccha yatpriyam|
pūrvoktaṃ cānyadapyasyai pradadyāt pāyasaṃ tathā|| 55.74 ||
pūjāvasāne deyaṃ syāt tajjātīyaṃ balitrayam|
sindūraṃ svarṇaratnāni strīṇāṃ vibhūṣaṇam|| 55.75 ||
nivedayed yathāśaktyā puṣpamālyaṃ ca bhūriśaḥ|
mahāśaktuṃ saśālyannaṃ gavyavyañjanasaṃyutam|| 55.76 ||
devyai navamyāṃ sampūrṇaṃ baliṃ dadyād ghṛtādibhiḥ|
dakṣiṇāṃ gurave dadyāt suvarṇaṃ gāṃ tathā tilam|| 55.77 ||
abhiśaptamaputraṃ ca sāvadyaṃ kitavaṃ tathā|
kriyāhīnamakalpajñaṃ vāmanaṃ gurunindakam|| 55.78 ||
sadā matsarasaṃyuktaṃ guruṃ mantreṣu varjayet|
gururmantrasya mūlaṃ syānmūlaśuddhau tadudgatam|| 55.79 ||
saphalaṃ jāyate yasmānmantraṃ yatnātparīkṣayet|
śāṭhyāt krodhāttu mohādvā nāsanmatyā gurormukhāt|| 55.80 ||
kalpeṣu dṛṣṭvā vā mantraṃ gṛhṇīyācchadmanā’tha vā|
sa mantrasteya pāpena tāmisre narake naraḥ|| 55.81 ||
manvantaratrayaṃ sthitvā pāpayoniṣu jāyate|
śaṭhe krūre ca mūrkhe ca chadmakāriṇyabhaktike|| 55.82 ||
mantraṃ na dūṣite dadyāt subījaṃ vipine tathā |
lakṣeṇa sādhayet kāmaṃ puraścaraṇapūrvakam|| 55.83 ||
pāpakṣayo bhaved yasmāt puraścaraṇakarmaṇā|
lakṣadvayena mantrasya japena narasattamau|| 55.84 ||
trisandhyāsuta pratidinaṃ bījasaṃghātakena ca|
kavirvāgmī paṇḍitaśca yaśasvī ca prajāyate|| 55.85 ||
sādhakaḥ sādhakaśreṣṭha pūjāsthānaṃ tataḥ śṛṇu|
yatra yatra naraḥ pūjāṃ nirjane kurute ca yaḥ || 55.86 ||
tasyādatte svayaṃ devī patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ jalam|
śilā praśastā pūjāyāṃ sthaṇḍilaṃ nirjanaṃ tathā|| 55.87 ||
japaścopāṃśu sarveṣāmuttamaḥ parikīrtitaḥ|
aśucirna mahāmāyāṃ pūjayet tu kadācana|| 55.88 ||
avaśyaṃ tu smarenmantraṃ yo’tibhaktiyuto naraḥ|
dantarakte samutpanne smaraṇaṃ ca na vidyate|| 55.89 ||
sarveṣāmeva mantrāṇāṃ smaraṇannarakaṃ vrajet|
jānūrdhve kṣataje jāte nityaṃ karma na cācaret|| 55.90 ||
naimittikaṃ ca tadadhaḥ sravadrakto na cācaret|
sūtake ca samutpanne kṣurakarmaṇi maithune|| 55.91 ||
dhūmodgāre tatā vānte nityakarmāṇi saṃtyajet|
dravye bhukte tvajīrṇe ca na vai bhuktvā ca kiñcana|| 55.92 ||
karma kuryānnaro nityaṃ sūtake mṛtake tathā|
patraṃ puṣpaṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ bheṣajatvena kalpitam|| 55.93 ||
kaṇādipippalyantaṃ ca phalaṃ bhuktvā na cācaret|
jalasyāpi naraśreṣṭha bhojanād bheṣajādṛte|| 55.94 ||
nityakriyā nivarteta saha naimittikaiḥ sadā|
jalaukāṃ gūḍhapādaṃ ca kṛmigaṇḍūpadādikam|| 55.95 ||
kāmāddhastena saṃspṛśya nityakarmāṇi saṃtyajet|
viśeṣataḥ śivāpūjāṃ pramītapitṛko naraḥ || 55.96 ||
yāvad vatsaraparyantaṃ manasāpi na cācaret|
mahāgurunipāte tu kāmyaṃ kiñcinna cācaret|| 55.97 ||
ārtvijyaṃ brahmayajñaṃ ca śrāddhaṃ devayajaṃ ca yat|
gurumākṣipya vipraṃ ca prahṛtyaiva ca pāṇinā|| 55.98 ||
na kuryānnityakarmāṇi retaḥpāte ca bhairava|
āsanaṃ cārdhyapātraṃ ca bhagnamāsādayennatu || 55.99 ||
ūṣare kṛmisaṃyukte sthāne mṛṣṭe’pi nārcayet|
nīcairāsanamāsādya śuciḥ prayatamānasaḥ|| 55.100 ||
arcayeccaṇḍikāṃ devīṃ devamanyaṃ ca bhairava|
digvibhāge tu kauberī dik chivā pratidāyinī || 55.101 ||
tasmāt tanmukha āsīnaḥ pūjayeccaṇḍikāṃ sadā|
puṣpaṃ ca kṛmisaṃmiśraṃ viśīrṇaṃ bhagnamṛdgate|| 55.102 ||
sakeśa mūṣikoddhūtaṃ yatnena parivarjayet|
yācitaṃ parakīyaṃ ca tathā paryuṣitaṃ ca yat|
antyasṛṣṭaṃ padā spṛṣṭaṃ yatnena parivarjayet|| 55.103 ||
idaṃ śivāyāḥ paramaṃ manoharaṃ karoti yo’nena tadīyapūjanam|
sa vāñchitārthaṃ samavāpya caṇḍikāgṛhaṃ prayātā nacireṇa bhairava|| 55.104 ||
iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe aurvva sagarasaṃvāde mahāmāyākalpaḥ pañcapañcāśo’dhyāyaḥ||

<56>
||śrībhagavānuvāca||
asya mantrasya kavacaṃ śṛṇu vetālabhairava|
vaiṣṇavītantrasaṃjñasya vaiṣṇavyāśca viśeṣataḥ|| 56.0 ||
tatra mantrādyakṣaraṃ tu vāsudevasvarūpadhṛk|
varṇo dvitīyo brahmaiva tṛtīyaścandraśekharaḥ|| 56.2 ||
caturtho gajavaktraśca pañcamastu divākaraḥ|
śaktiḥ svayaṃ pakāraśca mahāmāyā jaganmayī|| 56.3 ||
yakārastu mahālakṣmīḥ śeṣavarṇaḥ sarasvato|
yoginīpūrvavarṇasya śailaputrī prakīrtitā|| 56.4 ||
dvitīyasya tu varṇasya caṇḍikā yoginī matā|
candraghaṇṭā tṛtīyasya kuṣmāṇḍī tat parasya ca|| 56.5 ||
skandamātā takārasya pasya kātyāyanī svayam|
kālarātriḥ saptamasya mahādevīti saṃsthitā|| 56.6 ||
prathamaṃ varṇakavacaṃ yoginīkavacaṃ tathā tataḥ |
devaughakavacaṃ paścād devīdikkavacaṃ tathā|| 56.7 ||
tatastu pārśvakavacaṃ dvitīyāntāvyasya ca|
kavacaṃ tu tataḥ paścāt ṣaḍvarṇaṃ kavacaṃ tathā|| 56.8 ||
abhedyakavacaṃ ceti sarvatrāṇaparāyaṇam|
imāni kavacānyaṣṭau yo jānāti narottamaḥ|| 56.9 ||
so’hameva mahādevī devīrūpaśca śaktimān|
asya vaiṣṇavītantrakavacasya nāradaṛṣi ranuṣṭupchandaḥ|| 56.00 ||
kātyāyanī devatā sarvakāmārthasādhane viniyogaḥ|
aḥ pātu pūrvakāṣṭhāyāmāgneyyāṃ pātu kaḥ sadā|| 56.00 ||
pātu co yamakāṣṭhāyāṃ do naiṛtyāṃ ca sarvadā|
māṃ pātu to’sau pāścātye śaktirvāyavyadiggatā|| 56.02 ||
yaḥ pātu māṃ cottarasyāmaiśānyāṃ yastathāvatu|
mūrdhni rakṣatu māṃ so’sau bāhau māṃ dakṣiṇe tu kaḥ|| 56.03 ||
māṃ vāmabāhau caḥ pātu hṛdi ṭo māṃ sadāvatu|
taḥ pātu kaṇṭhadeśe māṃ kaṭyoḥ śaktistathāvatu|| 56.04 ||
yaḥ pātu dakṣiṇe pāde ṣo māṃ vāmapāde tathā|
śailaputrī tu pūrvasyāmāgneyyāṃ pātu caṇḍikā|| 56.05 ||
candraghaṇṭā pātu yāmyāṃ yamabhītivivardhinī|
nairṛtye tvatha kūṣmāṇḍī pātu māṃ jagatāṃ prasūḥ|| 56.06 ||
skandamātā paścimāyāṃ māṃ rakṣatu sadaiva hi|
kātyāyanī māṃ vāyavye pātu lokeśvarī sadā|| 56.07 ||
kālarātrī tu kauberyāṃ sadā rakṣatu māṃ svayam|
mahāgaurī tathaiśānyāṃ satataṃ pātu pāvanī|| 56.08 ||
netrayorvāsudevo māṃ pātu nityaṃ sanātanaḥ|
brahmā māṃ pātu vadane padmayonirayonijaḥ|| 56.09 ||
nāsābhāge rakṣatu māṃ sarvadā candraśekharaḥ|
gajavaktraḥ stanayugme pātu nityaṃ harātmajaḥ|| 56.20 ||
vāmadakṣiṇapāṇyormāṃ nityaṃ pātu divākaraḥ|
mahāmāyā svayaṃ nābhau māṃ pātu parameśvarī|| 56.20 ||
mahālakṣmīḥ pātu guhye jānunośca sarasvatī|
mahāmāyā pūrvabhāge nityaṃ rakṣatu māṃ śubhā|| 56.22 ||
agnijvālā tathāgneyyāṃ pāyānnityaṃ varāsinī|
rudrāṇī pātu māṃ yāmyāṃ nairṛtyāṃ caṇḍanāyikā|| 56.23 ||
ugracaṇḍā paścimāyāṃ pātu nityaṃ maheśvarī|
pracaṇḍā pātu vāyavye kauberyāṃ ghorarūpiṇī || 56.24 ||
īśvarī ca tathaiśānyāṃ pātu nityaṃ sanātanī|
ūrdhvaṃ pātu mahāmāyā pātvadhaḥ parameśvarī|| 56.25 ||
agrataḥ pātu māmugrā pṛṣṭhato vaiṣṇavī tathā|
brahmāṇī dakṣiṇe pārśve nityaṃ rakṣatu śobhanā|| 56.26 ||
māheśvarī vāmapārśve nityaṃ pāyād vṛṣadhvajā|
kaumārī parvate pātu vārāhī salile ca mām || 56.27 ||
nārasiṃhī daṃṣṭibhaye pātu māṃ vipineṣu ca|
aindrī māṃ pātu cākāśe tathā sarvajale sthale|| 56.28 ||
setuḥ sarvāṅgulīḥ pātu devādiḥ pātu karṇayoḥ|
devāntaścibuke pātu pārśvayoḥ śaktipañcamaḥ|| 56.29 ||
hā pātu māṃ tathaivorvormāyā rakṣatu jaṅghayoḥ|
sarvaindriyāṇi yaḥ pātu romakūpeṣu sarvadā|| 56.30 ||
tvaci māṃ vai sadā pātu māṃ śambhuḥ pātu sarvadā|
nakhadantakaroṣṭhādau rām̐ māṃ pātu sadaiva hi|| 56.30 ||
devādiḥ pātu māṃ vastau devāntaḥ stanakakṣayoḥ|
etadādau tu yaḥ seturbāhye māṃ pātu dehataḥ|| 56.32 ||
ājñācakre suṣamnāyāṃ ṣaṭcakre hṛdi sandhiṣu|
ādiṣoḍaśacakre ca lalāṭākāśa eva ca|| 56.33 ||
vaiṣṇavī tantramantro māṃ nityaṃ rakṣaṃśca tiṣṭhatu|
karṇanāḍīṣu sarvāsu pārśvakakṣaśikhāsu ca|| 56.34 ||
rudhirasnāyumajjāsu mastiṣkeṣu ca parvasu|
dvitīyāṣṭākṣaro mantraḥ kavacaṃ pātu sarvataḥ|| 56.35 ||
reto vāyau nābhirandhre pṛṣṭhasandhiṣu sarvataḥ|
ṣaḍakṣarastṛtīyo’yaṃ mantro māṃ pātu sarvadā|| 56.36 ||
nāsārandhre mahāmāyā kaṇṭharandhre tu vaiṣṇavī|
sarvasandhiṣu māṃ pātu durgā durgātihāriṇī|| 56.37 ||
śrotrayorhūṃ phaḍityevaṃ nityaṃ rakṣatu kālikā|
netrabījatrayaṃ netre sadā tiṣṭhatu rakṣitum|| 56.38 ||
oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ hraiṃ nāsikāyāṃ rakṣantī cāstu caṇḍikā|
oṃ hrīṃ hūṃ māṃ sadā tārā jihvāmūle tu tiṣṭhatu || 56.39 ||
hṛdi tiṣṭhatu me seturjñānaṃ rakṣitumuttamam|
oṃ kṣauṃ phaṭ ca mahāmāyā pātu māṃ sarvataḥ sadā || 56.40 ||
oṃ yum̐ saḥ prāṇān kauśikī māṃ prāṇān rakṣatu rakṣikā|
oṃ hrīṃ hūṃ sauṃ bhargadayitā dehaśūnyeṣu pātu mām|| 56.40 ||
oṃ namaḥ sadā śailaputrī sarvān rogān pramṛjyatām pramārjanām |
oṃ hrīṃ saḥ spheṃ kṣaḥ phaḍastrāya siṃhavyāghrabhayādraṇāt|| 56.42 ||
śivadūtī pātu nityaṃ hrīṃ sarvāstreṣu tiṣṭhat|
oṃ hāṃ hīṃ saścaṇḍaghaṇṭā karṇacchidreṣu pātu mām|| 56.43 ||
oṃ krīṃ saḥ kāmeśvarī kāmānabhitiṣṭhatu rakṣatu|
oṃ āṃ haṃ phaḍugracaṇḍā ripūn vighnān vimardatām|| 56.44 ||
o paṃ pātu nārasiṃhī māṃ kravyādebhyasrayāstrataḥ|
oṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ hrāṃ hram̐ kālarātriḥ khaḍgādrakṣatu māṃ sadā||
oṃ aṃ śūlāt pātu nityaṃ vaiṣṇavī jagadīśvarī|
oṃ kaṃ brahmāṇī pātu cakrāt caṃ rudrāṇī tu śaktitaḥ|| 56.45 ||
oṃ ṭaṃ kaumārī pātu vajrāt oṃ taṃ vārāhī tu kāṇḍataḥ|
oṃ paṃ pātu nārasiṃhī māṃ kravyādebhyastathāstrataḥ|| 56.46 ||
śastrāstrebhyaḥ samastebhyo yantrebhyo’niṣṭamantrataḥ|
caṇḍikā māṃ sāda pātu yaṃ saṃ devyai namo namaḥ|
viśvāsaghātakebhyo māmaindrī rakṣatu manmanaḥ || 56.47 ||
oṃ namo mahāmāyāyai oṃ vaiṣṇavyai name namaḥ|
rakṣa māṃ sarvabhūtebhyaḥ sarvatra parameśvari|| 56.48 ||
ādhāre vāyumārge hṛdi kamaladale cāndravat smerasūrye |
vastau vahnau samiddhe viśatuvaradayā mantramaṣṭākṣarantat|
yadbrahmā mūrdhni dhatte hariravati gale candracūḍo hṛdisthaṃ
taṃ māṃ pātu pradhānaṃ nikhilamatiśayaṃ padmagarbhābhabījam|| 56.49 ||
ādyāḥ śeṣāḥ svaraughairma mayavalavarairasvareṇāpi
yuktaiḥ sānusvārāvisargairhariharaviditaṃ yatsahasraṃ ca sāṣṭam|
mantrāṇāṃ setubandhaṃ nivasati satataṃ vaiṣṇavītantramantre
tanmāṃ pāyātpavitraṃ paramaparamajaṃ bhūjala bhūtalavyomabhāge|| 56.50 ||
aṅgānyaṣṭau tatāṣṭau vasava iha tathaivāṣṭamūrtirdalāni
proktānyaṣṭau tathāṣṭau madhumatiracitāḥ siddhayo’ṣṭau tathaiva|
aṣṭāvaṣṭāṣṭasaṃkhyā jagati ratikalāḥ kṣiprakāṣṭhāṅgayogā
mayyaṣṭāvakṣarāṇi kṣaratu na hi gaṇo yaddhṛdo yastvamūṣām || 56.50 ||
iyi tatkavacaṃ proktaṃ dharmakāmārthasādhanam |
idaṃ rahasyaṃ paramamidaṃ sarvārthasādhakam|| 56.52 ||
yaḥ sakṛcchṛṇuyādetat kavacaṃ mayakoditam|
sa sarvāṃllabhate kāmān paratra śivarūpatām|| 56.53 ||
sakṛd yastu paṭhedetat kavacaṃ mayakoditam|
sa sarvayajñasya phalaṃ labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ|| 56.54 ||
saṅgrāmeṣu jayecchatruṃ mātaṅgāniva keśarī|
dahet tṛṇaṃ yatāvahnistathā śatruṃ dahet sadā|| 56.55 ||
nāstrāṇi tasya śastrāṇi śarīre praviśanti vai|
na tasya jāyate vyādhirna ca duḥkhaṃ kadācana|| 56.56 ||
guṭikāñjanapātālapādaleparasāñjanam|
uccāṭanādyāstāḥ sarvāḥ prasīdanti ca siddhayaḥ|| 56.57 ||
vāyoriva gatistasya bhavedanyairavāritā|
dīrghāyuḥ kāmabhogī ca dhanavānabhijāyate|| 56.58 ||
aṣṭamyāṃ saṃyato bhūtvā navamyāṃ vidhivacchivām|
pūjayitvā vidhānena vicintya manamā śivām|| 56.59 ||
yo nyaset kavacaṃ deha tasya puṇyaphala śṛṇu|
jitavyādhiḥ śatāyuśca rūpavān guṇavān sadā|| 56.60 ||
dhanaratnaughasampūrṇo vidyāvān sa ca jāyate|
nāgnirdahati tatkāyaṃ nāpaḥ saṅkledayanti ca|| 56.60 ||
na śoṣayati taṃ vāyuḥ kravyāt taṃ na hinasti ca|
śastrāṇi nainaṃ chindanti na tāpayati bhāskaraḥ|| 56.62 ||
na tasya jāyate vighno nāsti tasya ca sañjvaraḥ|
vetālāśca piśācāśca rākṣasā gaṇanāyakāḥ|| 56.63 ||
sarve tasya vaśaṃ yānti bhūtagrāmāścaturvidhāḥ|
nityaṃ paṭhati yo bhaktayā kavacaṃ harinirmitam|| 56.64 ||
so’hameva mahādevo mahāmāyā ca mātṛkā|
dharmārthakāmamokṣāśca tasya nityaṃ kare sthitāḥ|| 56.65 ||
anyasya varadaḥ so’rthairnityaṃ bhavati paṇḍitaḥ|
kavitvaṃ satyavāditvaṃ satataṃ tasya jāyate|| 56.66 ||
vadecchlokasahasrāṇi bhavecchrutidharastathā|
likhitaṃ yasya gehe tu kavacaṃ bhairava sthitam|| 56.67 ||
na tasya durgatiḥ kvāpi jāyate tasya dūṣaṇam|
grahāśca sarve tuṣyanti vaśaṃ gacchanti bhūmipāḥ|| 56.68 ||
yadrājye kavacajño’sti jāyante tatra netayaḥ|
seturdevaḥ śaktibījaṃ pañcamohāya te namaḥ|| 56.69 ||
vāyurbalena caitāyai dvitīyāṣṭākṣaraṃ tvidam|
seturdevo’tha vaiṣṇavyai ṣaḍakṣaramidaṃ smṛtam|| 56.70 ||
etad dvayaṃ tu jihvāgre satataṃ yasya vartate|
tasya devī mahāmāyā kāye tiṣṭhati vai sadā|| 56.70 ||
mantrāṇāṃ praṇavaḥ setustatsetuḥ praṇavaḥ smṛtaḥ|
kṣaratyanoṅkṛtaḥ pūrvaṃ parastācca viśīryate|| 56.72 ||
namaskāro mahāmantro deva ityucyate suraiḥ|
dvijātīnāmayaṃ mantraḥ śūdrāṇāṃ sarvakarmaṇi|| 56.73 ||
akāra cātyukāraṃ ca makāraṃ ca prajāpatiḥ|
vedatrayātsamuddhṛtya praṇavaṃ nirmame purā|| 56.74 ||
sa udātto dvijātīnāṃ rājñāṃ syādanudāttakaḥ|
pracitaścorujātānāṃ manasāpi tathā smaret|| 56.75 ||
caturdaśasvaro yo’sau śeṣa aukārasaṃjñakaḥ|
sa cānusvāracandrābhyāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ seturucyate|| 56.76 ||
niḥsetu ca yathā toyaṃ kṣaṇānnimnaṃ prasarpati|
mantrastathaiva niḥsetuḥ kṣaṇāt kṣarati yajvanām|| 56.77 ||
tasmāt sarvatra mantreṣu caturvarṇā dvijātayaḥ|
pārśvayoḥ setumādāya jatakarmasamārabhet|| 56.78 ||
śūdrāṇāmādiseturvā dviḥseturvā yathecchataḥ|
dviḥsetavaḥ samākhyātāḥ sarvadaiva dvijātayaḥ|| 56.79 ||
||aurvva uvāca||
etat te sarvamākhyātaṃ kavacaṃ tryambakoditam|
abhedyaṃ kavacaṃ tat tu kavacāṣṭakamuttamam|| 56.80 ||
mahāmāyāmantrakalpaṃ kavacaṃ mantrasaṃyutam|
ṣaḍakṣarasamāyuktaṃ triṣu lokeṣu durlabham|| 56.80 ||
etat tvaṃ nṛpaṣārdūla nityabhaktiyutaḥ paṭhan|
japan mantraṃ ca vaiṣṇavyāḥ sarvasiddhimavāpsyasi|| 56.82 ||
iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe mahāmāyāmantrakalpe nāma ṣaṭpañcāśo’dhyāyaḥ ||

<57>
||mārkaṇḍeya uvāca||
śrutvemaṃ sagaro rājā saṃvādaṃ bhairaveṇa vai|
vetālenāpi bhargasya punaraurvvamapṛcchata|| 57.1 ||
||sagara uvāca||
mantraṃ kalevaragataṃ sāṅgaṃ proktaṃ tvayā dvija|
aṅgamantrāṇi me devyāḥ kathyantāṃ bho dvijottama|| 57.2 ||
tathā mantrāṇi sarvāṇi pūjāsthānāni sarvaśaḥ|
tathaivottaramantrāṇi kavacāni pṛthak pṛthak|| 57.3 ||
kāmākhyāyāśca māhātmyaṃ sarahasyaṃ samantrakam|
yathā śaśaṃsa bhagavān mahādeva umāpatiḥ|| 57.4 ||
vetālabhairavābhyāṃ tat samācakṣva savistarāt|
śṛṇvato na hi me tṛptirjāyate mahadadbhutam|| 57.5 ||
bhavatā kathyamānaṃ hi paraṃ kautūhalaṃ mama|
||aurvva uvāca||
śṛṇutvaṃ rājaśārdūla yatputrābhyāmumāpatiḥ|| 57.6 ||
uvāca mahādākhyānaṃ tanme nigadato’dhunā|
etadrahasyaṃ paramaṃ pavitraṃ pāpanāśanam|| 57.7 ||
paraṃ svastyayanaṃ puṃsāṃ garbhe puṃ savanaṃ smṛtam|
kalyāṇakārakaṃ bhadraṃ caturvargaphalapradam|| 57.8 ||
śaṭhāya calacittāya nāstikāyājitātmane|
devadvijagurūṇāṃ ca mithyānirbandhakāriṇe|| 57.9 ||
na pāpāyābhiśastāya khañjakāṇādirogiṇe|
na kathyaṃ na ca vā deyaṃ śraddhāvirahitāya ca|| 57.10 ||
mahāmāyāmantrakalpaṃ proktvā tābhyāmumāpatiḥ|
vetālabhairavābhyāṃ tu punarevābhyabhāṣata|| 57.11 ||
||bhagavānuvāca||
aṅgamantraṃ pravakṣyāmi proktavām̐stantramuttamam |
tadeva prathamaṃ viddhi sarvapūjāsu saṅgatam|| 57.12 ||
ācāntaḥ śucitāṃ prāptaḥ susnāto devapūjane|
pūjāvedyā bahiḥsthitvā caturhastāntare dhiyā|| 57.13 ||
gṛhe vāṃ dvāradeśasthaḥ praṇamya śirasā gurum|
praṇamediṣṭadevaṃ svaṃ dikpālānapi cetasā|| 57.14 ||
yat pūrvamarjitaṃ pāpaṃ taddine’nyadine’pi vā|
prāyaścittairnāpanunnaṃ tacca pāpaṃ smareddhiyā|| 57.15 ||
tatpāpasyāpanodāya mantradvayamudīrayet|
devi tvaṃ prākṛtaṃ cittaṃ pāpākrāntamabhūnmama|| 57.16 ||
tanniḥsāraya cittanme pāpaṃ hūṃ phaṭ ca te namaḥ|
sūryaḥ somo yamaḥ kālo mahābhūtāni pañca vai|| 57.17 ||
ete śubhāśubhasyeha karmaṇo nava sākṣiṇaḥ|
tataḥ punarhūṃ phaḍiti pārśvamūrdhvamadhastathā|| 57.18 ||
ātmānaṃ krodhadṛṣṭyātha nirīkṣya sumanā bhavet|
evaṃ kṛte prathamataḥ pāpotsāraṇakarmaṇi|| 57.19 ||
yat syād dṛḍhataraṃ pāpaṃ tad dūre cāvatiṣṭhate|
atīte pūjane sthānaṃ svaṃ prayāti punaśca yat|| 57.20 ||
yat syādalpataraṃ pāpaṃ tannāśamupagacchati|
oṃ aḥ phaḍitimantreṇa pūjāvedīṃ tato viśet|| 57.21 ||
pūjane tyaktapāpasya kāmamiṣṭaṃ kṣaṇād bhavet|
nārācamudrayā dṛṣṭvā samayā sampralokayet|| 57.22 ||
puṣpanaivedyagandhādi hrīṃ hūm̐ phaḍiti mantrakaiḥ|
yadātmanānavajñātaṃ samyak puṣpādidūṣaṇam|| 57.23 ||
aspṛśyasparśanaṃ vāpi yadanyāyārjitaṃ ca vā|
tathā nirmālyasaṃsṛṣṭaṃ kīṭādyārohaṇaṃ ca yat|| 57.24 ||
tatsarvaṃ nāśamāyāti naivedyādyavalokanāt|
tato ramitimantreṇa śikhāṃ dīpasya saṃspṛśet|| 57.25 ||
sa tasya subhago dīpo bhavet sparśanāmātrataḥ |
pataṅgakīṭakeśādidāhāt kravyādasaṃhataḥ || 57.26 ||
vasāmajjāsthisampūtiryajñādāvupayojanam |
ajñatarūpaṃ tatsarvaṃ doṣaṃ sparśād vināśayet|| 57.27 ||
nārasiṃhena mantreṇa devatīrthena saṃspṛśet|
pānīyaṃ ghaṭamadhyasthaṃ vīkṣannabhyukṣya yājakaḥ|| 57.28 ||
vāmena pāṇinā dhṛtvā vāmapārśve sthitaṃ tadā|
pātramādhāramantreṇa saṃskurvan saṃspṛśejjalam|| 57.29 ||
yajñadānādapeyādi saṃsṛṣṭiriha śaṅgatā|
yadanyad dūṣaṇaṃ pātre toye vā jñānato bhavet|| 57.30 ||
jalāśayaṃ śavasparśājjalaṃ snanācca saṅgatam|
dūṣaṇāni vinaśyanti tāni vai devapūjane|| 57.31 ||
prajāpatisuto hāntaprāntaḥ svarasamanvitaḥ|
candrārdhabindusahito mantro’yaṃ nārasiṃhakaḥ|| 57.32 ||
svasaṃjñādyakṣaraṃ binducandrārdhapariyojitam|
ādhāramantraṃ jānīyāt sādhakaḥ kāryasiddhaye|| 57.33 ||
tata ādhāramantreṇa pāṇibhyāmāsanaṃ svakam|
ādāya vinidhāyāśu punaḥ saṃspṛśya pāṇinā|| 57.34 ||
ātmamantreṇopaviśet tadā tasmin varāsane|
duḥśilpiracitatvādi yadvānyāsana dūṣaṇam|| 57.35 ||
ajñātaṃ vilayaṃ yāti upaveśāt samantrakāt |
āhūya svākṣaraṃ pūrvaṃ somasāmisamanvitam|| 57.36 ||
sabindukaṃ vijānīyādātmamantraṃ tu sādhakaḥ|
tatastu mātṛkānyāsaṃ nādabindusamanvitam|| 57.37 ||
kuryāt tu mātṛkāmantraiḥ svaśarīre vicakṣaṇaḥ|
kalpeṣu ca yadajñātaṃ mantroccāraṇakarmaṇi|| 57.38 ||
yad duṣṭaṃ vā tathā spṛṣṭaṃ mātābhraṣṭādidūṣaṇam|
tannyastā mātṛkāmantrā nāśayanti sadaiva hi|| 57.39 ||
vyañjanāni ca sarvāṇi tathā viṣṇvādayaḥ svarāḥ|
sarve te mātṛkāmantrāścandrabinduvibhūṣaṇā || 57.40 ||
sarve yugāntavandyeṣu nyasteṣu nyūnapūraṇam|
mantre kalpe ca kurvanti vinyastā mātṛkāḥ svayam || 57.41 ||
ekamātro bhaveddhrasvo dvimātro dīrgha ucyate|
plutastrimātro vijñeyo varṇā ete vyavasthitāḥ|| 57.42 ||
sarvaṣāmeva varṇānāṃ mātrādevyastu mātṛkāḥ|
śivadūtīprabhṛtayastannyāsāstattanusthitāḥ|| 57.43 ||
pūrayanti ca tān nyūnāṃścaturvargaṃ tathācirāt|
dadatyeva sadā rakṣāṃ kurvanti surapūjane|| 57.44 ||
caturvargapradaścāyaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradaḥ|
sarvadāmātṛkānyāsastuṣṭipuṣṭipradāyakaḥ|| 57.45 ||
yaḥ kuryād mātṛkānyāsaṃ vināpi surapūjanāt|
tasmād bibheti satataṃ bhūtagrāmaścaturvidhaḥ|| 57.46 ||
taṃ draṣṭumapi devāśca spṛhayanti mahaujasam|
sa sarvaṃ ca vaśaṃ kuryād na ca yāti parābhavam|| 57.47 ||
kusumaṃ viṣṇumantreṇa aṅgulyagreṇa sādhakaḥ|
vimardanārthaṃ gṛhṇīyāta karaśodhanakarmaṇi|| 57.48 ||
upāntaḥ sāmi candreṇa rañjitaḥ śūnyasaṃyutaḥ|
rudrāntoparisaṃsṛṣṭo mantro’yaṃ vaiṣṇavo mataḥ|| 57.49 ||
prāsādena tu mantreṇa aṅgulyagreṇa sādhakaḥ|
gṛhītvā ca tataḥ kuryāt karābhyāṃ puṣpamardanam|| 57.50 ||
nirmathet kāmabījena jighred brāhmeṇa tat punaḥ|
prāsādena parityāgo diśyaiśānyāṃ viśeṣataḥ|| 57.51 ||
evaṃ kṛte tu karayorviśuddhiratulā bhavet|
jalaukāgūḍhapādādisparśācchuddhirviśodhanāt|| 57.52 ||
durgandhyucchiṣṭasaṃsparśād dūṣaṇaṃ karayostu yat|
ajñātarūpaṃ tatsarvaṃ nāśayet suvidhānataḥ|| 57.53 ||
aṅgulyagrāṇi śuddhāni puṣpāṇāṃ grahaṇād bhavet|
taladvayaṃ mardanāt tu viśuddhamabhijāyate|| 57.54 ||
nirmañchanāt pāṇipṛṣṭhaṃ ghrāṇānnāsāgramuttamam|
tīrthāni ca samāyānti nāsikāyāṃ karaṃ prati|| 57.55 ||
tasmād yatnena kāryāṇi karmāṇyetāni bhairava|
prāntādirvāsudevena varṇenāpi ca saṃhitaḥ|| 57.56 ||
śambhucūḍābinduyuktaḥ prāsāda ca sa ucyate|
kāmabījaṃ tu vijñeyaṃ vāsudevendubindubhiḥ|| 57.57 ||
vyañjanaṃ cādyadantaṃ ca prāntadantyā tu pūrvakam|
ādyadantyadvayaṃ paścād vyañjanaṃ praṇavottaram|| 57.58 ||
brahmabījamidaṃ prokta sarvapāpapraṇāśanam|
praṇavaṃ dīrghamuccāryaṃ prathamaṃ mukhaśuddhaye|| 57.59 ||
vāsudevasya bījena prāṇāyāmaṃ samācaret|
yasya devasya yadrūpaṃ tathā bhūṣaṇavāhanam|| 57.60 ||
tadeva pūjane tasya cintayet pūrakādibhiḥ |
vaiṣmavītantramantrasya kaṇṭhādyaṃ yatpuraḥsaram|| 57.61 ||
tad bījaṃ vāsudevasya pūrṇacandranibhaṃ sadā|
gaṅgāvatārabījena prathamaṃ dhenumudrayā|| 57.62 ||
amṛtīkaraṇaṃ kuryādarghapātrāhite jale|
śaśikhaṇḍayutaḥ kaṇṭhyaḥ pañcamībalabījakaḥ || 57.63 ||
gaṅgāvatāramantro’yaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśakaḥ|
mātrādvayayuto viṣṇurbalabījamudāhṛtam|| 57.64 ||
amṛtīkaraṇe vṛtte toyaṃ yad dīyate’mṛtam|
bhūtvā prayāti devasya prīyate surapūjane|| 57.65 ||
gaṅgāpi svayamāyāti pūjāpātrajalaṃ prati|
amṛtīkaraṇaṃ kuryād dharmakāmārthasiddhaye|| 57.66 ||
svastikaṃ gomukhaṃ padmamardhasvastikameva ca|
paryaṅkamāsanaṃ śastamabhīṣṭasurapūjane|| 57.67 ||
pādayantramidaṃ proktaṃ sarvamantrottamottamam|
tad gṛhṇīyād varāhasya bījena prathamaṃ budhaḥ|| 57.68 ||
māyādiragnibījasya caturthaḥ samavyāptikaḥ |
ṣaṣṭhasvaroparicaro vārāhaṃ bījamucyate|| 57.69 ||
vārāhabījasaṃśuddhaṃ mantrapādadvaye kṛtam|
paśyannabhīṣṭadevaṃ tu pādadoṣaṃ na paśyati|| 57.70 ||
na yuktamanyathā pādadarśanaṃ surapūjane|
mantreṇa labhate’bhīṣṭāṃstasmānmantraparo bhavet || 57.71 ||
pāṇikacchapikāṃ kuryāt kūrmamantreṇa sādhakaḥ|
tatra saṃskṛtapuṣpeṇa pūjayedātmano vapuḥ|| 57.72 ||
pūjite tena puṣpeṇa devatvaṃ svasya jāyate|
dvitīyaṃ vaiṣṇavītantraṃ bījaṃ bindvindusaṃyutam|| 57.73 ||
ṣaṣṭhasvaroparicaraṃ kūrmabījaṃ prakīrtitam|
dahanaplavanasyādau randhrasya daśamasya tu|| 57.74 ||
bhedanaṃ sādhakaḥ kuryānmantreṇa praṇavena tu|
bījena vāsudevasya ākāśe vinidhāpayet|| 57.75 ||
prāṇena sahitaṃ bījaṃ tatapūrvaṃ pratipāditam|
ajñātā prayatānāṃ tu maṇḍalasthānamārjanāt|| 57.76 ||
dravyāṇāṃ viprakāraḥ syāt saṃsargāṇāṃ tathaiva ca|
madhukaiṭabhayormedaḥ saṃghātairdṛḍhatāṃ gatā|| 57.77 ||
medinī sarvadā śuddhā surapūjāsu sarvataḥ|
adyāpi sarve tridaśā na spṛśanti padā kṣitim|| 57.78 ||
na ca svīyatanucchāyāṃ yojayanti ca bhūtale|
tasya doṣasya mokṣārthaṃ mantrarājaṃ likhet kṣitau|| 57.79 ||
prokṣaṇād vīkṣaṇād vāpiśuddhā bhavati medinī|
vīkṣaṇaṃ dharmabījena sthaṇḍilasya samācaret|| 57.80 ||
dānto balena saṃyuktaścūḍābindusamanvitaḥ|
dharmabījamiti proktaṃ dharmakāmārthasādhanam|| 57.81 ||
ādānaṃ dhāraṇaṃ tathā saṃsthānapūjane|
pūraṇaṃ saṃlilenaiva niḥkṣepo gandhapuṣpayoḥ|| 57.82 ||
maṇḍalasyātha vinyāsaḥ punaḥ puṣpasya saṃśrayaḥ|
amṛtīkaraṇa pātrapratipattiriyaṃ naraḥ|| 57.83 ||
āniruddhena cādāya astramantreṇa dhāraṇam|
pātre tu maṇḍalanyāsaṃ vāgbījāgreṇa yojayet|| 57.84 ||
āniruddhaṃ bhavedbījamādyaṃ bindudvayottaram |
phaḍantenāniruddhaṃ tu astramantraṃ prakīrtitam|| 57.85 ||
śambhurādyavalaḥ prāntaḥ sampūrṇā sahitā ime|
parataḥ parataḥ pūrvaṃ samāptyantāḥ sabindukāḥ|| 57.86 |
tṛtīyaṃ vāgbhavaṃ bījaṃ sakalaṃ niṣkalāhvatam|
svaraścaturthaḥ sakalaḥ saṃsṛṣṭau bindunendunā|| 57.87 ||
vargādyādirdvitīyaṃ tu vāgbhavaṃ bījamucyate|
kāmarājāhvayaṃ caitad dharmakāmārthasādhanam|| 57.88 ||
manobhavasya bīja tu kuṇḍalīśaktisaṃyutam|
vāsudevena sampṛktamādyaṃ vāgbhavamucyate|| 57.89 ||
idaṃ sārasvataṃ nāma yadādyaṃ vāgbhavaṃ smṛtam|
ekaikaṃ kāmabījādi tribhistu tripurāmahaḥ|| 57.90 ||
ādyaṃ tṛtīyaṃ sāmīndubindubhyaḥ samalaṃkṛtam|
madanasya tu mantro’yaṃ kāmabhogaphalapradaḥ|| 57.91 ||
audetorūpavinyastaṃ yantraṃ bhāskarasannibham|
tad vakṣye kuṇḍalīśaktimabhedāt tu nigadyate|| 57.92 ||
bhūtāpasāraṇaṃ kuryānmantreṇānena yājakaḥ|
yasmin kṛte sthānabhūtā dūraṃ yānti surārcane|| 57.93 ||
sthiteṣu tatra bhūteṣu naivedyamāṇḍalaṃ tathā|
vilumpanti sadā lubdhā na gṛhṇanti ca devatā|| 57.94 ||
tasmād yatnena kartavyaṃ bhūtānāmapasāraṇam|
astramantreṇa sahitaṃ tasya mantramidaṃ smṛtam|| 57.95 ||
apasarpantu te bhūtā ye bhūtā bhūmipālakaḥ|
bhūtānāmavirodhena pūjākarma karomyaham|| 57.96 ||
anena sthaṇḍilād bhūtānapasāryātha sādhakaḥ|
tato digbandhanaṃ kṛtvā digbhyastānapasārayet|| 57.97 ||
viṣṇubījaṃ phaḍantaṃ tu mantraṃ digbandhane sthitam|
kareṇa choṭikāpūrvaṃ veṣṭanaṃ bandhanaṃ diśaḥ|| 57.98 ||
ātmanaḥ pūjanenātha karmārambhādhikāritā|
pūjitaṃ cāsanaṃ yogapīṭhasya sadṛśaṃ bhavet|| 57.99 ||
svabhāvataḥ sadā śuddhaṃ pañcabhūtātmakaṃ vapuḥ|
malapūtisamāyukta śleṣmaviṇmūtrapicchilam|| 57.100 ||
retoniṣṭhīvalālābhiḥ sravadbhirapariṣkṛtam|
bījabhūtāni caitasya mahābhūtāni pañca vai|| 57.101 ||
teṣāṃ tu sarvabhūtānāṃ bījānāṃ dehasaṅginām|
vāyutejaḥpṛthavyambhoviyatāṃ śuddhaye kramāt|| 57.102 ||
śoṣaṇaṃ dahanaṃ bhasmaprotsādo’mṛtavarṣaṇam|
āplāvanaṃ ca kartavyaṃ cintāmātraviśuddhaye|| 57.103 ||
aṇḍasya cintanād bhedāttanmadhye devacintanāt|
svakīyasyeṣṭadevasya cintā sarvātmanā bhavet|| 57.104 ||
so’hamityasya satataṃ cintanād devarūpatā|
ātmano jāyate samyak saṃskṛtiḥ puṣpadānataḥ|| 57.105 ||
ahaṃ devo’tha naivedyaṃ puṣpagandhādikaṃ ca yat|
pūjopakaraṇārthaṃ ca devatvamiha jāyate|| 57.106 ||
devādhāro hyahaṃ devo devaṃ devāya yojayet|
sarveṣāṃ devatāsṛṣṭayā jāyate śuddhatāpi ca|| 57.107 ||
manojīvātmanoḥ śuddhiḥ prāṇāyāmena jāyate|
antargataṃ yacca malaṃ tacca śuddhaṃ prajāyate|| 57.108 ||
gṛhe cet pūjayed devaṃ tadā tasya vilokanam|
kuryādādityabījena catuḥ pārśvaṣvapi kramāt|| 57.109 ||
hāntaḥ samāptisahito vahnibījena saṃhitaḥ|
upāntaḥ sacaturthastu sa tathā sakalo’grataḥ|| 57.110 ||
ādityabījaṃ kathitaṃ sarvarogavināśanam|
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ kāraṇaṃ toṣadāyakam|| 57.111 ||
aśuddhapakṣisaṃyogapakṣiviṣṭhāprasecane|
mūṣikāṇāṃ tathā sparśaḥ kṛmikīṭādisaṅgamaḥ|| 57.112 ||
evamādīni naśyanti lokanād gṛhadūṣaṇam|
tatastu yogapīṭhasya dhyānaṃ prathamataścaret|| 57.113 ||
dhyānamātraṃ yogapīṭhaṃ praviśatyeva maṇḍalam|
yogapīṭhe smṛte sarva yogapīṭhamaya samam|| 57.114 ||
na yogapīṭhādadhikaṃ vidyate paramāsanam|
yasya dhyānājjagad vyāptaṃ sacarācaramānuṣam|| 57.115 ||
taccintanasya māhātmyaṃ ko vā vaktuṃ samutsahet|
cintāmātreṇa mānuṣyaṃ paśya śokavināśanam|| 57.116 ||
dhāraṇād yogapīṭhaṃ tu caturvargaphalapradam|
śuddhaphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ catuṣkoṇaṃ caturvṛtim|| 57.117 ||
ādhāraśaktyā vihitaṃ pragrahaṃ sūryasannibham|
āgneyādiṣu koṇeṣu caturṣu kramataḥ sthitam|| 57.118 ||
dharmo jñānaṃ tathaiśvaryaṃ vairāgyaṃ kramataḥ sadā|
pūrvādidikṣu caitāni sthitāni kramato yathā|| 57.119 ||
adharmaśca tathājñānamanaiśvaryaṃ tataḥ param|
avairāgyaṃ paraṃ tasmāddhāraṇārthaṃ vyavasthitam|| 57.120 ||
tasyopari jalaughastu tasmin brahmaṇḍamāsthitam|
brahmāṇḍābhyantare toyaṃ kūrmastasyopari sthitaḥ|| 57.121 ||
kūrmopari tathānantaḥ pṛthvī tasyopari sthitā|
anantagātrasaṃyuktaṃ nālaṃ pātālagocaram|| 57.122 ||
pṛthvīmadhye sthitaṃ padmaṃ dikpatraṃ girikeśaram|
tasyāṣṭadikṣu dikpālāḥ svargo madhye vyavasthitaḥ|| 57.123 ||
karṇikāyāṃ brahmaloko maharlokādayo hyadhaḥ|
svarge jyotīṃṣi devāśca caturvedāstadantare|| 57.124 ||
sattaṃva rajastama iti guṇāḥ prakṛtisambhavāḥ|
sadā sthitā padmamadhye paraṃ tattvaṃ tathaiva ca|| 57.125 ||
ātmatattvaṃ tatra saṃsthamūrdhvacchadanamūrdhataḥ|
adho’dhaśchadanaṃ tatra keśarāgre sthitaṃ punaḥ|| 57.126 ||
sūryāgnicandramarutā maṇḍalāni kramāt tataḥ|
śāvāsanaṃ yogapīṭhe sukhāsanamataḥ pare|| 57.127 ||
ārādhyāsanamasmācca tataśca vimalāsanam|
madhye vicintayet sarvaṃ jagadvai sacarācaram|| 57.128||
brahmaviṣṇuśivaṃścaiva bhāgatrayaviniścitān|
ātmānaṃ cintayet tatra pūjane samupasthitam|| 57.129 ||
maṇḍalaṃ yogapīṭhaṃ tu padmaṃ padmaṃ tu cintayet|
śāvādīnyāsanānīha catvāryapi vicantayet|| 57.130 ||
yogapīṭhaṃ pṛthagdhyātvā maṇḍalena sahaikatām|
punardhyātvā tataḥ paścāt pūjayedāsanaṃ tataḥ|| 57.131 ||
dhyānena yogapīṭhasya yathā yaddīyate jalam|
naivedyapuṣpadhūpādi tat svayaṃ copatiṣṭhate|| 57.132 ||
sarve devāḥ sagandharvāḥ sacarācaraguhyakāḥ|
cintatāḥ pūjitāśca syuryogapīṭhasya pūjane|| 57.133 ||
abhīṣṭadevatāpūjāṃ vinā yasya vicintanāt|
labhate vai caturvargaṃ tuṣṭiḥ puṣṭhiśca jāyate|| 57.134 ||
āvāhanānantarataḥ pāṇibhyāmavatārayet|
prāguttānau karau kṛtvā ūrdhvamutkṣipya sāntarau|| 57.135 ||
nirantarāvadhaḥ kuryānnāmayan pūjakastathā|
herambasya tu bījena tasmādavatareti ca|| 57.136 ||
āmreḍitena cābhīṣṭadevānāṃ lambanāya vai|
nāsikāvāyuniḥsārādviyatsthā devanā bhavet|| 57.137 ||
evaṃ kṛte maṇḍale tu sthitistasya prajāyate|
svāntaḥ śuddhāṃśubindubhyāṃ hairambaṃ bījamucyate|| 57.138 ||
nāśanaṃ vighnabījānāṃ dharmakāmārthasādhanam|
gandhapuṣpe tathā dhūpadīpau naivedyameva ca|| 57.139 ||
yadanyad dīyate vastramalaṅkārādikaṃ ca yat |
teṣāṃ daivatamuccārya kṛtvā prokṣaṇapūjane|| 57.140 ||
utsṛjya mūlamantreṇa pratināmnā nivedayet|
varuṇasya tu bījena teṣāṃ prokṣaṇamācaret|| 57.141 ||
iṣṭena mūlamantreṇa tathotsarganivedane|
laparaścandrabindubhyāṃ bījaṃ vāruṇamucyate|| 57.142 ||
vilokanaṃ pūjanaṃ ca tathā dānaṃ pṛthak pṛthak|
japakarmaṇi mālāyāḥ pratipattiridaṃ trayam|| 57.143 ||
iṣṭamantreṇa mālāyāḥ prokṣaṇaṃ parikīrtitam|
bījaṃ gāṇapataṃ pūrvamuccāryaṃ tadanantaram|| 57.144 ||
avighnaṃ kuru māle tvaṃ gṛhṇīyādityanena ca|
japānte śirasi nyāso mālāyāḥ parikīrtitaḥ|| 57.145 ||
srajamādāya pāṇibhyāṃ śrībījena tathārcayet|
antyadantyāntamātrābhyāṃ cādivargatṛtīyakau|| 57.146 ||
parataḥ parataḥ pūrvaṃ śrībījaṃ bindunendunā|
mālāyā avatārastu śirasaḥ kriyate tadā|| 57.147 ||
tāṃ samādāya pāṇibhyāṃ kuryāt sārasvatena vai|
śrībījānāmādyamādyaṃ binducandrārdhasaṃyutam|| 57.148 ||
etaccatuṣṭayaṃ bījaṃ sārasvatamudīritam|
paurāṇikairvaidikaiśca mūlamantreṇa caiva hi|| 57.149 ||
pradakṣiṇāṃ praṇāmaṃ ca kuyāddharmārthasādhakam|
bhūmiṃ vīkṣya tathābhyukṣya kṣitibījena pūrvataḥ|| 57.150 ||
spṛśaṃstāṃ śirasā bhūmiṃ praṇamediṣṭadevatāḥ|
samāptihīnaṃ bārāhaṃ bījaṃ bindvindusaṃyutam|| 57.151 ||
kṣitibījaṃ vijānīyāccaturvargapradāyakam|
darpaṇaṃ vyajanaṃ ghaṇṭāṃ cāmaraṃ prokṣayet punaḥ|| 57.152 ||
naivedyālokamantreṇa pūrvaproktena bhairava|
nāmākṣarāṇi cādyāni caiteṣāṃ bindunendunā|| 57.153 ||
tasmai nama iti prānte grahaṇe mantra ucyate|
nivedanamathaiteṣāmiṣṭamantreṇa cācaret|| 57.154 ||
vāgbhavasya dvitīyena kāmabījena bhairava|
mudrāyā bandhanaṃ kāryaṃ mūlamantreṇa darśanam|| 57.155 ||
parityāgaṃ tu mudrāyāstārābījena cācaret|
prāntādiścandrabindubhyāṃ ṣaṣṭhasvarasamanvitaḥ|| 57.156 ||
tārābījamiti proktaṃ dharmakāmārthasādhanam|
mudaṃ dadāti yasmāt sā mudrā tena prakīrtitā|| 57.157 ||
darśitāyāṃ tu mudrāyāṃ bhavet pūjāsamāpanam|
kāmaṃ mokṣaṃ tathā dharmamarthamodayutā svayam|| 57.158 ||
dadāti sādhakāyāśu devatā gantumutsukā|
mudrānte tu mahāmantrān ṣaḍimān samudīrayet|| 57.159 ||
yad dattaṃ bhaktimātreṇa patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ jalam|
āveditaṃ ca naivedyaṃ tadgṛhāṇānukampayā|| 57.160 ||
āvāhanaṃ na jāmāmi na jānāmi visarjanam|
pūjābhāvaṃ na jānāmi tvaṃ gatiḥ parameśvari|| 57.161 ||
karmaṇā manasā vācā tvatto nānyaṃ gatirmama|
antaścareṇa bhūtānāṃ tvaṃ gatiḥ parameśvari|| 57.162 ||
mātaryonisahasreṣu yeṣu yeṣu vrajāmyaham|
teṣu teṣvacyutā bhaktiracyute’stu sadā tvayi|| 57.163 ||
devī dātrī ca bhoktrī ca devī sarvamidaṃ jagat|
devī jayati sarvatra yā devī so’hameva ca|| 57.164 ||
yadakṣaraparibhraṣṭaṃ mātrāhīnaṃ ca yad bhavet|
tatsarvaṃ kṣmyatāṃ devi kasya na skhalitaṃ manaḥ|| 57.165 ||
mantreṣu paṭhiteṣveṣu svayameva prasīdati|
dātuṃ devīṃ caturvargaṃ na cirādeva bhairava|| 57.166 ||
aiśānyāṃ maṇḍalaṃ kuryād dvārapadmavivarjitam|
visarjanārtha nirmālyadhāriṇyāḥ pūjanāya vai|| 57.167 ||
pādmādibhiḥ pūjayitvā dhyātvā nirmālyadhāriṇīm|
niḥkṣipya tasmin nirmālyaṃ mantrema tu visarjayet|| 57.168 ||
gaccha gaccha padaṃ sthānaṃ svasthānaṃ parameśvari|
yatra brahmādayo devā na viduḥ paramaṃ padam|| 57.169 ||
visṛjya mantreṇānena tataḥ pūrakavāyunā|
dhyāyaṃstu mantreṇānena natvā tāṃ sthāpayeddhṛdi|| 57.170 ||
tiṣṭha devi pare sthāne svasthāne parameśvari|
yatra brahmādayaḥ sarve surāstiṣṭhanti me hṛdi|| 57.171 ||
tata ekajaṭābījairiṣṭadevīṃ dhiyā smaran|
nirmālyaṃ mūrghni gṛhṇīyād dharmakāmārthasādhanam|| 57.172 ||
maṇḍalapratipattiṃ tu tataḥ kuryād vibhūtaye|
sarvāṅgulīnāmagraudhaiḥ padmamaṣṭadalānvitam|| 57.173 ||
nirmanthet kṣitibījena maṇḍalaṃ cāpi bhairava|
tatastu mūlamantreṇa sarvavaśyena vā punaḥ|| 57.174 ||
anāmikānāmagreṇa lalāṭamapi saṃspṛśet|
samāptisahitaḥ prāntastārābījaṃ tataḥ param|| 57.175 ||
smarabījaṃ visargeṇa parataḥ parataḥ param|
bhabedekajaṭābījaṃ dharmakāmārthasādhanam|| 57.176 ||
tato bhāskarabījena sahitenātmanā punaḥ|
mantreṇa bhāskarāyārdhamacchidrārthaṃ nivedayet|| 57.177 ||
namo vivasvate brahman bhāsvate viṣṇutejase|
jagatsavitre śucaye savitre karmadāyine|| 57.178 ||
tataḥ kṛtāñjalirbhūtvā paṭhitvā mantramīritam|
ekāgramanasā vāgbhiracchidramavadhārayet|| 57.179 ||
yajñaccidraṃ tapaśchidraṃ yacchidraṃ pūjane mama|
sarvaṃ tadacchidramastu bhāskarasya prasādataḥ|| 57.180 ||
tatastu puṣpanaivedya toyapātrādikaṃ ca yat|
devībījena tatsarvaṃ punareva vilokayet|| 57.181 ||
hastena cakṣuṣā vāpi yatra yatra kṛtaḥ purā|
mantranyāsastatra tatra visṛṣṭiramunā bhavet|| 57.182 ||
prāntādipañcamo vahnibījaṣaṣṭhasvarāhitaḥ|
tathopāntaṃ vāgbhavādyaṃ durgābījaṃ pracakṣate|| 57.183 ||
sthaṇḍile jvaladagnau ca toye sūryamarīciṣu|
pratimāsu ca śuddhāsu śālagrāmaśilāsu ca|| 57.184 ||
śivaliṃge śilāyāṃ tu pūjā kāryā vibhūtaye|
sarvatra maṇḍalanyāsaṃ kuryādekāgramānasaḥ|| 57.185 ||
yogapīṭhasya bījena sthaṇḍilādiṣu sādhakaḥ|
vāsudevasya rudrasya brahmaṇo mihirasya ca|| 57.186 ||
kuryāt sarvatra pūjāsu pratipattimimāṃ budhaḥ|
evaṃ yaḥ pūjayed viṣṇumamībhiḥ pratipattibhiḥ|| 57.187 ||
caturvargapradastasya na cirājjāyate hariḥ|
śivo vā mihiro vāpi ye’nye lambodarādayaḥ|| 57.188 ||
prasīdanti surāḥ sarve pūjāyā vidhināmunā|
viśeṣato mahādevī mahāmāyā jaganmayī|| 57.189 ||
pratipattimimāṃ nityaṃ spṛhayatyeva pūjane|
evaṃ yaḥ kurute pūjāṃ samyak sa phalabhāgbhavet|| 57.190 ||
etairvihīnā yā pūjā tato’lpāpaṃ phalaṃ bhavet|
aṅgahīnastu puruṣo na samyagyājhiko yathā|| 57.191 ||
aṃgahīnā tathā pūjā na samyak phalabhāgbhavet|
idaṃ rahasyaṃ paramamidaṃ svastyayanaṃ param|
mantravedamayaṃ śuddhaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam|| 57.192 ||
yaḥ śrāvayed brāhmaṇasannidhāne śrāddheṣu yajñe surapūjaneṣu|
samyak phalaṃ tasya labhet sa karmaṇo vināpi pūjāṃ tadanantaramaśnute|| 57.193 ||
iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe uttaratantre saptapañcāśo’dhyāyaḥ||

<58>
||śrī bhagavānuvāca||
devyāstantraṃ viśeṣeṇa śṛṇutaṃ sāmprataṃ yuvām|
yena cārādhitā devī nacirādvaradā bhaveta|| 58.1 ||
pūrvatantrādviśeṣaṇa tathā vai tantramuttaram|
viśeṣeṇa ca sāmānyāt kathitaṃ bhavatoḥ purā|| 58.2 ||
punardevyā viśeṣeṇa pūjāyāṃ bhaktikarmaṇi|
yāni tantrāṇi śeṣāṇi tāni vakṣyāmyahaṃ punaḥ|| 58.3 |
yaḥ kuryāt tu mahāmāyābhaktimekāgramānasaḥ|
aṅginā vāṅgimantreṇa tena kāryamidaṃ śubham|| 58.4 ||
phalaṃ puṣpaṃ ca tāmbūlamannapānādikaṃ ca yat|
adattvā tu mahādevyai na bhoktavya kadācana|| 58.5 ||
pathi vā parvatāgre vā sabhāyāmapi sādhakaḥ|
yathā tathā nivedyaiva svamarthamupakalpayet|| 58.6 ||
dṛṣṭvaiva madirābhāṇḍaṃ raktavarṇāstathā striyaḥ |
siṃhaṃ śavaṃ raktapadmaṃ vyāghravārāṇasaṅgamam|| 58.7 ||
guruṃ rājānamathavā mahāmāyāṃ tato namet|
pativratāyāṃ bhāryāyāṃ sadaiva ṛtusaṅgamaḥ|| 58.8 ||
kriyate caṇḍikāṃ dhyātvā tadā kāryo vibhūtaye|
śāntikaṃ pauṣṭikaṃ vāpi tatheṣṭāpūrttakarmaṇī|| 58.9 ||
yadā kuryāt tadā natvā devīyātrāṃ samācaret|
tauryyatrikaṃ yadā paśyet kevalaṃ gītameva vā|| 58.10 ||
tacca devyai nivedyaiva kartavyaṃ svopayojanam|
yadeva bhūṣaṇaṃ vāso malayodbhavameva vā|| 58.11 ||
svakāye pariyuñjīta tatra mantraṃ dhiyā nyaseta|
vyāyāme ca vidhāne ca sabhāyāṃ vā jale sthale|| 58.12 ||
yatra yatra svayaṃ gacchet tatra devīṃ sadā smaret|
yad yat karma tu pūjāṅgaṃ tattanmantreṇa cācaret|| 58.13 ||
mantrahīnaṃ pūjanāṅgaṃ karma yat tattu niṣphalam|
yasmin karmaṇi yoddiṣṭo mantrarpūjāsu bhairava|| 58.14 ||
naivedyālokamantreṇa tat tat karma samācaret|
devyāstu maṇḍalanyāsamiṣṭamantreṇa cācaret|| 58.15 ||
pūjānte maṇḍalaṃ liptvā tilakaṃ tena kārayet |
sarvavaśyena mantreṇa dharmakāmārthadāyinā|| 58.16 ||
balidāne baliṃ chitvā khaḍgasthai rudhiraiḥ svakaiḥ|
sarvavaśyena mantreṇa lalāṭe tilakaṃ nyaset|| 58.17 ||
jagadvaśe bhavet tasya caturthaḥ kasya vahninā |
ṣaṣṭhasvareṇa saṃyuktaḥ kalābindusamanvitaḥ|| 58.18 ||
athopāntasthakārāntaḥ saparo’pi tathā punaḥ|
dvirmohīti hakārāsya turyo dvisvarasaṃyutaḥ|| 58.19 ||
tṛtīyavargaprāntena tṛtīyasvarasaṃjñinā|
pūritānto dvidhā varṇastathā vādicaturthakaḥ|| 58.20 ||
svaro dvitīyaśca tathā kṣobhaśabdaḥ puraḥ saraḥ|
pureti sahitaḥ so’pi mitraṃ śatruśca rākṣasaḥ|| 58.21 ||
dakṣaprajā tathā rājā sarvaśāstra iti śrutaḥ|
vināpi pūjanaṃ kuryād yo rahistilakaṃ naraḥ|| 58.22 ||
mantreṇānena satataṃ sarvaṃ tasya vaśe bhavet|
rājā vā rājupatro vā striyo vā yakṣarākṣasāḥ|| 58.23 ||
sarve tasya vaśaṃ yānti bhūtagrāmāścaturvidhāḥ|
pravāse pathi vā durge sthānāpāptau jale’pi vā|| 58.24 ||
kārāgāre nibaddho vā prāyoveśagato’pi vā|
kuryāt tatra mahāmāyāpūjāṃ vai mānasīṃ budhaḥ|| 58.25 ||
manobhaye samutpanne siṃhavyāghrasamākule|
paracakrāgame vāpi kuryānmānasapūjanam|| 58.26 ||
manasā hṛdayasyāntardhyātvā yogākhyapīṭhakam|
tatraiva pṛthivīmadhye pūjāṃ tatra samācaret|| 58.27 ||
maitraṃ prasādhanaṃ snānaṃ dantadhāvanakarma vai|
anyacca sarvaṃ manasā kṛtvā kuryācca pūjanam|| 58.28 ||
paścāt puṣpādibhiḥ pūjā bahirdeśe vidhīyate|
tathā hṛdyapi kartavyā sarvāśca pratipattayaḥ|| 58.29 ||
aṣṭamyāṃ satataṃ devīyājakaḥ syāt sadā vratī|
navamyāṃ tu tathā pūjā kartavyā nijaśoṇitaiḥ|| 58.30 ||
liṅgasthāṃ pūjayed devīṃ pustakasthāṃ tathaiva ca|
sthaṇḍilasthāṃ mahāmāyāṃ pādukāpratimāsu ca|| 58.31 ||
citre ca triśikhe khaḍgaṃ jalasthāṃ vāpi pūjayet|
pañcāśadaṅgulaṃ khaḍgaṃ triśikhaṃ ca triśūlakam|| 58.32 ||
śilāyāṃ parvatasyāgre tathā parvatagahvare|
devīṃ sampūjayennityaṃ bhaktiśraddhāsamanvitaḥ|| 58.33 ||
vārāṇasyāṃ sadā pūjā sampūrṇaphaladāyinī|
tatastaddviguṇā proktā puruṣottamasannidhau|| 58.34 ||
tato’pi dviguṇā proktā dvārāvatyāṃ viśeṣataḥ|
sarvakṣetreṣu tīrtheṣu pūjā dvārāvatīsamā|| 58.35 ||
vindhye śataguṇā proktā gaṅgāyāmapi tatsamā|
āryāvarte madhyadeśe brahmāvarte tathaiva ca|| 58.36 ||
vindhyavat phaladā pūjā prayāge puṣkare tathā|
tataścaturguṇā proktā karatoyā nadījale|| 58.37 ||
tasmāccaturguṇaphalā nandikuṇḍe ca bhairava|
tataścaturguṇā proktā jalpiṣe śvarasannidhau|| 58.38 ||
tatra siddheśvarīyonau tato’pi dviguṇā smṛtā|
tataścaturguṇā proktā lauhityanadapāthasi|| 58.39 ||
tatsamā kāmarūpe tu sarvatraiva jale sthale|
sarvaśreṣṭho yatā viṣṇurlakṣmīḥ sarvottamā yathā|| 58.40 ||
devīpūjā tathā śastā kāmarūpe surālaye|
devīkṣetraṃ kāmarūpaṃ vidyate’nyatra tatatsamam|| 58.41 ||
anyatra viralā devī kāmarūpe gṛhe gṛhe|
tataḥ śataguṇā proktā nīlakūṭasya mastake|| 58.42 ||
tato’pi dviguṇā proktā heruke śivaliṅgake|
tato’pi dviguṇā proktā śailaputryādiyoniṣu|| 58.43 ||
tataḥ śataguṇā proktā kāmākhyāyonimaṇḍale|
kāmākhyāyāṃ mahāmāyāpūjāṃ yaḥ kṛtavān sakṛt|| 58.44 ||
sa ceha labhate kāmān paratra śivarūpatām|
na tasya sadṛśo’nyo’sti kṛtyaṃ tasya na vidyate|| 58.45 ||
vāñchitārthamavāpyeha cirāyurabhijāyate|
vāyoriva gatistasya bhavedanyairabādhitā|| 58.46 ||
saṅgrāme śāstravāde vā durjayaḥ sa ca jāyate|
vaiṣṇavītantramantreṇa kāmākhyāyonimaṇḍale|
sakṛt tu pūjanaṃ kṛtvā phalaṃ śataguṇaṃ labhet|| 58.47 ||
mūlamūrtirmahāmāyā yoganindrā jaganmayī|
tasyāstu vaiṣṇavītantraṃ mantraṃ pratipāditam|| 58.48 ||
anyā yā mūrtayaḥ proktāḥ śailaputryādayo’parāḥ|
tasyā eva vibhāgāstāstaccharīravinirgatāḥ|| 58.49 ||
niḥsaranti yathā nityaṃ sūryabimbānmarīcayaḥ|
devyāstathogracaṇḍādyā mahāmāyāśarorataḥ|| 58.50 ||
tāsāmevāṅgarūpāṇi vaktavyāni mayā tava |
ekaiva tu mahāmāyā kāryārthaṃ bhinnatāṃ gatā|| 58.51 ||
kāmākhyā tu mahāmāyā mūlamūrtiḥ pragīyate|
pīṭhairbhinnāhvayā sā tu mahāmāyā pragoyate|| 58.52 ||
eka eva yathā viṣṇurnityatvād hi sanātanaḥ|
janānāmardanāt so’pi janārdana iti śrutaḥ|| 58.53 ||
tathaiva sā mahāmāyā kāmārthaṃ saṅgatā girau|
kāmākhyeti sadā devairgadyate satataṃ naraiḥ|| 58.54 ||
yathā hi puruṣaḥ ko’pi cchatro cchatragrahād bhavet|
snāpakaḥ snānakāle vai kāmākhyāpi tathāhvayā|| 58.55 ||
mahāmāyāśarīraṃ tu kāmārthaṃ samupasthitam|
lohitaiḥ kuṅkumaiḥ pītaṃ kāmārthamupayojitaiḥ|| 58.56 ||
khaḍgaṃ tyaktvā kāmakāle sā gṛhṇāni srajaṃ svayam|
yadā tu tyaktakāmā sā tadā syādasidhāriṇī|| 58.57 ||
kāmakāle śivaprete nyastalohitapaṅkaje|
ramate tyaktākāmā tu sitapretopari sthitā|| 58.58 ||
tathaivetastato gatyā siṃhasthā kāmadā bhavet|
kadācit sā sitaprete kadācidraktapaṅkaje|| 58.59 ||
kadācit keśarīpṛṣṭhe ramate kāmarūpiṇī|
yadā lohitapadmasthā tathāgre keśarī caraḥ|| 58.60 ||
yadā pretagatā devī tadāgre’nyaṃ nirīkṣate|
mahāmāyāsvarūpeṇa yadā sā varadā bhavet|| 58.61 ||
pūjākāle tadā pretapadmasiṃhopari sthitā|
raktapadme yadā dhyāyet tadāgre cintayeddharim|| 58.62 ||
yadā dhyāyeddharau cānyadvayamagre vicintayet|
triṣu dhyāteṣu yugapat pretapadmaharai kramāt|| 58.63 ||
sthiteṣu kāmadā devī teṣu dhyāyena kāmadām|
ekaikasminnapi tathā yathāvacchintayecchivām|| 58.64 ||
ekā samastā jagatāṃ prakṛtiḥ sā yatastataḥ|
viṣṇubrahmaśivairdevairdhriyate sā jaganmayī|| 58.65 ||
sitapreto mahādevo brahmalohitapaṅkajam|
harirharistu vijñeyo vāhanāni mahaujasaḥ|| 58.66 ||
svamūrtyā vāhanatvaṃ tu teṣāṃ yasmānna yujyate|
tasmānmūrtyantaraṃ kṛtvā vāhanatvaṃ gatāstrayaḥ|| 58.67 ||
yasmin yasmin mahāmāyā prīṇāti satataṃ śivā|
tena tenaiva rūpema āsanānyabhavaṃstrayaḥ|| 58.68 ||
siṃhopari sthitaṃ padmaṃ raktaṃ tasyordhvagaḥ śivaḥ|
tasyopari mahāmāyā varadā’bhayadāyinī|| 58.69 ||
evaṃ rūpeṇa yo dhyātvā pūjayet satataṃ śivām|
brahmaviṣṇuśivāstena pūjitāḥ syurasaṃśayam|| 58.70 ||
evaṃ sadā mahāmāyā kāmākhyā caikarūpiṇī|
dhyānato rūpato bhinnā tasmāttāṃ tatra pūjayet|| 58.71 ||
evaṃ viśeṣatantrāṇi durgāyāḥ kathitāni vām|
aṅgamantrāṇi tasyāstu śrūyatāṃ narasattamau|| 58.72 ||
iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe aṣṭapañcāśo’dhyāyaḥ||

<59>
||śrī bhagavānuvāca||
aṅgamantrāṇyahaṃ vakṣye caṇḍikāyā viśeṣataḥ|
yaiḥ samārādhitā devī catuvaṃrgapradā bhavet|| 2.59.1 ||
tālavyānto yutaḥ ṣaṣṭhasvarabindvinduvahnibhiḥ |
tathopāntaḥ svarastvete bāhyaṃ vāgbhavameva ca|| 2.59.2 ||
netrabījaṃ caṇḍikāyāstrayametat prakīrtitam|
vāmalalāṭadākṣiṇyanetreṣu tritayaṃ kramāt|| 2.59.3 ||
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ sarvadā kāraṇaṃ param|
mantrametanmahaguhyaṃ durgābījamiti smṛtam|| 2.59.4 ||
yadā kātyāyānamunerāśrameṣu divaukasām |
tejobhirdhṛtakāyābhūd devī devaughasaṃstutā|| 2.59.5 ||
tadā netratrayād devyā mūlamūrtirviniḥ sṛtā|
tejomayī jagaddhātrī mahiṣāsuraghātinī|| 2.59.6 ||
tejobhiḥ sarvadevānāṃ sā dhṛtvā vapuruttamam|
astrāṇyanekānyādāya devairdattāni bhāgaśaḥ|| 2.59.7 ||
sagaṇaṃ sānuvandhaṃ ca sāmātyabalavāhanam|
brahmādyaiḥ saṃstutā devī jaghāna mahiṣāsuram|| 2.59.8 ||
hate tu mahiṣe devī pūjitā tridaśaistataḥ|
anenaiva tu mantreṇa loke khyātiṃ ca sā gatā|| 2.59.9 ||
tataḥ prabhṛti sā mūrtiḥ sarvaiḥ sarvatra pūjyate|
mūlamūrttiḥ suguptābhūt svamūrtyā khyātimāgatā|| 2.59.10 ||
devānāṃ varadānena brahmādyairupayojanāt|
yanmūrtiḥ pūjyate sarvaistāṃ mūrtiṃ śṛṇu bhairava|| 2.59.11 ||
jaṭājūṭasamāyuktāmarddhendukṛtaśekharām|
locanatrayasaṃyuktāṃ pūrṇendusadṛśānanām|| 2.59.12 ||
taptakāñcanavarṇābhāṃ supratiṣṭhāṃ sulocanām|
navayauvanasampannāṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām|| 2.59.13 ||
sucārudaśanāṃ tīkṣṇāṃ pīnonnatapayodharām|
tribhaṅgasthānasaṃsthānāṃ mahiṣāsuramardinīm|| 2.59.14 ||
mṛṇālāyatasaṃsparśadaśabāhusamanvitām|
triśūlaṃ dakṣiṇe deyaṃ khaḍgaṃ cakraṃ kramādadhaḥ|| 2.59.15 ||
tīkṣṇabāṇaṃ tathā śaktiṃ bāhusaṃgheṣu saṅgatām|
kheṭakaṃ pūrṇacāpaṃ ca pāśaṃ cāṅkuśamūrdhataḥ|| 2.59.16 ||
ghaṇṭāṃ ca paraśuṃ cāpi vāme’dhaḥ pratiyojayet|
adhastānmahiṣaṃ tadvadviśiraskaṃ pradarśayet|| 2.59.17 ||
śiraśchedodbhavaṃ tadvaddānavaṃ khaḍgapāṇinam|
hṛdi śūlena nirbhinnaṃ niryadantravibhūṣitam|| 2.59.18 ||
raktaraktokṛtāṅgaṃ ca raktavisphuritekṣaṇam|
veṣṭitaṃ nāgapāśena bhrukuṭīkuṭilānanam|| 2.59.19 ||
sapāśavāmahastena dhṛkeśaṃ ca durgayā|
vamadrudhiravaktraṃ ca devyāḥ siṃhaṃ pradarśayet|| 2.59.20 ||
devyāstu dakṣiṇaṃ pādaṃ samaṃ siṃhopari sthitam|
kiñcidūrdhvaṃ tathā vāmamaṅguṣṭhaṃ mahiṣopari|| 2.59.21 ||
ugracaṇḍā pracaṇḍā ca caṇḍogrā caṇḍanāyikā|
caṇḍā caṇḍavatī caiva cāmuṇḍā caṇḍikā tathā|| 2.59.22 ||
ābhiḥ śaktibhiraṣṭābhiḥ satataṃ pariveṣṭitām|
cintayet satataṃ devīṃ dharmakāmārthamokṣadām|| 2.59.23 ||
etasyāścāṅgamantraṃ tu durgātantramiti śrutam|
śṛṇuṣvaikamanā bhūtvā dharmakāmārthasādhanam|| 2.59.24 ||
vahnibhāryā svaraḥ ṣaṣṭho hāntaḥ prānto’gnireva ca|
durgādiriti soṅkāraṃ durgāmantraṃ miti śrutam|| 2.59.25 ||
ravau makararāśisthe yā bhavet sitapañcamī|
tasyāmanena mantreṇa sampūjya vidhivacchivām|| 2.59.26 ||
śuklāṣṭamyāṃ punardevīṃ pūjayitvā yathāvidhi|
navamyāṃ balidānāni prabhūtāni samācaret|| 2.59.27 ||
sandhyāyāṃ ca baliṃ kuryāgnijagātrāsṛgukṣitam|
evaṃ kṛte tu kalyāṇairyukto nityaṃ pramodate|| 2.59.28 ||
putrapautrasamṛddhastu dhanadhānyasamṛddhibhiḥ|
dīrghāyuḥ sarvasubhago loke’smin sa ca jāyate|| 2.59.29 ||
sitāṣṭamyāṃ tu caitrasya puṣpaistatkālasambhavaiḥ|
aśokairapi yaḥ kuryānmatreṇānena pūjanam|| 2.59.30 ||
na tasya jāyate śoko rogo vāpyatha durgatiḥ|
jyaiṣṭhe tu śuklapakṣasya aṣṭābhyāṃ samupoṣitaḥ|| 2.59.31 ||
navamyāṃ satilairannairyāvakairatha modakaiḥ|
kṣīrairājyaistathā kṣaudraiḥ śarkarābhiḥ sapiṣṭakaiḥ|| 2.59.32 ||
nānāpaśūnāṃ rudhirairmāṃsairapi ca pūjayet|
tato daśamyāṃ śuklāyāmadbhistu tilamiśritaiḥ|| 2.59.33 ||
durgātantreṇa mantreṇa dātavyamañjalitrayam|
eva kṛte daśamyāṃ tu yatpāpaṃ daśajanmabhiḥ|| 2.59.34 ||
kṛtaṃ tatpralayaṃ yāti dīrghāyurapi jāyate|
āṣāḍhe śuklapakṣasya yāṣṭamī śrāvaṇasya ca|| 2.59.35 ||
pavitrāropaṇaṃ kuryād devīprītikaraṃ param|
durgātantreṇa mantreṇa durgābījena bhairava|| 2.59.36 ||
vaiṣṇavītantramantreṇa durgābījena bhairava|
vaiṣṇavītantramantreṇa pavitrāropaṇaṃ caret|
viśeṣācchrāvaṇaṃ prāpya devyāḥ kuryāt pavitrakam|| 2.59.37 ||
sarveṣāmeva devānāṃ pavitrāropaṇaṃ caret|
āṣāḍhe śrāvaṇe vāpi saṃvatsaraphalapradam|| 2.59.38 ||
pratipaddhanadasyoktā pavitrāropaṇe tithiḥ|
dvitīyā tu śriyo devyāstithīnāmuttamā smṛtā|| 2.59.39 ||
tṛtīyā bhavabhāvinyāścaturthī tatsutasya ca|
pañcamī somarājasya ṣaṣṭhī proktā guhasya ca|| 2.59.40 ||
saptamī bhāskarasyoktā durgāyāśca tathāṣṭamī|
mātṛṇāṃ navamī proktā vāsukerdaśamī matā|| 2.59.41 ||
ekādaśo ṛṣīṇāṃ ca dvādaśī cakrapāṇinaḥ|
trayodaśī tvanaṅgasya mama caiva caturdaśī|| 2.59.42 ||
brahmaṇo dikpatīnāṃ ca paurṇamāsī tithirmatā|
pavitrāropaṇaṃ yo vai devānāṃ na samācaret|| 2.59.43 ||
tasya sāṃvatsarīpūjāphalaṃ harati keśavaḥ|
tasmād yatnena kartavyaṃ pavitrāropaṇaṃ param|| 2.59.44 ||
kṛte bahuphalaprāptistatpūjā saphalā bhavet|
pavitraṃ yena sūtreṇa yathā kāryaṃ vijānatā|| 2.59.45 ||
tacchraṇuṣva pramāṇaṃ tu vacanānmama bhairava|
prathamaṃ darbhasūtraṃ ca padmasūtraṃ tataḥ param|| 2.59.46 ||
tataḥ kṣaumaṃ supuṇyaṃ syāt kārpāsakamataḥ param|
paṭṭasūtraṃ tathānyena pavitrāṇina kārayet|| 2.59.47 ||
vicitrāṇi pavitrāṇi kartavyāni tu yatnataḥ|
gandhamālyaiḥ surabhibhiḥ racitāni yathoditam|| 2.59.48 ||
kanyā ca kartayet sūtraṃ pramadā ca pativratā|
vidhavā sādhuśīlā vā duḥkhaśīlā na kartayet|| 2.59.49 ||
yatsūcibhinnaṃ dagdhaṃ ca bhasmadhūmābhiguṇṭhitam|
tadvarjanīyaṃ yatnena sūtramasmin pavitrake|| 2.59.50 ||
upayuktaṃ cākhujagdhaṃ madyaraktādidūṣitam|
malinaṃ nīlaraktaṃ ca prayatnena vivarjayet|| 2.59.51 ||
sūtraiḥ pavitraṃ kurvīta kaniṣṭhottamamadhyamam|
kaniṣṭhaṃ yat pavitraṃ tu saptaviṃśatitantubhiḥ|| 2.59.52 ||
martyaloke yaśaḥ kīrtiḥ sukhasaubhāgyavardhanam|
catuḥpañcāśatā proktaṃ tantūnāṃ madhyamaṃ param|| 2.59.53 ||
divyabhogāvahaṃ puṇyaṃ svargamokṣa pradāyakam|
uttamaṃ caiva tantūnāmaṣṭottaraśatena vai|| 2.59.54 ||
taddatvā tu mahādevyai śivāsayujyamāpnuyāt|
uttamaṃ vāsudevāya dadyād yadi pavitrakam|| 2.59.55 ||
tadā yāti harerlokaṃ sādhako nātra saṃśayaḥ|
aṣṭottarasahasraṃ tu ratnamāleti gīyate|| 2.59.56 ||
pavitraṃ su mahādevyā bhuktimuktipradāyakam|
ratnamālyāṃ tu yo yacchenmahādevyai pavitrakam|| 2.59.57 ||
kalpakoṭisahasrāṇi svarge sthitvā śivo bhavet|
etat tu nāgahārākhyaṃ śaṅkarasya pavitrakam|| 2.59.58 ||
aṣṭottarasahasreṇa tantunā sumanoharam|
yaḥ prayacchati mahyaṃ tu sa yāvāṃstantusañcayaḥ|| 2.59.59 ||
tāvatkalpasahasrāṇi mama loke pramodate|
aṣṭottarasahasreṇa vanamālā hareḥ smṛtā|| 2.59.60 ||
tantūnāṃ tasya dānena viṣṇusāyujyamāpnuyāt|
yat kaniṣṭhaṃ pavitraṃ tu nābhimātraṃ bhavet tu tat|| 2.59.61 ||
dvādaśagranthisaṃyuktamātmamāne na yojayet|
ūrupramāṇaṃ madhyaṃ syād granthīnāṃ tatra yojayet|| 2.59.62 ||
caturviṃśatimapyasya mānamātmana eva ca|
vapitramuttamaṃ proktaṃ jānumātraṃ ca bhairava|| 2.59.63 ||
ṣaṭtriṃśattantugranthīnāṃ yojayedātmamānataḥ|
śatamaṣṭottaraṃ kāryaṃ granthīnāṃ suvidhānataḥ|| 2.59.64 ||
nāgahārāhvayaṃ tadvadanyeṣu ca vidhānataḥ|
vapitraṃ kriyate yena sūtreṇa granthayaḥ punaḥ|| 2.59.65 ||
tadanyavarṇasūtreṇa kartavyā lakṣaṇānvitā|
granthiṃ tu saptabhiḥ kuryād veṣṭanaistu kaniṣṭhake|| 2.59.66 ||
dviguṇairmadhyame kuryāttriguṇaruttame tathā|
adhivāsya pavitrāṇi pūrvasmin divase tataḥ|| 2.59.67 ||
mantranyāsaṃ pavitre tu kuryāt tatrāpare’hani|
durgābījena mantreṇa mantranyāsaṃ dvijaścaret|| 2.59.68 ||
vaiṣṇavītantramantreṇa kuryuranye ca bhairava|
pratigranthi svayaṃ kuryānmantranyāsaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ|| 2.59.69 ||
aṅguṣṭhāgraṇa japanaṃ mālāyāmiha bhairava|
yāvanto granthayaścātra tāvantyeva ca sannyaset|| 2.59.70 ||
mantrāṇi tasya tena syādevāṅgopaniyojanam|
durgātantreṇa mantreṇa tattvanyāsaṃ tu kārayet|| 2.59.71 ||
ekatra nyasya sakalaṃ yajñapātre pavitrakam|
tasmin nidhāya gandhādi puṣpāṇi ca suśobhanam|| 2.59.72 ||
tattvanyāsaṃ tataḥ kuryādaṅgulyagreṇa bhairava|
viṣṇostu mūlamantreṇa tattvanyāsaṃ tu kārayet|| 2.59.73 ||
idaṃ viṣṇuriti proktaṃ mantranyāsaṃ dvijasya hi|
śūdrāṇāṃ mantravinyāse mantro vai dvādaśākṣaraḥ|| 2.59.74 ||
prāsādena tu mantreṇa tattvanyāso mama smṛtaḥ|
anena mantranyāsaṃ ca dānaṃ cānena kārayet|| 2.59.75 ||
kuḍkumośīrakarpūrai ścandanādivilepanaiḥ|
pavitrāṇi vilipyātha tattvanyāsaṃ tu yojayet|| 2.59.76 ||
sampūjya maṇḍale devīṃ vidhivat prayato naraḥ|
vaiṣṇavītantramantreṇa durgātantreṇa bhairava|| 2.59.77 ||
durgābījena dadyāt tu devyā mūrdhni pavitrakam|
yasya devasya yaḥ proktastasya tenaiva maṇḍalam|| 2.59.78 ||
yasya yasya tu yo mantro yathā dhyānādipūjanam|
tat tat tenaiva mantreṇa pūjayitvā prayatnataḥ|| 2.59.79 ||
tasyaiva bījamantrābhyāṃ mūrdhni dadyāt pavitrakam|
pavitraṃ mama yo dadyād devebhyaśca pavitrakam|| 2.59.80 ||
sarveṣāmeva devānāṃ sampūrṇārthaśca bhairava|
agnirbrahmā bhavānī ca gajavaktro mahoragaḥ|| 2.59.81 ||
skando bhānurmātṛgaṇo dikpālāśca nabagrahāḥ|
etān ghaṭeṣu pratyekaṃ pūjayitvā yathāvidhi|| 2.59.82 ||
pavitraṃ mūrdhni caikaikaṃ dadyādebhyaḥ samāhitaḥ|
pañcagavyacaruṃ kṛtvā devyai dattvāhutitrayam|| 2.59.83 ||
tenaiva viṣṇave dattvā śambhave ca yathāvidhi|
ājyairaṣṭottaraśataṃ tilairājyaistathaiva ca|| 2.59.84 ||
aṣṭottaraśataṃ dadyānmahādevyai ca sādhakaḥ|
evameva vidhānena niṣṇvādīnāṃ ca sādhakaḥ || 2.59.85 ||
pavitrāropaṇaṃ kuryād dharmakāmārthasiddhaye|
naivedyairvividhaiḥ peyairvaṭapiṣṭakamodakaiḥ|| 2.59.86 ||
kūṣmāṇḍairnārikelaiśca kharjjūraiḥ panasaistathā|
āmradāḍimakarkārudrākṣādivividhaiḥ phalaiḥ|| 2.59.87 ||
bhakṣyabhojyādibhiḥ sarvairmatsyairmāṃsestathaudanaiḥ|
gandhaiḥ puṣpaistathā dhūpairdīpaiśca sumanoharaiḥ|| 2.59.88 ||
vāsobhirbhūṣaṇaiścaiva bhavānīsādhako yajet |
naṭanartakasaṅghaiśca veśyābhiścaiva bhairava|| 2.59.89 ||
nṛtyagītaḥ samudito jāgaraṃ kārayenniśi|
bhojayed brāhmaṇāṃścāpi jñātīnapi dvijātibhiḥ|| 2.59.90 ||
pavitrāropaṇe vṛtte dakṣiṇāmupadāpayet|
hiraṇyaṃ gāṃ tilaghṛtaṃ vāso vā śākameva vā|| 2.59.91 ||
imaṃ mantraṃ tataḥ paścāt sādhakaḥ samudīrayet|
maṇividrumamālābhirmandārakusumādibhiḥ|| 2.59.92 ||
iyaṃ sāṃvatsarī pūjā tavāstu parameśvari|
tato visarjayed devīṃ pūjābhiḥ pratipattibhiḥ|| 2.59.93 ||
evaṃ kṛte pavitrāṇāṃ dāne devyā yathāvidhi|
saṃvatsarasya yā pūjā sampūrṇā vatsarād bhavet|| 2.59.94 ||
kalpakoṭiśataṃ yāvad devīgehe vasennaraḥ|
tatrāpi sukhasaubhāgyasamṛddhiratulā bhavet|| 2.59.95 ||
|| iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe ekonaṣaṣṭitamo’dhyāyaḥ||

<60>
||śrībhagavānuvāca||
durgātantreṇa mantreṇa kuryād durgāmahotsavam|
mahānavamyāṃ śaradi balidānaṃ nṛpādayaḥ|| 60.1 ||
āśvinasya tu śuklasya bhaved yā aṣṭamī tithiḥ|
mahāṣṭamīti sā proktā devyāḥ prītikarī parā|| 60.2 ||
tano’tu navamī yā syāt sā mahānavamī smṛtā|
sā tithiḥ sarvalokānāṃ pūjanīyā śivapriyā|| 60.3 ||
anayorvatsa pūjāyāṃ viśeṣaṃ śṛṇu bhairava|
sampūjya maṇḍale devīṃ vidhivat prayato naraḥ|| 60.4 ||
vaiṣṇavītantramantreṇa durgātankṣeṇa bhairava|
mūrtibhede yathā devī pūjāṃ gṛhṇāti bhūtaye|| 60.5 ||
kanyāsaṃsthe ravau vatsa śuklāmārabhya nandikām|
ayācitāśo naktāśī ekāśī tvatha cāpadaḥ|| 60.6 ||
prātaḥsnāyī jitadvandvastrikālaṃ śivapūjakaḥ|
japahomasamāyukto bhojayecca kumārikā|| 60.7 ||
bodhayed bilvaśākhāsu ṣaṣṭyāṃ devīphaleṣu ca|
saptamyāṃ bilvaśākhāṃ tāmāhṛtya pratipūjayet|| 60.8 ||
punaḥ pujāṃ tathāṣṭamyāṃ viśeṣeṇa samācaret|
jāgaraṃ ca svayaṃ kuryād balidānaṃ mahāniśi|| 60.9 ||
prabhūtabalidānaṃ tu navamyāṃ vidhivaccaret|
dhyāyed daśabhujāṃ devīṃ durgātantreṇa pūjayet|| 60.10 ||
visarjanaṃ daśamyāṃ tu kuryād vai sādhakotamaḥ|
kṛtvā visarjanaṃ tasyāṃ tithau naktaṃ samācaret|| 60.11 ||
yadā tu ṣauḍaśabhujāṃ mahāmāyāṃ prapūjayet|
durgātantreṇa mantreṇa viśeṣaṃ tatra vai śṛṇu|| 60.12 ||
kanyāyāṃ kṛṣṇapakṣasya ekādaśyāmupoṣitaḥ|
dvādaśyāmekabhaktaṃ tu naktaṃ kuryāt pare’hani|| 60.13 ||
caturdaśyāṃ mahāmāyāṃ bodhayitvā vidhānataḥ|
gītavāditranirghoṣairnnānānaivedyavedanaiḥ|| 60.14 ||
ayācitaṃ budhaḥ kuryādupavāsaṃ pare’hani|
evameva vrata kuryād yāvadvai navamī bhavet|| 60.15 ||
jyeṣṭhāyāṃ ca samabhyarcya mūlena pratipūjayet|
uttareṇārcanaṃ kṛtvā śravaṇānte visarjayet|| 60.16 ||
yadā tvaṣṭādaśabhujāṃ mahāmāyāṃ prapūjayet|
durgātantreṇa mantreṇa tatrāpi śṛṇu bhairava|| 60.17 ||
kanyāyāṃ kṛṣṇapakṣasya pūjayitvārdrabhe divā|
navamyāṃ bodhayed devīṃ gītavāditranisvanaiḥ|| 60.18 ||
śuklapakṣe caturthyā tu devīkeśavimocanam|
prātareva tu pañcamyāṃ snāpayet tu śubhairjalaiḥ || 60.19 ||
saptamyāṃ patrikāpūjā aṣṭamyāṃ cāpyupoṣaṇam|
pūjājāgaraṇaṃ caiva navamyāṃ vidhibadbaliḥ|| 60.20 ||
sampreṣaṇaṃ daśamyāṃ tu krīḍākautukamaṅgalaiḥ|
nīrājanaṃ daśamyāṃ tu balavṛddhikaraṃ mahat|| 60.21 ||
yadā vai vaiṣṇavīṃ devīṃ mahāmāyāṃ jaganmayām|
pūjayet tatra ca tadā viśeṣaṃ śṛṇu bhairava|| 60.22 ||
kanyāsaṃsthe ravau pūjā yā śuklā tithiraṣṭamī|
tasyāṃ rātrau pūjitavyā mahāvibhavavistaraiḥ|| 60.23 ||
navamyāṃ balidānaṃ tu karttavyaṃ vai yathāvidhi|
japaṃ homaṃ ca vidhivat kuryāt tatra vibhūtaye|| 60.24 ||
sampūjayenmahādevīmaṣṭapuṣpikayā naraḥ|
rāmasyānugrahārthāya rāvaṇasya vadhāya ca|| 60.25 ||
rātrāveva mahādevī brahmaṇā bodhitā purā|
tatastu tyaktanidrā sā nandāyāmāśvine site|| 60.26 ||
jagāma nagarīṃ laṅkāṃ yatrāsīdrāghavaḥ purā|
tatra gatvā mahādevī tadā tau rāmarāvaṇau|| 60.27 ||
yuddhaṃ niyojayāmāsa svayamantarhitāmbikā|
rakṣasāṃ vānarāṇāṃ ca jagdhvā sā māṃsaśoṇite || 60.28 ||
rāmarāvaṇayoryuddhaṃ saptāhaṃ sā nyayojayat|
vyatīte saptame rātrau navyāṃ rāvaṇaṃ tataḥ|| 60.29 ||
rāmeṇa ghātayāmāsa mahāmāyā jaganmayī|
yāvattayoḥ svayaṃ devī yuddhakelimudaikṣata|| 60.30 ||
tāvat tu saptarātrāṇi saiva devaiḥ supūjitā|
nihate rāvaṇe vīre navamyāṃ sakalaiḥ suraiḥ|| 60.31 ||
viśeṣapūjāṃ durgāyāścakre lokapitāmahaḥ|
tataḥ sampreṣitā devī daśamyāṃ śārvarotsavaiḥ|| 60.32 ||
śākro’pi devasenāyā nīrājanamathākarot|
śāntyarthaṃ surasainyānāṃ devarājyasya vṛddhaye|| 60.33 ||
rāmarāvaṇabāṇena yuddhaṃ cāvekṣya bhītidam|
tṛtīyāyāṃ tu laṅkāyāḥ pūrvottaradiśi sthitam|| 60.34 ||
svātīnakṣatrayuktāyāṃ bhītaṃ surabalaṃ mahat|
śāntyarthaṃ varayāmāsa devendro vacanād hareḥ|| 60.35 ||
tatastu śravaṇenātha daśamyāṃ caṇḍikāṃ śubhām|
visṛjya cakre śāntyarthaṃ balanīrājanaṃ hariḥ|| 60.36 ||
nīrājitabalaḥ śaktastatra rāmaṃ ca rāghavam|
samprāpya prayayau svargaṃ saha devaiḥ śacīpatiḥ|| 60.37 ||
itivṛttaṃ purākalpe manoḥ svāyambhuve’ntare|
prādurbhūtā daśabhujā devī devahitāya vai|| 60.38 ||
nṛṇāṃ tretāyugasyādau jagatāṃ hitakāmyayā|
purākalpe yathāvṛttaṃ pratikalpaṃ tathā tathā|| 60.39 ||
pravartate svayaṃ devī daityānāṃ nāśanāya vai|
pratikalpaṃ bhavedrāmo rāvamaścāpi rākṣasaḥ|| 60.40 ||
tathaiva jāyate yuddhaṃ tathā tridaśasaṃgamaḥ|
evaṃ rāmahasrāṇi rāvaṇānāṃ sahasraśaḥ|| 60.41 ||
bhavitavyāni bhūtāni tathā devī pravartate|
pūjayanti surāḥ sarve balaṃ nīrājayantyapi|| 60.42 ||
tathaiva ca narāḥ sarve kuryuḥ pūjāṃ yathāvidhi|
balanīrājanaṃ rājā kuryād balavivṛddhaye|| 60.43 ||
divyālaṅkārayuktābhirvāruṇībhiḥ pravartanam|
karttavyaṃ nṛtyagītāni krīḍākautukamaṅgalaiḥ|| 60.44 ||
modakaiḥ piṣṭakaiḥ peyairbhakṣyabhojyairanekaśaḥ|
kūṣmāṇḍairnārikelaiśca kharjūraiḥ panasaistathā|| 60.45 ||
drākṣāmalakaśāḍilyaiḥ plīhaiśca karuṇaistathā|
kaśerukramukairmūlaiḥ sajambūtindukādibhiḥ || 60.46 ||
gavyairguḍaistathā māṃsairmadyairmadhubhireva ca|
bālapriyaiśca naivedyairlājākṣataphalādibhiḥ|| 60.47 ||
ikṣudaṇḍaiḥ sitābhiśca lavalīnāgaraṅgakai|
ajābhirmahiṣairātmaśoṇitasañcayaiḥ || 60.48 ||
pakṣyādibalijātīyaistatā nānāvidhairmṛgaiḥ|
pūjayecca jagaddhātrīṃ māṃsaśoṇitakardamaiḥ|| 60.49 ||
rātrau skandaviśākhasya kṛtvā piṣṭakaputrikām|
pūjayecchatrunāśāya durgāyāḥ prītaye tathā|| 60.50 ||
homaṃ ca satilairājyairmāsairapi tathā caret|
ugracaṇḍādikāḥ pūjyāstathāṣṭau yoginīḥ śubhāḥ|| 60.51 ||
yoginyaśca catuḥṣaṣṭhistathā vai koṭiyoginīḥ|
navadurgāstathā pūjyā devyāḥ sannihitāḥ śubhāḥ|| 60.52 ||
jayantyādirgandhapuṣpaistā devyā mūrtayo yataḥ|
devyaḥ sarvāṇi cāstrāṇi bhūṣaṇāni tathaiva ca|| 60.53 ||
aṅgapratyaṅgayuktāni vāhanaṃ siṃhameva ca|
mahiṣāsuramardinyāḥ pūjayed bhūtaye sadā|| 60.54 ||
purākalpe mahādevī manoḥ svāyambhuve’ntare|
nṛṇāṃ kṛtayugasyādau sarvadevaiḥ stutā sadā|| 60.55 ||
mahiṣāsuranāśāya jagatāṃ hitakāmyayā|
yoganidrā mahāmāyā jagaddhātrī jaganmayī|| 60.56 ||
bhujaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiryukyā bhadrakālīti viśrutā|
kṣīrodasyottare tīre bibhratī vipulāṃ tanum || 60.57 ||
atasīpuṣpavarṇābhā jvalatkāñcanakuṇḍalā|
jaṭājūṭasakhaṇḍendumukuṭatrayabhūṣitā|| 60.58 ||
nagahārema sahitā svarmahāravibhūṣitā|
śūlaṃ cakraṃ ca khaḍgaṃ ca śaṅkhaṃ bāṇaṃ tathaiva ca|| 60.59 ||
śaktiṃ vajraṃ ca daṇḍaṃ ca nityaṃ dakṣiṇabāhubhiḥ|
bibhratī satataṃ devī vikāśidaśanojjvalā|| 60.60 ||
kheṭakaṃ carma cāpaṃ ca pāśaṃ cāṅkuśameva ca|
ghaṇṭāṃ parśuṃ ca muṣalaṃ bibhratī vāmapāṇibhiḥ|| 60.61 ||
siṃhasthā nayanai raktavarṇaistribhiratijvalā|
śūlena mahiṣaṃ bhittvā tiṣṭhantī parameśvarī|| 60.62 ||
vāmapadena cākramya tatra devī jaganmayī|
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā sakalā devāḥ praṇamya parameśvarīm|| 60.63 ||
kiñcana taṃ tṛṣṭvā nihataṃ mahiṣāsuram|
tataḥ provāca devāṃstān brahmādīn parameśvarī|| 60.64 ||
smitaprabhinnavadanā vikāśivadanojjavalā|
gacchantu bhoḥ suragaṇā jambudvīpāntaraṃ prati|. 60.65 ||
himavat parvatāsanne varaṃ kātyāyanāśramam|
tatraiva bhavatāṃ sādhyaṃ bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ|| 60.66 ||
ityuktvā sā mahādevī tatraivāntaradhīyata|
devā āpi tadā jagmuḥ kātyāyanamuneḥ puram|| 60.67 ||
āśramaṃ prati te gatvā vismayāviṣṭamānasāḥ|
nihato mahiṣo devyā diṣṭo’smābhiryadarthataḥ|| 60.68 ||
stutā caiṣā mahādevī jagaddhātrī jaganmayī|
kimarthamāha sā devī gantuṃ kātyāyanāśramam|| 60.69 ||
kimanyad vāñchitaṃ kāryamasmākaṃ vā bhaviṣyati|
iti bruvantaste sarve gacchanti sma parasparam|| 60.70 ||
himavatparvatāsannaṃ munikātyāyanāśramam|
tataḥ sendrāḥ sadikpālā brahmaviṣṇuśivāstathā|| 60.71 ||
niṣeduḥ suciraṃ prītā durgādarśanalālasāḥ|
tato rudragaṇāḥ sarve mahiṣāsuraceṣṭitam|| 60.72 ||
āgatya kathayāmāsurdevalokaparābhavam|
tatastatra mahākopaṃ brahmaviṣṇuśivādayaḥ|| 60.73 ||
cakruḥ ko’nyo’sti mahiṣo hato devyā sa dānavaḥ|
punaryeneha kriyate jagadvidhvaṃsanaṃ bhṛśam|| 60.74 ||
iti prakupyatāṃ teṣāṃ śarīrebhyaḥ pṛthak pṛthak|
niścakramuśca tejāṃsi śaktirūpāṇi tatkṣaṇāt|| 60.75 ||
tattejobhirdhṛtavapurdevī kātyāyanna vai|
sandhukṣitā pūjitā ca tena kātyāyanī smṛtā|| 60.76 ||
tatastenaiva mantreṇa daśabāhuyutena vai|
paścājjaghāna mahiṣaṃ jagaddhātrī jaganmayī|| 60.77 ||
yadā stutā mahādevī bodhitā cāśvinasya ca|
caturdaśī kṛṣṇapakṣe prādurbhūtā jaganmayī|| 60.78 ||
devānāṃ tejasāṃ mūrtiḥ śuklapakṣe suśobhane|
saptamyāṃ sā’karod devī aṣṭamyāṃ tairalaṅkṛtā|| 60.79 ||
navamyāmupahāraistu pūjitā mahiṣāsuram|
nijaghāna daśamyāṃ tu visṛṣṭāntarhitā śivā|| 60.80 ||
||mārkaṇḍeya uvāca||
śrutvemāṃ sagaro rājā devyāḥ saṅgatimuttamām|
saṃśayāluśca tadrūpe punaraurvvamapṛcchat|| 60.81 ||
||sagara uvāca||
yadi pascānmahādevī jaghāna mahiṣāsuram|
