kathaṃ pūrvaṃ bhadrakālīrūpābhūnmahiṣāsuram|| 60.82 ||
tathāhi darśanaṃ tasyāḥ pādākrāntascakāra ca|
hṛdi śūlena nirbhinnaṃ dadṛśuḥ sakalāḥ surāḥ|
evaṃ tu saṃśayaṃ chindhi muniśreṣṭha samādhunā|| 60.83 ||
||aurvva uvāca||
śṛṇutvaṃ nṛpaśārdūla bhadrakālī yathā purā|
prādurbhūtā mahāmāyā mahiṣeṇa sahaiva tu|| 60.84 ||
mahiṣāsura evāsau nidrāyāṃ niśi parvate |
svapnaṃ pradadṛse vīro dāruṇaṃ ghoradarśanam|| 60.85 ||
mahāmāyā bhadrakālī chittvā khaḍgena me śiraḥ|
papau tasya ca raktāni vyāditāsyātibhīṣaṇā|| 60.86 ||
tataḥ prātarbhayayutaḥ sa daityo mahiṣāsuraḥ|
tāmeva pūjayāmāsa suciraṃ sānugastadā|| 60.87 ||
ārādhitā tadā devī mahiṣeṇāsureṇa vai|
prādurbhūtā bhadrakālī bhujaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiryutā|| 60.88 ||
tataḥ praṇamya mahiṣo mahāmāyāṃ jaganmayīm|
uvācedaṃ vaco namramūrtirbhaktiyuto’suraḥ|| 60.89 ||
||mahiṣa uvāca||
devi khaḍgena sañchidya śoṇitāni śiro mama|
tvayā muktāni dṛṣṭāni mayā svapnena niścitam|| 60.90 ||
avaśyaṃ tu tvayā kāryaṃ mayā jñātaṃ pramāṇataḥ|
etadrudhirapānaṃ me tatraikaṃ dehi me varam|| 60.91 ||
vadhyastavāhaṃ nātrāsti saṃśayaḥ parameśvari|
mamāpi tatra no duḥkhaṃ niyatiḥ kena laṅghyate|| 60.92 ||
kintu tvayaiva sahitaḥ śambhurārādhitaḥ purā|
mama pitrā madarthena jātaḥ paścādahaṃ tataḥ|| 60.93 ||
mayāpyārādhitaḥ śambhuḥ prāptāśceṣṭāstathāvidhāḥ|
manvantaratrayaṃ yāvadāsuraṃ rājyamuttamam|| 60.94 ||
akaṇṭakaṃ mayā bhuktamanutāpo na vidyate|
kātyāyanena muninā śapto’haṃ śiṣyakāraṇāt|| 60.95 ||
sīmantinī vināśaṃ te kariṣyati na saṃśayaḥ|
purā muniṃ tapasyantaṃ raudrāśvaṃ nāma sattamam|| 60.96 ||
muneḥ kātyāyanākhyasya śiṣyaṃ himavadantike|
divyastrīrūpamatulaṃ kṛtvāhaṃ kautukāt tadā|| 60.97 ||
mayā sammohito vipro’tyajat sadyastadā tapaḥ|
nadūrāt saṃsthitenāhaṃ muninā kātyasūnunā|| 60.98 ||
jñātvā māyāṃ tadā śaptaḥ śiṣyārthe krodhavahninā|
yasmāt tvayā me siṣyo’yaṃ mohitastapasaścyutaḥ|| 60.99 ||
kṛtastvayā strīrūpeṇa tat tvāṃ strī nihaniṣyati|
iti māṃ śaptavān pūrvaṃ muniḥ kātyāyanaḥ svayam|| 60.100 ||
tasya śāpasya kālo’yamāgatya samupasthitaḥ|
devendratvaṃ mayā prāptaṃ bhuktaṃ tribhuvanaṃ samam|| 60.101 ||
kiñcinna śocyaṃ me’trāsti vāñchanīyaṃ hi yanmayā|
tasmāt tvāṃ vai prapanno’haṃ prārthyaṃ śeṣaṃ hi yanmama|
yadū dehi devi durge tvaṃ bhūyastubhyaṃ namo namaḥ|| 60.102 ||
||devyuvāca||
prārthanīyo varo yaste taṃ vṛṇuṣva mahāsura|
dāsyāmi te varaṃ prārthyaṃ saṃśayo nātra vidyate|| 60.103 ||
||mahiṣa uvāca||
yajñabhāgamahaṃ bhoktumicchāmi tvatprasādataḥ|
yathā makheṣu sarveṣu pūjyo’haṃ syāṃ tathā kuru|| 60.104 ||
tvatpādasevāṃ na tyakṣye yāvatsūryaḥ pravartate|
evaṃ varadvayaṃ dehi yadi deyo varo mama|| 60.105 ||
||devyuvāca||
yajñabhāgāḥ surebhyastu kalpitā vai pṛthak pṛthak|
bhāgo na vidyate cānyo yaṃ dāsyāmi tavādhunā|| 60.106 ||
kintu tvayi mayā yuddhe nihate mahiṣāsura|
naiva tyakṣyasi matpādaṃ satataṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ|| 60.107 ||
mama pravartate pūjā yatra yatra ca tatra te|
pūjyaścintyaśca tatraiva kāyo’yaṃ tava dānava|| 60.108 ||
iti śrutvā vacastasyāḥ pratyūṣe mahiṣāsuraḥ|
varaṃ prāpyeha muditaḥ prasannavadanastadā|| 60.109 ||
ugracaṇḍe bhadrakāli durge devi namo’stute|
prabhūtā mūrtayā devi bhavatyā sakalātmikāḥ|| 60.110 ||
kābhiste mūrtibhiḥ pūjyo yajñe’haṃ parameśvari|
tat samācakṣva yadi me bhavatyeha kṛpā kṛtā|| 60.111 ||
||devyuvāca||
yāni nāmāni proktāni tvayeha mahiṣāsura|
tāsu mūrtiṣu saṃpṛṣṭaḥ pūjyo loke bhaviṣyasi|| 60.112 ||
ugracaṇḍeti yā mūrtirbhadrakālī hyahaṃ punaḥ|
yayā mūrtyātvāṃ haniṣye sā durgeti prakīrtitā|| 60.113 ||
etāsu mūrtiṣu sadā pādalagno nṛṇāṃ bhavān|
pūjyo bhaviṣyati svarge devānāmapi rakṣasām|| 60.114 ||
ādisṛṣṭāvugracaṇḍāmūrtyā tvaṃ nihitaḥ purā|
dvitīyasṛṣṭau tu bhavān bhadrakālyā mayā hataḥ|| 60.115 ||
durgārūpeṇādhunā tvāṃ haniṣvāmi sahānugam|
kintu pūrvaṃ na gṛhītastvaṃ mayā pādayostale|| 60.116 ||
adhunā prārthitavaro gahītaḥ pūrvakāmayoḥ|
grahītavyaśca paścāt tvaṃ yajñabhāgopabhuktaye|| 60.117 ||
||aurvva uvāca||
ityuktvā sā mahāmāyā ugracaṇḍāhvayāṃ tanum|
darśayāmāsa ca tadā mahiṣāyāsurāya vai|| 60.118 ||
yā mūrtiḥ ṣoḍaśabhujā bhadrakālīti viśrutā|
tathaiva mūrtiṃ bāhubhyāmaparābhyāṃ tu bibhratī|| 60.119 ||
dakṣiṇādho gadāṃ vāmapāṇinā pānapātrakam|
surāpūrṇaṃ ca śirasā muṇḍamālāṃ bileśayam|| 60.120 ||
bhinnāñjanacayaprakhyā pracaṇḍā siṃhavāhinī|
raktanetrā mahākāyā yuktā’ṣṭādaśabāhubhiḥ|| 60.121 ||
ugracaṇḍā bhadrakālī devyā mūrtidvayaṃ tathā|
mahiṣaḥ praṇanāmāśu duṣṭvā vismayamāgataḥ || 60.122 ||
tato yathā padākramya nihato mahiṣāsuraḥ|
tathaiva jagṛhe pādatale devīdvayaṃ tu tam|| 60.123 ||
hṛdi śūlena nirbhinnaṃ māhiṣaṃ viśiraskakam|
gṛhītakeśaṃ devyā tu niryadantravibhūṣitam|| 60.124 ||
vamadraktaṃ mahākāyaṃ dṛṣṭvā pūrvatanuṃ svakam|
bhayaṃ prāpyāsuraḥ so’tha śuśoca mumoha ca|| 60.125 ||
tatastu kṣaṇamātmānaṃ saṃstabhya sa tu dānavaḥ|
praṇamya vacanaṃ devīmidamāha sa gadgadam|| 60.126 ||
||mahiṣa uvāca||
yadi devī prasannāsi yajñabhāgāśca kalpitāḥ|
tadā mamānyadā nāśa evametad bhavenna hi|| 60.127 ||
yathāhaṃ na suraiḥ sārdhaṃ kariṣye vairamadbhutam|
tathā māṃ kuru bho devi na janma pralabhe yathā|| 60.128 ||
||devyuvāca||
ārādhitā’haṃ bhavatā varo datto mayā tava|
vadhyaśca tvaṃ mamaiveha nātra kāryā vicāraṇā|| 60.129 ||
yat tvayā prārthitaṃ cāpi sarvaiḥ suragaṇaiḥ saha|
virodho me sadā mā bhūditi cāpi bhaviṣyati|| 60.130 ||
matpādatalasaṃsparśāccharoraṃ tava dānava|
yajñabhāgopabhogāya viśīrṇaṃ na bhaviṣyati|| 60.131 ||
tava jīvātmabhiḥ prāṇāḥ sarva eva mahāsuraḥ|
harasya pādasaṃyogācciraṃ sthāsyati kevalam|| 60.132 ||
kalpakoṭisahasrāṇi triṃśat tvaṃ mahiṣāsura|
śatāni cāṣṭāvanyāni janma te na bhaviṣyati|| 60.133 ||
iti devī varaṃ dattvā mahiṣāyāsurāya vai|
praṇatā tena śirasā tatrevāntaradhīyata|| 60.134 ||
mahiṣo’pi nijasthānaṃ yayau saṃmohitaḥ punaḥ|
māyayā cāsuraṃ bhāvamādaya nṛpa pūrvavat|| 60.135 ||
||sagara uvāca||
aneke nihitā daityā māyayā lokabhūtaye|
na te punaḥ pragṛhītāstebhyo dattvā varāñ śubhān|
kena vā kāraṇenāyaṃ pragṛhīto varaḥ katham|
dattāstasmai samācakṣva mama samyag dvijottama|| 60.136 ||
||aurvva uvāca||
ārādhito mahādevo rambheṇa suravairiṇā|
cireṇa sa ca suprītastapasā tasya śaṅkaraḥ|| 60.137 ||
atha tuṣṭo mahādevaḥ pratyakṣaṃ rambhamūcivān|
prīto’smi te varaṃ rambha varayasva yathepsitam|| 60.138 ||
evamuktaḥ pratyuvāca rambhastaṃ candraśekharam|
aputro’haṃ mahādeva yadi te mayyanugrahaḥ|| 60.139 ||
mama janmatraye putro bhavān bhavatu śaṅkara|
avadhyaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ jetā ca tridivaukasām|| 60.140 ||
cirāyuśca yaśasvī ca lakṣmīvān sa ca śaṅkara|
evamuktastu daityena pratyuvāca vṛṣadhvajaḥ|| 60.141 ||
bhavatvetadvāñchitaṃ te bhaviṣyāmi sutastava|
ityuktvā sa mahādevastatraivāntaradhīyata|| 60.142 ||
rambho’pi yātaḥ svasthānaṃ harṣotphullavilocanaḥ|
pathi gacchan sa rambho’tha dadarśa mahiṣīṃ śubhām|| 60.143 ||
trihāyaṇīṃ citravarṇāṃ sundarīmṛtuśālinīm|
sa tāṃ dṛṣṭvātha mahiṣīṃ rambhaḥ kāmena mohitaḥ|| 60.144 ||
dorbhyāṃ gṛhītvā ca tadā cakāra suratotsavam|
tayoḥ pravṛtte surate tadā sā tasya dejasā|| 60.145 ||
dadhāra mahiṣī garbhaṃ tadā’bhūnmahiṣāsuraḥ|
tasyāṃ svāṃśena giriśastatpu6tvamavāptavān|| 60.146 ||
vavṛdhe sa tadā rāmbhiḥ suklapakṣaśaśāṅkavat |
taṃ ca kātyāyanamuniḥ śaptavānmahiṣāsuram|| 60.147 ||
durnayaṃ vīkṣya śiṣyārthe śiṣyānugrahakārakaḥ|
kātyāyanena śaptaṃ taṃ vijñāya mahiṣāsuram|
prāha praṇāmapūrvaṃ tu caṇḍikāṃ candrasekharaḥ|| 60.148 ||
||īśvara uvāca||
devī kātyāyanenāyaṃ śapto’dya mahiṣāsuraḥ|
yoṣidvināśakartrīti bhaviteti jaganmaye|| 60.149 ||
nisaṃśayamṛṣervākyaṃ bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ|
madīyo māhiṣaḥ kāyo devi kāryastvayā tvayi || 60.150 ||
hantavyaḥ satataṃ yogayuktaḥ pūrve pare’pi ca|
harirharisvarūpeṇa na tvāṃ voḍhuṃ kṣamo’dhunā|| 60.151 ||
mamāyaṃ māhiṣaḥ kāyastava voḍhā bhaviṣyati|
iti pūrvaṃ mahādevī devīṃ prārthitavān purā|| 60.152 ||
tena devī mahādevaṃ jagrāha mahiṣāsuram|
triṣu janmasu putro’bhūdrambhasya bhagavān haraḥ|| 60.153 ||
sṛṣṭitraye sa rambho’pi rambha eva vyajāyata|
āsura tādṛśaṃ tepe tapaḥ paramadāruṇam|| 60.154 ||
tathaivārādhitaḥ śambhuḥ putrārthe pradadau varam|
tathaiva mahiṣīṃ bheje prathamaṃ suratāya saḥ|| 60.155 ||
tasyāṃ tathā’bhavadvīro dānavo mahiṣāsuraḥ|
tathaiva śepe bhagavān muniḥ kātyāyanastu tam|| 60.156 ||
iti pravṛtte pūrvo’smin parasmin sa tu janmani|
mahiṣaḥ pūjayitvā’tha devīṃ varamayācata|| 60.157 ||
tṛtīye janmani varaṃ prāpya kalpānaśeṣataḥ|
neha me janma bhavitetyevaṃ varamayācata|| 60.158 ||
tena devīpādatale tiṣṭhatyeṣo’suro’dhunā|
notpattirapi tasyātha saṃvartāntādabhūnnṛpa|| 60.159 ||
evaṃ devīprasādena mahādevāṃśasambhavaḥ|
parāmavāpa satataṃ pratipattiṃ mahāsuraḥ|| 60.160 ||
iti te kathitaṃ rājan yathā sa mahiṣāsuraḥ|
devīpādatalaṃ prāpya yathā so’dyāpi modate|
prastutaṃ śṛṇu bho rājan kathayāmi nṛpottama|| 60.161 ||
||mārkaṇḍeya uvāca||
iti vaḥ kathitaṃ rājā sagaraḥ sahito yathā|
aurvveṇa cakre saṃvādaṃ devīmahiṣayojane|| 60.162 ||
punaryadāha bhūyo’pi sagarāya mahātmane|
tacchṛṇvantu muniśreṣṭhā guhyād guhyataraṃ param|| 60.163 ||
iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe mahiṣāsuropākhyāno nāma ṣaṣṭitamo’dhyāyaḥ ||

<61>
||aurvva uvāca||
yathāha bhagavān devo bhairavāya mahātmane|
vetālāya nṛpaśreṣṭha tathā tvaṃ prastutaṃ śṛṇu|| 61.1 ||
||śrībhagavānuvāca||
ugracaṇḍā ca yā mūrtiraṣṭādaśabhujā’bhavat|
sā navamyāṃ purā kṛṣṇapakṣe kanyāṃ gate ravau|| 61.2 ||
prādurbhūtā mahāmāyā yoginīkoṭibhiḥ saha|
āṣāḍhasya tu pūrṇāyāṃ satraṃ dvādaśavārṣikam|| 61.3 ||
dakṣaḥ kartuṃ samārebhe vṛtāḥ sarve divaukasaḥ|
tato’haṃ na vṛtastena dakṣeṇa sumahātmanā|| 61.4 ||
kapālīti satī cāpi tajjāyeti ca no vṛtā|
tato roṣasamāyuktā prāṇāstatyāja sā satī|| 61.5 ||
tyaktadehā satī cāpi caṇḍamūrtistadā’bhavat|
tataḥ pravṛtte yajñe’pi tasmin dvādasavārṣike|| 61.6 ||
navamyāṃ kṛṣṇapakṣe tu kanyāyāṃ caṇḍamūrtidhṛk|
yoganidrā mahāmāyā yoginīkoṭibhiḥ saha|| 61.7 ||
satīrūpaṃ parityajya yajñabhaṅgamathākarot|
śaṅkarasya gaṇaiḥ sarvaiḥ sahitā śaṅkareṇa ca|| 61.8 ||
svayaṃ babhañja sā devī mahāsatraṃ mahātmanaḥ|
tato devyā mahākrodhe vyatīte tridivaukasaḥ|| 61.9 ||
pūjayāñcakruratulāṃ devīṃ pūrvoditena vai|
pūrvoditavidhānena pūjāmasyā divaukasaḥ|| 61.10 ||
kṛtvaiva paramāmāpurnivṛtiṃ duḥkhahānaye|
evamanyairapi sadā kāryaṃ devyāḥ prapūjanam|| 61.11 ||
vibhūtimatulāṃ prāptaṃ caturvargapradāyikām|
yo mohādathavā’’lasyād devīṃ durgāṃ mahotsave|| 61.12 ||
na pūjayati dambhād vā dveṣādvā’pyatha bhairava|
kruddhā bhagavatī tasya kāmāniṣṭānnihanti vai|| 61.13 ||
paratra ca mahāmāyābalirbhūtvā prajāyate |
aṣṭamyāṃ rudhiraiścaiva mahāmāṃsaiḥ sugandhibhiḥ|| 61.14 ||
pūjayedbahujātīyairbalibhirbhojanaiḥ śivām|
sindūraiḥ paṭṭavāsobhirnānāvidhavilepanaiḥ|| 61.15 ||
puṣpairanekajātīyaiḥ phalairbahuvidhairapi|
upavāsaṃ mahāṣṭamyāṃ putravān na samācaret|| 61.16 ||
yathā tathaiva pūtātmā vratī devīṃ prapūjayet|
pūjayitvā mahāṣṭamyāṃ navamyāṃ balibhistathā || 61.17 ||
visarjayed daśamyāṃ tu śravaṇe sāvarotsavaiḥ|
antyapādo divābhāge śravaṇasya yadā bhavet|| 61.18 ||
tadā sampreṣaṇaṃ devyā daśamyāṃ kārayed budhaḥ|
suvāsinī kumārībhirveśyābhirnartakaistathā|| 61.19 ||
saṅkhatūryaninādaiśca mṛdaṅgaiḥ paṭahaistathā|
dhvajairvastrairbahuvidhārlājapuṣpaprakīrṇakaiḥ|| 61.20 ||
dhūlikardamavikṣepaiḥ krīḍākautukamaṅgalaiḥ|
bhagaliṅgābhidhānaiśca bhagaliṅgapragītakaiḥ|| 61.21 ||
bhagaliṅgādiśabdaiśca kraḍayeyuralaṃ janāḥ|
parairnākṣipyate yastu yaḥ parānnakṣiped yadi|| 61.22 ||
kruddhā bhagavatī tasya śāpaṃ dadyāt sudāruṇam|
ādipādo niśābhāge śravaṇasya yadā bhavet|| 61.23 ||
tadā devyāḥ samutthānaṃ navamyāṃ na punardivā|
antyapādo niśābhāge śravaṇasya yadā bhavet|| 61.24 ||
tadā devyāḥ samutthānaṃ navamyāṃ dinabhāgataḥ|
visarjanamanenaiva mantreṇa vatsa bhairava|| 61.25 ||
kartavyamambhasi sthāpya visṛjya ca vibhūtaye|
uttiṣṭha devi caṇḍeśe subhāṃ pūjāṃ pragṛhya ca|| 61.26 ||
kuruṣva mama kalyāṇamaṣṭabhiḥ śaktibhiḥ saha|
gaccha gaccha paraṃ sthānaṃ svasthānaṃ devi caṇḍike || 61.27 ||
yat pūjatiṃ mayā devi paripūrṇaṃ tadastu me|
vraja tvaṃ srotasi jale tiṣṭha gehe ca bhūtaye || 61.28 ||
nimajjāmbhasi santyajya patrikāvajite jale|
putrāyurdhanavṛddhyarthaṃ sthāpitāsi jale mayā|| 61.29 ||
ityanena tu mantreṇa devīṃ saṃsthāpayejjale|
sarvalokahitārthāya sarvalokavibhūtaye|| 61.30 ||
durgātantreṇa mantreṇa pūjitavye ubhe api|
bhadrakālīmugracaṇḍāṃ mahāmāyāṃ mahotsave|| 61.31 ||
netrabījaṃ tu sarvāsāṃ pūjane parikīrtitam|
yoginīnāṃ tu sarvāsāṃ mūlamūrtestathaiva ca|| 61.32 ||
mantraṃ tathogracaṇḍāyāḥ pṛthak tvaṃ śṛṇu bhairava|
ādyadvayaṃ netrabījaṃ mantrasyopāntamantare|| 61.33 ||
vahninā’ntaḥsvareṇendubindubhyāṃ tantramaugrakam|
netrabījaṃ dvitīyaṃ ta dvidhāvartitamucyate|| 61.34 ||
bhadrakālyāstu mantro’yaṃ dharmakāmārthasiddhaye|
yadā tu vaiṣṇavī devī mahāmāyā jaganmayī|| 61.35 ||
pūjyate vaiṣṇavī devī tantroktā aṣṭayoginīḥ|
tāḥ proktāḥ śailaputryāśca pūrvakalpe ca bhairava|| 61.36 ||
ugracaṇḍādayaścāṣṭau durgātantrasya kīrtitāḥ|
bhadrakālyāstu mantreṇa bhadrakālīṃ prapūjayet || 61.37 ||
pūjayed bhūtivṛddhyarthametā evāṣṭayoginīḥ|
jayantīṃ maṅgalāṃ kālīṃ bhadrakālīṃ kapālinīm|| 61.38 ||
durgāṃ śivāṃ kṣamāṃ dātrīṃ daleṣvaṣṭasu pūjayet|
yadogracaṇḍātantreṇa sā devī tatra pūjyate || 61.39 ||
yoginyastatra pūjyāḥ syuraṣṭāvanyāśca bhairava|
kauśikī śivadūtī ca umā haimavatīśvarī|| 61.40 ||
śākambharī ca durgā ca saptamī ca mahodarī|
umāyāḥ saumyamūrtestu tantraṃ tvaṃ śṛṇu bhairava|| 61.41 ||
pādiḥ samāptisahitaḥ phaḍanto nānta eva ca|
ekākṣarastryakṣaraśya umāmantra iti smṛtaḥ|| 61.42 ||
suvarṇasadṛśīṃ gaurīṃ bhujadvayasamanvitām|
nīlāravindaṃ vāmena pāṇinā bibhratīṃ sadā|| 61.43 ||
śuklaṃ tu cāmaraṃ dhṛtvā bhargasyāṅge’tha dakṣiṇe|
vinyasya dakṣiṇaṃ hastaṃ tiṣṭhantīṃ paricintayet|| 61.44 ||
vināpi śambhūṃ rudrāṇīṃ bhaktastu paricintayet|
dvibhujāṃ svarṇagaurāṅgīṃ padmacāmaradhāriṇīm|| 61.45 ||
vyāghracarmasthite padme padmāsanagatā sadā|
etasyāḥ pūjane proktā aṣṭau vetālabhairava|| 61.46 ||
yoginyo nāyikāścāpi pṛthaktvena vyavasthitāḥ|
jayā ca vijayā caiva mātaṅgī lalitā tathā|| 61.47 ||
nārāyaṇyatha sāvitrī svadhā svāhā tathā’ṣṭamī|
pūrvaṃ śumbho niśumbhaśca dānavau bhrātarāvubhau|| 61.48 ||
babhūvaturmahāsattvau mahākāyau mahābalau|
andhakasya sutau dvau tau dantināviva durmadau|| 61.49 ||
mayā vinahate tasminnandhakākhye mahābale|
sasainyavāhanau tau tu pātālatalamāśritau|| 61.50 ||
tatastaptvā tapastovraṃ brahmāṇantau mahāsurau|
samyak tadā’toṣayātāṃ sa suprīto varaṃ dadau|| 61.51 ||
tau brahmavaradṛptau tu samāsādya jagattrayam|
indratvamakarocchumbhaścandratvaṃ ca niśumbhakaḥ|| 61.52 ||
sarveṣāmeva devānāṃ yajñabhāgānupāharat|
svayaṃ śumbho niśumbhaśca dikpālatvaṃ ca tau gatau|| 61.53 ||
sarve suragaṇāḥ sendrāstato gatvā himācalam|
gaṅgāvatāranikaṭe mahāmāyāṃ pratuṣṭuvuḥ|| 61.54 ||
anakeśaḥ stutā devī tadā sarvāmarotkaraiḥ|
mātaṅgavanitāmūrtirbhūtvā devānapṛcchata|| 61.55 ||
yuṣmābhiramarairatra stūyate kā ca bhāminī|
kimarthamāgatā yūyaṃ mātaṅgasyāśramaṃ prati|| 61.56 ||
evaṃ bruvantyā mātaṅgyāstasyāstu kāyakoṣataḥ|
samudbhūtā’bravod devī māṃ stuvanti surā iti|| 61.57 ||
śumbho niśumbho hyasurau bādhaite sakalān surān|
tasmāt tayorvadhāyāhaṃ stūye taiḥ sakalaiḥ suraiḥ|| 61.58 ||
viniḥsṛtāyāṃ devyāṃ tu mātaṅgyāḥ kāyakoṣataḥ|
bhinnāñjananibhā kṛṣṇā sā’bhūd gaurī kṣaṇādapi|| 61.59 ||
kālikākhyā’bhavat sāpi himācalakṛtāśrayā|
tāmugratārāmṛṣayo vadantīha manīṣiṇaḥ|| 61.60 ||
ugrādapi bhayāttrāti yasmād bhaktān sadāmbikā|
etasyāḥ prathamaṃ bījaṃ kathitaṃ trayameva ca|| 61.61 ||
eṣaivaikajaṭākhyā tu yasmāttasmājjaṭaikikā |
śṛṇutaṃ cintaṃ cāsyāḥ samyagvetālabhairavau|| 61.62 ||
yathā dhyātvā mahādevīṃ bhaktaḥ prāpnotyabhīpsitam|
caturbhujāṃ kṛṣṇavarṇāṃ muṇḍamālāvibhūṣitām|| 61.63 ||
khaḍgaṃ dakṣiṇapāṇibhyāṃ bibhratīṃ cāmaraṃ tvadhaḥ|
kartrīṃ ca kharparaṃ caiva kramādvāmena bibhratīm || 61.64 ||
likhantīṃ jaṭāmekāṃ bibhratīṃ śirasā svayam|
muṇḍamālādharāṃ śīrṣe grīvāyāmapi sarvadā|| 61.65 ||
vakṣasā nāgahāraṃ tu bibhratīṃ saktalocanām|
kṛṣṇavastradharāṃ kaṭyāṃ vyāghrajinasamanvitām|| 61.66 ||
vāmapādaṃ śavahṛdi saṃsthāpya dakṣiṇaṃ padam|
vinyasya siṃhapṛṣṭhe tu lelihānāṃ śavaṃ svayam|| 61.67 ||
sāṭṭahāsāṃ mahāghorāṃ rāvayuktātibhīṣaṇām|
cintyāgre tārā satataṃ bhaktimadbhiḥ sukhepsubhiḥ|| 61.68 ||
etasyāḥ sampravakṣyāmi yā aṣṭau yoginīḥ smṛtāḥ|
mahākālyatha rudrāṇī ugrā bhīmā tathaiva ca|| 61.69 ||
ghorā ca bhrāmarī caiva mahārātriśca saptamī|
bhairavī cāṣṭamī proktā yoginīstāḥ prapūjayet|| 61.70 ||
yā kāyakoṣānniḥ sṛtā kālikāyāstu bhairava|
sā kauśikīti vikhyātā cārurūpā manoharā|| 61.71 ||
niḥsṛtā hṛdayād devyā rasanāgreṇa caṇḍikā|
naitasyāḥ sadṛśī mūrtyā cārurūpeṇa vidyate|| 61.72 ||
triṣu lokeṣu kāntyā vā nāsyāstulyā bhaviṣyati|
yoganidrā mahāmāyā yā mūlaprakṛtirmatā|| 61.73 ||
tasyāḥ prāṇasvarūpeyaṃ devī yā kauśikī smṛtā|
netrabījaṃ tathaitasyā bījaṃ tu parikīrtitam|| 61.74 ||
mantramasyāḥ pravakṣyāmi mūrtirūpaṃ ca bhairava|
samāptināntyadantyastu ṣaḍvargādisabindubhiḥ|| 61.75 ||
ṣaṣṭhasvareṇa saṃspṛṣṭho bindunā samalaṅkṛtaḥ|
kauśikīmantratantro’yaṃ sarvakāmārthadāyakaḥ|| 61.76 ||
tasyāstu sampravakṣyāmi yā mūrtiriha bhairava|
śṛṇuṣvaikamanā bhūtvā jagadāhlādakārakam|| 61.77 ||
dhammillasaṃyatakacāṃ vidhoścādhomukhīṃ kalām|
keśānte tilakasyordhve dadhatī sumanoharā|| 61.78 ||
maṇikuṇḍalasaṃghṛṣṭagaṇḍā mukuṭamaṇḍitā|
sajjayotiḥ karṇapūrābhyāṃ karṇamāpūrya saṅgatā|| 61.79 ||
suvarṇamaṇimāṇikyanāgahāravirājitā|
sadā sugandhibhiḥ padmairamlānairatisundarī|| 61.80 ||
mālāṃ bibharti grīvāyāṃ ratnakeyūradhāriṇī|
mṛṇālāyatavṛttaistu bāhubhiḥ komalaiḥ śubhaiḥ|| 61.81 ||
rājantī kañcukopetapīnonnatapayodharā|
kṣīṇamadhyā pītavastrā trivalīprakhyabhūṣitā|| 61.82 ||
śūlaṃ vajraṃ ca bāṇaṃ ca khaḍgaṃ śaktiṃ tathaiva ca|
dakṣiṇaiḥ pāṇibhirdevī gṛhītvā tu virājitā|| 61.83 ||
gadāṃ ghaṇṭāṃ ca cāpaṃ ca carma śaṅkhaṃ tathaiva ca|
ūrdhvādikramato devī dadhatī vāmapāṇibhiḥ|| 61.84 ||
siṃhasyopari tiṣṭhantī vyāghracarmāṇi kauśikī|
bibhratī rūpamatulaṃ sasurāsuramohanam|| 61.85 ||
etasyāḥ śṛṇu vatsa tvaṃ yāḥ pūjyā aṣṭayoginīḥ|
tāḥ pūjitāśca kurvanti caturvargaṃ nṛṇāṃ sadā|| 61.86 ||
brahmāṇī prathamā proktā tato māheśvarī matā|
kaumārī caiva vārāhī vaiṣṇavī pañcamī tathā|| 61.87 ||
nārasiṃho tathaivaindrī śivadūtī tathā’ṣṭamī|
etāḥ pūjyā mahābhāgā yoginyaḥ kāmadāyikāḥ|| 61.88 ||
devyā lalāṭaniṣkrāntā yā kālīti ca viśrutā|
tasyā mantraṃ pravakṣyāmi kāmada śṛṇu bhairava|| 61.89 ||
samāptisahito dantyaḥ prāntastasmāt puraḥ saraḥ|
ṣaṣṭhasvarāgnibindvindusahitaḥ sādireva ca|| 61.90 ||
kālīmantramiti proktaṃ dharmakāmārthadāyakam|
etanmūrti pravakṣyāmi vatsaikāgramanāḥ śṛṇu|| 61.91 ||
nīlotpaladalaśyāmā caturbāhusamanvitā|
khaṭvāṅgaṃ candrahāsaṃ ca bibhratī dakṣiṇe kare|| 61.92 ||
vāme carma ca pāśaṃ ca ūrdhvādhobhāgataḥ punaḥ|
dadhatī muṇḍamālāṃ ca vyāghracarmadharā varām|| 61.93 ||
kṛśāṅgī dīrghadaṃṣṭrā ca atidīrghātibhīṣaṇā|
lolajihvā nimnaraktanayanā nādabhairavā|| 61.94 ||
kabandhavāhanāsīnā vistāraśravaṇānanā|
eṣā tārāhvayā devī cāmuṇḍeti ca gīyate|| 61.95 ||
etasyā yoginīścāṣṭau pūjayeccintayed yadi|
tripurā bhīṣaṇā caṇḍī kartrī hartrī vidhāyinī|| 61.96 ||
karālā śūlinī ceti aṣṭau tāḥ parikīrtitāḥ|
eṣā’tikāmadā devī jāḍyahānikarī sadā|| 61.97 ||
etasyāḥ sadṛśī kācit kāmadā na hi vidyate|
kauśikyā hṛdayād devī niḥsṛtā dhyāyato hareḥ|| 61.98 ||
śivadūtīti sā khyātā yā ca devaśatairvṛtā|
mantramasyāḥ pravakṣyāmi dharmakāmārthadāyakam|| 61.99 ||
yacchrutvā sādhako yāti durlabhaṃ sivamandiram|
yāmārādhya mahādevīṃ śivadūtīṃ śivātmikām|| 61.100 ||
nacirāllabhate kāmān naraḥ sarvajayī bhavet|
antaḥ samāptisahito bindvindubhyāṃ daśāvaraḥ|| 61.101 ||
svareṇopāntadantyena saṃspṛṣṭo’ntena pūrvaśaḥ|
sa eva binduyugalapūrvasthopāntapāvakaḥ|| 61.102 ||
ṣaṣṭhasvarakalāśūnyaiḥ sahitaḥ prathamasthitaḥ|
mantro’yaṃ śivadūtyāstu śivadūtījayapradaḥ|| 61.103 ||
rūpamasyāḥ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu vatsaikasammataḥ|
caturbhujaṃ mahākāyaṃ sindūrasadṛśadyuti|| 61.104 ||
raktadantaṃ muṇḍamālājaṭājūṭārdhacandradhṛk|
nāgakuṇḍalahārābhyāṃ śobhitaṃ nakharojjavalam|| 61.105 ||
vyāghracarmaparidhānaṃ dakṣiṇe sūlakhaḍgadhṛk|
vāme pāśaṃ tathā carma bibhradūrdhvāparakramāt|| 61.106 ||
sthūlavaktraṃ ca pīnoṣṭhaṃ tuṅgamūrti bhayaṅkaram|
nikṣipya dakṣiṇaṃ pādaṃ santiṣṭhat kuṇapopari|| 61.107 ||
vāmapādaṃ śṛgālasya pṛṣṭhe pheruśatairvṛtam|
īdṛśīṃ śivadūtyāstu mūrtiṃ dhyāyed vibhūtaye|| 61.108 ||
dhyānamātrādathaitasyā naraḥ kalyāṇamāpnuyāt|
pūjanādacirād devī sarvān kāmān dadāti ca|| 61.109 ||
yaḥ śivāvirutaṃ śrutvā viśadūtīṃ śubhapradām|
praṇamet sādhako bhaktyā tasya kāmāḥ kare sthitāḥ|| 61.110 ||
yadā jaghāna jagatāṃ raktabījaṃ hitāya vai|
mahādevī mahāmāyā tadāsyāḥ kāyataḥ sṛtāḥ|| 61.111 ||
dūtaṃ prasthāpayāmāsa śivaṃ śumbhāya sāmbikā|
sā śivadūtīti devaiḥ sarvaiḥ pragīyate|| 61.112 ||
kṣemakārī ca śāntā ca vedamātā mahodarī|
karālā kāmadā devī bhagāsyā bhagamālinī || 61.113 ||
bhagodarī bhagārohā bhagajihvā bhagā tathā|
etā dvādaśa yoginyaḥ pūjane parikīrtitāḥ|| 61.114 ||
etā dvādaśa yoginyaḥ śivadūtyāḥ sadaiva hi|
vicarantī svayaṃ devī yatra tatraiva gaccati|| 61.115 ||
yoginyo hyatha sakhyaḥ syuryathānyāsāṃ tathā punaḥ|
caṇḍikāyāstu yoginyaḥ sakhyo’tra ca prakīrtitāḥ|| 61.116 ||
iti te tvaṅgamantrāṇi kathitāni samāsataḥ|
kāmākhyāyāśca mahātmyaṃ kalpamātraṃ vadāmi vām|| 61.117 ||
iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe kāmākhyāmāhātmye ekaṣaṣṭitamo’dhyāyaḥ||

<62>

<63>
||īśvara uvāca||
vaiṣṇavītantramantrasya yathāpūrvaṃ mayoditam|
maṇḍalaṃ pratipattyā tu paryāyo maṇḍalasya yaḥ|| 2.63.1 ||
sa evaṃ prathamaṃ kāryaḥ śilāyāṃ puṣpacandanaiḥ|
pātrādīnāṃ pratiṣṭhānaṃ tathaivātrāpi yojayet|| 2.63.2 ||
vaiṣṇavītantramantrasya proktā yāḥ pratipattayaḥ|
atra tāḥ sakalā yojyā āsanādyaiśca pūjanam|| 2.63.3 ||
tebhyo’nyo yo viśaṣo’tra tad vakṣye śṛṇu bhairava|
prathamaṃ bhāskarāyārghyaṃ pradadyācśvetasarṣapaiḥ|| 2.63.4 ||
puṣpacandanasaṃvītaiḥ sagaṇāya mahātmane|
āsanārcanaśeṣe tu paṭhoktāḥ sarvadevatāḥ|| 2.63.5 ||
pīṭhanāmnā tu saṃyojya maṇḍalasya tu maghyataḥ|
dhyānasvarūpaṃ bhinnaṃ tad vaiṣṇavyā saha bhairava|| 2.63.6 ||
kāmāyāḥ sarvamanyat tu mahāmāyāstavoditam |
yoginistu catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ pūjayecca pṛthak pṛthak|| 2.63.7 ||
guhāṃ manobhavāṃ cāpi mahotsāhāṃ tathā sakhīm|
anantaraṃ pūjayet tu dikpālāṃśca navagrahān|| 2.63.8 ||
rūpatastān samuddiśya pūjayediṣṭasiddhaye|
pūrvadvāre gaṇapatiṃ prathamaṃ tu prapūjayet|| 2.63.9 ||
nandinaṃ ca hanūmantaṃ paścimadvāri pūjayet|
bhṛṅgī cottarataḥ pūjyo mahākālastu dakṣiṇe|| 2.63.10 ||
ete mama dvārapālā devyā dvāre prapūjayet|
pātrāmṛtīkṛtividhau kuryād vai kāmudrayā|| 2.63.11 ||
bhūtāpasāraṇaṃ kuryāt pūrvaṃ tālatrayeṇa tu|
vāmahaste dakṣiṇena pāṇinā tālamāharet|| 2.63.12 ||
hūm̐ hūm̐ phaḍitimantreṇa vetālādīṃśca sārayet|
sarvamuttaratantroktaṃ tantraṃ kuryāt tu sādhakaḥ|| 2.63.13 ||
atroktena svarūpeṇa praṇāyāmaṃ tathā caret|
snāpayet prathamaṃ devīṃ mūlamantrema pūjakaḥ|| 2.63.14 ||
madhukṣīrājyadadhibhirgomūrtrairgomayaistathā|
ratnodakaiḥ śarkarābhirguḍaratnakuśodakaiḥ|| 2.63.15 ||
sitasarṣapamudgābhyāṃ tilakṣīraistathā yavaiḥ|
raktacandanapuṣpaiśca dūrvābhī rocanāyutaiḥ|| 2.63.16 ||
navabhirvitaredarghyaṃ śilāyāṃ yonisannidhau|
āsanaṃ pādyamarghyaṃ ca tata ācamanīyakam|| 2.63.17 ||
madhuparkaṃ snānajalaṃ vastraṃ candanabhūṣaṇam|
puṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ ca dīpaṃ ca netrāñjanamataḥ param|| 2.63.18 ||
naivedyācamanīye ca pradakṣiṇanamaskṛtī|
ete ṣoḍaśa nirdiṣṭā upacārāstu pīṭhataḥ|| 2.63.19 ||
āvāhayenmahādevīṃ gāyatryā kāmayogayā|
tāmeva viddhi vetāla guhyaṃ bhairavadaivatam|| 2.63.20 ||
kāmākhye tvamihāgaccha yathāvanmama sannidhau|
pūjākarmaṇi sānnidhyamiha kalpaya kāmini|| 2.63.21 ||
kāmākhyāyai ca vidmahe kāmeśvarye tu dhīmahi|
tataḥ kuryānmahādevī tataścānu pracodayāt|| 2.63.22 ||
eṣā tu kāmagāyatrī pūjayedanayā śubhām|
pūjāvasāne ca balīndevyāḥ prītyai nivedayet|| 2.63.23 ||
rudrākṣamālayā jāpyamādāyaiva samācaret|
trayakṣarairmūlamantrasya tridhā vṛttaḥ prapūjayet|| 2.63.24 ||
kāmākhyāyāḥ ṣaḍaṅgāni āhvānānantare tathā|
vaiṣṇavītantramantrasya karāṅganyāsayośca ye|| 2.63.25 ||
svarāḥ proktāstaiḥ svaraistu sārdacandraiḥ sabindukaiḥ|
mūlamantrādyakṣarābhyāṃ yugapattu niyojitaiḥ|| 2.63.26 ||
kaniṣṭhādikrameṇaiva hyaṅganyāsaṃ samācaret|
aṅganyāsakaranyāsau kṛtvā paścāttu sādhakaḥ|| 2.63.27 ||
hṛcchirastu śikhāvarmanetrāsyodarapṛṣṭhataḥ|
bāhvoḥ pāṇyorjaṅghayosca pādayoścāpi vinyaset|| 2.63.28 ||
abhayaṃ varadaṃ hastamakṣamālāṃ ca sūtrakam|
pūjayecchaśinaṃ sūryaṃ śiraścādrakalāṃ tathā|| 2.63.29 ||
raktapadmaṃ śavaṃ caiva lauhityaṃ brahmaputrakam|
manobhavāṃ śilāṃ tatra śaktisthāṃ śavamadhyataḥ|| 2.63.30 ||
devyāḥ prapūjayedbhaktaḥ kāravālaṃ ca pārśvataḥ|
pīṭhādidevatāstatra yajet kāmeśvarīṃ śubhām|| 2.63.31 ||
tripurāṃ pūjayenmadhye pīṭhapratyadhidevatām|
śāradāṃ ca mahotsāhāṃ madhya eva prapūjayet|| 2.63.32 ||
caṇḍeśvarī mahādevī devyā nirmālyadhāriṇī|
yonimudrā samākhyātā kāmākhyā visarjane|| 2.63.33 ||
idaṃ dravyaṃ tu sindūracdanāgurukuṃṅkumaiḥ|
iti yo hi mayā prokto viśeṣaḥ paripūjane|| 2.63.34 ||
ebhirviśeṣaiḥ sahitaṃ vaiṣmavītantragocaram|
sarvaṃ kalpaṃ samāsādya kāmākhyāṃ paripūjayet|| 2.63.35 ||
anenaiva vidhānena kāmākhyāṃ yastu pūjayet|
manobhavaguhāmadhye sa yāti paramāṃ gatim|| 2.63.36 ||
brahmāṇī caṇḍikā raudrī gaurīndrāṇī tathaiva ca|
kaumārī vaiṣṇavī durgā narasiṃhī ca kālikā|| 2.63.37 ||
cāmuṇḍā śivadūtī ca vārāhī kauśikī tathā|
māheśvarī śāṅkarī ca jayantī sarvamaṅgalā|| 2.63.38 ||
kālī kapālinī medhā śivā sākambharī tathā|
bhīmā śāntā bhrāmarī ca rudrāṇī cāmbikā tathā|| 2.63.39 ||
kṣamā dhātrī tathā svāhā svadhāparṇā mahodarī|
ghorarūpā mahākālī bhadrakālī bhayaṅkarī|| 2.63.40 ||
kṣemakarī cogracaṇḍā caṇḍogrā caṇḍanāyikā|
caṇḍā caṇḍavatī caṇḍī mahāmohā priyaṅkarī|| 2.63.41 ||
kalavikariṇī devo balapramathinī tathā|
madanonmathinī devī sarvabhūtasya dāmano|| 2.63.42 ||
umā tārā mahānidrā vijayā ca jayā tathā|
pūrvoktāḥ sailaputryādyā yoginyaṣṭau ca yāḥ kramāt|| 2.63.43 ||
tābhirebhiśca sahitāḥ catuḥṣaṣṭiṃ ca yoginoḥ|
pūjayenmaṇḍalasyāntaḥ sarvakāmārthasiddhaye|| 2.63.44 ||
nānāvidhaṃ tu naivedyaṃ pānaṃ pāyasameva ca|
modakāpūpapiṣṭādi devyai samyak pradāpayet|| 2.63.45 ||
evaṃ tu pūjayed devīṃ kāmākyāṃ varadāyinīm|
bhaktiyukto naro yastu sarvān labhate priyān|| 2.63.46 ||
mahotsāhā tu yā devī mahāmāyā tu sā smṛtā|
vaiṣṇavītantramantreṇa sā pūjya yonimaṇḍale|| 2.63.47 ||
tadeva maṇḍalaṃ cāsya hyaṅganyāsaṃ tathaiva ca|
sā eva pūjāparyāye taddhyānaṃ saiva daivatā|| 2.63.48 ||
tantraṃ tadevamuktaṃ tu tasmānnānyaṃ tu kiñcana|
maṇḍalādivisṛṣṭyarthaṃ mahāmāyāmahotsave|| 2.63.49 ||
yatproktaṃ tena tāṃ devīṃ mahotsāhāṃ tu maṇḍale|
snānapūrvaṃ pūjayettu madhvājyādibhirāsavaiḥ|| 2.63.50 ||
śṛṇutaṃ tripurāmūrteḥ kāmākhyāyāḥ prapūjanam|
etasyā mūlamantraṃ tu pūrvamuttaratantrake|| 2.63.51 ||
yuvayoriṣṭayoḥ smyak kramāt tat pratipāditam|
vāgbhavaṃ kāmabojaṃ tu ḍāmaraṃ ceti tattrayam|| 2.63.52 ||
sarvadharmārthakāmādisādhakaṃ kuṇḍalīyutam|
trīṇyasmāt purato dadyād durgā dhyātā maheśvarī|| 2.63.53 ||
tripureti tataḥ khyātā kāmākhyā kāmarūpiṇī|
tasyāstu snāpanaṃ yādṛkkāmākhyāyāḥ prakīrtitam|| 2.63.54 ||
tenaiva snāpanaṃ kuryānmūlamantreṇa pūjakaḥ |
trikoṇaṃ maṇḍalaṃ cāsyāstripuraṃ tu trirekhakam|| 2.63.55 ||
mantraṃ tu akṣaraṃ jñeyaṃ tathā rūpaṃ trayaṃ punaḥ|
trividhā kuṇḍalī śaktistridevānāṃ ca sṛṣṭaye|| 2.63.56 ||
sarvaṃ trayaṃ trayaṃ yasmāt tripurā tena sā smṛtā|
udīcyādyatha pūrvāntā rekhāḥ kāryāstu maṇḍale|| 2.63.57 ||
tristrirekhāstu kartavyāstā eva puṣpacandanaiḥ|
aiśānyamatha nairṛtyāṃ mantraṃ kṛtvā tu saṃlikhet|| 2.63.58 ||
naiṛtyāṃ caiva vāyavyāṃ tato hyaiśānyagāṃ punaḥ|
evaṃ trikoṇaṃ vilikhenmaṇḍalasyāntare punaḥ|| 2.63.59 ||
aiśānyādyāstu yā rekhā sā tu śaktirnigadyate|
nairṛtyāṃ vāyavīṃ yātā tato hyaiśānyagā tu yā|| 2.63.60 ||
sā tu śambhuḥ samākhyātā śaktyā śambhuṃ vibhedyet|
śaktyā vibhinnaṃ bhūteśaṃ veṣṭayet kamalena tu|| 2.63.61 ||
aṣṭapatreṇa tāṃ dhyātvā trivarṇāṃ prāk prapūjayet|
tribhistribhistu rekhābhiḥ śaktiṃ śambhuṃ ca veṣṭayet|| 2.63.62 ||
sthānasyābyukṣaṇaṃ samyaddha mārjanaṃ likhanaṃ tathā|
astramantraprayogāṇāṃ bhūtānāmapasāraṇam|| 2.63.63 ||
vaiṣṇavītantramantroktaṃ tathaivottaratantrake|
yat proktaṃ tat tu sāmānyaṃ prāk kuryāt sādhako naraḥ|| 2.63.64 ||
tripurāyā viśeṣeṇa sahitaṃ pūjanakramam|
etat trikoṇaṃ devānāṃ trayāṇāṃ sthānamiṣyate|| 2.63.65 ||
aiśānyāṃ tu tatheśāno nairṛtyāṃ caturānanaḥ|
vāyavyāṃ tu tathā brahmā ṣaṭkoṇeṣu prakīrtitāḥ|| 2.63.66 ||
dalaṃ tvekapuraṃ proktaṃ keśaraṃ cāparaṃ puram|
puraṃ śeṣaṃ trikoṇaṃ tu trikoṇaṃ maṇḍalaṃ smṛtam|| 2.63.67 ||
daleṣu keśare cāpi trikoṇe ca tridhā tridhā|
rekhāstu vihitāḥ samyak kuryāt tatra punaḥ punaḥ || 2.63.68 ||
uttaraṃ tad bhaved dvāraṃ tasya vai dhanurākṛtiḥ|
pūrvadvāraṃ tu ṣaṭkoṇaṃ catuṣkoṇaṃ tu dakṣiṇe|| 2.63.69 ||
paścimaṃ toraṇākāraṃ yathā cānyatra maṇḍale|
aiśānyāṃ pañcabāṇāṃstu likheda vahnau ca taddhanuḥ|| 2.63.70 ||
nairṛtyāṃ pustakaṃ cāpi vāyavyāmakṣamālikām|
evaṃ kṛtvā maṇḍalaṃ tu dhṛtvā vāmena pāṇinā|| 2.63.71 ||
vāgveśmane nama iti maṇḍalaṃ pūjayet tataḥ|
pūjayitvā tato bhūtān nālikātritayena tu|| 2.63.72 ||
mūlamantreṇa pūrvoktairmantrairapi samācaret|
navabhiśchoṭikābhistu tridā kṛtvā tu veṣṭanam|| 2.63.73 ||
abhyukṣaṇaṃ tataḥ kuryād bhūtānāmapasāraṇam|
pratipattistu pātrasya ardhyārthaṃ navadhā punaḥ|| 2.63.74 ||
pūrvavat sādhakaḥ kuryād dahanaṃ plavanaṃ tathā|
amṛtīkaraṇaṃ kuryāt prathamaṃ dhenumudrayā|| 2.63.75 ||
yonimudrāṃ tataḥ kuryāt pātratoyaṃ tu triḥ spṛśet|
mārtaṇḍabhairavāyārghyaṃ dūrvābhiḥ siddhasarṣapaiḥ|| 2.63.76 ||
raktapuṣpaiścandanaiśca sagaṇāya nivedayet|
pāṇikacchapikāṃ kṛtvā cintanaṃ yonimudrayā|| 2.63.77 ||
ādau madhye ca kartavyaṃ kramād vetālabhairava|
astramantreṇa pātrasya sthāpanārthaṃ tu maṇḍalam|| 2.63.78 ||
ṣaṭkoṇaṃ tu likhet pūrvaṃ tanmantrasthāpane’pi ca|
aim̐ ām̐ klīmiti mantreṇa tridhā pātre jalaṃ kṣipet|| 2.63.79 ||
tridhā gandhaṃ ca puṣpaṃ ca tridhā dūrvākṣataṃ punaḥ|
hrām̐ hrīm̐ hrūm̐ hraim̐ hraumiti ca aṅguṣṭhādi kramānnyaset|| 2.63.80 ||
oṃ hra ityastramantreṇa pāṇipṛṣṭhatale tathā|
hṛdayādikramāt paścānnyāsaṃ kuryāt tridhā tridhā|| 2.63.81 ||
saṃyojya pāṇyoḥ kramataścāṅguṣṭhādi dvayaṃ dvayam|
tridhā tridhā pṛthak kuryāccheṣāṅgāni ca vinyaset|| 2.63.82 ||
karṇaranghre tathā brahmadvāraṃ keśatalaṃ tathā|
nāsikārandhrayugalaṃ jānuyugmaṃ padadvayam|| 2.63.83 ||
tridhā tridhā nyasedebhiḥ ṣaḍbhirmantraiḥ pṛthak pṛthak|
prāṇāyāmaṃ tataḥ kuryāt pūrakaiḥ stambhakaistathā|| 2.63.84 ||
recakenāpi tripurāmūrti devīṃ vicintayet|
dahanaplavanaṃ kṛtvā ādyāṃ mūrtiṃ vicintayet|| 2.63.85 ||
tridhādṛtyātha hṛdaye tāṃ murtiṃ śṛṇu bhairava|
sindūrapuñjasaṃkāśāṃ trinetrāṃ tu caturbhujām|| 2.63.86 ||
vāmordhve puṣpakodaṇḍaṃ dhṛtvādhaḥ pustakaṃ tathā|
dakṣiṇordhve pañcabāṇānakṣamālāṃ dadhātyadhaḥ|| 2.63.87 ||
caturṇāṃ kuṇapānāṃ tu pṛṣṭhe’nyaṃ kuṇapāntaram|
nidhāya tasya pṛṣṭhe tu samapādena saṃsthitām|| 2.63.88 ||
jaṭājūṭārdhacandreṇa samābaddhaśirodharāma|
nagnāṃ trivalibhedena cārumadhyāṃ manoharām|| 2.63.89 ||
sarvālaṅkārasampūrṇāṃ sarvāṅgasundarī śubhām|
sravaddraviṇasandohāṃ sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutām|| 2.63.90 ||
enāṃ tu prathamaṃ dhyātvā tridhātmānaṃ tu cintayet|
tadrūpaṃ ca tataḥ paścāt puṣpaṃ tadvāgbhavena tu|| 2.63.91 ||
svamastake punardadyādaṅganyāsaṃ punastathā|
mantradvaṃya tridhā japtvā vāgbhavādyaṃ tu sādhakaḥ|| 2.63.92 ||
aghyapātrasya toyeṣu taistoyaiḥ secayecciraḥ|
pajopakaraṇaṃ cāpi trirabhyukṣya tathaiva tu|| 2.63.93 ||
kāmapīṭhaṃ tato dhyātvā pūjayet kramatastvimān|
gaṇeśaṃ ca gaṇādhyakṣaṃ gaṇanāthaṃ tathaiva ca|| 2.63.94 ||
gaṇakrīḍaṃ ca pūrvādidvāre mantreṇa pūjayet|
hairambabījameteṣāṃ mantrastu parikīrtitaḥ|| 2.63.95 ||
vidyāśāntinivṛttiśca pratiṣṭhā dvārapālakāḥ|
kalāntāḥ pūjayet samyak pūrvādikramatastathā|| 2.63.96 ||
siddhaputraṃ jñānaputraṃ tathā sahajaputrakam|
śeṣaṃ samayaputraṃ tu pūjayet baṭukānimān|| 2.63.97 ||
pratyekaṃ tu śriyaṃ devīṃ baṭukānāṃ pare vare|
śrīmityanena mantreṇa pūrvādau pūjayet kramāt|| 2.63.98 ||
siddhasya sahajasyātha jñānasya samayasya ca|
kumārīṃ pūjayet koṇe aiśānyādo tu maṇḍale|| 2.63.99 ||
goraṭaṃ ḍāmaraṃ caiva lohajaṅghaṃ tathaiva ca|
bhūtanāthaṃ kṣetrapālamīśānādau prapūjayet|| 2.63.100 ||
maṇḍalasya ca madhye tu pañcabāṇān prapūjayet|
drāvaṇaṃ śoṣaṇaṃ caiva bandhanaṃ mohanaṃ tathā|| 2.63.101 ||
ākarṣaṇaṃ ca madhyena mantreṇaiva prapūjayet|
tatastriṣvatha koṇeṣu pūjayet tu triyoginīḥ|| 2.63.102 ||
bhagaṃ ca bhagajihvāṃ ca bhagāsyāmuttarādikam |
kramāttu pūjyastisro anyā madhye trikoṇake|| 2.63.103 ||
bhāgamālinīṃ tu prathame dvitīye tu bhagodarīm|
tṛtīye bhagarohāṃ tu yoginīṃ kāmarūpiṇīm|| 2.63.104||
anaṅgakusumāṃ devīṃ tathaivānaṅgamekhalām|
anaṅgamadanāṃ caiva hyanaṅgamadanāturām || 2.63.105 ||
anaṅgaveśāṃ cānaṅgamālinīṃ madanānuturām |
dalakeśaramadhye tu hyaṣṭamīṃ madanāṅgakuśām|| 2.63.106 ||
śailaputryādayaścāṣṭau tripurāpūjanakrame|
etannāmabhiravyagrā babhūvuḥ kāmayoginīḥ|| 2.63.107 ||
vāgbhavena tathā durgāṃ netrabījāntakena tu|
aṅganyāsaṃ samantraistu ṣaḍbhiraṣṭāvimān punaḥ|| 2.63.108 ||
pūjayet kṣetrapālāṃstu mavye kiñjalkapatrayoḥ|
hetukaṃ vipuraghnaṃ ca agnijihvaṃ tathaiva ca|| 2.63.109 ||
agnivetālasaṃjñaṃ ca kālaṃ cātha karālakam|
ekapādaṃ bhīmanāthamuttarādikrameṇa tu|| 2.63.110 ||
ebhirevāṣṭabhirmantraiḥ kāmarājena saṃyutaiḥ|
navaitānasitāṅgādīn nāyakān pūjayet kramāt|| 2.63.111 ||
maṇḍalasya caturdikṣu dvau dvau pūrvādiṣu kramāt|
padmamaṇḍalayormadhye śeṣamekaṃ tu pūjayet|| 2.63.112 ||
asitāṅgo rurucaṇḍaḥ krodhonmattau bhayaṅkaraḥ|
kapālī bhīṣaṇaścaiva saṃhāraśceti vai nava|| 2.63.113 ||
aiśānyādikramād dve dve nāyikāṃ pūjayennaraḥ|
padmamaṇḍalayormadhye agnau dve ca prapūjayet|| 2.63.114 ||
brahmāṇīṃ bhairavīṃ caiva tathā māhesvarīmapi|
kaumārīṃ vaiṣṇavīṃ caiva nārasiṃhīṃ tathaiva ca|| 2.63.115 ||
vārāhīṃ ca tathendrāṇīṃ cāmuṇḍāṃ caṇḍikāṃ tathā|
ādhāraśaktiprabhṛtīn maṇḍalasya tu madhyataḥ|| 2.63.116 ||
vaiṣṇavī tantrakalpoktān sarvān bhairava pūjayet|
śivasya pañca yāḥ proktāḥ sadyojātādayaḥ purā|| 2.63.117 ||
mūrtayastāḥ padmadhye pañcapretatvamāgatāḥ|
tāḥ pañca pūjayenmadhye raktapadmaṃ śavaṃ tathā|| 2.63.118 ||
siṃhaṃ ca pūjayet tatra jagadādhārasaṃjñitam|
jayantīṃ maṅgalāṃ kālīṃ bhadrakālīṃ kapālinīm|| 2.63.119 ||
durgāṃ kṣamāṃ śivāṃ dhātrīṃ svadhāṃ svāhāṃ ca pūjayet|
ugracaṇḍā pracaṇḍā caṇḍogrā caṇḍanāyikā|| 2.63.120 ||
caṇḍā caṇḍavatī caiva caṇḍarūpāticaṇḍikā|
etāḥ sampūjayenmadhye maṇḍalasya viśeṣataḥ|| 2.63.121 ||
ādityādīn grahān sarvān rūpato hyastrasaṃyutān.
kramāt pratyekamuddiśya pārśve pārśve prapūjayet|| 2.63.122 ||
dikpālānāṃ tu mantreṇa tathā sarvāṃstu dikpatīn|
astramantraistu tān sarvāṃsteṣāṃ mantrāṇi bhārava|| 2.63.123 ||
nāthaṃ kāmeśvaraṃ tatra ekavaktraṃ caturbhujam|
bhasmasvetaṃ madhyahṛdi raktapuṣpaistu kuṅkumaiḥ|| 2.63.124 ||
triśūlaṃ ca pinākaṃ ca vāmahastadvaye sthitam |
utpalaṃ bījapūraṃ ca dakṣiṇadvitaye tathā|| 2.63.125 ||
śvetapadmoparisthaṃ ca dhyātvā madhye prapūjayet|
kāmākhyāṃ mūrtito dhyātvā kāmākhyāmapi pūjayet|| 2.63.126 ||
kāmeśvarīṃ tatra devīṃ pūjayet parameśvarīm|
vakṣyamāṇena rūpeṇa tatra vetālabhairavau|| 2.63.127 ||
karālaṃ kṣetrapālaṃ la kartrikharparadhāriṇam|
pūjayedīśamatyarthaṃ daṃṣṭrābhinnādharaṃ bhayam || 2.63.128 ||
tintiḍīṃ kalpavaṛkṣaṃ succhāyaṃ ratnabhūṣitam|
trikūṭaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ ca nīlaśailaṃ mahādyutim|| 2.63.129 ||
manobhavāṃ guhāṃ tatra pañcavyāmāyatāṃ subhāvam|
ratnamaṇḍalasaṃyuktāṃ raktavarṇāṃ suvarttulām|| 2.63.130 ||
aparājitāṃ ca vallīṃ ca vyāmatrayasuvistṛtām|
āraktavarṇāṃ satataṃ kusumairūpaśobhitām|| 2.63.131 ||
baṭukaṃ kambalākhyaṃ tu svarṇagauraṃ gajāsanam|
dvibhujaṃ dakṣiṇe daṇḍapāṇiṃ vāme kapālakam || 2.63.132 ||
bibhrataṃ purato devyāḥ pūjyo vighnavipattaye|
bhairavaḥ pāṇḍunāthaśca raktagauścarbhujaḥ|| 2.63.133 ||
gadāṃ padmaṃ ca śaktiṃ ca cakraṃ cāpi kareṣu ca|
bibhrad devyāḥ purobhāge pūjyo’yaṃ viṣṇurūpadhṛk|| 2.63.134 ||
śmaśānaṃ herukākhyaṃ ca raktavarṇaṃ bhayaṅkaram|
asicarmadharaṃ raudraṃ bhuñjanāṃ manujāmiṣam|| 2.63.135 ||
tisṛbhirmuṇḍamālābhirgaladraktābirājitam|
agninirdagdhavigaladdantapretoparisthitam|| 2.63.136 ||
pūjyeccintanenaiva śatrasvāhanabhūṣaṇam|
mahotsāhāṃ yoginīṃ tu mahāmāyāsvarūpiṇīm|| 2.63.137 ||
dhyānato rūpatastāṃ tu devyā agre prapūjayet|
purīṃ candravatīṃ devyā nīlaparvatapūrvata|| 2.63.138 ||
yojanadvayavistīrṇāmardhayojanamāyatām |
uccairanekaprasāda saudhasadmavibhūṣitām|| 2.63.139 ||
maṇiratnasuvarṇau ghajātaprāsādavistṛtām|
krīḍāsarovaraiḥ sadbhiḥ sañchannāṃ vikacaiḥ kacaiḥ || 2.63.140 ||
saṃyutāṃ pūjayet tatra devyā agre samantrakam|
lauhityaṃ raktagaurāṅgaṃ nīlavastravibhūṣitam|| 2.63.141 ||
ratnamālāsamāyuktaṃ caturbāhusamanvitam|
pustakaṃ śvetapadmaṃ ca bibhrataṃ dakṣiṇe kare|| 2.63.142 ||
vāme śaktidhvajaṃ caiva śiśumārasthitaṃ śubham|
pīṭheśvarānimān madhye mantreretaiḥ prapūjayet|| 2.63.143 ||
nātha kāmeśvaraṃ devaṃ prāsādena prapūjayet|
kāmeśvaryāstu mantreṇa yajet kāmeśvarī śubhām|| 2.63.144 ||
dvāvupāntau balenaiva madanānte ca tatkramāt|
yojayennādabindubhyāṃ māyākaraṇamantrakam || 2.63.145 ||
caṇḍikānetrabījasya yaccheṣamakṣaraṃ tu tat|
kalpaṃ tintiḍikāvṛkṣamantrametat prakīrtitam|| 2.63.146 ||
ugrāyā madhyabījaṃ tu nīlaśailasya mantrakam|
manobhavasya bījaṃ tu mahādevena saṃhitam|| 2.63.147 ||
ādisthenendunā binduyuktaṃ vāntena yojitam|
manobhavaguhāyāṃ tu mantrametat prakīrtitam|| 2.63.148 ||
vaiṣṇavotantramantrasya yaccheṣaṃ bījamasvaram|
tadadho vāntasaṃśliṣṭaṃ caturthasvarasaṃyutam|| 2.63.149 ||
candrabindusamāyuktaṃ tanmantrañcāparājitam|
hayagrīvasvarūpasya viṣṇoryadbījamuttamam || 2.63.150 ||
kambalasya tu tanmantraṃ pūjanaṃ parikīrtitam|
kevalaḥ saprarohādiṣaṣṭhasvarasamanvitaḥ|| 2.63.151 ||
candrabindusamāyuktaṃ hayagrīvasya bījakam|
bhairavaṃ pāṇḍunāthaṃ ca vanamālisvarūpiṇam|| 2.63.152 ||
vārāhema tu bījena pūjayet tu vidhānataḥ|
saparau dvāvanusvāravisargābhyāṃ tu saṃyutau|| 2.63.153 ||
mahābhairavamantreṇa bhairavāntena pūjayet|
mahotsāhāṃ mahāmāyāṃ dvitīyāṣṭākṣareṇa tu|| 2.63.154 ||
devītantroditenaiva pūjayed bhūtivṛddhaye|
ādyākṣaraṃ tu sāmīndubindubhyāṃ samalaṅkṛtam|| 2.63.155 ||
svanāmnaścandravatyāstu pūjāmantraṃ prakīrtitam|
sarvalakṣaṇasampūrṇaṃ sarvālaṅkārabhūṣitam|| 2.63.156 ||
lauhityanadarājasya brahmaputrasya bhūtidam|
brahmabījaṃ tu manmatraṃ vahnibhāryāntamiṣyate|| 2.63.157 ||
dvitīyaṃ tripurārūpaṃ tathaiva tu tṛtīyakam|
āvāhanārthaṃ devyāstu cintayed yonimudrayā|| 2.63.158 ||
bandhūkapuṣpasaṅkāśāṃ jaṭājūṭendumaṇḍitām|
sarvalakṣaṇasampūrṇāṃ sarvālaṅkārabhūṣitām|| 2.63.159 ||
udyadraviprabhāṃ padmaparyaṅkeṣu susaṃsthitām|
muktāratnāvalīyuktāṃ pīnonnatapayodharām|| 2.63.160 ||
valīvibhaṅgacaturāmāsavāmodamoditām|
netrāhṇādakarīṃ śubhrāṃ kṣomaṇīṃ jagatāṃ tathā|| 2.63.161 ||
trinetrāṃ yonimudrāyāmīṣaddhāsasamāyutām|
navayauvanasampannāṃ mṛṇālābhacaturbhujām|| 2.63.162 ||
vāmārdhe pustakaṃ dhatte akṣamālāṃ tu dakṣiṇe|
vāmenābhayadāṃ devīṃ dakṣiṇārdhe varapradām|| 2.63.163 ||
sravadraktaughasūryābhāṃ śiromālāṃ tu bibhratīm|
āpādalambinīṃ kalpadru mamāsādya saṃsthitām|| 2.63.164 ||
kadambopavanāntasthāṃ kāmāhlādakarīṃ śubhām|
dvitīyāṃ tripurāṃ dhyāyedevaṃrūpāṃ manoharām|| 2.63.165 ||
tṛtīyaṃ tripurārūpaṃ śṛṇu vetālabhairava|
javākusumasaṅkāśāṃ muktakeśīṃ śubhānanām|| 2.63.166 ||
sadāśivaṃ hasantaṃ tu pretavad vinidhāya vai|
hṛdaye tasya devasya hyarddhapadmāsanasthitām|| 2.63.167 ||
raktotpalairmiśritāṃ tu muṇḍamālāṃ padānugām|
grīvāyāṃ dhārayantī tu pīnonnatapayodharām|| 2.63.168 ||
caturbhujāṃ tathā nagnāṃ dakṣiṇārdhe’kṣamālinīm|
varadāṃ tadadho vāme jaganmāyāṃ tathābhayām|| 2.63.169 ||
adhastu pustakaṃ dhatte trintrāṃ hasitānanām|
sravadrudhirabhogārtāṃ tathā sarvāṅgasundarīm|| 2.63.170 ||
evaṃvidhaṃ tṛtīyaṃ tu rūpaṃ dhyāyet tu pūjakaḥ|
ādyaṃ tu vāgbhavaṃ rūpaṃ dvitīyaṃ kāmarājakam|| 2.63.171 ||
ḍāmaraṃ mohanaṃ cāpi tṛtīyaṃ parikīrtitam|
ekaikaṃ tu trirūpāṇi prāgvicintyārthasādhakaḥ|| 2.63.172 ||
mantratrayeṇa pratyekaṃ hṛdi ṣoḍaśakaistathā|
pūjayedupacāraistu bahiryadvattathaiva ca|| 2.63.173 ||
mantratrayaṃ tathaikatra kṛtvā ca manamūrtayaḥ|
kartavyā ekatastatra madhyarūpe niveśayet|| 2.63.174 ||
nasāpuṭena niḥsārya dakṣiṇenātha tāṃ punaḥ|
avatārya karābhyāṃ tu devīmāvāhayet tridhā|| 2.63.175 ||
gāyatrītrayamucārya snāpayet prathamaṃ tu tām|
āvāhane tu mantro’yaṃ paṭhitavyaśca sādhakaiḥ|| 2.63.176 ||
ehi devi śubhāvarte yajñe’smin mama sannidhau|
avyucchinnāṃ tataḥ śubhrāṃ vācaṃ kaṇṭhasya dehi me|| 2.63.177 ||
ehye hi bhagavatyamba tripure kāmadāyini|
imaṃ bhāgabaliṃ gṛhya sānnidhyamiha kalpaya|| 2.63.178 ||
nārāyaṇyai ca vidmahe vāgmayāyai ca dhīmahi|
evamuktvā tataḥ paścāt tanno devī pracodayāt|| 2.63.179 ||
nārāṇyai vidmahe tvāṃ caṇḍikāya ca dhīmahi|
śeṣabhāge prayuñjīta tannaḥ kubji pracodayāt|| 2.63.180 ||
mahāmāyāyai vidmahe tvāṃ sammohinyai ca dhīmahi|
paścādevaṃ prayuñjīta tannāścaṇḍi pracodayāt|| 2.63.181 ||
etāstu tripurādevyā gāyatryaḥ parikīrtitāḥ|
pratyekaṃ snāpanaṃ kuryāt tripurāṇāṃ ca tisṛbhiḥ|| 2.63.182 ||
vāgbhavena tu mantreṇa prathamaṃ pūjayecchivām|
kāmarājena vai paścāḍḍāmareṇāpi pūjayet|| 2.63.183 ||
paścādenāṃ tribhirmantraikatraiva tu pūjayet|
tato mantreṇa vai dadyādupacārāṃstu ṣoḍaśa|| 2.63.184 ||
kāmākhyātantragaditān sampūjyāṅgākṣarān punaḥ|
aṅgatyāsasya yanmatrairdevyā aṅgāni pūjayet|| 2.63.185 ||
śeṣaṃ tu mūlamantreṇa cāṣṭāṅgānāṃ prapūjanam|
ekaikaṃ prakramaṃ pūjya tripurāyai namastataḥ|| 2.63.186 ||
navadhā pūjayed devīṃ tripurāṃ kāmarūpiṇīm|
uttarādicatuṣpatre padmasyaitān prapūjayet|| 2.63.187 ||
brahmāṇaṃ mādhavaṃ śambhuṃ bhāskaraṃ ca tathaiva ca|
aiśānyādiṣu teṣvevaṃ kramād devyāḥ prapūjayet|| 2.63.188 ||
jayantīṃ prathamaṃ paścād vāyavyāmaparājitām|
nairṛtyāṃ vijayāṃ caiva tathāgneyyāṃ jayāhvayām|| 2.63.189 ||
trikoṇe keśarasyānte kāmaṃ prītiṃ ratiṃ tathā|
pūjayet pañcabāṇāṃśca puṣpaṃ cāpaṃ ca pustikām|| 2.63.190 ||
akṣamālāṃ pañcaśarān ratnaparyaṅkameva ca|
pretapadmaśivaṃ caiva samyak tatraiva pūjayet|| 2.63.191 ||
sampūjya pūrvavanmālāṃ sphāṭikāmeva bhairava|
ādāyātottarīyeṇa tāmācchādya prayatnataḥ|| 2.63.192 ||
pūrvoddhṛtaṃ japet samyak sādhakastripurāmanum|
japtvā stutiṃ paṭhitvā ca praṇamya ca muhūrmuhuḥ|| 2.63.193 ||
tripurāyai baliṃ dadyāt sambhavāt tat trijātikam|
saphenaistoyasaṃyuktaiḥ śarkarāmadhusaindhavaiḥ|| 2.63.194 ||
abhyukṣya rudhiraṃ dadyāt kāmarājena bhairava|
chedayed vāgbhavenaiva ḍāmarairviharecchiraḥ|| 2.63.195 ||
yatra yatra baliṃ dadyāt sādhako devātarccane|
vaiṣṇavītantrakalpoktamādadyāt pūjane balim|| 2.63.196 ||
tato devyai valīn dadyādetadvarṇakramāt punaḥ|
gokṣīraṃ brāhmaṇoṃ dadyād gavyamājyaṃ tu rājajaḥ|| 2.63.197 ||
vaiśyastu mākṣikaṃ dadyācchūdraḥ puṣpāsavādikram|
ghrātvā puṣpamathaiśānyāṃ nirmālyaṃ nikṣiped budhaḥ|| 2.63.198 ||
nirmālyadhāriṇī cāsyā devī tripuracaṇḍikā|
visṛjyādau yonimudrāṃ padmamudrāṃ tathaiva ca|| 2.63.199 ||
ardhamudrāṃ trimudrāṃ ca pratyekamapi darśayet|
nirmālyamatha gṛhṇīyāt kāmarājahvayena tu|| 2.63.200 ||
evaṃ yaḥ pūjayed devī tripurāṃ kāmarūpiṇīm|
sa kāmanākhilān prāpya devīlokamavāpnuyāt|| 2.63.201 ||
iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe tripurāpūjane triṣaṣṭitamo’dhyāyaḥ||

<64>
||mārkaṇḍeya uvāca||
devyāḥ kāmeśvarīṃ mūrtiṃ śṛṇu vakṣyāmi bhairava|
yasyāścintanamātreṇa sādhako labhate priyān|| 64.1 ||
tantraṃ tasyāḥ prathamatastato’nudhyānagocaram|
tataḥ pūjākramaṃ vakṣyeṃ kramād vetāla bhairava|| 64.2 ||
prajāpatistato vahnirandrabījaṃ tataḥ param|
cūḍācandrārdhasahitaṃ caturthasvarasaṃyutam|| 64.3 ||
idaṃ kāmeśvaraṃ bījamantraṃ sarvārthasādhanam|
sthānābhyukṣaṇayantrādi pātranyāsādikaṃ yathā|| 64.4 ||
bhūtāpasāraṇādīṃśca vaiṣṇavītantrabhāṣitān|
tathoktānuttare yantre gṛhṇīyāt sādhakottamaḥ || 64.5 ||
prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kuryād dahanaṃ plavana tathā|
viśeṣamaṇḍalaṃ cāsyāḥ śṛṇu vetāla bhairava|| 64.6 ||
ṣaṭkoṇaṃ maṇḍalaṃ kuryādraktavarṇaṃ tu cintayet|
vibhedya śaktyā śambhuṃ tu tripurātantravad budhaḥ|| 64.7 ||
tataḥ śaktiṃ śambhunāpi bhedayet kramataḥ sudhīḥ|
aiśānyādinairṛtāntāṃ rekhāṃ kṛtvātha dakṣiṇe || 64.8 ||
paścimāt pūrvagāṃ rekhāṃ pūrvādapi tathottarām|
uttarāt paścimāntāṃ tu kṛtvā rekhāstu yojayet|| 64.9 ||
dhanustoraṇasaṅkāśaṃ dvāre cottarapaścime|
dakṣiṇaṃ tu trikoṇaṃ syāt ṣaṭkoṇaṃ pūrvamucyate|| 64.10 ||
jālandharaṃ likhet pīṭhamuttare paścime likhet|
oḍrapīṭhaṃ dakṣiṇe tu kāmarūpaṃ tu pūrvataḥ|| 64.11 ||
devyā dvādaśaguhyāni yāni dvādaśabhiḥ karaiḥ|
likhenmaṇḍalakoṇeṣu tāni dikṣu trayaṃ trayam|| 64.12 ||
ṣaḍbhiḥ ṣaḍbhistu rekhābhiḥ kartavyo maṇḍalakramaḥ|
anyaduttaratantroktaṃ vaiṣṇavītantrabhāṣitam|| 64.13 ||
maṇḍalasya kramaṃ sarvaṃ viddhi vetāla bhairava|
oṃ klīṃ maṇḍalatattvāya nama ityatra maṇḍalam|| 64.14 ||
pūjayet prathamaṃ dhyātvā maṇḍalaṃ yogapīṭhakam|
pīṭhe śilāyāṃ vilikhenmaṇḍalaṃ yonimaṇḍalam || 64.15 ||
trikoṇaṃ vilikhet paścād veṣṭayet kamalena tu|
rūpaṃ tu cintayed devyāḥ kāmeśvaryā manoharam|| 64.16 ||
prabhinnāñjanasaṅkāśāṃ nīlasnigdhasiroruhām|
ṣaḍvaktrāṃ dvādaśabhujāmaṣṭādaśavilocanām|| 64.17 ||
pratyekaṃ ṣaṭsu śīrṣeṣu candrārdhakṛtaśekharām|
maṇimāṇikyamuktādikṛtamālāmuraḥ sthale|| 64.18 ||
kaṇṭhe ca bibhratīṃ nityaṃ sarvālaṅkāramaṇḍitām|
pustakaṃ siddhasūtraṃ ca pañcabāṇaṃ tu taṃ tathā|| 64.19 ||
khaḍgaṃ śaktiṃ ca śūlaṃ ca bibhratīṃ dakṣiṇaiḥ karaiḥ|
akṣamālāṃ mahāpadmaṃ kodaṇḍaṃ cābhayaṃ tathā|| 64.20 ||
carma paścāt pinākaṃ ca bibhratīṃ vāmapāṇibhiḥ|
śuklaṃ raktaṃ ca pītaṃ ca haritaṃ kṛṣṇameva ca|| 64.21 ||
vicitraṃ kramataḥ śīrṣamaiśānyāṃ pūrvameva ca|
dakṣiṇaṃ paścimaṃ caiva tathaivottaraśīrṣakam|| 64.22 ||
madhyaṃ ceti mahābhāga kramācchīrṣāṇi varṇataḥ|
śuklaṃ māheśvarīvaktraṃ kāmākhyāraktamucyate|| 64.23 ||
tripurā pītasaṅkāśā śāradā haritā tathā|
kṛṣṇaṃ kāmeśvarīvaktraṃ caṇḍāyāścitramiṣyate|| 64.24 ||
dhammillasaṃyatakacaṃ pratiśīrṣa prakīrtitam|
siṃhoparisitapretaṃ tasmiṃllohitapaṅkajam|| 64.25 ||
kāmeśvarī sthitā tatra īṣatprahasitānanā|
vicitrāṃśukasaṃvītāṃ vyāghracarmāmbarāṃ tathā|| 64.26 ||
evaṃ kāmeśvarīṃ dhyāyed dharmakāmārthasiddhaye|
pīṭhe’nyatrāthavādevyāḥ pūjāyāṃ kathyate kramaḥ|| 64.27 ||
pīṭhe viśeṣo vaktavyaḥ sāmānye tvanyadiṣyate|
aṅguṣṭhādikramādeva saṃyojyātha yugaṃ yugam|| 64.28 ||
mūlamantrasyākṣareṇa dīrghasvarayutena ca|
ṣaḍbhirādyairnyaset pūrvamaṅgulīyakameva ca|| 64.29 ||
hṛcchirastu śīrṣavarmanetrāstrāṇi punastathā|
nyased dakṣiṇahastena ṣaḍbhirmantraistathā kramāt|| 64.30 ||
āsyaṃ bāhuyugaṃ kukṣi guhyaṃ jānuyugaṃ tathā|
pādayugmaṃ kramāt testu ṣaḍbhirmantrainyaset tathā|| 64.31 ||
aṣṭadhā mūlamantraṃ tu japtvāthārghāhite jale|
tenopakaraṇaṃ deyaṃ cābhyukṣya kramamārabhet|| 64.32 ||
daiśikaḥ pūjayed devīṃ pīṭhenādaiśikaḥ kvacit|
tasyaiva hi karasparśād devī nodvijate śivā|| 64.33 ||
yadi deśāntarād yātaḥ pīṭhaṃ deśāntaraṃ prati|
taddaiśikopadeśena tadā pūjāṃ samārabhet|| 64.34 ||
yadyanyataḥ samāyātā kāmarūpādṛte naraḥ|
taddeśajopadeśena sampūjyaphalamāpnuyāt|| 64.35 ||
yasmin deśe tu yaḥ pīṭha oḍrapāñcalakādiṣu|
taddeśajopadeśena pūjyaḥ pīṭhe suro naraiḥ|| 64.36 ||
ito’nyathā pūjane na samyak phalamavāpnuyāt|
mahāvibhavasmpūrṇairvihitenaiva bhairava|| 64.37 ||
anukto yaḥ kramaścātra vaiṣṇavītantragocare|
tathaivottaratantre’pi prokto grāhyastu sādhukaiḥ|| 64.38 ||
pūrvadvāri prathamataḥ kāmatattvaṃ prapūjayet|
dakṣiṇe prītitattvaṃ tu ratitattvaṃ ca paścime|| 64.39 ||
uttare mohanaṃ tattvaṃ kramādetāni pūjayet|
aiśānyāṃ pūjayed deva gaṇaiśaṃ dvārapālakam|| 64.40 ||
agnau tu cāgnivetālaṃ nairṛtyāṃ kālameva ca|
vāyavyāṃ nandinaṃ cāpi pūjayet kramatastvimān|| 64.41 ||
catuṣkaṃ pañcakaṃ ṣaṭkaṃ catuṣkaṃ pañcakaṃ catuḥ|
ṣaṭkāraṃ caiva yo veda sa yogyaḥ pīṭhapūjane|| 64.42 ||
auḍrākhyaṃ prathamaṃ pīṭhaṃ dvitīyaṃ jālaśailakam|
tṛtīyaṃ pūrṇapīṭhaṃ tu kāmarūpaṃ caturthakam|| 64.43 ||
oḍrapīṭhaṃ paścime tu tathaivoḍreśvarī śivām|
kātyāyanīṃ jagannāthamoḍreśaṃ ca prapūjayet|| 64.44 ||
uttare pūjayet pīṭhaṃ praśastaṃ jālasailakam|
jāleśvaraṃ mahādevaṃ caṇḍīṃ jāleśvarīṃ tathā|| 64.45 ||
dīrghikāṃ cogracaṇḍāṃ ca tatraiva paripūjayet|
dakṣiṇe pūrṇaśailaṃ tu tathā pūrṇeśvarīṃ śivām|| 64.46 ||
pūrṇanāthaṃ mahānāthaṃ sarojāmatha caṇḍikām|
pūjayed damanīṃ devīṃ śāntāmapi tathā śivām || 64.47 ||
kāmarūpaṃ mahāpīṭhaṃ nāthaṃ kāmeśvarīṃ śivām|
nīlaṃ ca parvataśreṣṭhaṃ nāthaṃ kāmeśvaraṃ tathā|| 64.48 ||
pūjayed dvāri pūrve tu kramādetāṃstu bhairava|
auḍrādīnāṃ tu pīṭhānāṃ kṣetrapālān gurūṃstathā|| 64.49 ||
anyāṃstu dvārapālādīn sve sve sthāne prapūjayet|
viśeṣāt kāmarūpasya kāmeśvarīṃ prapūjayan|| 64.50 ||
tāneva nīlasailasthān śṛṇu vetāla bhairava|
nāthaḥ kāmeśvaro devo devī kāmeśvarī tathā|| 64.51 ||
karālaḥ kṣetrapālāśca ciñcāvṛkṣastathaiva ca|
trikūṭe nīlaśailastu guhā cāpi manobhavā|| 64.52 ||
baṭukaḥ kambalo nāma vallī caivāparājitā|
bhairavaḥ pāṇḍunāthaśca śmaśānaṃ herukāhvayam|| 64.53 ||
yoginī ca mahotsāhā tathā candravatī purī|
lauhityo nadarājaśca prāntā dikkaravāsinī|| 64.54 ||
jalpīśākhyastu vāyavyāṃ kedārākhyo’tha rākṣase|
etān sampūjayed dvāri tathā devyāstu maṇḍale|| 64.55 ||
dvārapālo yoginī ca baṭukādyā yathā tathā|
kāmarūpe pīṭhavare oḍrādiṣvatha tat tathā|| 64.56 ||
madhye tu maṇḍalasyātha drāvaṇaṃ śoṣaṇaṃ tathā|
bandhanaṃ mohanaṃ caiva tathaivākarṣaṇāhvayam|| 64.57 ||
manobhavasya bāṇāṃstu paścaitān paripūjayet|
ṣaṭkoṇāgreṣūttarādau bhagādiṣaṭkameva ca|| 64.58 ||
tripurātantramantroktaṃ pūjayet kramataḥ sudhīḥ|
gaṇākrīḍādikaṃ tadvat tathā vidyākalādikān|| 64.59 ||
baṭukān siddhaputrādīna siddhādyāśca kumārikāḥ|
catuścatuṣkamityetaccatuṣkamiti cocyate|| 64.60 ||
kāmaṃ ratiṃ ca prītiṃ ca anaṅgamekhalādikam|
sapta vai tripuraghnādyā asitāṅgādayo nave|| 64.61 ||
māheśvaryādikā devyo daśabhiḥ pañcabhirgaṇaiḥ|
dvitīyaṃ pañcakaṃ proktaṃ pīṭhe kāmaphalapradam|| 64.62 ||
ādhāraśaktimukhyā ye nityaṃ tatra pratiṣṭhitā|
dharmādyāśca tathaivāṣṭau tathā sattvādikā guṇāḥ|| 64.63 ||
ekatra grahadikpālāścatuṣkamaparaṃ smṛtam|
devyāstathogracaṇḍādyā nāyikāḥ paripūjayet|| 64.64 ||
pūrvoktadeśe mantreṇa bhaktyā vetāla bhairava|
āvāhanaṃ ṣoḍaśopacārāṇāṃ pratipādanam|| 64.65 ||
japaṃ ca balidānaṃ ca aṅgāstrāṇāṃ prapūjanam|
mudrā pūrvā visṛṣṭiśca ṣaṭkametat prakīrtitam|| 64.66 ||
etāni sapta jānāti prakārān pūjakaḥ sudhīḥ|
sa evoḍrādipīṭhāni sampūjayitumarhati|| 64.67 ||
yo’jñātvā samyagetāni kurute pīṭhapūjanam|
na samyak phalamāpnoti hīnāyurapi jāyate|| 64.68 ||
tripurātantramantroktasthāneṣveteṣu bhairava|
pūjayitvā prathamataḥ pūjayet parameśvarīm|| 64.69 ||
kāmeśvari ihāgaccha sammukhī bhava ceśvari|
cintayitvātha manasā’bhyarcya kāmeśvarīṃ hṛdi|| 64.70 ||
mānasairgandhapuṣpādyaistato dakṣiṇanāsayā|
niḥsārya vāyuṃ tat puṣpamāropya maṇḍalāntare|| 64.71 ||
āvāhayenmahādevīṃ sarvakāmeśvareśvarīm|
kāmeśvari ihāgaccha sammukhī bhava sannidhau|| 64.72 ||
kāmeśvari vidmahe tvāṃ kāmākhyāyai ca dhīmahi|
tannaḥ kubji mahāmāye tataḥ paścāt pracodayāt|| 64.73 ||
ehyehi bhagavatyamba lokānugrahakāriṇi|
kāmeśe kāmarūpe tvaṃ kāmakānte prasoda me|| 64.74 ||
tatastu prathama snānaṃ jalaṃ dattvā tu pūjakaḥ|
mūlamantreṇa vitaredupacārāṃstu ṣoḍaśa|| 64.75 ||
pūjayenmadhyabhāge tu ṣaḍaṅgāni tato’rccayet|
aṅganyāse tu ye mantrāḥ krame pūrvaṃ tu bhāṣitāḥ|| 64.76 ||
taireva mantrairaṅgāni devyā api ca pūjayet|
pūrvādyaṣṭadaleṣvetā yoginīḥ paripūjayet|| 64.77 ||
yathākrameṇa kāmānāṃ siddhyarthaṃ kāmadāyikāḥ|
guptakāmāṃ tu śrīkāmāṃ tathaiva vindhyavāsinīm|| 64.78 ||
koṭeśvarīṃ vanasthāṃ tu yoginīṃ pādacaṇḍikām|
dīrgheśvarīṃ tu prakaṭāṃ bhuvaneśīṃ kramād yajet|| 64.79 ||
vaiṣṇavītantramantrasya yānyaṣṭāvakṣarāṇi tu|
tāni bindibanduyuktāni mantranyāsāṃśca ca prakṣate|| 64.80 ||
mantreṣu ṣaṣṇāṃ koṇānāṃ ṣaḍimāḥ paripūjayet|
aiśānyādikrameṇaiva kāmākhyāṃ tripurāṃ tathā|| 64.81 ||
śāradāṃ ca mahotsāhāṃ prakaṭāṃ bhuvaneśvarīm|
siddhakāmeśvarīṃ cāpi devyā rūpāṇi bhairava|| 64.82 ||
aṣṭapuṣpikayā devīṃ punaḥ sampūjya cāṣṭadhā|
japtvā stutvā baliṃ dattvā natvā mudrāṃ pradarśya ca|| 64.83 ||
devyāstu siddhacaṇḍyā vai nirmālyaṃ pratipādya ca|
visṛjya maṇḍalād devīṃ sthāpayed yonimaṇḍale|| 64.84 ||
etat kāmeśvarītantraṃ kathitaṃ yuvayoḥ sutau|
śāradāyā mahātantraṃ samantraṃ śṛṇu bhairava|| 64.85 ||
iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe tripurāpūjanaṃ nāma catuḥṣaṣṭitamo’dhyāyaḥ||

<65>
||śrībhagavānuvāca||
śaratkāle purā yasmānnavamyāṃ bodhitā suraiḥ|
śāradā sā samākhyātā poṭhe loke ca mānava || 65.1 ||
tasyāṃ tu netrabījākhyaṃ mantraṃ prāk pratipāditam|
durgātantraṃ ca tanmantramaṅgamantraṃ puroditam|| 65.2 ||
tābhyāmeva tu mantrābhyāṃ pūjayet tāṃ jaganmayīm|
tṛtīyaṃ pīṭhamantraṃ tu śāradāyā anuttamam|| 65.3 ||
śṛṇutaṃ caikamanasā caturvargapradayākam|
caturthasvarasaṃyuktamupānto vahninā yutaḥ|| 65.4 ||
kāmarājaṃ tathā nāntamupāntasvarasaṃyutam|
vahninā cāpi sandīptaḥ sarvabindbindusaṃyutaḥ|| 65.5 ||
hādiḥ samāptisahita etadbījaṃ caturthakam|
caturbhirebhiḥ kathito mantroktaiśca ṣaḍakṣaraiḥ|| 65.6 ||
ayaṃ tṛtīyo mantrastu śāradāyāḥ prakīrtitaḥ|
anena pūjayet pīṭhe sarvasiddhimavāpnuyāt|| 65.7 ||
rūpamasyāḥ purā proktaṃ siṃhasthaṃ daśabāhubhiḥ|
tatra pūjākramaṃ samyak śṛṇutaṃ putrakau mama|| 65.8 ||
caturdvāramaṇḍalaṃ tu kuryāt tatra vibhūtaye|
mahāmāyāmaṇḍalaṃ tu śāradāyastu maṇḍalam|| 65.9 ||
vaiṣṇavītantrakalpoktairmantrasthānādimārjanam|
kṛtvā tu netrabījena maṇḍalaṃ prastare likhet|| 65.10 ||
yonāvaṣṭadalaṃ kṛtvā trikoṇaṃ madhyato nyaset|
ayaṃ viśeṣaḥ kathito vaiṣṇavīmaṇḍalāt punaḥ|| 65.11 ||
maṇḍalollekhanaṃ caiva tathā bhūtāpasāraṇam|
pātrasya pratipattistu amṛtīkaraṇaṃ tathā|| 65.12 ||
gandhapuṣpāmbhasāṃ kṣepa ātmāsanaprapūjanam|
prāṇāyāmaśca trividho bhūtiśuddhipraveśanam|| 65.13 ||
dahanaplavane caiva pāṇikacchapikā tathā|
yogapīṭhasya ca dhyānaṃ vaiṣṇavotantrabhāṣitam|| 65.14 ||
tathaivottaratantroktaṃ kuryād devyāḥ prapūjanam |
amṛtīkaraṇaṃ kuryāt salile dhenumudrayā|| 65.15 ||
rūpaṃ tvevaṃ daśabhujaṃ pūrvoktaṃ tu vicintayet|
aṅganyāsakaranyāsau durgātantreṇa bhairava|| 65.16 ||
navākṣareṇa vai kuryādaṅguṣṭhādi krameṇa tu|
hṛdayādikramād paścād vaktrādāvapi pūrvavat|| 65.17 ||
etadevārghapātre cāṣṭadhā mantraṃ japet sudhīḥ|
tat toyaiḥ secayecchīrṣaṃ puṣpagandhādikaṃ tathā|| 65.18 ||
evaṃ pūjākramaṃ tatra kuryād devyāstu maṇḍale|
ādityaṃ caṇḍikārūpaṃ dhyātvā pūrve śilātale|| 65.19 ||
tasmai nivadayedarghyaṃ siddhārthākṣata puṣpakaiḥ|
ādhāraśaktiprabhṛtīn klīm̐ mantreṇa ca sādhakaḥ|| 65.20 ||
pūjayet prathamaṃ madhye dharmādīnapi pūrvavat|
sattvādon gurupādāntān pūrvatantroditān budhaḥ|| 65.21 ||
pūjayenmadhyapadme tu sumerumapi madhyataḥ|
pūrvabhāge maṇḍalasya devyāḥ śaktīḥ prapūjayet|| 65.22 ||
nāthakāmesvarādīṃstu lauhityāntān viśeṣataḥ|
sarvān vai pīṭhadevāṃstu maṇḍalasyottare yajet|| 65.23 ||
maṇikarṇaṃ citrarathaṃ bhasmakūṭaṃ tathaiva ca|
śvetaṃ nīlaṃ ca citraṃ ca vārāhaṃ gandhamādanam|| 65.24 ||
maṇikūṭaṃ nandanaṃ ca paścime pūjayedimān|
jalpīśamatha kedāraṃ devīṃ dikkaravāsinīm|| 65.25 ||
dhātrīṃ svadhāṃ tathā svāhāṃ mānastokāparājite|
dakṣiṇe pūjayedetāścatuḥṣaṣṭiṃ ca yoginī|| 65.26 ||
grahāṃśca daśadikpālān pūrvādyuktakrameṇa tu|
pūrvavat pūjayed dhīmān bhairava bhairavīmapi|| 65.27 ||
tataḥ kacchapikāṃ baddhvā punareva tu pūjakaḥ|
dhyāyecca pūrvavad devīṃ hṛdisthāṃ manasāpi ca|| 65.28 ||
mānasairgandhapuṣpādyaiḥ pūjayitvā hṛdi sthitām|
nāsāpuṭena niḥsārya dakṣiṇenātha maṇḍale|| 65.29 ||
puṣpamāropya kāmākhyāṃ śāradāmāhvayenmuhuḥ|
ehyehi parameśāni sānnidhyamiha kalpaya|| 65.30 ||
pūjābhāgaṃ gṛhāṇemaṃ mūkhaṃ rakṣa namo’stu te |
durge durge ihāgaccha sarvaiḥ parikaraiḥ saha|| 65.31 ||
pūjābhāgaṃ gṛhāṇema makhaṃ rakṣa namo’stu te|
nārāyaṇyai vidmahe caṇḍikāyai tu dhīmahi|| 65.32 ||
śeṣabhāge tu gāyatryāstannaścaṇḍi pracodayāt|
dattvā snānamanenaiva durgā tantraṇa vai punaḥ|| 65.33 ||
netrabījena ca tathā pīṭhamantreṇa cāntaram|
caturakṣareṇa śeṣeṇa tribhirmantraiḥ prapūjayet|| 65.34 ||
caturakṣaramantreṇa pādyādīnatha ṣoḍaśa|
vitaredupacārāṃstu pūrvoktāṃstāṃstu bherava|| 65.35 ||
durgātantreṇa mantreṇa devyaṅgāni prapūjayet|
dūrgetyanena hṛdayaṃ punardurgetyanena kam|| 65.36 ||
śikhākavacanetrañca pādapādāṃśca pañcabhiḥ|
vādipañcākṣaraiḥ śeṣaiḥ pūjayet kramataḥ sudhīḥ|| 65.37 ||
pūrvādyaṣṭadaleṣvetāḥ pūjayennādhikakramāt|
jayantīṃ pūrvapatre tu āgneyyādau tu maṅgalām|| 65.38 ||
kālīṃ ca bhadrakālīṃ ca tathā caiva kapālinīm|
durgāṃ śivāṃ kṣamāṃ caiva kramādeva tu nāmataḥ|| 65.39 ||
keśavasya tu madhye tu aṣṭāvetāstu nāyikāḥ|
netrabījasya mantreṇa bījena ṣaṭsu nāyikāḥ|| 65.40 ||
amīṣāṃ ca tathavāsau ṣaḍbhiretāntarāhitaiḥ|
hrām̐ hrām̐ śromityupāntāṃ tu prāntāmādyasvareṇa vai|| 65.41 ||
ugracaṇḍāṃ pracaṇḍāṃ ca caṇḍogrāṃ caṇḍanāyikām|
caṇḍāṃ caṇḍavatīṃ caiva caṇḍarūpāṃ ca caṇḍikām|| 65.42 ||
trikoṇakeśarāntaṃ ca kāmaṃ prītiṃ ratiṃ tathā|
pañcabāṇān puṣpadhanuḥ pūjayet kāmamantrakaiḥ|| 65.43 ||
aṣṭapuṣpikayā paścāt sampūjya parameśvarīm|
devyāstu karagṛhyāṇi śastrāṇyastrāṇi vāhanam|| 65.44 ||
pañcānanaṃ keśaraṃ ca devyagre tu prapūjayet|
pīṭhadevīṃ śāradāṃ tu kāmākhyāmadhidevatām|| 65.45 ||
tripurākhyāṃ mahādevīṃ pīṭhamatyadhidevatām|
kāmeśvarīṃ mahotsāhāṃ madhya eva prapūjayet|| 65.46 ||
caturakṣaramantreṇa dadyāt puṣpāñjalitrayam|
japtvā stutvā baliṃ dattvā namaskṛtyāvaguṇṭhya ca|| 65.47 ||
yonimudrāṃ pradarśyātha nirmālyaṃ diśi śūlinaḥ |
caṇḍeśvaryai namaḥ iti nikṣipya ca visarjayet|| 65.48 ||
tatastu bhāskarāyārghya dadyācchidrāvadhāraṇam|
devīṃ ca hṛdaye sthāpya sthāpayed yonimaṇḍale|| 65.49 ||
evaṃ devī tu kāmākhyāṃ yonimudrāṃ jaganmayīm|
śāradākhyāṃ mahādevīṃ yogena vidhinā yajet|| 65.50 ||
sarvakāmān susamprāpya śivalokamavāpnuyāt|
yadi pīṭhaṃ vinānyatra pūjayet kāmarūpiṇīm|| 65.51 ||
nīlakūṭe tadāpyetat sarvameva samācaret|
yadānyatra yajed devīṃ jale vā sthaṇḍile’pi vā|| 65.52 ||
śilādiṣu ca vahnau vā devapīṭhe yathecchayā|
yajed vā na yajed vāpi pīṭhe’vaśyaṃ prapūjayet|| 65.53 ||
evaṃ yaḥ pañcabhirmantraiḥ pañcamūrtidharāṃ śivām|
ekaikenātha vā tasya svayaṃ syād varadāyikā || 65.54 ||
vighnā na tasya jāyante nādhayo vyādhayastathā|
na tasya sadṛśo’nyaḥ syād dhanadhānyasamṛddhibhiḥ|| 65.55 ||
gavāṃ koṭipradānāt tu yatphalaṃ jāyate nṛṇām|
tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti kāmākhyāṃ pūjayannaraḥ|| 65.56 ||
daśapūrvān daśaparān vaṃśānuddhṛtya pāpataḥ|
sakṛt sampūjanenaiva mama lokamavāpnuyāt|| 65.57 ||
dviḥ sampūjya mahādevīṃ kāmākhyāṃ yonimaṇḍale|
śataṃ vaṃśmān samuddhṛtya devīlokamavāpnuyāt|| 65.58 ||
yastrivārān pūjayet tu vidhinānena mānavaḥ|
nīlaparvatamāruhya kāmākhyāṃ yonimaṇḍale|| 65.59 ||
sa sahasraṃ tu vaṃśānāmuddhṛtya pāpakoṣataḥ |
ihaloke sukhaiśvaryacirāyuṣyamavānuyāt|| 65.60 ||
dehānte madgṛhaṃ prāpya gaṇānāmadhipo bhavet|
yasyāṃ kasyāmathāṣṭamyāṃ navamyāṃ vāpi sādhakaḥ|| 65.61 ||
pañcarūpāṃ tu kāmākhyāṃ pañcamantraiḥ satantrakaiḥ|
pūjayed varadāṃ devīṃ maṇḍalaiśca pṛthak pṛthak|| 65.62 ||
dhyātvā tu pañcarūpāṇi japtvā mantrāṃśca pañca vai|
kalpakoṭisahasrāṇi mama loke ca mānavaḥ|| 65.63 ||
sthitvā devīprasādena pare nirvāṇamāpnuyāt|
iha loke vāñchitārthaṃ sukhaṃ prāpya yaśastathā|| 65.64 ||
ripūñjitvā sa dharmātmā mātaṅgāniva kesarī|
cirāyuḥ putrapautraiśca vibhavaiśca samanvitaḥ|| 65.65 ||
krīḍayitvā hyamaravad yuvatībhiśca sādarāt|
yakṣarakṣaḥpiśācānāṃ netā bhavati nityaśaḥ|
sarvān kāmānavāpyaiva dvijarājasamo bhavet|| 65.66 ||
iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe pañcaṣaṣṭitamo’dhyāyaḥ||

<66>
||aurvva uvāca||
etattantraṃ samastaṃ tu śrutvā vetālabhairavau|
papracchatustryambakaṃ ca harṣotphullavilocanau|| 66.1 ||
||vetālabhairavāvūcatuḥ||
kāmākhyāyāḥ śrutaṃ tantraṃ sāṅgaṃ yuṣmatprasādataḥ|
namaskāraṃ tathā mudrāṃ balidānaṃ tathaiva ca|| 66.2 ||
tathaiva mātṛkānyāsaṃ pūjāyāṃ cānyataḥ kramam|
etat sarvaṃ samācakṣva vistareṇa jagatprabho|
śṛṇvato nahi nau tṛptirjāyate modabhūmiṣu|| 66.3 ||
||śrībhagavānuvāca||
vakṣyāmi yadahaṃ pṛṣṭo bhavadbhyāṃ putrakottamau|
śṛṇutaṃ naraśārdūlāvekāgramanasādhunā|| 66.4 ||
trikoṇamatha ṣaṭkoṇamardhacandraṃ pradakṣiṇam|
daṇḍamaṣṭāṅgamugraṃ ca saptadhā natilakṣaṇam|| 66.5 ||
aiśānī vātha kauberī dik kāmākhyāprapūjane|
praśastā sthaṇḍilādau ca sarvamūrteśca sarvataḥ|| 66.6 ||
trikoṇādivyavasthā tu yadi pūrvamukho yajet|
paścimācchāmbhīṃ gatvā vyavasthāṃ nirddiśet tadā|| 66.7 ||
yadottarāmukhaḥ kuryāt sādhako devapūjanam|
tadā yāmyāṃ tu vāyavyāṃ gatvā kuryāt tu saṃsthitim|| 66.8 ||
dakṣiṇād vāyavīṃ gatvā diśaṃ tasmācca śāmbhavīm|
tato’pi dakṣiṇāṃ gatvā namaskārastrikoṇavat|| 66.9 ||
trikoṇākhyo namaskārastripurāprītidāyakaḥ|
dakṣiṇād vāyavīṃ gatvā vāyavyācchāmbhavīṃ tataḥ|| 66.10 ||
tato’pi dakṣiṇāṃ gatvā tāṃ tyaktvāgnau praviśya ca|
agnito rākṣasīṃ gatvā tatpaścāduttarāṃ diśam|| 66.11 ||
uttarācca tathāgneyīṃ bhramaṇaṃ dvitrikoṇavat|
ṣaṭhkoṇo’yaṃ namaskāraḥ prītidaḥ śivadurgayoḥ|| 66.12 ||
dakṣiṇād vāyavīṃ gatvā tasmādāvṛtya dakṣiṇam|
gatvā yo’sau namaskāraḥ so’rdhacandraḥ prakīrtitaḥ|| 66.13 ||
sakṛt pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā vartulākṛti sādhakaḥ|
namaskāraḥ kathyate’sau pradakṣiṇa iti dvijaiḥ|| 66.14 ||
tyaktvā svamāsanasthānaṃ paścād durgānamaskṛtiḥ|
pradakṣiṇaṃ vinā yātu nipatya bhuvi daṇḍavat|| 66.15 ||
daṇḍa ityucyate devaiḥ sarvadevaughamodadaḥ|
pūrvavad daṇḍavad bhūmau nipatya hṛdayena tu|| 66.16 ||
cibukena mukhenātha nāsayā hanukena ca|
brahmarandhreṇa karṇābhyāṃ yadbhūmisparśanaṃ kramāt|| 66.17 ||
sa cāṣṭāṅga iti prokto namaskāro manīṣibhiḥ|
pradakṣiṇatrayaṃ kṛtvā sādhako vartulākṛtiḥ|| 66.18 ||
brahmarandhreṇa saṃsparśaḥ kṣiteryasmānnamaskṛtau|
sa ugra iti devaughairucyate viṣṇutuṣṭidaḥ|| 66.19 ||
nadānāṃ sāgaro yadvad dvipadaṃ brāhmaṇo yathā|
nadīnāṃ jāhnavo yādṛg devānāmapi cakradhṛk|| 66.20 ||
namaskāreṣu sarveṣu tathaivograḥ praśasyate|
trikoṇādyairnamaskāraiḥ kṛtaireva tu bhaktitaḥ|| 66.21 ||
caturvargaṃ labhed bhakto nacirādeva sādhakaḥ|
namaskāro mahāyajñaḥ prītidaḥ sarvataḥ sadā|| 66.22 ||
sarveṣāmeva devānāmanyeṣāmapi bhairava|
yo’sāvugro namaskāraḥ prītidaḥ satata hareḥ|| 66.23 ||
mahāmāyāprītikaraḥ sa namaskaraṇottamaḥ|
uktāstatra namaskārāḥ śṛṇutaṃ parato yuvām|| 66.24 ||
mudrāṇāṃ parisaṃkhyānaṃ svarūpaṃ ca yathākramam|
dhenuśca sampuṭaścaiva prāñjalirbilvapadmakau|| 66.25 ||
nārāco muṇḍadaṇḍau ca yonirardhaṃ tathaiva ca|
vandanī ca mahāmudrā mahāyonistathaiva ca|| 66.26 ||
bhagaśca puṭakaścaiva niṣaṅgovā’rdhacandrakaḥ|
aṅgaśca dvimukhaṃ caiva śaṅkhamudrā ca muṣṭikaḥ|| 66.27 ||
vajraṃ caiva tathā randhraṃ ṣaḍyonirvimala tathā|
ghaṭaḥ śikhariṇītuṅgaḥ puṇḍro’tha hyardhapuṇḍrakaḥ 66.28 ||
sammilanī ca kuṇḍaśca cakraṃ śūlaṃ tathaiva ca|
siṃhavaktraṃ gomukhaṃ ca pronnāmonnamanaṃ tathā|| 66.29 ||
bimbaṃ pāśupataṃ śuddhaṃ tyāgo’thotsāriṇī tathā|
prasāriṇī cogramudrā kuṇḍalīvyūha eva ca|| 66.30 ||
trimukhā cāsivallī ca yogo bhedo’tha mohanam|
bāṇo dhanuśca tūṇīraṃ mudrā etāśca sattamāḥ|| 66.31 ||
aṣṭottaraśataṃ mudrā brahmaṇā yāḥ prakīrtitāḥ|
tāsāṃ tu pañcapañcāśadetā grāhyāstu pūjane|| 66.32 ||
śeṣāstu yāstripañcāśanmudrāstāḥ samayeṣu ca|
dravyānayanasaṃketa naṭanādiṣu tāḥ smṛtāḥ|| 66.33 ||
devānāṃ cintane yoge dhyāne japye visarjane|
ādyāstu pañcapañcāśanmudrā bhairava kīrtitā|| 66.34 ||
mudrāṃ binā tu yajjapyaṃ prāṇāyāmaḥ surārccanam|
yogo dhyānāsane cāpi niṣphalāni ca bhairava|| 66.35 ||
pratyekaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ teṣāṃ śṛṇutaṃ tanayau yuvām|
dakṣiṇāmadhyamāgreṇa savyahastasya tarjanīm|| 66.36 ||
yojayet savyamadhyāṃ tu tarjanyā dakṣiṇena vai|
tathā dakṣiṇānāmikayā vāmahastakaniṣṭhitakām|| 66.37 ||
anāmikāṃ tu vāmasya dakṣiṇasya kaniṣṭhayā|
yojayed bhaktimān smyag dakṣiṇāvartanena tu|| 66.38 ||
dhenumudrā samākhyātā sarvadevasya tuṣṭidā|
saṃyojya dvau talau sarvāṇyaṃgulyagrāṇi hastayoḥ|| 66.39 ||
saṃyojya pāśvato’ṅguṣṭhau sampuṭaḥ procyate suraiḥ|
sarveṣāmatha devānāṃ sampuṭaḥ prītidāyakaḥ|| 66.40 ||
dhyānacintanayogādau sampuṭaḥ śasyate sadā|
nikubjayugalaṃ pāṇyostaṃ saṃyojyārdha eva ca|| 66.41 ||
madhyaśūnyaḥ puṭākāraḥ prāñjaliḥ parikīrtitaḥ|
aṅguṣṭhamantaraṃ kṛtvā pāṇyoṣṭiṃ vidhāya ca|| 66.42 ||
saṃyojya bilvavatte tu bilvamudrā prakīrtitā|
maṇibandhādākarabhaṃ sayojya karayordvayoḥ|| 66.43 ||
aṅguṣṭhe cāpi saṃyojya tathaiva ca kaniṣṭhike|
tisrastisrastayoḥ pāṇyoraṅgulorviralāstathā|| 66.44 ||
padmamudrā samākhyātā caturvargaphalā nṛṇām|
aṅguṣṭhāgreṇa tarjanyā saṃyojyāthordhvarekhayā|| 66.45 ||
anyāṅgulīstathānamya nārācaḥ syāt prasārya te|
mama caiva śivāyāśca prītideyaṃ priyaṅkarī|| 66.46 ||
nārācamudrā satataṃ prītyai vetālabhairava|
antaraṅguṣṭhamuṣṭiṃ ca kṛtvā vāmakarasya tu|| 66.47 ||
madhyamāyā dakṣiṇasya tathānamya prayatnataḥ|
madhyamenātha tarjanyā aṅguṣṭhāgraṃ niyojya ca|| 66.48 ||
dakṣiṇaṃ yojayet pāṇiṃ vāmamuṣṭīṃ ca sādhakaḥ|
darśayed dakṣiṇe bhāge muṇḍamudreyamiṣyate|| 66.49 ||
iyaṃ tu gaṇanāthasya prītidā mudrikottamā|
sarveṣāmapi devānāṃ tuṣṭidā sarvakarmasu|| 66.50 ||
aṅguṣṭhamadhyamādīṃśca samyagānamya tarjanīm|
prasārya daṇḍamudreti dakṣiṇasya karasya ca|| 66.51 ||
sarvāṅgulīstu saṃyojya karayorubhayorapi|
saṃveṣṭya rajjuvad veti pāṇyorapi kaniṣṭhike|| 66.52 ||
vāmasyānāmamūleṃ vai udagraṃ viniyojayet|
dakṣasya madhyamāmūle tathāgraṃ vāmameva ca|| 66.53 ||
yojayed yojanāt paścādāvartya karaśākhikāḥ|
yonyākāraṃ tu tanmadhyaṃ yonimudrā prakīrtitā|| 66.54 ||
kāmākhyāyāḥ pañcamūrterdugāyā api bhairava|
prītidā yonimudreyaṃ mama kāmasya ca priyā|| 66.55 ||
saṃsaktā aṅgulīḥ sarvāḥ prasāryāṅguṣṭhaparvaṇā|
agreṇa ca kaniṣṭhāyā agreṇāpi ca yojayet|| 66.56 ||
karasya dakṣiṇasyaivamardhayoniḥ prakīrtitā|
mahāyonistu kathitā vaiṣṇavītantraṇe vare|| 66.57 ||
sampuṭaṃ prāñjaliṃ vāpi yadi śīrṣe pradarśayet|
vandanīyā samakhyātā mudrā viṣṇupramodinī || 66.58 ||
saiva cecchravaṇāsaktā mahāmudrā prakīrtitā|
dakṣiṇāṅge tu sā saktā vaiṣmavī parikīrtitā|| 66.59 ||
mahāyonistu kathitā vaiṣṇavī tantragocare|
dvayostu mūle’ṅguṣṭhāgramaṅgulīṃ ca kaniṣṭhayoḥ|| 66.60 ||
niyojya prasṛtīkṛtya dvau pāṇī yojayet punaḥ|
bhagamudrā samākhyātā lakṣmīvāṇīśivapriyā|| 66.61 ||
sarvāṅgulīnāmagraughaṃ dakṣiṇasya ca|
saṃyojyaikatra purato nirdeśaḥ puṭakaḥ smṛtaḥ|| 66.62 ||
kaniṣṭhānāmikāṅguṣṭhāṅgulīnā yojayed budhaḥ|
agrāṇyekatra madhyāṃ tu tarjanīṃ ca prasārya vai|| 66.63 ||
kubjīkṛtya karadvandvaṃ pṛthagagre nidarśayet|
niḥsaṅganāmamudreyaṃ narasiṃhavarāhayoḥ|| 66.64 ||
kaniṣṭhānāmikādhyamākuñcan dakṣiṇena tu|
karasya tarjanyaṅguṣṭhe prasārya kriyate tu yā|| 66.65 ||
sā mudrā hyardhacandrākhyā grahāṇāṃ prītidāyinī|
ūrdhvīkṛtya tathāṅguṣṭhaṃ karasya dakṣiṇasya tu|| 66.66 ||
kṛtvā madhyāṃ tadaṅguṣṭhaṃ vāmamuṣṭiṃ tathordhvataḥ|
ūrdhvāṅguṣṭhāṃ tathā kuryādaṅgamudrā prakīrtitā|| 66.67 ||
etasyā eva mudrāyāḥ kaniṣṭhādiviyogataḥ|
aṣṭau mudrāḥ samākhyātā nāma tāsāṃ pṛthak śṛṇu || 66.68 ||
dvimukhaṃ caiva muṣṭiṃ ca vajramābaddhameva ca|
vimalaśca ghaṭaścaiva tuṅgaḥ puṇḍrastathaiva ca|| 66.69 ||
navānāṃ viṣṇumūrtitā sārdhasaṅgena mudrikāḥ|
kramānnava samākhyātā nāyikānāṃ tathaiva ca|| 66.70 ||
saṃyojya karayoḥ pṛṣṭhe tathāvartya tu vai samam|
prasārya tarjanīyugmaṃ saṃyuktaṃ sarvataḥ punaḥ|| 66.71 ||
aṅguṣṭhoca tatāsaktau śaṅkhamudrā prakīrtitā|
uttānamañjaliṃ kṛtvā aṅguṣṭhe dve kaniṣṭhayoḥ|| 66.72 ||
mūle nikṣipya tu karau saṃyojyātha pradarśayet|
sā yoniriti vikhyātā mudrā devaughatuṣṭidā|| 66.73 ||
muṣṭirdakṣiṇahastasya yadordhāṅguṣṭhikā bhavet|
sā syāñchikhariṇīmudrā brāhmīsūryapriyā ca sā|| 66.74 ||
anāmike kaniṣṭhe ca saṃyojya vāyunā punaḥ|
madhyamā tarjanīnāṃ tu dhenumudreva bandhanam|| 66.75 ||
sārdhadhenuriti khyātā candraprīti vivardhinī|
karayoraṅgulīnāṃ tu sarvāgrāṇyekataḥ sthitā|| 66.76 ||
niyojya dve tale caiva tadadho’pi niyojya ca|
agrairagraiyojayet tu mudrā sammīlanī tu sā|| 66.77 ||
bhaumabhūmimunīśānāmiyaṃ prītivivardhinī|
sarvāṅgulīstu saṃyojya dakṣisya karasya ca|| 66.78 ||
kiyadbhāgaṃ tathānamya talaṃ kuryāt tu kuṇḍavat|
samākhyātā kuṇḍamudrā budhavāṇośivapriyā|| 66.79 ||
sarvāṅgulīnāṃ madhyaṃ tu vāmahastasya cāṅgulīḥ|
prasāryāṅguṣṭhayugalaṃ saṃyojyāgreṇa bhairava|| 66.80 ||
tadaṅguṣṭhadvayaṃ kārya sammukhaṃ vitarem tataḥ|
cakramudrā samākhyātā guruviṣṇuśivapriyā|| 66.81 ||
aṅguṣṭhaṃ madhyamāṃ caiva nāmayitvā karasya tu|
dakṣiṇasya parāstisro yojayedagrataḥ punaḥ|| 66.82 ||
śūlamudrā samākhyātā mama śukragrahapriyā|
nikubjīkṛtya tu karau vāmaṅguligaṇasya tu|| 66.83 ||
agrāṇi yojayenmadhye talasyāsavyahastataḥ |
adhaḥ kṛtvā vāmahastaṃ mudrā siṃhamukhī smṛtā|| 66.84 ||
iyaṃ prītyai tu durgāyāḥ sūryaputrasya cakriṇaḥ|
bhagamudrā karṇamūle gomukhākhyā prakīrtitā|| 66.85 ||
mama viṣṇostathā rāhoḥ sarvadā prītidāyinī|
muṣṭidvayamathottānaṃ kṛtvā saṃyojya pārśvataḥ|| 66.86 ||
dakṣiṇasya kaniṣṭhādīna prasārya kramataḥ punaḥ|
tathā vāmakaniṣṭhābhyāmekaikena prasārayet|| 66.87 ||
aṣṭau mudrāḥ samākhyātā nāmataḥ kramataḥ śṛṇu|
prollāsonnamanaṃ caiva vimbaṃ pāśupataṃ tathā|| 66.88 ||
śuddhaṃ tyāgaḥ sāraṇī ca tathā caiva prasāraṇī|
ākuñcakaraśākhāstu dakṣiṇā sā tu mudrikā|| 66.89 ||
ugramudrā samākhyātā svahastasya viparyayāt|
indrādilokapālānāṃ daśamudrāḥ prakīrtitāḥ|| 66.90 ||
sarveṣāmeva devānāṃ paramaprītivardhanāḥ |
aṅguṣṭhāgraṃ tu tarjanyā age bhārgena yojayet|| 66.91 ||
ākuñcamadhyamādyāstu dakṣahastasya cāṅgulīḥ|
daśayet kuṇḍalākāraṃ kuṇḍalīśaktituṣṭidam|| 66.92 ||
sarveṣāmapi devānāṃ yathā tuṣṭikaraṃ mahat|
aṅguṣṭhatarjanīmadhyā agrabhāge niyojya ca|| 66.93 ||
madhyamāṃ ca kaniṣṭhāṃ ca ākuñcya dakṣiṇe kare|
trimukhākhyā samākhyātā viśvadevapriyā sadā|| 66.94 ||
ketoḥ priyeyaṃ satata mātṛṇāmapi tuṣṭidā|
tarjanyaṅguṣṭhayoragrabhāgau saṃyojya cāṅgulīḥ|| 66.95 ||
anyā ākuñcayet tisraḥ sā’sivallī prakīrtitā|
pitṛṇāmatha sādhyānāṃ rudrāṇāṃ viśvakarmaṇaḥ|| 66.96 ||
sarvadā prītijananī sā’sivallī prakīrtitā|
pādau talābhyāṃ saṃyojya tadaṅguṣṭhadvayaṃ yataḥ|| 66.97 ||
ūrdhvaṃ sayojayennābhau tasyopari tathāñjaliḥ|
yogamudrā samākhyātā yogināṃ tattvadāyinī|| 66.98 ||
sarveṣāmapi devānāṃ pūjane cintane tathā|
yogamudrā samākhyātā tuṣṭaprītikaro sadā|| 66.99 ||
prāñjalirnāma mudrā tu ūrdhvādho bhāvayojitā|
vibhidya darśayeddhastau ūrdhvādhaḥ prasṛtīkṛtau|| 66.100 ||
bhedamudrā samākhyātā mama viṣṇovidheḥ priyā|
aṅguṣṭhe dve tu nikṣipya karayorubhayorapi|| 66.101 ||
agreṇa yojayet paścāt kaniṣṭhāyugalaṃ tataḥ |
ubhayorhastayoścānyāstarjanyādyāśca yojayet|| 66.102 ||
agrāgraistu pṛthakkṛtya darśayet tu kaniṣṭhikām|
mudrā sammohanaṃ nāma kāmadurgāramāpriyā || 66.103 ||
sarveṣāmiha devānāṃ mohanaṃ prītidaṃ smṛtam|
ānamyāsavyahastasya madhyamānāmike tathā|| 66.104 ||
tayoḥ pṛṣṭhe susaṃyojya aṅguṣṭhāgraṃ tataḥ param|
kaniṣṭhāṃ tarjanīṃ caiva agreṇāyojayet tataḥ|| 66.105 ||
bāṇamudrā samākhyātā sarvadevasya tuṣṭidā|
sarvāṅgulīstu saṅkocya aṅguṣṭhamathā tarjanīm|| 66.106 ||
prasāyaṃ karayo paścādaṅguṣṭhāgraṃ tu yojayet|
achguṣṭhāgreṇa tarjanyā agreṇāpi ca tarjanīm|| 66.107 ||
yathāśakti prasāryāpi dhenumudrā prakīrtitā|
sarvāṅgulīnāmagrāṇibrāhme tīrthe niyojayet|| 66.108 ||
anāmikāyāḥ pṛṣṭhe tu aṅguṣṭhāgraṃ niyojya ca|
śūnyaṃ tūṇīravat kṛtvā teṣāmantastu bhairava|| 66.109 ||
tūṇīramudrā cākhyātā sarveṣāṃ prītivardhinī|
mudrāsu saṃsthitā pūjā sarveṣu paricintanam|| 66.110 ||
mudrāsu saṃsthitā yogā mudrā modakarāstataḥ|
yadā yadā pūjaneṣu cintane dhyānakarmaṇi|| 66.111 ||
yajñādau stavane vāpi hastakṛtyaṃ na vidyate|
tadā mudrānvitaṃ kuryādiṣṭāpūrte karadvayam|| 66.112 ||
yajñakṛtyeṣu cecchakto hasto mudrāsu ca kṣamaḥ|
tadā mudrāṃ vidhāyaiva tattat kṛtyaṃ samācaret|| 66.113 ||
mudrāvimuktahastaṃ tu kriyate karma daivikam|
kṛtvā tanniṣphalaṃ yasmāt tanmānmudrānvito bhavetu|| 66.114 ||
visarjane tu devānāṃ yasya yā parikīrtitā|
mudrāṃ tāṃ pūjanādau tu tasya naiva prayojayet|| 66.115 ||
visṛjyoktāmṛte mudrāṃ mudrāyuktaḥ samācaret|
pūjanādi samastaṃ tu karmavṛddhau vicakṣaṇaḥ|| 66.116 ||
ato mudrā paraṃ nāma mudrā puṇyapradāyinī|
devānāṃ modadā mudrā tasmāt tāṃ yatnataścaret|| 66.117 ||
ardhayonirmahāyoniryonirbrāhmī ca vaiṣṇavī|
mudrā visarjane proktā śivātripurayoḥ sadā|| 66.118 ||
durgāyāḥ sarvarūpeṣu mudrā etāḥ prakīrtitāḥ|
yoniṃ ca sampuṭaṃ caiva mahāyoniṃ tathaiva ca|| 66.119 ||
varjayitvā vyastabhāvāduktādanyatra yojayet|
bhaved yāstu tripañcāśadanyā mudrāḥ samantataḥ|| 66.120 ||
tā vyastabhāvād vāmāḥ syurmudrā modakarāḥ parāḥ|
evaṃ vāṃ kathitā mudrāḥ pūjane pūjyatuṣṭidā|
kramastu balidānasya śṛṇu vetālabhairava|| 66.121 ||
iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe mudrākathane ṣaṭṣaṣṭitamo’dhyāyaḥ ||

<67>
||śrībhagavānuvāca||
kramastu balidānasya svarūpa rudhirāditaḥ |
yatā syāt prītaye samyak tad vāṃ vakṣyāmi putrakau|| 67.1 ||
vaiṣṇavītantrakalpoktaḥ kramaḥ sarvatraḥ sarvadā|
sādhakairbalidānasya grāhyaḥ sarvasurasya ca|| 67.2 ||
pakṣiṇaḥ kañchapā grāhā matsyā navavidhā mṛgāḥ|
mahiṣo godhikā kāvaśchāgo ruruśca śūkaraḥ || 67.3 ||
khaḍgaśca kṛṣṇasāraśca godhikā śarabho hariḥ|
śārdūlaśca naraścaiva svagātrarudhiraṃ tathā|| 67.4 ||
caṇḍikābhairavādonāṃ balayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ|
balibhiḥ sādhyate muktirbalibhiḥ sādhyate divam|| 67.5 ||
balidānena satataṃ jayecchatrūnnṛpān nṛpaḥ|
matsyānāṃ kacchapānāṃ tu rudhiraiḥ satataṃ śivā|| 67.6 ||
māsaikaṃ tṛptimāpnoti grāhairmāsāṃstu trīnatha|
mṛgāṇāṃ śoṇitairdevī narāṇāmapi śoṇitaiḥ || 67.7 ||
aṣṭau māsānavāpnoti tṛptiṃ kalyāṇadā ca sā|
godhikānāṃ gaurudhirairvāṣikāṃ tṛptimāpnuyāt|| 67.8 ||
kṛṣṇasārasya rudhiraiḥ śūkarasya ca śoṇitaiḥ|
prāpnoti satataṃ devī tṛptiṃ dvādaśavārṣikom|| 67.9 ||
ajāvikānāṃ rudhiraiḥ pañcaviṃśativārṣikīm|
mahiṣāṇāṃ ca khaḍgānāṃ rudhiraiḥ śatavārṣikīm|| 67.10 ||
tṛptimāpnoti paramāṃ śārdūlarudhiraistathā|
siṃhasya śarabhasyātha svagātrasya ca śoṇitaiḥ|| 67.11 ||
devī tṛptimavāpnoti sahasraṃ parivatsarān|
māṃsairapi tathā prīti rudhirairyasya yāvatī|| 67.12 ||
kṛṣṇasāraṃ mṛgaṃ khaḍgaṃ tatā matsyaṃ ca rohitam|
vārdhrīṃṇasayugaṃ cāpi phalaṃ teṣāṃ pṛthak pṛthak|| 67.13 ||
kṛṣṇasārasya māṃsena tathā khaḍgena caṇḍikā|
varṣāṇāṃ ca śatānyeva taptimāpnoti kevalam|| 67.14 ||
rohitasya tu matsyasya māṃsairvārdhrīṇasasya ca|
tṛptiṃ prāpnoti varṣāṇāṃ śatāni trīṇi matpriyā|| 67.15 ||
tṛpnuvantvindriyakṣīṇaṃ svetaṃ vṛddhamajāpatim|
vārdhrīṇasaḥ procyate’sau havye kavye ca satkṛtaḥ|| 67.16 ||
nīlagrīvo raktaśīrṣaḥ kṛṣṇapādaḥ sitacchadaḥ|
vārdhrīṇasaḥ syātpakṣī ca mama viṣṇorapi priyaḥ|| 67.17 ||
nareṇa balinā devī sahasraṃ parivatsarān|
vidhidattena cāpnoti tṛptiṃ lakṣaṃ tribhirnaraiḥ|| 67.18 ||
nareṇevātha māṃsena trisahasraṃ ca vatsarān|
tṛptimāpnoti kāmākhyā bhairavī mama rūpadhṛk|| 67.19 ||
mantrapūtaṃ śoṇitaṃ tu pīyūṣaṃ jāyate sadā|
mastakaṃ cāpi tasyāti māṃsaṃ cāpi tathā śivā || 67.20 ||
tasmāt tu pūjane dadyād valeḥ śīrṣa ca lohitam|
bhojye home ca māṃsāni niyuñjīyād vicakṣaṇaḥ|| 67.21 ||
pūjāsu nāmamāṃsāni dadyād vai sādhakaḥ kvacit|
ṛte tu lohitaṃ śīrṣamamṛtaṃ tattu jāyate|| 67.22 ||
kūṣmāṇḍabhikṣudaṇḍaṃ ca madyamāsavameva ca|
ete balisamāḥ prāktāstṛptau chāgasamāḥ sadā|| 67.23 ||
candrahāsena kartryā vā chadanaṃ mukhyamiṣyate|
dātrāsidhenukrakacaśaṃkulābhistu madhyamam || 67.24 ||
kṣurakṣuraprabhallaiśca vādhama parikīrtitam|
ebhyo’nyaḥ śaktibāṇādyai rbaliśchedyaḥ kadāpi na|| 67.25 ||
nātti devī baliṃ tattu dātā mṛtyumavāpnuyāt|
hastena cedayed yastu prokṣita sādhakaḥ paśum || 67.26 ||
pakṣiṇaṃ vā brahmavadhyāmavāpnoti suduḥsahām |
nāmantrya khaṇḍaṃ tu bali niyuñjīta vicakṣaṇaḥ|| 67.27 ||
khaḍgasyāmantraṇe mantrā yāvantaḥ kathitāḥ purā|
mahāmāyābalau te vai yojyāstatroditā budhaiḥ|| 67.28 ||
taiḥ sārdhamete mantrāstu yojyāḥ khaḍgādimantraṇe|
pūjane śāradādīnāṃ kāmākyāyā viśeṣataḥ|| 67.29 ||
dviḥ kālīti tato devyā vajreśvaripadaṃ tataḥ|
tato’nu lauhadaṇḍāyai namaḥ śeṣe tu yojayet|| 67.30 ||
sampūjyānena mantreṇa khaḍgamādāya pāṇinā|
kālarātryāstu mantreṇa taṃ khaḍgamabhimantrayet|| 67.31 ||
netrabījasya madhyaṃ tu dvirāvartya prayojayet|
tato’tu kālikālīti karāloṣṭhī tataḥ param || 67.32 ||
hāntādīṃśca tṛtīyena svareṇaikādaśena vai |
yojitā nābindubhyāṃ dvau tata paścānniyojayet|| 67.33 ||
phetkāriṇipadaṃ tasmāt khādayacchedayetyataḥ|
sarvān duṣṭāniti tato dvimariya lulāyakam|| 67.34 ||
khaḍgena chindhi chindhīti tataḥ kilakileti vai|
tataḥ cikicikītyevaṃ tataḥ pibapibeti ca|| 67.35 ||
tato’nu rudhiraṃ ceti sphaiṃkiri kirīti ca|
kālikāyai nama iti kālarātryāstu mantrakam|| 67.36 ||
ityanena tu mantreṇa karavāle’bhimantrite|
kālarātrī svayaṃ tatra prasīdatyarihānaye|| 67.37 ||
baleḥ pūrvoditā mantrā nityaṃ guhyāstu sādhakaiḥ|
ayaṃ mantrastu vaktavyastasya hatyāvihānaye || 67.38 ||
yajñārthe paśavaḥ sṛṣṭāḥ svayameva svayambhuvā|
atastvāṃ ghātayiṣyāmi tasmād yajñe vadho’vadhaḥ|| 67.39 ||
tato daivatamuddiśya kāmamuddiśya cātmanaḥ|
chedayet tena khaḍgena baliṃ pūrvānanaṃ tu tam|| 67.40 ||
athavottaravakttraṃ taṃ svayaṃ pūrvamukhastathā|
pūrvoktān saindhavādīṃstu vakttre’vaśyaṃ niyojayet|| 67.41 ||
sauvarṇaṃ rājata tāmraṃ raityaṃ patrapuṭa ca vā|
māheyaṃ kāṃsyamathavā yajñakāṣṭhamayaṃ ca vā|| 67.42 ||
pātraṃ rudhiradānāya karttavyaṃ vibhavāvadhi|
na lauhe valkale vāpi vaitre rāṅge’tha saisake || 67.43 ||
dadyādraktaṃ balīnāṃ tu bhūmau sruci sruve tathā |
na ghaṭe bhūtale vāpi deyaṃ kṣudre na bhājane || 67.44 ||
rudhirāṇi pradadyāttu bhūtikāmo narottamaḥ|
narasya tu sadā raktaṃ māhaiye taijase’tha vā|| 67.45 ||
dadyānnarapatistattu na patrādau kadācana|
hayamedhamṛte dadyānna kadāciddhayaṃ balim|| 67.46 ||
tathā dikpālamede tu gajaṃ dadyānnarādhipaḥ|
na kadācit tadā devyai pradadyāddhayahastinau|| 67.47 ||
hayākarṣe cāmaraṃ tu baliṃ dadyānnarādhipaḥ|
siṃhaṃ vyāghraṃ naraṃ cāpi svagātrarudhiraṃ tathā|| 67.48 ||
na dadyāt brāhmaṇo madyaṃ mahādevyai kadācana|
siṃhaṃ vyaghrannaraṃ datvā brāhmaṇo naraka vrajet|| 67.49 ||
ihāpi syāt sa hīnāyuḥ sukhasaubhāgyavarjitaḥ|
svagātrarudhiraṃ dadyāccātmavadhyāmavāpnuyāt|| 67.50 ||
madyaṃ dattvā brāhmaṇastu brāhmaṇyādeva hīyate|
na kṛṣṇāsāraṃ vitared baliṃ tu kṣatriyādikaḥ|| 67.51 ||
dadataḥ kṛṣṇasāraṃ tu brahmahatyā bhaved yataḥ|
yatra siṃhasya vyāghrasya narasya vihito vadhaḥ|| 67.52 ||
brahmaṇoktā tu balyādau tatrāyaṃ vihitaḥ kramaḥ|
kṛtvā ghṛtamayaṃ vyāghraṃ naraṃ siṃhaṃ ca bhairava|| 67.53 ||
athavā pūpavikṛtaṃ yavakṣodamayaṃ ca vā|
ghātayeccandrahāsena tena mantreṇa saṃskṛtam|| 67.54 ||
prabhūtabalidāne tu dvau vā trīn vāgrataḥ kṛtān|
pūjayet pramukhān kṛtvā sarvān mantreṇa sādhakaḥ|| 67.55 ||
sāmānyapūjā kathitā balīnāṃ pūrvato mayā|
viśeṣo yatraḥ yatrāsti tanmattaḥ śṛṇu bhairava|| 67.56 ||
mahiṣaṃ pradaded devyai bhairavyai bhairavāya vā |
anenaiva tu mantreṇa tadā taṃ pūyayed balim|| 67.57 ||
yathā vāhaṃ bhavān dveṣṭi yathā vahasi caṇḍikām|
tathā mama ripūn hiṃsa śubhaṃ vaha lūlāyaka|| 67.58 ||
yamasya vāhanastvaṃ tu vararūpadharāvyaya|
āyurvittaṃ yaśo dehi kāsarāya namo’stu te|| 67.59 ||
khaḍgasya tu yadā dānaṃ kriyate tantramantrakam|
jalenābhyukṣya kurvīta guhājāteti bhāṣayan|. 67.60 ||
daive paitre ca śubhagaḥ khaḍgastvaṃ khaḍgasannibhaḥ|
chindhi vighnān mahābhāga guhājāta namo’stu te|| 67.61 ||
pradāne kṛṣṇasārasya mantro’yaṃ parikīrtitaḥ|
kṛṣṇasāra brahmamūrte brahmatejovivardhana|| 67.62 ||
caturvedamayaṃ prājña prajñāṃ dehi yaśī mahat |
tathā śarabhapūjāyāṃ mantrametat prakīrtitam|| 67.63 ||
tvamaṣṭapādo vibhraṣṭacandrabhāgasamudbhava|
aṣṭamūrte mahābāho bhairavākhya namo’stu te|| 67.64 ||
yathā bhairavarūpema varāho nihatastvayā|
tathā śarabharūpeṇa ripūn pighnān niṣūdaya|| 67.65 ||
haristvaṃ hararūpeṇa yathā vahasi caṇḍikām|
tathā śubhāni me nityaṃ bahuvighnāṃśca sūdaye|| 67.66 ||
tvaṃ hariḥ siṃharūpeṇa jagatpratyūharūpiṇam|
jaghāna yena satyena hiraṇyakaśipuṃ haran|| 67.67 ||
ityevaṃ siṃhapūjāyāṃ krama ukto mayānagha|
nare svagātrarudhire paryāyaṃ śṛṇu bhairava|| 67.68 ||
pīṭhe ced dīyate martyo baliṃ dadyāt śmaśānake|
śmaśānaṃ herukākhyaṃ tu tatpūrvaṃ pratipāditam|| 67.69 ||
kāmākhyānilaye śaile oḍādau viddhi tat kramam|
mama rūpaṃ śmaśānaṃ tad bhairavākhyaṃ ca kathyate|| 67.70 ||
tatrāṅgatvaṃ tapaḥsiddhau tribhāgāṃ tu bhaviṣyati|
pūrvāṅge bhairavākhye tu samutsṛṣṭirnarasya tu|| 67.71 ||
dakṣiṇāṅge śiro dadyād bhairavyā muṇḍamālayā|
rudhiraṃ paścimāṅge tu herukākhye niyojayet|| 67.72 ||
dattvā sampūjya tu naraṃ visṛjyāgamanakrame|
pīṭhaśmaśāneṣu baliṃ nekṣettu balidīpakam || 67.73 ||
anyatrāpi yato yatra dīyate yanmahābaliḥ|
tatrāpyanyatra cotsṛjyacchitvānyatra śiro’mṛtam || 67.74 ||
niyojayet sādhakastu visṛjya na vilokayet|
sasnātaṃ manujaṃ dīptaṃ pūrvāhṇaniyatāśanam|| 67.75 ||
māṃsamaithunabhogyena hīnaṃ srakcandanokṣitam|
kṛtvottarāmukhaṃ taṃ tu tadaṅgeṣvaṅgadevatāḥ|| 67.76 ||
pūjayet taṃ tu nāmnā tu daivatena ca mānuṣam|
tadbrahmarandhre brahmāṇaṃ tannāsāyāṃ ca medinīm|| 67.77 ||
karṇayostu tathākāśaṃ jihvāyāṃ sarvatomukham|
jyotīṃṣi netrayorviṣṇuṃ vadane paripūjayet|| 67.78 ||
lalāṭe pūjayeccandra śaktaṃ dakṣiṇagaṇḍataḥ|
vāmāṇḍe tathā vahniṃ grīvāyaṃ samavartinam|| 67.79 ||
keśāgre nirṛtiṃ madhye bhravoścāpi pracetasam|
nāsāmale tu śvasanaṃ skandhaṃ cāpi dhaneśvaram|| 67.80 ||
hṛdaye sarparājaṃ tu pūjayitvā paṭhedidam|
naravaryya mahābhāga sarvadevamayottama|| 67.81 ||
