rakṣa māṃ śaraṇāpannaṃ saputrapaśubāndhavam|
sarājyaṃ māṃ mahāmātyaṃ caturaṅga samanvitam|| 67.82 ||
rakṣa parityajya prāṇānmaraṇe niyate sati|
mahātapobhirjñānaiśca yajñairyat sādhyate’mṛtam || 67.83 ||
tanme dehi mahābhāga tvaṃ cāpi prāpnuhi śriyam|
rākṣasāśca paśācāśca vetālādyāḥ sarīsṛpāḥ|| 67.84 ||
nṛpāśca ripavaścānye na māṃ te dhnantu tvatkṛte|
tvatkaṇṭhanālagalitaiḥ śoṇitairaṅgasaṃyutaiḥ|| 67.85 ||
āpyāyasvātmavanmṛtvā maraṇe niyate sati|
evaṃ sampūjya vidhivat pūrvatantraiśca pūjayet|| 67.86 ||
pūjito matsvarūpo’yaṃ dikpālādhiṣṭhito bhavet|
adhiṣṭhitastathānyaiśca brahmādyaiḥ sakalaiḥ suraiḥ|| 67.87 ||
kṛtapāpo’pi manujo niṣpāpmā sa tu jāyate|
tasya niṣkaluṣasyāśu pīyūṣaṃ śoṇitaṃ bhavet|| 67.88 ||
prīṇāti ca mahādevī jaganmātā jaganmayī|
so’pi kāyaṃ parityajya mānuṣaṃ nacirānmṛtaḥ|| 67.89 ||
bhaved gaṇānāmadhipo mayāpi bahusatkṛtaḥ|
ito’nyathā pāpayuktaṃ malamūtravasāyutam|| 67.90 ||
taṃ baliṃ na hi gṛhṇāti kāmākhyānyāpi nāmataḥ|
anyeṣāṃ mahiṣadīnāṃ balīnāmatha pūjanāt|| 67.91 ||
kāyo medhyatvamāyāti raktaṃ gṛhṇāti vai śivā|
anyebhyo’pi ca devebhyo yadā yattu pradīyate|| 67.92 ||
tadarcitaṃ pradadyāt tu pūjitāya surāya vai|
kāṇaṃ paṅguṃ cātivṛddhaṃ rogiṇaṃ ca galadvraṇam|| 67.93 ||
klībaṃ hīnāṅgamathavā vṛddhaliṅgaṃ kulakṣaṇam|
śvitriṇaṃ cātihrasvaṃ ca mahāpātakinaṃ tathā|| 67.94 ||
advādaśakavarṣīyaṃ śiśu sūtakasaṃyutam|
ūrdhvaṃ saṃvatsarāccāpi mahāgurunipātinam|| 67.95 ||
balikarmaṇi cetāṃstu varjayet pūjitānapi|
paśūnāṃ pakṣiṇāṃ vāpi narāṇāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ|| 67.96 ||
striyaṃ na dadyāt tu balīn dattvā narakamāpnayāt|
saṅghātavalidāneṣu yoṣitaṃ paśupakṣiṇaḥ|| 67.97 ||
baliṃ dadyānmānuṣīṃ tu tyaktvā saṅghātapūjitam|
na trimāsīyakānyūnaṃ paśuṃ dadyācchivābalim|| 67.98 ||
na ca traipakṣikān nyūna pradadyād vai patattriṇam|
kāṇavyaṅgādiduṣṭaṃ tu na paśuṃ pakṣiṇaṃ tathā|| 67.99 ||
devyai dadyāt tathā martyaṃ tathaiva paśupakṣiṇau|
chinnalāṅgūlakarṇādīn bhagnadantāṃstathaiva ca|| 67.100 ||
bhagnaśṛṅgādikaṃ vāpi na dadyāt tu kadācana|
na brāhmaṇaṃ baliṃ dadyāccāṇḍālamapi pārthiva|| 67.101 ||
notsṛṣṭaṃ dvijadevebhyo bhūpatestanayaṃ tathā|
raṇena vijitaṃ dadyāttanayaṃ ripubhūbhṛtaḥ|| 67.102 ||
svaputraṃ bhrātaraṃ vāpi pitaraṃ cāvirodhinam|
viṭpatiṃ ca na dadyāttu bhāgineyaṃ ca mātulam|| 67.103 ||
anuktānnāpi dadyāt tu tathājñātān mṛgadvijān|
uktālābhe pradadyāttu gardabhaṃ coṣṭrameva ca|| 67.104 ||
lābhe’nyeṣāṃ na vitared vyāghramuṣṭraṃ kharaṃ tayā|
sampūjya vidhivanmartyaṃ paśuṃ pakṣiṇameva vā|| 67.105 ||
sacchinnaṃ cāpi mantreṇa mantreṇaiva nivedayet|
nāraṃ martyaśiroraktaṃ devyāḥ samyag nivedayet|| 67.106 ||
chāgaṃ tu vāmato dadyānmāhiṣaṃ vitaret puraḥ|
pakṣiṇaṃ bāmato dadyādagrato dehaśoṇitam|| 67.107 ||
kravyādānāṃ paśūnāṃ tu pakṣiṇāṃ tu siro’sṛjam|
vāme nivedayet pārśve jalajānāṃ ca sarvaśaḥ|| 67.108 ||
kṛṣṇasārasya kūrmasya khaḍgasya śaśakasya ca|
grāhāṇāmatha matsyānāmagre eva nivedayet|| 67.109 ||
siṃhasya dakṣime dadyāt khaḍgino’pi ca dakṣiṇe|
pṛṣṭhadeśe na dadyāt tu śiro vā rudhiraṃ valeḥ|| 67.110 ||
naivedyaṃ dakṣiṇe vāme purato ne tu pṛṣṭataḥ|
dīpaṃ dakṣiṇato dadyāt purato vā na vāmataḥ|| 67.111 ||
vāmatastu tathā dhūpamagre vā na tu dakṣiṇe|
nivedayeta purobhāge gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ ca bhūṣaṇam || 67.112 ||
maṇḍale cenmadhyābhāge vāmadakṣādipūrvavat|
madirāṃ pṛṣṭhato dadyādanyat pānaṃ tu vāmataḥ|| 67.113 ||
avaśyaṃ vihita yatra madyaṃ tatra dvijaḥ punaḥ|
nārikelajalaṃ kāṃsye tāmre vā visṛjenmadhu|| 67.114 ||
nāpadyapi dvijo madyaṃ kadācid visṛjedapi|
ṛte puṣpāsavāduktāda gṛñjanād vā viśeṣataḥ|| 67.115 ||
rājaputrastathāmātyaḥ sacivaḥ sauptikādayaḥ |
dadyurnarabaliṃ bhūpa sampattyai vibhavāya ca|| 67.116 ||
nṛpānanumate martyaṃ dattvā pāpamavāpnuyāt|
upaplave raṇe vāpi yathecchaṃ vitarennaram|| 67.117 ||
yaḥ kaścidrājapuruṣo nānyastvapi kadācana|
balidānadināt pūrvaṃ divase tu baliṃ naram|| 67.118 ||
mānastoketi mantreṇa devīsūktena yena ca|
gandhadvāretyanenāpi khaḍgaṃ śīrṣe nidhāya ca|| 67.119 ||
tasmin khaḍge sugandhādi dattvā tenādhivāsayet|
gandhādikaṃ ta khaḍgasthaṃ || 67.120 ||
ambe’mbiketi mantreṇa raudreṇa bhairavasya ca|
evaṃ tu saṃskṛte martye devī rakṣati taṃ balim|| 67.121 ||
na tasya vyādhayaścāpi kṣuṇṇatārajasī na va|
na sūtakaṃ dūṣayettajjñātyutpattimṛtādikam|| 67.122 ||
chinnaṃ narasya śīrṣaṃ tu patitaṃ yatra yatra ca|
yacchubhaṃ cāśubhaṃ vāpi paśvādīnāṃ ca tacchṛṇu|| 67.123 ||
chinnaṃ sirastataiśānyāṃ nāraṃ diśyatha rākṣase|
patitaṃ rājyahāniṃ ca vināśaṃ ca vinirdiśet|| 67.124 ||
pūrvāgniyāmyavāruṇyavāyavyādigataṃ kramāt|
śriyaṃ puṣṭiṃ bhayaṃ lābhaṃ putralābhaṃ dhanaṃ tathā|| 67.125 ||
kramād vinirdiśennāraṃ chinnaśīrṣaṃ tu bhairava|
uttarādikramādeva mahiṣasyāpi mastakaḥ|| 67.126 ||
patito vāyukāṣṭhānte sūcayed yacchṛṇuṣva tat|
bhāgyaṃ hānintathaiśvarya vittaṃ ripujayaṃ bhayam|| 67.127 ||
rājyalābhaṃ śriyaṃ cāpi kramād viddhi tu bhairava|
paśūnāṃ caiva sarveṣāṃ chāgādīnāmaśeṣata || 67.128 ||
evaṃ phalaṃ kramād vidyādṛte jalajavāṇḍajau|
jalajānāṃ pakṣiṇāṃ tu yāmyanairṛtyayorbhayam|| 67.129 ||
anyatra tu śriyaṃ dadyāt patitaṃ śātitaṃ śiraḥ|
yaḥ syāt kaṭakaṭāśabdo dantānāṃ chinnamastake|| 67.130 ||
narāṇāṃ paśupakṣyādigrāhādīnāṃ ca rogadaḥ|
lotakaṃ cakṣuṣorjātaṃ yadi sravati mastake|| 67.131 ||
chinne narasya rājyasya tā hāniṃ vinirdiśet|
māhiṣe mastake netrād yadi sravati lotakam|| 67.132 ||
chinne niveditaṃ vairibhūpamṛtyuṃ tadādiśet|
anyeṣāmatha paśvādivalīnāṃ śiraso’rditāt|| 67.133 ||
nigaṃtaṃ lotakaṃ dhatte parāṃ bhītiṃ gadaṃ tathā|
hasati cchinnaśīrṣaṃ cennāraṃ syāt tu ripukṣayaḥ|| 67.134 ||
śrīvṛddhirayuṣo vṛddhiḥ sadā dāturasaṃśayaḥ|
yad yadvākyaṃ nigadati tathā bhavati cācirāt|| 67.135 ||
hūṅkārādrājyahāniḥ syācchaleṣmasrāvācca pañcatā|
devānāṃ yadi nāmāni bhāṣate chinnamastakaḥ|| 67.136 ||
vibhūtimatulāṃ vidyāt ṣaṇmāsābhyantare tadā|
rudhirādānakāle tu śakṛnmūtre yadi sravet|| 67.137 ||
kāyaṃ tadādhaścordhvaṃ vā dātuḥ syānmaraṇaṃ tadā|
ākṣepād vāmapādasya mahārogaḥ prajāyate|| 67.138 ||
anyadākṣepacalanaiḥ kalyāṇamupajāyate|
mahiṣasya tu raktasya mānuṣasya tu sādhakaḥ|| 67.139 ||
aṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ tu kiṃciduddhṛtya bhūtale|
mahākauśikamantreṇa nikṣiped balimuttamam|| 67.140 ||
devebhyaḥ pūtanādibhyo nairṛtyāṃ diśī pūrvataḥ|
mahiṣaḥpañcavarṣīyaḥ pañcaviṃśativārṣikaḥ || 67.141 ||
balirdeyo naro devyai tasya raktaṃ tu bhūtaye|
netrabījatrayaṃ kāmavījaṃ hantā prajāpatiḥ|| 67.142 ||
vahnibījaṃ ṣaṭsvarābhyāṃ saṃpṛktaśca tathā paraḥ|
sa evaitāstathaitāvadādivargāntasaṃyutaḥ|| 67.143 ||
ṣaṣṭhasvaraśikhābinduścandrayuktasthāparaḥ|
dvirmāsikābojakāntaḥ kauśikītyabhimantraṇam|| 67.144 ||
eṣa baliḥ svāheti mantro’yaṃ kauśikī smṛtaḥ|
nṛpo vairibaliṃ dadyāt khaḍgamāmantrya pūrvataḥ|| 67.145 ||
mahiṣaṃ cātha chāgaṃ vā vairināmnābhimantrya ca|
sūtreṇa vadane baddhaṃ tridhā tasya tu mantrakaiḥ|| 67.146 ||
chittvā tasyottamāṅgaṃ tu devyai dadyāt prayatnataḥ|
yadā yadā riporvṛddhirvalidānaṃ tadā param|| 67.147 ||
dadyāt tadā śiraśchittvā ripostasya kṣayāya ca|
prāṇapratiṣṭhāṃ ca ripoḥ kuryāt tasmin paśāvatha|| 67.148 ||
tasmin kṣīṇe ripoḥ prāṇāḥ kṣīyante vipadā yutāḥ|
ādau viruddharūpiṇi caṇḍike ca tataḥ param|| 67.149 ||
vairiṇantvamukaṃ ceti yāhītyāmreḍitaṃ punaḥ|
vahnibhāryā tataḥ paścāt khaḍgamantraṃ prakīrtitam|| 67.150 ||
svayaṃ sa vairī yo dveṣṭi tamimaṃ paśurūpiṇam|
vināśaya mahāmārī spheṃ khādaya khādaya|| 67.151 ||
ityanena tu mantreṇa vadvyaḥ śirasi puṣpakam|
dadyāt tatastadrudhiraṃ dvyakṣarābhvāṃ nivedayet|| 67.152||
mahānavamyāṃ śaradi yadyevaṃ dīyate baliḥ|
tadā tadaṣṭāṅgabhavairmāṃsairhomaṃ samācaret|| 67.153 ||
durgātantreṇa mantreṇa praṇīte dahane śucau|
evaṃ dattvā valiṃ martyo ripukṣayamavāpnuyāt|| 67.154 ||
nābheradhastādradhiraṃ pṛṣṭhabhāgasya ca śriye|
svagātrarudhiraṃ dadyānna kadācana sādhakaḥ|| 67.155 ||
noṣṭhasya cibukasyāpi nendriyāṇāṃ ca mānavaḥ|
kaṇṭhādho nābhitaścordhvaṃ bāhvoḥ pāṇimṛte tathā|| 67.156 ||
pradadyādrudhiraṃ ghātaṃ nātikuryācca sādhakaḥ|
gaṇḍayośca lalāṭasya bhruvormadhyasya śoṇitam|| 67.157 ||
karṇāgrasya ca bāhvosca galayo rudarasya ca|
kaṇṭhādho nābhitaścordhvaṃ hṛdbhāgasya yatastataḥ|| 67.158 ||
pārśvayoścāpi rudhiraṃ durgāyai vinivedayet|
na gulphato’sṛkpradadyānna jatrornāpi vaktrataḥ|| 67.159 ||
na ca rogabilādaṅgānnānyaghātācca bhairava|
tadarthe ca kṛtāghātaḥ saśraddho’bhubdhamānasaḥ|| 67.160 ||
śrute raktaṃ pradadyāttu padmapuṣpasya patrake|
sauvarṇe rājate kāṃsye lauhe phali na vā naraḥ|| 67.161 ||
nidhāya devyai dadyāt tu tadraktaṃ mantrapūrvakam|
khananaṃ kṣurikākhaḍgaśaṅkulādi yadastrakam|| 67.162 ||
ghātena bṛhadastrasya mahāphalamavāpnuyāt|
padmapuṣpasya patraṃ tu yāvad gṛhṇāti śoṇitam|| 67.163 ||
tatpramāṇe caturbhāgādhikaṃ raktaṃ tu sādhakaḥ|
na kadācit pradadyāttu nāṅgacchedamathācaret|| 67.164 ||
yaḥ svahṛdayasañjātamāṃsaṃ māṣapramāṇataḥ|
tilamudgapramāṇād vā devyai dadyāt tu bhaktitaḥ|| 67.165 ||
ṣaṇmāsābhyantare tasmāt kāmamiṣṭamavāpnuyāt|
bāhvostu skandhayorvāpi yo dadyād dīpavartikām|| 67.166 ||
hṛdaye vā snehapātraṃ vinā bhavatyā tu sādhakaḥ|
kṣaṇamātreṇa taddīṣapradānasya phalaṃ śṛṇu|| 67.167 ||
bhuktvā ca vipulān bhogān devīgehe yadṛcchayā|
kalpatrayaṃ tu saṃsthāya sārvabhaumo nṛpo bhavet|| 67.168 ||
mahiṣasya śiraśchinnaṃ sapradīpaṃ śivāpuraḥ|
hastābhyāṃ yaḥ samādāya ahorātraṃ tu tiṣṭhati|| 67.169 ||
sa cirāyuḥ pūtamūrtiriha bhuktvā manoramān|
bhogānte madgṛhago gaṇānāmadhipo bhavet|| 67.170 ||
narasya śīrṣamādāya sādhako dakṣiṇe kare|
vāmena raudhiraṃ pātraṃ gṛhītvā niśi jāgrataḥ|| 67.171 ||
yāvadrātraṃ sthito martyo rājā bhavati ceha vai|
mṛte mama gṛhaṃ prāpya gaṇānāmadhipo bhavet|| 67.172 ||
kṣaṇamātraṃ valīnāṃ yaḥ śiroraktaṃ karadvaye|
gṛhītvā cintayed devīṃ purastiṣṭhati mānavaḥ|| 67.173 ||
sa kāmāniha samprāpya devīloke mahīyate|
mahāmāye jagannāthe sarvakāmapradāyini|| 67.174 ||
dadāmi deharudhiraṃ prasīda varadā bhava|
ityuktvā mūlamantrema natipūrva vicakṣaṇaḥ|| 67.175 ||
svagātrarudhiraṃ dadyād mānavaḥ siddhasannibhaḥ |
yenātmamāṃsaṃ satyena dadāmīśvari bhūtaye || 67.176 ||
nirvāṇaṃ tena satyana dehi haṃ haṃ namo namaḥ|
ityanena tu mantreṇa svamāṃsaṃ vitared budhaḥ|| 67.177 ||
saubhāgyaṃsukhasampannaṃ pradīpaṃ paramaṃ ruciḥ|
dīpayonmāṃsamiha taṃ dīpaṃ hnauṃ hnauṃ namo namaḥ|| 67.178 ||
ityanena tu mantreṇa dīpaṃ dadyād vicakṣaṇaḥ|
mahānavamyāṃ śiradi rātrau skandaviśākhayoḥ|| 67.179 ||
yavacūrṇaṃmayaṃ kṛtvā ripuṃ mṛnmayameva vā|
śiraśchittvā valiṃ dadyāt kṛtvā tasya tu mantrataḥ|| 67.180 ||
anenaiva tu mantrema khaḍgamāmantrya yatnataḥ|
raktaṃ kilikilī ghora ghorādhāravihiṃsakaḥ|| 67.181 ||
brahmaśiṣyāmbikāśiṣyamamukaṃ cārisattamam|
mānto visargasahitaḥ sa ca binduyuto’paraḥ|| 67.182 ||
śiraśchittvā valiṃ dadyāt kṛtvā tasya tu mantrataḥ|
anenaiva tu mantreṇa bindunā ca samanvitaḥ|| 67.183 ||
brahmagniryogacandreṇa bindunā ca samanvitaḥ|
phaḍanto valiṣu proktaḥ khaḍgaḥ skandaviśākhayoḥ|| 67.184 ||
raktadravyaiḥ śocayitvā kṛtrimaṃ taṃ valiṃ ripum|
kucandanasya tilakaṃ lalāṭe viniveśya ca|| 67.185 ||
raktamālyāmbaraṃ kṛtvā raktavastradharaṃ tathā|
kaṇṭhe baddhvā raktasūtrairnābhau śalyaṃ ca kṛtrimam|| 67.186 ||
dattvottaraśiraḥ skandhaṃ kṛtvā khaḍgena chedayet|
śirastasya tato dadyāt skandamantreṇa mantritam|| 67.187 ||
caturdaśasvarāgnibhyāṃ sampṛktaḥ stāt puraḥsakam |
parataḥ parataḥ pūrvaṃ candrabindusamanvitam|| 67.188 ||
skandasya mūlamantro’yaṃ tena tasmai baliṃ sṛjet|
caturdaśasvarāgnibhyāṃ tṛtīyaṃ tu ca pūrvavat|| 67.189 ||
prokto viśākhamantro’yaṃ tena tasmai baliṃ sṛjet|
kuṭilākṣau kṛṣṇapiṅgavarṇau raktāṅgadhāriṇau|| 67.190 ||
triśūlaṃ karavālaṃ ca pāṇibhyāṃ dakṣiṇe tathā|
bibhratau nṛkapālaṃ ca katriṃkāṃ cāti vāmataḥ|| 67.191 ||
trinetrau naramuṇḍānāṃ mālāmurasi bibhratau|
vikaṭau daśanairbhīmairgaṇeśau dvārapālakau|| 67.192 ||
dhyānena cintayed devyāḥ purataḥ saṃsthitau sadā|
caitre māsyasite pakṣe caturdaśyāṃ viśeṣataḥ|| 67.193 ||
valibhirmahiṣaiśchāgaiḥ māṃ ca bairavarūpiṇam|
toṣayenmadhumirmāṃsaistena tuṣyāmyahaṃ sutau || 67.194 ||
caṇḍikā validāne tu valiśīrṣaṃ jalena ca|
abhiṣicya tu mantreṇa mūlenaiva nivedayet || 67.195 ||
īṣatprāṇaṃ tu bahudhā calitaṃ pūrvamarcitam|
vīkṣet kāyasamṛddhiṃ tu siddhabhāvaṃ ca sādhakaḥ|| 67.196 ||
sitapreto rathasteṣāṃ yogapīṭhasya sannibhaḥ|
dhyāyāmyasmin mahāmāye siddhiṃ bodhayate namaḥ|| 67.197 ||
anenāmantritaṃ śīrṣaṃ na cirād yadi vepate|
tatkāryasya tadā siddhirasiddhistu viparyayāt|| 67.198 ||
evaṃ dadad valiṃ vīro yathokta vidhinā’munā|
validānādeva caturvargamāpnotyasaṃśayam || 67.199 ||
evaṃ valipradānasya kramo rūpaṃ tathaiva ca|
kathito rudhirādhyāya upacārāñ śṛṇuṣva me|| 67.200 ||
iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe validānavivaraṇaṃ nāma saptaṣaṣṭitamo’dhyāyaḥ||

<68>
||śrībhagavānuvāca||
upacārān pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu ṣoḍaśa bhairava|
yaiḥ samyak tuṣyate devī devo’pyanyo hi bhaktitaḥ|| 68.1 ||
āsanaṃ prathamaṃ dadyāt pauṣṇyaṃ dāravameva vā|
vāstraṃ vā cārmaṇaṃ kauśaṃ maṇḍalasyottare sṛjet|| 68.2 ||
yadaiva dīyate padme maṇḍalasya tadutsaḍajet |
vākpuṣpatoyaiḥ kusumaṃ vinā yacchādakaṃ bhavet|| 68.3 ||
padmasya tadbahirdeśe dvārādau vinivedayet|
ardhyaṃ pādyaṃ cācamanaṃ snānīyaṃ netrarañjanam|| 68.4 ||
madhuparkaṃ ca gandhaṃ ca puṣpaṃ padme nivedayet|
pratimāsu ca yadyogyaṃ gātre dātuṃ ca tat tanau|| 68.5 ||
dadyād yogyaṃ tu purato naivedyaṃ bhojanādikam|
pauṣpāsanaṃ yad vihitaṃ yasya tad yadi garbhakam|| 68.6 ||
nivedayet tadā padme vipulaṃ dvāri cotsṛjet|
pauṣpaṃ puṣpaugharacitaṃ kuśasūtrādisaṃyutam|| 68.7 ||
atiprītikaraṃ devyā mamāpyanyasya bhairava|
yajñakāṣṭhasamudbhūtamāsanaṃ masṛṇaṃ śubham|| 68.8 ||
nocchrāyaṃ nātivistīrṇamāsanaṃ viniyojayet|
anyad dārubhavaṃ cāpi dadyādāsanamuttamam|| 68.9 ||
sakaṇṭakaṃ kṣīrayuta dārusāravivarjitam|
caityaśmaśānasambhūtaṃ varjayitvā vibhītakam|| 68.10 ||
valkalaṃ koṣajaṃ śāṇaṃ vastrametat trayaṃ matam|
romajaṃ kambalaṃ caitadanena tu catuṣṭayam|| 68.11 ||
anena racitaṃ dadyādāsanaṃ ceṣṭabhūtaye|
siṃhavyāghratarakṣūṇāṃ chāgasya mahiṣasya vā|| 68.12 ||
gajānāṃ turagāṇāṃ ca kṛṣṇasārasya carmaṇaḥ|
sṛmarasyātha rāmasya mṛgāṇāṃ navabhedinām|| 68.13 ||
carmabhiḥ sarvadevānāmāsanaṃ prītidaṃ śrutam|
vastreṣu kambalaṃ śastamāsanaṃ devatuṣṭaye|| 68.14 ||
rāṅkavaṃ cārmaṇaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ dāravaṃ candanodbhavam|
yaccāsanaṃ kuśamayaṃ tadāsanamanuttamam|| 68.15 ||
sarveṣāmapi devānāmṛṣīṇāṃ ca yatātmanām|
yogapīṭhasya sadṛśamāsanaṃ sthānamucyate|| 68.16 ||
āsanasya pradānena saubhāgyaṃ muktimāpnuyāt|
śambaro rohito rāmo nyaṅkuraṅkaśaśā ruruḥ|| 68.17 ||
eṇaśca hariṇaśceti mṛgā navavidhā matāḥ|
hariṇaścāpi vijñeyo pañcabhedo’tra bhairava|| 68.18 ||
ṛṣyaḥ khaḍgo ruruścaiva pṛṣataśca magastathā|
ete valipradāneṣu carmadāneṣu kīrtitāḥ|| 68.19 ||
sarveṣāṃ taijasānāṃ ca āsanaṃ śreṣṭhamucyate|
āyasaṃ varjayitvā tu kāṃsyaṃ sīsakameva vā|| 68.20 ||
śilāmayaṃ maṇimayaṃ tathā ratnamayaṃ matam|
āsana devatābhyastu bhuktyai muktyai samutsṛjet|| 68.21 ||
atraiva sādhakānāṃ ca āsanaṃ śṛṇu bhairava|
yatrāsīnaḥ pūjayaṃstu sarvasiddhimavāpnuyāt|| 68.22 ||
aindhanaṃ cārmaṇaṃ vāstraṃ taijasaṃ ca catuṣṭayam|
āsanaṃ sādhakānāṃ ca satataṃ parikīrtitam|| 68.23 ||
tat sarvamāsanaṃ śastaṃ pūjākarmaṇi sādhake|
na yatheṣṭāsano bhūyāt pūjākarmaṇi sādhakaḥ|| 68.24 ||
kāṣṭhādikāsanaṃ kuryāt sitameva sadā budhaḥ|
caturviṃśatyaṅgulena dorghaṃ kāṣṭhāsanaṃ matam|| 68.25 ||
ṣoḍaśāṅgulavistīrṇamucchrāyaṃ caturaṅgulam|
ṣaḍaṅgulaṃ vā kuryāt tu nocchritañcāta ācaret|| 68.26 ||
pūrvoktaṃ varjayed varjyamāsanaṃ pūjaneṣvapi|
vastraṃ dvihastānno dīrghaṃ sārdhahastānna vistṛtam|| 68.27 ||
na tryaṅgulāt tathocchrāyaṃ pūjākarmaṇi saṃśrayet|
yatheṣṭaṃ cārmaṇaṃ kuryāt pūrvoktaṃ siddhidāyakam|| 68.28 ||
ṣaḍaṅgulādhikaṃ kuryānnocchritaṃ ca kadācana|
kāmbalaṃ cārmaṇaṃ śailaṃ mahāmāyāprapūjane|| 68.29 ||
praśastamāsanaṃ proktaṃ kāmākhyāyāstathaiva ca|
tripurāyāśca satataṃ viṣṇoścāpi kuśāsanam|| 68.30 ||
bahudīrghaṃ bahūṃcchrāyaṃ tathaiva bahuvistṛtam|
dāru bhūmisamaṃ proktamaśmāpi sarvakarmaṇi|| 68.31 ||
pṛthak pṛthak kalpayet tu bahirdvāri tathāsanam |
na patramāsanaṃ kuryāt kadācidapi pūjane|| 68.32 ||
na prāṇyaṅgasamudbhūtamasthijaṃ dviradādṛte|
mātaṅgadantasañjātaṃ kāmikeṣvāsanaṃ caret|| 68.33 ||
cārmaṃ pūrvoditaṃ grāhyaṃ tathā ganadhamṛgasya ca|
salile yadi kurvīta devatānāṃ prapūjanam|| 68.34 ||
tatrāpyāsana āsīno notthitastu kadācana|
toye śilāmayaṃ kuryādāsanaṃ kauśameva vā|| 68.35 ||
dāravaṃ taijasaṃ vāpi nānyadāsanamācaret|
āsanāropasaṃsthānaṃ sthānābhāve tu pūjakaḥ|| 68.36 ||
āsanaṃ kalpayitvā tu manasā pūjayejjale|
yadyāsituṃ na saṃsthānaṃ vidyate toyamadhyataḥ|| 68.37 ||
anyatra vā tadā sthitvā devapūjāṃ samācaret|
ityetat kathitaṃ putra pūjyapūjakasaṅgatam|| 68.38 ||
āsanaṃ pādyamadhunā śṛṇu vetāla bhairava|
pādārthamudakaṃ pādyaṃ kevalaṃ toyameva tat|| 68.39 ||
tat taijasena pātreṇa śaṅkhenāpi pradāpayet|
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ sasthānaṃ pādyamiṣyate|| 68.40 ||
tadāsanottaraṃ dadyānmūlamantreṇa sarvataḥ|
kuśapuṣpākṣataiścaiva siddhārthaiścandanaistathā|| 68.41 ||
toyairgandhairyathālabdhairghyaṃ dadyāt tu siddhaye|
ardhyeṇa labhate kāmānardhyeṇa labhate dhanam|| 68.42 ||
putrāyuḥsukhamokṣāṇi dānādardhyasya vai labhet|
na dadyād bhāskarāyārghyaṃ śaṅkhatoyairvicakṣaṇaḥ|| 68.43 ||
tathā na śuktipātreṇa viṣṇave’rghyaṃ nivedayet |
dadyādācamanīyaṃ tu sugandhisalilaiḥ śubhaiḥ|| 68.44 ||
karpūravāsitairvāpi kṛṣṇāguruvidhūpitaiḥ|
yathā tathā sugandhairvā prasaṅgaiḥ phenavarjitaiḥ|| 68.45 ||
tat taijasena pātreṇa śaṅkhenāpi pradāpayet|
udakaṃ dīyate yat tu prasannaṃ phenavarjitam|| 68.46 ||
ācamanāya devebhyastadācamanamucyate|
kevalaṃ toyamātreṇa tad vā dadyānna miśritam|| 68.47 ||
vāsitaṃ tu sugandhādyaiḥ kartavyaṃ yadi labhyate|
āyurbalaṃ yaśovṛddhiṃ pradāyācamanīyakam|| 68.48 ||
labhate sādhako nityaṃ kāmāṃścaiva yathotthitān |
dadhirsarpirjala kṣaudraṃ sitā tābhiśca pañcabhiḥ|| 68.49 ||
procyate madhuparkastu sarvadevaughatuṣṭaye|
jalaṃ tu sarvataḥ svalpaṃ sitādadhighṛtaṃ samam|| 68.50 ||
sarvebhya ścādhikaṃ kṣaudraṃ madhuparkeprayojayet|
tad dadyāt kāṃsyapātreṇa raukmasvetamayena vā|| 68.51 ||
jyotiṣṭomāśvamedhādau pūrte ceṣṭe ca pūjane|
madhuparkaḥ pradiṣṭo’yaṃ sarvadevaughatuṣṭidaḥ|| 68.52 ||
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ sādhakaḥ parikīrtitaḥ|
madhuparkaḥ saukhyabhogyatuṣṭipuṣṭipradāyakaḥ|| 68.53 ||
piṣṭātako’tha kastūrī rocanaṃ kuṅkumaṃ tathā|
guḍaḥ kṣaudraṃ pañcagavyaṃ sarvauṣadhigaṇastathā|| 68.54 ||
sitā nirṇejanaṃ tailaṃ snigdhasnehena tattilāḥ |
prānte toyamiti prāktaṃ snānīyaṃ kalpakovidaiḥ|| 68.55 ||
svarṇaratnodakaṃ caiva karpūrādyadhivāsitam|
taijasaiḥ kāṃsyapātrairvā śaṅkhairvā tannivedayet|| 68.56 ||
maṇḍale keśare deyamādityapratimāsu ca|
śivaliṅge tathā bhoge pīṭhe devatanau tathā|| 68.57 ||
sadyaḥ snigdhaṃ mṛnmayaṃ vā sarpiḥsinduraje tathā|
śrīcandanapratiṣṭhe vā lepayet pratimātanau|| 68.58 ||
svastikasthāpite khaḍge snāpayed darpaṇe’tha vā|
evaṃ dadyāt tu snānīyaṃ mahādevyai viśeṣataḥ|| 68.59 ||
ravi viṣṇuśivebhyo vā yatra tatra prapūjane|
pūjakaḥ snānadānāt t cirāyurupajāyate|| 68.60 ||
samyak snānapradānāt tu kalpāntaṃ svargabhāgbhavet|
yadeva dīyate pādyaṃ gandhapuṣpādikaṃ tathā|| 68.61 ||
upācārāṃstathā sarvānarghyapātrāhitairjalaiḥ|
amṛtīkaraṇādyaistu saṃskṛtaistvabhiṣicya taiḥ|| 68.62 ||
pradadyādiṣṭadevebhyo gṛhṇāti ca tataḥ svayam|
arghyapātrāṇi taistoyairvinā yadvinivedanam|| 68.63 ||
dīyate ceṣṭadevebhyaḥ sarvaṃ tanniṣphalaṃ bhavet|
rāgāllobhāt pramādād vā hyardhyaṃ pātrāmṛtīkṛtam|| 68.64 ||
toyaṃ srutaṃ syāt pātrāt tu punaḥ kuryāt tadāmṛtam|
svalpāvaśeṣatoye tu pātrasthe hyamṛtīkṛte|| 68.65 ||
tatrānyadudakaṃ dadyāt tattainaivāmṛtaṃ bhavet|
bahūni yadi puṣpāṇi mālā vā pracurā yadi || 68.66 ||
dīyante cārghyapātrasthairjalaiḥ saṃsicya cotsṛjet|
anyatoyairyadutsṛṣṭamardhyapātrasthitetaraiḥ|| 68.67 ||
tanna gṛhvātīṣṭadevo dattaṃ vidhiśatairapi|
saṃskṛte tvarghyapātre tu navabhiḥ pratipattibhiḥ|| 68.68 ||
tiṣṭhanti savatīrthāni pīyūṣāṇi ca sarvataḥ|
tasmāt tatra sthitaistoyairabhyukṣyopacārānutsṛjet|| 68.69 ||
na yogyamarghyapātreṣu nidhāya vinivedayet|
idaṃ te bhairava proktaṃ ṣaṭkaṃ caivāsanādikam|
vastrādi daśa vakṣyāmi śṛṇu vijñānavṛddhaye|| 68.70 ||
iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe aṣṭaṣaṣṭitamo’dhyāyaḥ||

<69>
||śrībhagavānuvāca||
kārpāsaṃ kambalaṃ bālkaṃ kośajaṃ vastramiṣyate|
tatpūrvaṃ pūjayitvaiva mantrairdevāya cotsṛjet|| 69.1 ||
nirdaśaṃ malinaṃ jīrṇaṃ chinnaṃ gātrāvaliṅgitam|
parakīyaṃ hyākhudaṣṭaṃ sūcīviddhaṃ tathoṣitam|| 69.2 ||
uptaleśaṃ vidhautaṃ ca śleṣmamūtrādidūṣitam|
pradāne devatābhyāśca daive pitrye ca karmaṇi|| 69.3 ||
varjayet svopayoge na yajñādāvupayojane|
uttarīyottarāsaṅgairnicolo modacelakaḥ|| 69.4 ||
paridhānaṃ ca pañcaitānyasyūtāni prayojayet|
śāṇa vastraṃ niśāraṃ ca tathaivātapavāraṇam|| 69.5 ||
caṇḍātakaṃ tathā dūśyaṃ pañca syūtānyaduṣṭaye|
patākādhvajadaṇḍādau syūtaṃ vastraṃ prayojayet|| 69.6 ||
anyatrāvaraṇādau ca tadvināśasya tena tat|
pītaṃ kauśeyavastraṃ ca mahādevyai praśasyate|| 69.7 ||
pītaṃ tathaiva kauśeyaṃ vāsudevāya cotsṛjet|
raktaṃ tu kambalaṃ dadyācchivāya paramātmane|| 69.8 ||
vicitraṃ sarvadevebhyo devīrbhyoṃ’śu nivedayet|
kārpāsaṃ sarvatobhadraṃ dadyāt sarvebhya eva ca|| 69.9 ||
naikāntaraktaṃ dadyāt tu vāsudevāya cailakam|
tathā naikāntanīlaṃ tu śivāya vinivedayet|| 69.10 ||
nīliraktaṃ tu yadvastraṃ tat sarvatra vivarjitam|
daive pitrye tūpayoge varjayet tu vicakṣaṇaḥ|| 69.11 ||
nīlīraktaṃ pramādāttu yo dadyād viṣṇave budhaḥ|
niṣphalā tasya tatpūjā tadā bhavati bhairava|| 69.12 ||
vicitre vāsasi punarlagnaṃ nīlīvirañjitam |
vastraṃ dadyānmahādevyai nānyasmai tu kadācana|| 69.13 ||
dvipadāṃ brāhmaṇo yadvaddevānāṃ vāsavo yathā|
tathā bhūṣaṇavargeṣu vastramuttamamucyate|| 69.14 ||
vastreṇa jīryate lajjā vastreṇa hīyate tvagham|
vastrāt syāt sarvataḥ siddhiścaturvarga pradaṃ ca tat|| 69.15 ||
vastraṃ te kathitaṃ putra sarvaprītipradāyakam|
bhogyaṃ bhūṣottamaṃ nityaṃ bhūṣaṇāni śṛṇuṣva me|| 69.16 ||
kirīṭaṃ ca śiroratnaṃ kuṇḍalaṃ ca lalāṭikā|
tālapatraṃ ca hāraśca graiveyakamathormikā|| 69.17 ||
prālambikāratnasūtramuttaṅgotarkṣamālikā|
pārśvadyoto nakhadyoto hyaṅgulīcchādakastathā|| 69.18 ||
jūṭālakaṃ mānavako mūrdhatārā khalantikā |
aṅgado bāhuvalayaḥ śikhābhūṣaṇa iṅgikā || 69.19 ||
prāgdaṇḍabandhamudbhāsanābhipūro’tha mālikā|
saptakī śṛṅkhalaṃ caiva dantapatraṃ ya karṇakaḥ|| 69.20 ||
ūrusūtraṃ ca nīvīṃ ca muṣṭibandhaṃ prakīrṇakam|
pādāṅgadaṃ haṃsakaśca nūpuraṃ kṣudraghaṇṭikā|| 69.21 ||
sukhapaṭṭamiti proktā alaṅkārāḥ suśobhanāḥ|
catvāriṃśadamī proktā loke vede tu saukhyadāḥ|| 69.22 ||
alaṅkārapradānena caturvargaprasādhanam|
eteṣāṃ pūjanaṃ kṛtvā pradadyādiṣṭasiddhaye|| 69.23 ||
teṣāṃ daivatamuccārya pūjayet tu vicakṣaṇaḥ|
śirogatāni vā dadyāt sauvarṇāni tu sarvadā|| 69.24 ||
cūḍāratnādikānīha bhūṣaṇāni tu bhairava|
graiveyakādihaṃsāntaṃ sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ ca vā|| 69.25 ||
nivedayet tu devabyo nānyat taijasambhavam|
rītiraṅgādi saṃjātaṃ pātropakaraṇādikam|| 69.26 ||
dadyādāyasavarjaṃ tu bhūṣaṇaṃ na kadācana|
ghaṇṭācāmara kumbhādipātropakaraṇādikam|| 69.27 ||
tadbhūṣaṇāntare dadyādayasmāt tadupabhūṣaṇam|
sarvaṃ tāmramayaṃ dadyād yat kiṃcid bhūṣaṇādikam|| 69.28 ||
sarvatra svarmavat tāmramarghyapātre tato’dhikam|
pūjārghyapapātranaivedyādhārapātraṃ ca pānakam|| 69.29 ||
audumbaraṃ sadā viṣṇoḥ prītidaṃ toṣadaṃ tathā|
tāmre devāḥ pramodante tāmre devāḥ sthitāḥ sadā|| 69.30 ||
sarvaprītikaraṃ tāmraṃ tasmāt tāmraṃ prayojayet|
svopayoge naraḥ kuryād devānāmapi bhairava|| 69.31 ||
grīvordhvadeśe raupyaṃ tu na kadācicca bhūṣaṇam|
prāvāraḥ pānapātraṃ ca gaṇḍako gṛhameva ca|| 69.32 ||
paryaṅkādi yadanyacca sarvaṃ tadupabhūṣaṇam|
ayomayamṛte kāṃsyamṛte yadbhūṣaṇaṃ bhavet|| 69.33 ||
svarṇaraupyasya cābhāve tvadhaḥ kāye niyojayet|
eteṣāṃ bhūṣaṇādīnāṃ yad dātuṃ śakyate naraiḥ|| 69.34 ||
tat tad dadyāt sambhave tu sarvameva pradāpayet|
caturvargapradaṃ tvitthaṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ sarvasaukhyadam|| 69.35 ||
tuṣṭipuṣṭiprītikaraṃ yathāśaktīṣṭaye sṛjet|
idaṃ vā bhūṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ sarvadevasya tuṣṭidam|| 69.36 ||
gandhaṃ ca samyak śṛṇutaṃ putrau vetālabhairavau|
cūrṇīkṛto vā ghṛṣṭo vā dāhākarṣita eva vā|| 69.37 ||
rasaḥ sammardajo vāpi prāṇyaṅgodbhava eva vā|
gandhaḥ pañcavidhaḥ prokto devānāṃ prītidāyakaḥ|| 69.38 ||
gandhacūrṇaṃ gandhapātraṃ cūrṇaṃ sumanasastathā|
praśastagandhayuktānāṃ syuḥ patracūrṇāni yāni tu|| 69.39 ||
tāni gandhavahāni syuḥ sagandhaḥ prathamaḥ smṛtaḥ|
ghṛṣṭo malayajo gandhaḥ sacūrṇīkṛtameruṇā|| 69.40 ||
aguruprabhṛtiścāpi yasya paṅkaḥ pradīyate|
gandho dṛṣṭvāmaghṛṣṭo’yaṃ dvitīyaḥ parikīrtitaḥ|| 69.41 ||
devadārvagurupadmagandhasārānta candanāḥ|
priyādīnāṃ ca yo dagdhvā gṛhyate dāhajo rasaḥ|| 69.42 ||
sadāhākarṣito gandhastṛtīyaḥ parikīrtitaḥ|
sugandhakaravībilvagandhīni tilakaṃ tathā|| 69.43 ||
prabhṛtīnāṃ raso yo’sau niṣpīḍya parigṛhyate|
sasammardodbhavo gandhaḥ sammardaja itīṣyate|| 69.44 ||
mṛganābhisamudbhūtastatkoṣodbhava eva vā|
gandhaḥ prāṇyaṅgajaḥ prokto modadaḥ svargavāsinām|| 69.45 ||
karpūragandhasārādyāḥ kṣode ghṛṣṭe ca saṃsthitāḥ|
candrabhāgādayaścāpi rase paṅke ca saṅgatāḥ|| 69.46 ||
gandhasāraṃ sarvarasaṃ gandhādau ca prayujyate|
mṛganābhirbhaved ghṛṣṭaścūrṇo’pyanayasya yogataḥ|| 69.47 ||
evaṃ sarvaṃ tu sarvatra gandho bhavati pañcadhā|
ghṛṣṭādibhāvādanyonyaṃ gandhaḥ prītikaraṃ paraḥ|| 69.48 ||
ganadhasya vistaro bhedaḥ proktaḥ kālīyakādayaḥ|
sarvaḥ pañcavidheṣveva praviṣṭo bhavati kṣaṇāt|| 69.49 ||
gandho malayajo yastu daive pitrye ca sammataḥ|
tasya paṅko raso vāpi cūrṇo vā viṣṇutuṣṭidaḥ|| 69.50 ||
sarveṣu gandhajāteṣu praśasto malayodbhavaḥ|
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena dadyānmalayajaṃ sadā|| 69.51 ||
kṛṣṇāguruḥ sakarpūraḥ sahito malayodbhavaiḥ|
vaiṣṇavīprītido gandhaḥ kāmākhyāyāśca bhairava|| 69.52 ||
kuṅkumāgurukastūrīcandrabhāgaiḥ samīkṛtaiḥ|
tripurāprītido gandhastathā caṇḍyāśca śasyate|| 69.53 ||
daivatoddeśapūrveṇa gandhaṃ sampūjya sādhakaḥ|
devāyeṣṭāya vitaret sarvasiddhipradaṃ sadā|| 69.54 ||
gandhena labhate kāmān gandho dharmapradaḥ sadā|
arthānāṃ sādhako gandho gandhe mokṣaḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ|| 69.55 ||
ayaṃ vāṃ kathito gandhaḥ putrau vetālabhairavau|
puṣpāṇi devyā vaiṣṇavyāḥ priyāṇi śṛṇu samprati|| 69.56 ||
bakulaiścaiva mandāraiḥ kundapuṣpaiḥ kuruṇṭakaiḥ|
karavīrārkapuṣpaiśca śālmalaiścāparājitaiḥ|| 69.57 ||
damanaiḥ sindhuvāraiśca surabhī kuruvakaistathā|
latābirbrahmavṛkṣasya dūrvāṅkuraiśca kaumalaiḥ|| 69.58 ||
mañjarībhiḥ kuśānāṃ ca bilvapatraiḥ suśobhanaiḥ|
pūjayet vaiṣṇavīṃ devīṃ kāmākhyāṃ tripurāṃ tathā|| 69.59 ||
anyāśca yā śivāprītyai jāyante puṣpajātayaḥ|
tā imāḥ śṛṇu kathyante mayā vetāla bhairava|| 69.60 ||
mālatī mallikā jātī yūthikā mādhavī tathā|
pāṭalā karavīraśca javā tarkārikā tathā|| 69.61 ||
kubjakastagaraścaiva karṇikāro’tha rocanā|
campakāmrātakau bāṇo barbarā mallikā tathā|| 69.62 ||
aśoko lodhratilakau aṭarūṣaśirīṣakau|
śamīpuṣpaṃ ca droṇaśca padmotpalabakāruṇāḥ|| 69.63 ||
śvetāruṇastrisandhye ca palāśaḥ khadirastathā|
vanamālā’tha sevantī kumudo’tha kadambakaḥ|| 69.64 ||
cakraṃ kokanadaṃ caiva taṇḍilo girikarṇikā|
nāgakeśarapunnāgau ketakyañjalikā tathā|| 69.65 ||
dohadā bījapuraśca nameruḥ śāla eva ca|
trapuṣī caṇḍabilvaśca jhiṇṭī pañcavidhāstathā|| 69.66 ||
evamādyuktakusumaiḥ pūjayed varadāṃ śivām|
apāmārgasya patraṃ tu tato bhṛṅgārapatrakam|| 69.67 ||
tato’pi gandhinopatraṃ valāhakamataḥ param|
tasmāt khadirapatraṃ tu vañjulastavaka stathā|| 69.68 ||
āmraṃ tu bakagucchaṃ tu jambupatraṃ tataḥ param|
bījapūrasya patraṃ tu tato’pi kuśapatrakam|| 69.69||
dūrvākuraṃ tataḥ proktaṃ śamīpatramataḥ param|
patramāmalakaṃ tasmādāmalaṃ patramantataḥ || 69.70 ||
sarvato bilvapatraṃ tu devyāḥ prītikaraṃ matam|
puṣpaṃ kokanadaṃ padmaṃ javā bandhuka eva ca|| 69.71 ||
patraṃ bilvasya sarvebhyo vaiṣṇavītuṣṭidaṃ matam|
sarveṣāṃ puṣpajātīnāṃ raktapadmamihottamam|| 69.72 ||
raktapadmasahasreṇa yo mālāṃ samprayacchati|
bhaktiyukto mahādevyai tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu|| 69.73 ||
kalpakoṭisahasrāṇi kalpakoṭiśatāni ca|
sthitvā mama pure śrīmāṃstato rājā kṣitau bhavet|| 69.74 ||
patreṣu bilvapatraṃ tu devīprītikaraṃ matam|
tatsahasrakṛtā mālā pūrvavat phaladā bhavet|| 69.75 ||
kiṃcātra bahunoktena samānyenedamucyate|
uktānuktaistathāpuṣpairjalajaiḥ sthalasambhavaiḥ|| 69.76 ||
patraiḥ sarvairyathālābhaṃ sarvauṣadhigaṇairapi|
vanajaiḥ sarvapuṣpaiśca patrairapi śivāṃ yajet|| 69.77 ||
pūjayet parameśānīṃ puṣpābhāve’pi patrakaiḥ|
patrāṇāmapyabhāve tu tṛṇagulmauṣadhādibhiḥ|| 69.78 ||
auṣadhīnāmabhāve tu tphalairapi pūjayet|
akṣatairvā jalairvāpi tadabhāve tu sarṣapaiḥ|| 69.79 ||
sitaistasyāpyalābhe tu mānīsaṃ bhaktimācaret|
vājidantakapatraiśca puṣpaughairapi pūjayet || 69.80 ||
tulasīkusumaiḥ patrairarcayecchrīvivṛddhaye|
puraścaraṇakāryeṣu bilvapatrayutaistilaiḥ|| 69.81 ||
sākṣataiḥ saghṛtairvāpi śivāmuddiśya yatnataḥ|
juhuyādanalaṃ vṛddhaṃ saṃskṛtaṃ kāmavṛddhaye|| 69.82 ||
saṃsakalpitaḥ kāmasiddhyai saṃkhyayā yaḥ kṛto japaḥ|
tadante pūjanaṃ yattu vihitaṃ kriyate dvijaiḥ|| 69.83 ||
puraścaraṇasaṃjñaṃ tu kīrtitaṃ dvijasāttamaiḥ|
tasmin purāṇake pūrvaṃ pūrvoktaurvistaroditaiḥ|| 69.84 ||
vidhānaiḥ pūyajet devīṃ kāmākhyāṃ vaiṣṇavīmapi|
yathāsambhavamevātra dadyāt ṣoḍaśa sādhakaḥ|| 69.85 ||
upacārāṃstathaivoktān vidhikṛtyānna laṃghayet|
sampūrṇaṃ pūjanaṃ kṛtvā kalpoktaṃ śatadhā japet|| 69.86 ||
japānte juhuyādagniṃ homānte tu balitrayam|
trijātīyaṃ tu vitarettauryatrikamataḥ param|| 69.87 ||
patnī svayaṃ vā bhrātā vā gururvā viniyojayet|
naivedyādīni sarvāṇi svaputraḥ śiṣya eva vā|| 69.88 ||
yajñāvasāne dadyāt tu gurave dakṣiṇāṃ śubhām|
cāmokaraṃ tilān gāñca tadaśaktau tu celakam|| 69.89 ||
aṣṭamyāṃ śuklapakṣasya brahmacāro jitendriyaḥ|
navamyāṃ vā caturdaśyāṃ mahādevyāḥ puraścaret|| 69.90 ||
ādadyād guruvaktrāt tu vidhinā vistareṇa tu|
kalpoditena sampūjya tithiṣvetāsu bhairava|| 69.91 ||
sampūrṇapūjāṃ no kṛtvā nādadyānmantramīpsitam|
na puraścaraṇaṃ vāpi kuryāt kṛtvā’vasīdati|| 69.92 ||
nityapūjā sā tu punaḥ sampūrṇā yadi śakyate|
kalpoditaṃ pūjayituṃ tadā kuryādatandritaḥ|| 69.93 ||
na ced vistaraśaḥ kartuṃ devyāḥ pūjāṃ tu bhairava|
kalpoktāṃ vā’nyadevasya tatrāyaṃ vidhirucyate|| 69.94 ||
mārjanādyaistu saṃskṛtya sthaṇḍile maṇḍalaṃ likhet|
pātrasya pratipattiṃ tu kṛtvā dāha plavaṃ tathā|| 69.95 ||
dhyāyedātmānamatha ca saṃskṛtyāṅgasvarūpataḥ|
aṅguṣṭhādyastraparyantaṃ dvādaśāṅgasya śuddhaye|| 69.96 ||
ardhyapātre’ṣṭadhā japtvā upacārān prasecayet|
ādhāraśaktipramukhaṃ mūlavarṇān prayujya ca|| 69.97 ||
hṛdisthāṃ devatāṃ dhyātvā bahiḥkṛtyaṃ ca vāyunā|
āropya maṇḍale dadyādupacārān yathāvidhi || 69.98 ||
pūjayitvā ṣaḍaṅgāni tathāṣṭau daladevatāḥ|
puṣpāḍhjalitrayaṃ dattvā japtvā stutvā praṇamya ca|| 69.99 ||
mudrāmagre pradarśyātha tataḥ paścād visarjayet|
sarveṣāmeva devānāmeṣa eva vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ|| 69.100 ||
samyak kalpoditā pūjā yadi kartuṃ na śakyate|
ucārāṃstathā dātuṃ pañcaitān vitaret tadā|| 69.101 ||
gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ ca dhūpaṃ ca dīpaṃ ca naivedyameva ca|
abhāve puṣpatoyābhyāṃ tadabhāve tu bhaktitaḥ|| 69.102 ||
saṃkṣepapūjā kathitā tathā vastrādikaṃ punaḥ|
puraścaraṇakṛtye ca pradīpaṃ śṛṇu bhairava|| 69.103 ||
dīpena lokāñjayati dīpastejomayaḥ smṛtaḥ|
caturvargaprado dīpastasmād dīpairyajecchriyam|| 69.104 ||
satataṃ puṣpadīpābhyāṃ pūjayed yastu devatām|
tābhyāmeva caturvargaḥ kathito nātra saṃśayaḥ|| 69.105 ||
puṣpairdevāḥ prasīdanti puṣpe devāśca saṃsthitāḥ|
carācarāśca sakalāḥ sadā puṣparasāḥ smṛtāḥ|| 69.106 ||
kiṃcāti bahunoktena puṣpasyoktirmatallikā|
paraṃ jyotiḥ puṣpagataṃ puṣpeṇaiva prasīdati|| 69.107 ||
trivargasādhanaṃ puṣpaṃ tuṣṭiśrīpuṣṭimokṣadam |
puṣpamūle vased brahmā puṣpamadhye tu keśavaḥ|| 69.108 ||
puṣpāgre tu mahādevaḥ sarve devāḥ sthitā dale|
tasmāt puṣpairyajed devānnityaṃ bhaktiyuto naraḥ|| 69.109 ||
uccāritaṃ nāmamātraṃ jāyate sarvabhūtaye|
ghṛtapradīpaḥ prathamastilatailodbhavastataḥ|| 69.110 ||
sārṣapaphalaniryāsajāto vā rājikodbhavaḥ|
dadhijaścānnajaścaiva dīpāḥ sapta prakīrtitāḥ|| 69.111 ||
padmasūtrabhavā darbhagarbhasūtrabhavā’thavā|
śaṇajā bādarī vāpi phalakoṣodbhavā tathā|| 69.112 ||
vartikā dīpakṛtyeṣu sadā pañcavidhāḥ smṛtāḥ|
taijasaṃ dāravaṃ lauhaṃ mārttikyaṃ nārikelajam|| 69.113 ||
tṛṇadhvajodbhavaṃ vāpi dīpapātraṃ praśasyate|
dīpavṛkṣāśca kartavyāstaijasādyaistu bhairava|| 69.114 ||
vṛkṣeṣu dīpo dātavyo na tu bhūmau kadācana|
sarvasahā vasumatī sahate na tvidaṃ dvayam|| 69.115 ||
akāryapādaghātaṃ ca dīpatāpaṃ tathaiva ca|
tasmād yathā tu pṛthvī tāpaṃ nāpnoti vai tathā|| 69.116 ||
dīpaṃ dadyānmahādevyai anyebhyo’pi ca bhairava|
kurvantaṃ pṛthivītāpaṃ yo dīpamutsṛjennaraḥ|| 69.117 ||
sa tāmratāpaṃ narakaṃ prāpnotyeva śataṃ samāḥ|
suvṛttavartiḥ susnehaḥ pātramagnaḥ sudarśanaḥ || 69.118 ||
sūcchrāye vṛkṣakoṭau tu dīpaṃ dadyāt prayatnataḥ|
labhyate yasya tāpastu dīpasya caturaṅgulāt|| 69.119 ||
na sa dīpa iti khyāto hyoghavahnistu sa śrutaḥ|
netrāhlādakaraḥ svarcirdūratāpavivarjitaḥ|| 69.120 ||
susikhaḥ śabdarahito nirdhūmo nātihrasvakaḥ|
dakṣiṇāvartavartistu pradīpaḥ śrīvivṛddhaye|| 69.121 ||
dopavṛkṣasthite pātre śuddhasnehaprapūrite|
dakṣiṇāvartavartyā tu cārudīptaḥ pradīpakaḥ|| 69.122 ||
uttamaḥ procyate putra sarvatuṣṭipradāyakaḥ|
vṛkṣeṇa varjito dīpo madhyamaḥ parikīrtitaḥ|| 69.123 ||
vihīnaḥ pātratailābhyāmadhamaḥ parikīrtitaḥ|
śāṇaṃ vā dāravaṃ vastraṃ jīrṇaṃ malinameva vā|| 69.124 ||
upayuktaṃ ca nādadyād vartikārthaṃ tu sādhakaḥ|
upādadyānnūtnameva satataṃ śrīvivṛddhaye|| 69.125 ||
koṣajaṃ romajaṃ vastraṃ vartikārthaṃ na cādadet|
na miśrīkṛtya dadyāt tu dīpe snehaghṛtādikān|| 69.126 ||
kṛtvā miśrīkṛtaṃ snehaṃ tāmisraṃ narakaṃ vrajet|
vasāmajjāsthiniryāsaiḥ snehaiḥ prāṇyaṅgasambhavaiḥ|| 69.127 ||
pradīpaṃ naiva kuryāt tu kṛtvā paṅke’vasīdati|
asthipātre’tha vā pacyed durgandhāsthipavāsini|| 69.128 ||
naivaṃ dīpaḥ pradātavyo vibudhaiḥ śrīvivṛddhaye|
naiva nirvāpayed dīpaṃ kadācidapi yatnataḥ|| 69.129 ||
satataṃ lakṣaṇopetaṃ devārthamupakalpitam|
na harejjñānato dīpaṃ tathā lobhādinā naraḥ|| 69.130 ||
dīpahartā bhavedandhaḥ kāṇo nirvāpako bhavet|
uddīptadīpapratimaṃ kāṣṭhakāṇḍasamudbhavaṃ|| 69.131 ||
bilvedhmodbhavamevātha dīpālābhe nivedayet|
ulmukaṃ naiva dīpārthe kadācidapi cotsṛjet|| 69.132 ||
prasannārthaṃ tu taṃ dadyādupacārād bahiṣkṛtam|
evaṃ vāṃ kathito dīpo dhūpaṃ ca śṛṇutaṃ sutau|| 69.133 ||
nāsākṣirandhrasukhadaḥ sugandho’timanoharaḥ|
dahyamānasya kāṣṭhasya prayatasyetarasya ca|| 69.134 ||
parāgasyāthavā dhūmo nistāpo yasya jāyate|
sa dhūpa iti vijñeyo devānāṃ tuṣṭidāyakaḥ|| 69.135 ||
rāśīkṛtairna caikatra tairdravyaiḥ paridhūpayet |
tuṣāgnivartulāṃ kṛtvā na tat phalamavāpnuyāt|| 69.136 ||
śrīcandanaṃ ca saralaḥ śālaḥ kṛṣṇāgurustathā|
udayaḥ surathaskando raktavidruma eva ca|| 69.137 ||
pītaśālaḥ parimalo virmadī kāśalastathā|
namerurdevadāruśca bilvasāro’tha khādiraḥ|| 69.138 ||
santānaḥ pārijātaśca haricandanavallabhau|
vṛkṣeṣu dhapāḥ sarveṣāṃ prītidāḥ parikīrtitāḥ|| 69.139 ||
arālaḥ saha sūtrema śrīvāsaḥ paṭṭavāsakaḥ|
karpūraḥ śrīkaraścaiva parāgaḥ śrīharāmalau|| 69.140 ||
sarvauṣadhīpa jātīca varāhaścūrṇa utkalaḥ|
jātīkoṣasya curṇaṃ ca gandhaḥ kastūrikā tathā|| 69.141 ||
kṣode vṛtte ca gaditā dhūpā ete udāhṛtāḥ|
yakṣadhūpo vṛkṣadhūpaḥ śrīpiṣṭo’guru ḍharḍharaḥ|| 69.142 ||
patrivāhaḥ piṇḍadhūpaḥ sugolaḥ kaṇṭha eva ca|
anyonyayogā niryāsā dhūpā ete prakīrtitāḥ|| 69.143 ||
etairvidhūpayed devān dhūmibhiḥ kṛṣṇavatmanā|
yeṣāṃ dhūpodbhavairghrāṇaistuṣṭiṃ gacchanti jantavaḥ|| 69.144 ||
niryāsaśca parāgaśca kāṣṭhaṃ gandhaṃ tathaiva ca|
kṛtrimaśceti pañcaite dhūpāḥ prītikarāḥ parāḥ|| 69.145 ||
na yakṣadhūpaṃ vitarenmādhavāya kadācana|
na raktaṃ vidrumaṃ mahyaṃ surathaṃ kadrilaṃ tathā|| 69.146 ||
yakṣadhūpaḥ putrivāhaḥ pimḍadhūpaḥ sugolakaḥ|
kṛṣṇāguruḥ sakarpūro mahāmāyāpriyaḥ smṛtaḥ|| 69.147 ||
vṛkṣadhūpena vā devīṃ mahāmāyāṃ prapūjayet|
medomajjāsamāyuktān na dhūpān viniyojayet|| 69.148 ||
parakīyāṃstathāghrātāṃste’pi kṛtyābhimarditān|
puṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ ca gandhaṃ ca upacārāṃstathāparān|| 69.149 ||
ghrātvā nivedya devebhyo naro narakamāpnuyāt|
na bhūmau vitared dhūpaṃ nāsane na ghaṭe tathā|| 69.150 ||
yathātathādhāragataṃ kṛtvā tad vinivedayet|
raktavidrumaśālau ca surathaḥ suralastathā|| 69.151 ||
santānako nameruśca kālāgurusamanvitaḥ|
jātīkoṣākṣasaṃyukto dhūpaḥ kāmeśvarīpriyaḥ|| 69.152 ||
tripurāyāstathaivāyaṃ mātṛṇāmapi nityaśaḥ|
sarveṣāṃ pīṭhadevānāṃ rudrādīnāṃ ca putrakau|| 69.153 ||
eṣa vāṃ kathito dhūpaḥ śṛṇu tannetrarañjanam|
yena tuṣyati kāmākhyā tripurā vaiṣṇavī tathā|| 69.154 ||
sauvīraṃ yāmunaṃ tutthraṃ mayūragrīvakaṃ tathā|
darvikā meghanīlaśca añjanāni bhavanti ṣaṭ|| 69.155 ||
sravaddrumaṃ ca sauvīraṃ yāmunaṃ prastaraṃ tathā|
mayūragrīvakaṃ ratnaṃ meghanīlastu taijasam|| 69.156 ||
ghṛṣṭhāni grāhya caitāni śilāyāṃ taijase’tha vā|
pradadyāt sarvadevebhyo devībhyaścāpi putraka|| 69.157 ||
ghṛtatailādiyogena tāmrādau dīpavahninā|
yadañjanaṃ jāyate tu darvikā parikīrtitā|| 69.158 ||
sarvābhāve tu tad dadyād devībhyo dāhajāñjanam|
mahāmāyā jagaddhātrī kāmākhyā tripurā tathā|| 69.159 ||
āpnuvanti mahātoṣaṃ ṣaḍbhirebhiḥ sadāñjanaiḥ|
vidhavā nāñjanaṃ kuryānmahāmāyārthamṛttamam|| 69.160 ||
nādatte tvañjanaṃ devī vaiṣṇavī vidhavākṛtam|
ta mṛtpātre yojayet tu sādhako netrarañjanam|| 69.161 ||
na pūjāphalamāpnoti mṛtpātravihitāñjanaiḥ|
caturvargaprado dhūpaḥ kāmadaṃ netrarañjanam|| 69.162 ||
tasmād dvayamidaṃ dadyād devebhyo bhaktito naraḥ|
iti vāṃ gadito dhūpastathoktaṃ netrarañjanam|
naivedyaṃ tu mahādevyāḥ śṛṇvaikāgramanāḥ punaḥ|| 69.163 ||
iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe ekonaspatatitamo’dhyāyaḥ||

<70>
||śrībhagavānuvāca||
nivedanīyaṃ yad dravyaṃ praśastaṃ prayataṃ tathā|
tadbhakṣyādyaṃ pañcavidhaṃ naivedyamiti gadyate|| 70.1 ||
bhakṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ ca lehyaṃ ca peyaṃ coṣyaṃ ca pañcamam|
sarvatra caitannaivedyamārādhyeṣṭe nivedayet|| 70.2 ||
teṣu devyāḥ kathaye śṛṇutaṃ tu vām |
bhakṣyādipañcakairdevī dattairevābhituṣyati|| 70.3 ||
nādatte vidhivat kiṃcid dattaṃ caitanna vidyate |
nāgaraṃ ca kapitthaṃ ca drākṣāṃ kramukameva ca|| 70.4 ||
karakaṃ varadaṃ kolaṃ kuṣmāṇḍaṃ panasaṃ tathā|
bakulaṃ ca madhūkaṃ ca rasālāmrātakeśaram || 70.5 ||
ākṣoḍaṃ piṇḍakharjūraṃ karuṇaṃ śrīphalaṃ tathā|
audumbaraṃ ca punnāgaṃ mādhavaṃ karkaṭīphalam|| 70.6 ||
jāmbavaṃ piṇḍakharjūraṃ bījapūraṃ ca jāmbavam|
harītakīmāmalaka ṣaḍvidhaṃ nāgaraṅgakam|| 70.7 ||
devakaṃ madhukaṃ śītaṃ paṭolaṃ kṣīravṛkṣajam|
pāṭalaṃ śālajaṃ vṛntamagnijaṃ kadalīphalam|| 70.8 ||
tindukaṃ kusumaṃ potaṃ kāravindaṃ karūṣakam|
garbhāvartaṃ ca tatpuṣpaṃ kṣīrasrāvyamanaṅgajam|| 70.9 ||
kumudānāṃ paṅkajānāṃ phalāni vividhāni ca|
vanyānāṃ sakalairdevīṃ phalaiḥ puṣpaiḥ prapūjayet|| 70.10 ||
ṛte śleṣmātakaṃ bimbaśailakaṃ vaiṣṇavīṃ tathā|
sarveṣāṃ phalajātīnāṃ madhye devīpriyaṃ phalam|| 70.11 ||
lāṅgalaṃ mātuluṅgaṃ ca karamardaṃ rasālakam|
evaṃ phalāni deyāni kāmākhyāyai ca bhairava|| 70.12 ||
tripurāyai tathā samyak pīṭhadevībhya eva ca|
śṛṅgāṭakaṃ kaśeruṃ ca śālūkaṃ ca mṛṇālakam|| 70.13 ||
śṛṅgaveraṃ kāñcanaṃ ca sthūlaṃ kandaṃ bakulakam|
evamādīni kandāni devyai sarvāṇi cotsṛjet|| 70.14 ||
paramānnaṃ piṣṭakaṃ ca yāvakaṃ kṛśaraṃ tathā|
modakaṃ pṛthukādīni kandupakvāni cotsṛjet|| 70.15 ||
haviḥśālyodanaṃ divya mājyayuktaṃ saśarkaram|
nivedayenmahādevyai sarvāṇi vyañjanāni ca|| 70.16 ||
kṣīrādīnyatha gavyāni māhiṣyāṇi ca sarvaśaḥ|
ajāvikamṛgāṇāṃ ca kṣīrādīni nivedayet|| 70.17 ||
madhvādīni ca sarvāṇi guḍadhānāḥ sitāṃ tathā|
annāni caiva pānāni māṃsāni vinivedayet|| 70.18 ||
sarvaṃ surabhigandhāḍhyaṃ vyañjanaṃ sumanoharam|
śākamāṃsādisambhūtaṃ māhādevyai nivedayet|| 70.19 ||
ābhiṣaṃ paramānnaṃ ca dadhisarpiḥ saśarkaram|
mahādevyai nivedyātha vājimedhaphalaṃ labhet|| 70.20 ||
sitāsammiśritāṃ dattvā surāṃ madhusamanvitām|
devīloke ciraṃ sthitvā rājā kṣititale bhavet|| 70.21 ||
lāṅgalaṃ kramukaṃ dattvā rucakaṃ karamardakam|
saubhāgmamatulaṃ prāpya devīloke mahīyate|| 70.22 ||
māṣān mudgān masūrāṃ ścatilān bhaṅgāstathaiva ca|
yavādīnanyatha sarvāṇi yatāyogyaṃ nivedayet|| 70.23 ||
yathā yathā bhavedbhakṣyaṃ yatā dravyaṃ tathā tathā|
saṃskṛtya veśavārādyairmadevyai nivedayet|| 70.24 ||
mahāvīro munirvāpi brāhmaṇaścetaro’tha vā|
yad yad bhakṣyaṃ svamarthaṃ tu prakalpyaṃ syād yathā yathā|| 70.25 ||
tathā tathā mahādevyai bhaktiyukto nivedayet|
saṃskāryāṇyatha saṃskṛtya yayā saṃskārakaṃ bhavet|| 70.26 ||
saṃskāryaśca yathā tasyāstattad dadyāttathā tathā|
yatpūtigandhasaṃyuktaṃ dagdhaṃ bhojyaṃvivarjitam || 70.27 ||
taduktamapi no dadyānmahādevyai kadācana|
tāmbūlaṃ gandhasaṃyuktaṃ karpūrādyadhivāsitam|| 70.28 ||
sañcūrṇerjalajānāṃ ca saṃskṛtaṃ vinivedayet|
validāneṣu vihitā ya eva mṛgapakṣiṇaḥ|| 70.29 ||
teṣaāṃ māṃsāni matsyānāṃ māṃsāni ca nivedayet|
khaḍgavārdhrīṇasacchāgamāṃsairmiśrīkṛtaiḥ kṛtam|| 70.30 ||
vyañjanaṃ svādugandhāḍhyaṃ pāsitaṃ sumanoharam|
sakṛd dattvā mahādevyai sārvabhaumo nṛpo bhavet|| 70.31 ||
mūlakaireṇamāṃsena lohapātre susaṃskṛtam|
vyañjanaṃ gandhinaṃ dattvā devīlokamavāpnuyāt|| 70.32 ||
kharjūraṃ piṇḍakharjūraṃ yavacūrṇaṃ ca sājyakam|
vaiṣṇavyai vinivedyaiva rājasūyaphalaṃ labhet|| 70.33 ||
kṛśarānnapradānena saubhāgyamatulaṃ bhavet|
dattvaiva nārikelāmbu vahniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet|| 70.34 ||
jāmbavaṃ lavalīdhātrīśrīphalāni nivedya ca|
vahniṣṭomaphalaṃ labdhvā devīlokamavāpyuyāt|| 70.35 ||
drākṣāṃ sitāsamāyuktāṃ nāgaraṅgakasaṃyutām|
vinivedya mahādevye lakṣmīvān rūpavān bhavet|| 70.36 ||
dhānyaṃ ca pṛthukaṃ devyai dattvā śriyamavāpnuyāt|
ikṣudaṇḍaṃ mudgamaṇḍaṃ navanītaṃ nivedya ca|| 70.37 ||
saubhāgyamuttamaṃ prāpya devīloke mahīyate|
navanītasamāyuktaṃ tilaṃ devyai nivedya ca|| 70.38 ||
iha kāmānavāpyaiva mṛto mokṣamavāpnuyāt|
abhakṣyavarjyaṃ sarvānnaṃ vyañjanena samanvitam|| 70.39 ||
bhojyavat parikalpyātha mahādevyai nivedayet|
ratnatoyasamāyuktaṃ salilaṃ nārikelajam|| 70.40 ||
kṣorājyamadhubhirmiśraṃ sitādadhisamanvitam|
yastaijasena pātreṇa peyaṃ devyai nivedayet|| 70.41 ||
bhaktipravaṇacittena tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu|
kalpakoṭisahasrāṇi kalpakoṭiśatāni ca|| 70.42 ||
sthitvā devīpure dhīraḥ sārvabhaumau bhavet kṣitau|
tataḥ paraṃ tu kaivalyamāpnoti ca yathecchayā|| 70.43 ||
kalāyaṃ ca sanīvāraṃ kvathitaṃ dadhisaṃyutam|
mahādevyai nivedyaiva kāmamiṣṭamavāpnuyāt|| 70.44 ||
marīcaṃ pippalīkolaṃ jīrakaṃ tantubhaṃ tathā|
saṃskāre ca samakṣe ca mahādevyai nivedayet|| 70.45 ||
tintiḍīṃ khaṇḍasaṃyuktāṃ bhaktiyukto nivedya ca|
jyotiṣṭomaphalaṃ labdhvā devīlokamavāpnuyāt|| 70.46 ||
rājamāṣaṃ masūraṃ ca pālaṅgaṃ cātha potikām|
kālaśākaṃ kalāyaṃ ca brāhmomūlakameva ca|| 70.47 ||
vāstūkaṃ ca kalambīṃ ca kañcukaṃ hilamocikām|
cakraṃ vidrumapatraṃ ca tathaiva ca punarnavām|| 70.48 ||
śākānetān mahādevyai yojayed bhaktisaṃyutaḥ|
so’tulāṃ śriyamāpnoti mama loke mahīyate|| 70.49 ||
śraddhāparīṣṭisaṃskārabhaktidravyābhisambhramam |
rāgādhikyāt phalādhikyaṃ hīnād vai hīnatāṃ vrajet|| 70.50 ||
mantrakālaviruddhāni naivedyāni kadācana|
devebhyo nopayuñjīta gurutāvihitāni ca|| 70.51 ||
rājate vā’tha sauvarṇe tāmre vā prastare’pi ca|
padmapatre’thavā dadyānnaivedyaṃ matpriyāpriyam|| 70.52 ||
taijaseṣu ca pātreṣu sauvarṇaṃ tāmrameva vā|
prāśanārthamupādadyādardhyapātrārthameva vā|| 70.53 ||
yajñadārumayaṃ vāpi pātraḥ madhyamamiṣyate|
sarvālābhe tu māheyaṃ svahastaghaṭitaṃ yadi|| 70.54 ||
etad vāṃ kathitaṃ putrau naivedyaṃ vaiṣṇavīpriyam|
kāmākhyāyāstathā devyāstripurāyā viśeṣataḥ|
pradakṣiṇanamaskārau sāmprataṃ śṛṇutaṃ yuvām|| 70.55 ||
iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe saptatitamo’dhyāyaḥ||

<71>
||śrībhagavānuvāca||
prasāya dakṣiṇaṃ hastaṃ svayaṃ namraśirāḥ punaḥ|
dakṣiṇaṃ darśayat pārśvaṃ manasāpi ca dakṣiṇaḥ || 71.1 ||
sakṛt trirvā veṣṭayeyurdaivyāḥ prītiḥ prajāyate|
sa ca pradakṣiṇo jñeyaḥ sarvadevaughatuṣṭidaḥ|| 71.2 ||
aṣṭottaraśataṃ yastu devyāḥ kuryāt pradakṣiṇam|
sa sarvakāmamāsādya paścānmokṣamavāpnuyāt|| 71.3 ||
manasāpi ca yo dadyād devyai bhaktyā pradakṣiṇam|
pradakṣiṇād yamagṛhe narakāṇi na paśyati|| 71.4 ||
kāyiko vāgbhavaścaiva mānasastrividhiḥ smṛtaḥ|
namaskāraḥ śrutastajjñairuttamādhamamadhyamaḥ|| 71.5 ||
prasārya pādau hasto ca patitvā daṇḍavat kṣitau|
jānubhyāmavarni gatvā śirasāspṛśya medinīm|| 71.6 ||
kriyate yo namaskāra uttamaḥ kāyikastu saḥ|
jānubhyāṃ ca kṣitiṃ spṛṣṭvā śirasāspṛśya medinīm|| 71.7 ||
kriyate yo namaskāro madhyamaḥ kāyikaḥ smṛtaḥ|
puṭīkṛtya karau śīrṣe dīyate yad yathā tathā|
aspṛṣṭvā jānuśīrṣābhyāṃ kṣitiṃ so’dhama ucyate|| 71.8 ||
yā svayaṃ gadyapadyābhyāṃ ghaṭitābhyāṃ namaskṛtiḥ|
kriyate bhaktiyuktena vācikastūttamastu saḥ|| 71.9 ||
paurāṇikairvaidikairvā mantrairvā kriyate natiḥ|
sa madhyamo namaskāro bhaved vācanikaḥ sadā|| 71.10 ||
yat tu mānuṣyavākyena namanaṃ kriyate sadā|
sa vāciko’dhamo jñeyo namaskāreṣu putrakau|| 71.11 ||
iṣṭamadhyāniṣṭagatairmanobhistrividhaṃ punaḥ|
namanaṃ mānasaṃ proktamuttamādhamamadhyamam|| 71.12 ||
trividhe ca namaskāre kāyikaścottamaḥ smṛtaḥ|
kāyikaistu namaskārairdevāstuṣyanti nityaśaḥ|| 71.13 ||
ayameva namaskāro daṇḍādipratināmabhiḥ |
praṇāma iti vijñeyaḥ sa pūrvaṃ pratipāditaḥ|| 71.14 ||
naivedyena bhavet sarvaṃ naivedyanāmṛtaṃ bhavet|
dharmārthakāmamokṣāśca naivedyaṣu pratiṣṭhitāḥ|| 71.15 ||
sarvayajñamayaṃ nityaṃ naivedyaṃ sarvatuṣṭidam|
jñānadaṃ kāmadaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvabhogyamayaṃ tathā|| 71.16 ||
manasāpi mahādevyai naivedyaṃ dātumicchati|
yo naro bhaktiyuktaḥ san sa dīrghāyuḥ sukhī bhavet|| 71.17 ||
mahāmāyāṃ sadā devīmarcaṃyiṣyāmi bhaktitaḥ |
nānāvidhaistu nevedyauriti cintākulastu yaḥ|
sa sarvakāmān samprāpya mama loke mahīyate|| 71.18 ||
manasāpi ca yo dadyād devyai bhaktyā pradakṣiṇam|
sa dakṣiṇe yamagṛhe narakāṇi na paśyati|| 71.19 ||
devamānuṣagandharvā yakṣarākṣasapannagāḥ|
namaskāreṇa tuṣyanti mahātmānaḥ samantataḥ|| 71.20 ||
namaskāreṇa labhate caturvargaṃ mahāmattiḥ|
sarvatra sarvasiddhyarthaṃ natireva praśasyate|| 71.21 ||
natyā vijayate lokānnatyāyurapi vardhate|
namaskāreṇa dīrghāyucchinnā labhate prajāḥ|| 71.22 ||
namaskuru mahādevyai pradakṣiṇamatho kuru|
naivedyaṃ dehi nitarāmiti yo bhāṣate muhuḥ|
so’pi kāmānavāpyeha mama loke pramodate|| 71.23 ||
vidadhāti ca naivedyaṃ mahādevyai subhaktimān|
dātuṃ prati naraḥ so’pi devīlokamavāpnuyāt|| 71.24 ||
iti vāṃ kathitāḥ samyagupacārāstu ṣoḍaśa|
kimanyadrucitaṃ vāṃ tat kathayiṣyāmi pṛcchatoḥ|| 71.25 ||
iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe ṣoḍaśopacāranirṇaye ekasaptatitamo’dhyāyaḥ||

<72>
||śrībhagavānuvāca||
kāmākhyāyāśca māhātmyaṃ śṛṇutaṃ ca vadāmi vām|
sāṅga tad sarahasyaṃ ca śṛṇu vetāla bhairava|| 72.1 ||
ekadā garuḍenāśu viṣṇurviṣṇuparāyaṇau|
gacchan devīṃ tu kāmākhyāṃ nīlasthāmāsasāda ha|| 72.2 ||
āsādya taṃ giriśreṣṭhamavajñāya sa keśavaḥ|
gaccha gaccheti garuḍaṃ codayāmāsa taṃ gatau|| 72.3 ||
taṃ ca devī mahāmāyā kāmākhyā jagatāṃ prasūḥ|
garuḍena samaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ stambhayāmāsa rodasī|| 72.4 ||
sa tu gantuṃ mahāmāyāmāyayā parimohitaḥ
na gantumatha vāgantumaśakad baddhavat sthitaḥ|| 72.5 ||
aśaktaṃ garuḍaṃ dṛṣṭvā gamane garuḍadhvajaḥ|
kruddhastaṃ parvataśreṣṭhamutsārayitumudyataḥ|| 72.6 ||
tataḥ karābhyāṃ taṃ śailaṃ kroḍīkṛtya jagatpatiḥ|
abhūt kṣamaścālayituṃ manāgapi na keśavaḥ|| 72.7 ||
taṃ cicālayiṣuṃ śailaṃ kāmākhyā krodhatatparā|
siddhasūtrema vaikuṇṭhaṃ babandha garuḍena hi|| 72.8 ||
taṃ baddhvā siddhasūtreṇa grāhāgre lavaṇārṇave|
cikṣepa helayā devī saṃkṣepāt prāpatat talam|| 72.9 ||
taṃ sāgaratalaṃ prāptaṃ punareva svamāyayā|
yantriyitvā samākramya jagrāhābdhitalasthitam|| 72.10 ||
sa prayatnena mahatā notplutiṃ kartuṃmiṣṭavān|
mahāyatnaṃ prakurvāṇaḥ punarunmajjanehariḥ|| 72.11 ||
tasyāsāraṃ prasāraṃ ca kāmākhyā pratiṣedhayet|
jñānodgamanamapyasya sā devī pratiṣedhayet|| 72.12 ||
tataḥ prajñānarahitaḥ prasārāsāravarjitaḥ|
garuḍena samaṃ toyatale śīrṇamabhūcciram|| 72.13 ||
mārgamāṇastu taṃ sraṣṭā sāgarāntarasaṃsthitam|
harimāsādayāmāsa viśīrṇaṃ prākṛtaṃ yathā|| 72.14 ||
tamāsādya satārkṣyaṃ tu sraṣṭā lokapitāmahaḥ|
hastābhyāṃ ta samādāya votplāvayitumiṣṭavān|| 72.15 ||
tamutplāvayituṃ śakto nābhūllokapitāmahaḥ|
svayaṃ ca devīmāvābhirbaddhaḥ san vismayan sthitaḥ|| 72.16 ||
mārgamāṇāstu te sarve devāḥ śakrapurogamāḥ|
cirema cātha kālena samāse durjalāntare|| 72.17 ||
tāvāsādya tataḥ sarve surāḥ śakrapurogamāḥ|
samutplāvayituṃ yatnaṃ cakrurnāśaknuvaṃśca te|| 72.18 ||
tataḥ sarve’pi te devā mohitā māyayā bhṛśam|
bidhiṃviṣṇa sthitau yadvat tadvat te tatra saṃsthitāḥ|| 72.19 ||
mārgamāṇo’tha tān sarvān devān devagurustadā|
bṛhaspatiṃrmahādevaṃ himavatsānusaṃsthitam|| 72.20 ||
samāsādya sa devānāṃ vṛttāntaṃ devapūjitaḥ|
pṛṣṭavān sādaraṃ sammak stutvā natvā yathāvidhi|| 72.21 ||
gururuvāca
mahādeva jagaddhāma jagatpraśamakāraṇa|
śakrādīnmārgamāṇo’haṃ devāṃstvāṃ samupasthitaḥ|| 72.22 ||
brahmā viṣṇuśca na brahmasadane nāpi nākataḥ|
saṃsthitau nāpi kutrāpi jñāyene hyanyadā yathā|| 72.23 ||
tamimaṃ saṃśayaṃ deva cchindhi tvaṃ devadevatāḥ|
kutra tiṣṭhanti kasmād vā tathā bhūtvā hyavasthitāḥ|| 72.24 ||
anuyāsyāmi tān sarvānupadeśāt tava prabho|
teṣāṃ sthitiṃ tvaṃ kathaya yadi te vartate dayā|| 72.25 ||
tasya tad vacanaṃ śrutvā taduddeśamahaṃ punaḥ|
tat sarvamuktavān karma yathā baddhāśca māyayā|| 72.26 ||
avajñātā mahādevī mahāmāyā jaganmayī|
tena tanmāyayā baddho viṣṇustiṣṭhati sāgare|| 72.27 ||
taṃ mārgamāṇāstridaśā brahmādyā māyayā punaḥ|
nibaddhā nikaṭe tasya sthitāścātyarthasaṃyatāḥ|| 72.28 ||
tāṃstumārgayituṃ yāsiyadiha tvaṃ mayā vinā|
baddhastathaiva tvaṃ cāpi nāyātuṃ bhavitā prabhuḥ|| 72.29 ||
tasmād gacchāmyahaṃ tatra yatrāste garuḍadhvajaḥ|
brahmendrādyāstathā guptānmocayiṣye ca tān kramāt|| 72.30 ||
ityuktvā garuṇā sārdhaṃ sambhūya sa vṛṣadhvajaḥ|
devaughā yatra tiṣṭhanti gatastatra maheśvaraḥ|| 72.31 ||
tatra gatvā mahādevo viṣṇumābhāṣya vedhasam|
sarvāstān paripapraccha kimarthaṃ saṃsthitāstviha|| 72.32 ||
gatāgatavihīnāśca jajavajjñānavarjitāḥ|
kimarthamabhavan devāstanme bhāṣantu samprati|| 72.33 ||
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā mahādevasya keśavaḥ|
śanairbhargamuvācedaṃ brahmādīnāṃ purastadā|| 72.34 ||
nīlakūṭasya śikharādūrdhvabhāgena gacchatā|
viyatā garuḍasthena mayā nīlo mahāgiriḥ|| 72.35 ||
dhṛtaḥ kareṇa coddhartuṃ garuḍāgativāraṇe|
tatra māṃ sā mahāmāyā kāmākhyā kāmarūpiṇī|| 72.36 ||
yoganidrāṃ svayaṃ dhṛtvā cikṣepāmbudhipuṣkare|
tato’haṃ talamāsādya toyarāśeḥ savāhanaḥ|| 72.37 ||
patito nivasāmyatra ciramandhakasūdana|
nivasāni ciraṃ cāhamatra sāgaratoyake|| 72.38 ||
nādyāpi sā mahāmāyā nudate māṃ maheśvara|
madarthamāgatā devā brahmendrādyāḥ samantataḥ|| 72.39 ||
te’pi baddhā mahādevyā māyāpāśena vai haṭhāt|
tasmānno hyanugṛhṇīṣva nayedārnī śivālaye|| 72.40 ||
tāṃ ca prasādayiṣyāmaḥ samyagbandhavihiṃsayā|
harestadvacanaṃ śrutvā hyahaṃ ca karuṇāyutaḥ|| 72.41 ||
uvāca paramaprītyā vidhiviṣṇū prati svayam|
īśvaryāḥ kāmapūrvāyāḥ kavacaṃ sumanoharam|| 72.42 ||
baddhvā śarīre cāplāvya paścād gacchantu tāṃ prati|
ahaṃ nibaddhakavacastenāhaṃ māyayā tviha|| 72.43 ||
na baddho mama saṃsargāt tathā ceha bṛhaspatiḥ|
tasmād yūyaṃ tu kavacaṃ śṛṇudhva vacanānmama|| 72.44 ||
yena saukhyāt samutplutya drakṣyāmaḥ parameśvarīm|
oṃ kāmākhyākāvacasya ṛṣirbṛhaspatiḥ smṛtaḥ|| 72.45 ||
devī kāmeśvarī tasya anuṣṭupchanda iṣyate|
viniyogaḥ sarvasiddhau taṃ ca śṛṇvantu devatāḥ|| 72.46 ||
śiraḥ kāmeśvarī devī kāmākhyā cakṣuṣī mama|
sāradā karṇayugalaṃ tripurāvadanaṃ tathā|| 72.47 ||
kaṇṭhe pātu mahāmāyā hṛdi kāmeśvarī punaḥ|
kāmākhyā jaṭhare pātu śāradā māṃ tu nābhitaḥ|| 72.48 ||
tripurā pārśvayoḥ pātu mahāmāyā tu mehane|
gude kāmeśvarī pātu kāmākhyorudvaye tu mām|| 72.49 ||
jānunoḥ śāradā pātu tripurā pātu jaṅghayoḥ|
mahāmāyā pādayuge nityaṃ rakṣatu kāmadā|| 72.50 ||
keśe koṭeśvarī pātu nāsāyāṃ pātu dīrghikā|
bhairavī dantasaṃghāte mātaṃgyavatu cāṅgayoḥ|| 72.51 ||
bāhvormāṃ lalitā pātu pāṇyostu vanavāsinī|
vindhyavāsinyaṅguliṣu śrīkāmā nakhakoṭiṣu|| 72.52 ||
romakūpeṣu sarveṣu guptakāmā sadāvatu|
pādāṅgulipārṣṇibhāge pātu māṃ bhuvaneśvarī|| 72.53 ||
jihvāyāṃ pātu māṃ setuḥ kaḥ kaṇṭhābhyantare’vatu|
laḥ pātu cāntare vakṣa haḥ pātu jaṭharāntare|| 72.54 ||
sāmīnduḥ pātu māṃ vastāvindubindvantare’vatu|
takārastvaci māṃ pātu rakāro’sthiṣu sarvadā|| 72.55 ||
lakāraḥ sarvanāḍīṣu īkāraḥ sarvasandhiṣu|
candraḥ snāyuṣu māṃ pātu vindumajjāsu santatam|| 72.56 ||
pūrvasyāṃ diśi cāgneyyāṃ dakṣiṇe nairṛte tathā|
vāruṇe caiva vāyavyāṃ kaubere haramandire|| 72.57 ||
akārādyāstu vaiṣṇavyā aṣṭau varṇāstu mantragāḥ|
pāntu tiṣṭhantu satataṃ samudbhavavivṛddhaye|| 72.58 ||
ūdrdhvādhaḥ pātu satataṃ māṃ tu setudvayaṃ sadā|
navākṣarāṇi mantreṣu śāradāmantragocare|| 72.59 ||
navasvaraṃ tu māṃ nityaṃ nāsādiṣu samantataḥ|
vātapittakaphebhyastu tripurāyāstu tryakṣaram|| 72.60 ||
nityaṃ rakṣatu bhūtebhyaḥ piśācebhyastathaiva ca|
tatsetū satatāṃpātāṃ kravyādbhyo mānnivārakau|| 72.61 ||
namaḥ kāmeśvarī devīṃ mahāmāyāṃ jaganmayīm|
yā bhūtvā prakṛtirnityaṃ tanoti jagadādyatām|| 72.62 ||
kāmākhyāmakṣamālābhayavaradakarāṃ siddhasūtraikahastāṃśvetapretoparisthāṃ maṇikanakayutāṃ kuṅkumāpītavarṇām|
jñānadhyānapratiṣṭhāmatiśayavinayāṃbrahmaśakrādivandyāmagnau bindvantamantrapriyatamaviṣayāṃ naumi siddhyai ratisthām|| 72.63 ||
madhye madhyasya bhāge satatavinamitā bhāvahāvāvalīyālīlā lokasya koṣṭhe sakalaguṇayutā vyaktarūpaikanamrā|
vidyāvidyaikaśāntā samanaśamakarī kṣemakartrī varāsyā nityaṃ pātāt pavitrapraṇavavarakarā kāmapūrvaisvarī naḥ|| 72.64 ||
iti harakavacaṃtanusthitaṃ śamayati vai śamanaṃ tathā yadi|
iha gṛhāṇa yatasva vimokṣaṇe sahita eṣa vidhiḥ saha cāmaraiḥ|| 72.65 ||
ityayaṃ kavacaṃ yastu kāmākhyāyāḥ paṭhed budhaḥ|
sakṛt taṃ tu mahādevī tvanuvrajati nityadā|| 72.66 ||
nādhivyādhibhayaṃ tasya na kravyādbhyo bhayaṃ tathā|
nāgnito nāpitoyebhyo na ripubhyo na rājataḥ|| 72.67 ||
dīrghāyurbahubhogī ca putrapautrasamanvitaḥ|
āvartayañchataṃ devīmandire modate pare|| 72.68 ||
yathā tathā bhaved baddhaḥ saṃgrāme’nyatra vā budhaḥ|
tat kṣaṇādeva muktaḥ syāt smaraṇāt kavacasya tu|| 72.69 ||
īśvara uvāca
iti śrutvā tu kavacaṃ harirbrahmā surāstathā|
śakro’pi kavaca dehe nyāsaṃ cakruḥ pṛthak pṛthak|| 72.70 ||
te tu vinyastakavacā mahāmāyāprabhāvataḥ|
utplupya sāgarasyāmbha āseduḥ kṣitimañjasā|| 72.71 ||
āsādya pṛthivīṃ sarve brahmaviṣṇavādayaḥ surāḥ|
nīlakūṭaṃ samāsādyakāmākhyāṃ draṣṭumāgatāḥ|| 72.72 ||
dṛṣṭvā kāmeśvarīṃ devī keśavastāṃ jaganmayīm|
idamāha svayaṃ jñātvā prabhāvaṃ tat pratiṣṭhitam|| 72.73 ||
tvameva prakṛtirdevī tvameva pṛthivī jalam|
tvameva jagatāṃ mātā tvameva ca jaganmayī|| 72.74 ||
tvaṃ kartrī sarvajagatāṃ vidyā tvaṃ muktidāyinī|
parāparātmikā devī sthūlasūkṣmātmikā tathā|| 72.75 ||
prasīda tvaṃ mahādevī prasannāyāṃ śubhe tvayi|
devāḥ sarve prasīdanti caturvargaprade’naghe|| 72.76 ||
pratyakṣarūpā kāmākhyā keśavasya mahātmanaḥ|
pratyakṣarūpā kāmākhyā harimābhāṣya cābravīt|| 72.77 ||
devyuvāca
keśava brahmaṇā sārdhaṃ sarvairdevaistathā gaṇaiḥ|
madyonisalileṣvadya snānaṃ pānaṃ kuru drutam|| 72.78 ||
tatastvaṃ nirahaṅkāraḥ paravīryasamanvitaḥ|
āruhya garuḍaṃ yāhi tridivaṃ saha vedhasā|| 72.79 ||
evamukto mahādevyā keśavaḥ saha vedhasā|
yonimaṇḍalatoyeṣu snānaṃ pānaṃ cakāra ha|| 72.80 ||
kṛtaplāvāstato devāḥ kṛtasnānaśca keśavaḥ|
gatā devyāśca sammatyā tridivaṃ prati harṣitāḥ|| 72.81 ||
gacchantaste devagaṇāḥ sahitāḥ keśavena ca|
brahmaṇā ca tadādrākṣuḥ kāmākhyāṃ tāṃ viyadgatām|| 72.82 ||
nīlakūṭasahasrāṇi yonibhiḥ saha tadgatai|
ūrdhvādhobhāgayogena dadṛśuḥ saṃsthitāni ca|| 72.83 ||
tāni pratyekato devā āruhyāruhya tatkṣaṇāt|
papuḥ sasnuḥ pūrvavat te prītimāpustathātulām|| 72.84 ||
nirāmayāstathā jagmurvismayākliṣṭacetanāḥ|
stuvantaḥ prastuvantaśca kāmākhyāyonimaṇḍalam|| 72.85 ||
tato devaguruṃ natvā māṃ stutvā ca mayā punaḥ|
visṛṣṭāstridivaṃ yātā harṣotphullavilocanāḥ|| 72.86 ||
māhātmyamīdṛśaṃ devyāḥ kāmākhyāyāstu bhairava|
kavacaṃ cedṛśaṃ proktaṃ tattvamāsādya putraka|| 72.87 ||
yatheṣṭaviniyogena tamāsādya sukhī bhava|
kāmākhyāyāśca māhātmyaṃ kimanyat kathayāmi te|
yasyā yoniśilāyogāllohādyā yānti svarṇatām|| 72.88 ||
yadyonimaṇḍale snātvā sakṛt pītvā ca mānavaḥ|
nehotpattimavāpnoti paraṃ nirvāṇamāpnuyāt|| 72.89 ||
|| iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe kāmākhyākavacamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma dvisaptatitamo’dhyāyaḥ|| 72 ||

<73>
|| trisaptatitamo’dhyāyaḥ ||
||śrībhagavānuvāca||
mātṛkānyāsamadhunā śṛṇu vetāla bhairava|
yena devatvamāyāti naro’pi vihitena vai || 73.1 ||
vāg brahmāṇīmukhā devyo mātṛkāḥ parikīrtitāḥ|
tāsāṃ mantrāṇi sarvāmi vyañjanāni svarāstathā|| 73.2 ||
candrabinduprayuktāni sarvakāmapradāni ca|
ṛṣistu mātṛmantrāṇāṃ brahmaiva parikīrtitaḥ|| 73.3 ||
proktaśchandaśca gāyatrī devatā ta sarasvatī|
śarīraśuddhimukhye tu sarvakāmārthasādhane|| 73.4 ||
viniyogaḥ samuddiṣṭo mantrāṇāṃ nyūnapūraṇe|
akāreṇa samaṃ kādirvargo yaḥ prathamaḥ smṛtaḥ|| 2.74.5 ||
taiścandrabindusaṃyuktaistatrasthairakṣarairbahiḥ|
ākāraṃ ca tathoccārya aṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ namastathā|
prathamaṃ mātṛkāmantramaṅguṣṭhadvayato nyaset|| 73.6 ||
pare vargāḥ svaraiḥ sārdhaṃ ye vānye nyāsakarmaṇi|
te sarve candrabindubhyāṃ yuktāḥ kāryāstu sarvataḥ|| 73.7 ||
hrasvekāraścavargeṇa dīrghekārāntakena tu|| 73.8 ||
tarjanyorvinyaset samyak svāhāntena tu pūrvavat|
hrasvokāraṣṭavargeṇa dīrghokārāntakena tu|| 73.9 ||
madhyamāyugale samyagvaṣaḍanten vinyaset|
ekārāditavargantu aikārāntena caiva hum|| 73.10 ||
nyasedanāmikāyugme niyataṃ tatra bhairava|
okārādipavargaṃ tu aukārāntamaśeṣataḥ|| 73.11 ||
vauṣantaṃ kaniṣṭhāyāṃ vinyaset kāryasiddhaye|
aṃkārādiyakārādivargeṇa kṣāntakena tu|| 73.12 ||
aityantena valayorbinyaset pāṇipṛṣṭhayoḥ|
vaṣaṭkāraṃ śeṣabhāge aṃgatranyāse niyojayet|| 73.13 ||
hṛdayādiṣaḍaṅgeṣu pūrvavat kramato nyaset|
aṅguṣṭhādyuktavargaistu kramāt ṣaḍbhistathāvidhaiḥ|| 73.14 ||
punastathā pādajānusakthiguhyeṣu pārśvayoḥ|
vastau ca vinyasenmantrān kramāt pūrvavadakṣaraiḥ|| 73.15 ||
bāhvoḥ pāṇyostathā kaṭyāṃ nābhau ca jaṭhare tathā|
stanayorapi vinyāsaṃ tathā ṣaḍbhiḥ samācaret|| 73.16 ||
vaktre ca civuke gaṇḍe karṇayośca lalāṭake|
aṃse kakṣe ca ṣaḍvargaiḥ pūrvavannyāsamācareta|| 73.17 ||
romakūpe brahmarandhre gude jaṅghāyuge tathā|
nakheṣu pādapārṣṇyośca tathā pūrvavadācaret|| 73.18 ||
evaṃ tu mātṛkānyāsaṃ yaḥ kuryānnarasattamaḥ|
sa sarvayajñapūjāsu pūto yogyastu jāyate|| 73.19 ||
nātaḥ parataraṃ mantraṃ vidyate kvacideva hi|
yatsarvakāmadaṃ puṇyaṃ caturvargapradaṃ param|| 73.20 ||
vāgdevatāṃ hṛdi dhyātvā mūrtisarvākṣarāṇi ca|
tridhā ca mātṛkāmantraiḥ sakramaiśca pibejjalam|| 73.21 ||
sa vāgmī paṇiḍito dhīmān jāyate ca varaḥ kaviḥ|
candrabindusamāyuktān svarān pūrvaṃ paṭhed budhaḥ|| 73.22 ||
vyañjanāni tu sarvāṇi kevalāni paṭhet tataḥ|
akārādikṣakārāntānyevaṃ śvāsaiśca pūrakaiḥ|| 73.23 ||
jalaṃ karatale gṛhya paṭhitvākṣarasaṃkhyakam|
abhimantrya tu tat toyaṃ prathamaṃ pūrakaiḥ pibet|| 73.24 ||
kumbhakena dvitīyaṃ tu tṛtīyantvatha recakaiḥ|
evaṃ sakṛt trivāraṃ tu pītvā toyaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ|| 73.25 ||
daḍhāṅgaḥ paṇḍito bhūyāt putrapautrasamanvitaḥ|
trisandhyamatha pītvaiva mātṛkāmantramantritam|| 73.26 ||
toyaṃ kavitvamāpnoti sarvān kāmāṃstathaiva ca|
satataṃ kurute yastu mātṛkāmantramantritam|| 73.27 ||
toyapānaṃ mahābhāga pūrakumbhakarecakaiḥ|
sa sarvakāmān saṃprāpya putrapautrasamṛddhimān|| 73.28 ||
bhūtvā mahākavirloke balavān satyavikramaḥ|
sarvatra vallabho bhūtvā cānte mokṣamavāpnuyāt|| 73.29 ||
rājānamathavā rājaputraṃ bhāryāmathāpi vā|
vaśīkaroti nacirānmātṛkāmantrapānataḥ|| 73.30 ||
nyāsakrame kramaḥ prokto vargakrama ihaiva tu|
akṣarāṇāṃ krameṇātha toyapānaṃ samācaret|| 73.31 ||
ye ye mantrā devatānāmṛṣīṇāmatha rakṣasām|
te mantrā mātṛkāmantrai nityameva pratiṣṭhitāḥ|| 73.32 ||
sarvamantramayaścāyaṃ sarvavedamayastathā|
caturvargapradaścāyaṃ mātṛkāmantra ucyate|| 73.33 ||
iti te kathitaṃ putra mātṛkānyāsamadbhutam|
vibhāgamatha mudrāṇāṃ śṛṇu vetāla bhairava|| 73.34 ||
|| iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe mātṛkānyāsavarṇane trisaptatitamo’dhyāyaḥ|| 73 ||

<74>
|| catuḥsaptatitamo’dhyāyaḥ ||
||śrībhagavānuvāca||
yā yonimudrā kathitā mudrāvibhajane purā|
aṣṭadhā yonimudrā syāt prathamā sā tu kīrtitā|| 74.1 ||
dvitīyā khecarī mudrā kāmākhyāyāstu bhairava|
tāṃ viddhi cādbhutaṃ guhyaṃ yena tuṣyati caṇḍikā|| 74.2 ||
anāmikāṃ dakṣimasya tarjanyāṃ vāmato nyaset|
vāmānāmāṃ dakṣiṇasya tarjanyāṃ viniveśayet|| 74.3 ||
te dve tathā tarjanībhyāṃ veṣṭayedagrato’grataḥ|
madhye dvayaṃ tu vinyasya cordhvabhāge tvanāmayoḥ|| 74.4 ||
tadagrāgreṇa saṃyogāt tathaiva ca kaniṣṭhike|
agreṇaiva ca saṃyukte tanmūle’ṅguṣṭhake nyaset|| 74.5 ||
iyaṃ te khecarī yoniryonimudrā tu kāmadā|
eṣaivādhaḥ kaniṣṭhe dve niyojya yadi yujyate|| 74.6 ||
guhyayonistu sā khyātā kāmeśvaryāstu tuṣṭidā|
saṃveṣṭya pūrvavat pāṇyordve kaniṣṭhe tvanāmike|| 74.7 ||
adhobhāge niyojyātha madhyame cordhvatastathā|
tāsāṃ parasparaścāgrairanyo’nyaṃ yojayed yadi|| 74.8 ||
madhyāṃ madhye tathāṅguṣṭhe niḥkṣipyāgre niyojayet|
yonistriśāṅkarī proktā tripurā tuṣṭidā sadā|| 74.9 ||
madhye dve ca tathā veṣṭyā pūrvavaccāpyanāmikā|
kaniṣṭhābhyāṃ puro nyasya aṅguṣṭhau mūlayostayoḥ|| 74.10 ||
mudreyaṃ śāradī proktā śāradāyāstu tuṣṭidā|
mūlayonistu kathitā vaiṣmavītantragocare|| 74.11 ||
tarjanyanāmikaṃ madhye kaniṣṭhe’pi kramādapi|
karayoryojayitvaiva kaniṣṭhāmūladeśataḥ|| 74.12 ||
aṅguṣṭhāgraṃ tu niḥkṣipya mahāyoniḥ prakīrtitā|
aṅguṣṭhau cātha saṃveṣṭhya saṃyujyātha karāṅgulīḥ|| 74.13 ||
agrabhāgairmadhyaśūnyaṃ tatra kuryāt karadvayam|
iyaṃ tu yoginīyoniryoginīnāṃ priyaṃkarī|| 74.14 ||
etā aṣṭau samākhyātā yonyaḥ kāmeśvarīpriyāḥ|
mūrtibhedena cānyeṣāṃ devānāmapi tuṣṭidāḥ|| 74.15 ||
yātrāyāṃ yuddhaviṣaye vāgvāde kalahe tathā|
aṣṭau yonyaḥ smared yastu jayastasya sanātanaḥ|| 74.16 ||
visarjane pūjane ca smaraṇe karmabhedataḥ|
etā yonyaḥ samākhyātāścaṇḍikāpūjaneṣu ca|| 74.17 ||
etāstu kathitā yonyaḥ kramāt kramavisarjane|
rahasyaṃ vāmadākṣiṇyaṃ mantraśuddhiṃ śṛṇuṣva me|| 74.18 ||
mantreṇa kriyate yat tu śārīraṃ mantramuttamam|
tadrahasyamiti prāhurmantreṣu mantrakovidāḥ|| 74.19 ||
kāmākhyāyāstu ṣaṭkoṇaṃ maṇḍalasya dalāntare|
tridhā likhenmūlamantramūrdhvaṃ triṣvapi sandhiṣu|| 74.20 ||
adhastrisandhiṣu punarvidhiṃ śakraṃ haraṃ tathā|
sahitaṃ madanenaiva likhed bhūrjatvaci tridhā|| 74.21 ||
tantumādāya sāhasraṃ dakṣiṇena kareṇa vai|
mālāmapi sāmādāya saṃjapuduttarāmukhaḥ|| 74.22 ||
tadbhuje dakṣiṇe dhāryaṃ bāhau vā sādhakottamaiḥ|
japānte likhitaṃ yantraṃ tena sarvajayī bhavet|| 74.23 ||
dīrghāyuḥ sarvavaśakṛddhanadhānyasamṛddhimān|
mṛto devīgṛhe yāti yantrayantritabuddhimān|| 74.24 ||
ṣaṭkoṇānantarakṛtaṃ veṣṭitāṣṭadaleṣvatha|
likhitvā bhūrjapatreṣu vilīnairyāvakodakaiḥ|| 74.25 ||
uttarādikrameṇaiva vaiṣṇavītantrasaṅgatān|
aṣṭau varṇānmadhyabāge pūrvavat kāmarājakam|| 74.26 ||
trīn varṇān netrabījasya trikoṇasyāgrato likhet|
evaṃ tridhākṛtaṃ yantraṃ kṛtvā vāmakare sthitaḥ|| 74.27 ||
japet trīṇi sahasrāṇi mālāmādāya dakṣiṇe|
japānte vaiṣṇavīrūpadhyānaṃ kuryādatandritaḥ|| 74.28 ||
prāṇāyāmasahasraṃ tu tatastaṃ likhitottamam|
grīvāyāṃ dhārayed yantraṃ tena sarvajayī bhavet|| 74.29 ||
rājaputro bhavedrājā tadanyaḥ sacivo bhavet|
dvijarājo bhaved vidvān kavirvāgmī ca vā bhavet|| 74.30 ||
rākṣasebhyaḥ piśācebhyo bhūtebhyaścāpi cānyataḥ|
sādhu saṃvidyate tasya na kadācit parājayaḥ|| 74.31 ||
dīrghāyurbalavān prājño mṛte mokṣamavāpnuyāt|
sampūrṇaṃ maṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā aṣṭapatrasamanvitam|| 74.32 ||
bhūrjatvaci śrīphalasya niryāsaistasya madhyataḥ|
ṣaṭkoṇaṃ vilikhet tasya prāgagreṣvatha triṣvapi|| 74.33 ||
vilikhet tripurāvarmānadho bījaṃ tu netrakam|
daleṣvaṣṭāsu tu punarvaiṣṇavītantrasaṅgatān|| 74.34 ||
aṣṭau varṇāstu vilikhet tathā dvārṣu catuṣvaipi|
ṣaṭkoṇeṣūttarākoṇakrameṇaikāgramānasaḥ|| 74.35 ||
taddhṛtvā dakṣiṇakare vaiṣṇavītantramantrakam|
japet trirbhirdinairevāyutaṃ saṃyatamānasaḥ|| 74.36 ||
prāṇāyāmasahasrāṇi trīṇi kṛtvā tu harṣitaḥ|
sandhyākāle navamyāṃ tu śīrṣeṇa dhārayed budhaḥ|| 74.37 ||
śatāyuḥ sarvadamano matimān paṇḍitottamaḥ|
balavīryadhanaiśvaryuktaḥ pārthiva eva vā|| 74.38 ||
pratyakṣato mahāmāyāṃ kāmākhyāṃ tripurāmapi|
nityaṃ paśyati medhāvī mahocchavāsāṃ ca śāradām|| 74.39 ||
siṃhavyāghrau bhujaṅgo vā ye’nye vā tasya hiṃsakāḥ|
sarve tasya tanuṃ prāpya viṣīdanti na saṃśayaḥ|| 74.40 ||
jayaheturato’nyasmāt saṃgrāme śāstravādataḥ|
na vidyate tribhuvane tasmāt kuryāt tu yantrakam|| 74.41 ||
ante devīgṛhaṃ prāpya tato mokṣamavāpnuyāt|
mahāmāyā śāradākhyā kāmākhyā tripurā tathā|| 74.42 ||
mahotsāhā tathaiteṣāṃ mantrāṇāṃ yo gaṇo bhavet|
maṇḍalaṃ cāṣṭadalakaṃ tanmadhye vilikhet punaḥ|| 74.43 ||
likhitvā pūrvavat pūrvaṃ proktaṃ mantragaṇaṃ samam|
anyadvayaṃ dvāradeśe koṣṭheṣvakṣarato likhet|| 74.44 ||
śuklakauśaiyavastreṣu rasairvahniśikhasya tu|
uttarīyaṃ tu tadvastraṃ kṛtvā japyaṃ samācaret|| 74.45 ||
kṛtopavāsaḥ śuddhaśca mātṛkānyāsapūrvakam|
pañcānāmapi vargāṇāṃ sahasrāṇi tu paṃca vai|| 74.46 ||
divasaiḥ pañcabhirjaptvā tadante ca samācaret|
prāṇāyāmasahasrāṇi paṃca vai paṃcabhirdinaiḥ|| 74.47 ||
ante tu kavacanyāsaṃ kātyāyanyāḥ samācaret|
tatastu mātṛkāmantraiḥ śvāsarodhanapūrvakam|| 74.48 ||
triḥ pibet kapilākṣīraṃ jāgṛvāṃśca tadā niśi|
evaṃ yaḥ kurute yantraṃ śarīre śuklavāsasā|| 74.49 ||
so’tra siddhimavāpnoti devīlokaṃ ca gacchati|
ya uttarīyaṃ bibhṛyād vastraṃ mantreṇa mantritam|| 74.50 ||
nityameva mahābhāga prabhāvaṃ tasya vai śṛṇu|
na tasya dehe śastrāṇi pravekṣyanti kadācana|| 74.51 ||
nāgnirdahati tatkāyaṃ nāpaḥ saṃkledayanti ca|
rākṣasāśca piśācāśca bhūtādyā ye tu hiṃsakāḥ|| 74.52 ||
te taṃ dṛṣṭvā mahābhāgaṃ bhuvaṃ gacchanti vai bhiyā|
gacchedavāritaḥ so’pi sarvatra sādhakottamaḥ|| 74.53 ||
vaśīkaroti devāṃśca nṛpānanyāśca yoṣitaḥ|
utsahet yadi medhāvī vāgmī rājā ca vai bhavet|| 74.54 ||
cirajīvī mahābhāgo dhanadhānyasamṛddhimān|
kaviḥ prajñāsamāyuktaḥ so’bhedyo jāyate’ribhiḥ|| 74.55 ||
yasmin pure sa nivased vajrapāto na tatra vai|
rasaḥ śarīraṃ śastrāṇi dṛḍhahastojjhitānyapi|| 74.56 ||
etaṃ na ghnanti satataṃ jayaḥ sarvatra bhairava|
aparādhyanti satataṃ tasya sarvatra bhairava|| 74.57 ||
nādhayo vyādhayastasya jāyante tu kadācana|
devīputraḥ sa matimān mṛto mokṣamavāpnuyāt|| 74.58 ||
yantratā svāminā yantraṃ yā dadhāti pativratā|
putraiśvaryamavāpnoti dīrghāyuḥ sā vadhūrbhavet|| 74.59 ||
pratyekamekaṃ saṃhatyāvardhanāsahitena ca|
kramād viṃśatiyantrāṇi kathitāni mayeha vai|| 74.60 ||
tāni pratyekato buddhvā yo nyaset sarvadā hṛdi|
likhitvā sarvayantrāṇi bibhṛyādyo’tha vā gale|| 74.61 ||
devendro jāyate so’tra prabhāveṇeha bhūtale|
pūrvoktāni samastāni phalānyāpnoti tatkṣaṇāt|
pihitaḥ sarvalokāṃstronnītyameva prapaśyati|| 74.62 ||
evaṃ sārdhaṃ yantravargaiḥ samastairaṣṭābhiryat pūrvamuktaṃ sahasram|
śukle vastre saṃlikhitvā svadehe dhṛtvā nitya prāpnuyād vai samastam|| 74.63 ||
yaḥ kṣatrajātirhṛdaye sa kuryāt saṃgrāmakāle kavaceṣṭadhāmni|
mantrākṣarāṇyādikṛtāni devyā aṣṭau bahirgātraviśeṣataśca|| 74.64 ||
gale hari vakṣasi vai likhed vidhiṃ stanadvaye putrayutaṃ maheśvaram bāhvaṃgasandhyośca haraṃ ca vaiṣṇavīṃ bāhvostu lakṣmīṃ ca sarasvatīṃ ca ||75.65||
evaṃ raṇāṣṭāṅgamidaṃ vidhāya gātre savarmaṇyanucinatayecchivām|
likhellalāṭe tilakāntare naraḥ samastamantrākṣarayantramuttamam|| 74.66 ||
tato japedaṣṭadhā tu pāṇiṃ dattvāṣṭadhāmasu ca|
vaiṣṇavītantramantraṃ tu tato gacchedraṇājiram|| 74.67 ||
sa tu vīro mama samaḥ saṃgrāmeṣu ca jāyate|
tṛṇānīva parāstrāṇi jāyante’gnau tathātmani|| 74.68 ||
viniḥsaranti ripavo yācakā dhanino dhanam|
siṃhāgryānnaraśārdūlo vīryavān balavān bhavet|| 74.69 ||
idaṃ rahasyaṃ kathitaṃ kāmākhyāyāstu bhairava|
vaiṣṇavyāstantramukhyeṣu tripurāyāstataḥ śuṇuḥ|| 74.70 ||
tasyāstu sarvamantrāṇi trayodaśayutāni vai|
viṃśatiṃ tu sahasrāṇāṃ tatrādyaṃ vāgbhavaṃ smṛtam|| 74.71 ||
dvitīyaṃ kāmarājākhyaṃ mohanaṃ ca tṛtīyakam|
āmreḍitaṃ vāgbhavaṃ tu caturthaṃ parikīrtitam|| 74.72 ||
netrabījaṃ dvitīyaṃ tu dviruktaṃ vāgbhavaṃ tathā|
ādyaṃ tatpaṃcamaṃ proktaṃ caturbhirapi cākṣaraiḥ|| 74.73 ||
netrabījaṃ dvitīyaṃ tu prathamaṃ parikīrtitam|
dvitīyaṃ kāmabījaṃ tu tṛtīyaṃ vāgbhavaṃ tathā|| 74.74 ||
ebhistribhistu yanmantraṃ tat ṣaṣṭhaṃ parikīrtitam|
netrabījaṃ dvitīyaṃ tu vāgbhavaṃ tena saptamam|| 74.75 ||
tadevaṃ vāgbhavādyaṃ tu aṣṭamaṃ parikīrtitam|
vāgbhavaṃ kāmabījaṃ tu netrābhyāṃ navamaṃ smṛtam|| 74.76 ||
kāmabījaṃ tathaivādyaṃ daśamaṃ caiva mohanam|
ekādaśamidaṃ proktaṃ ḍāmarādyaṃ tu vāgbhavam|| 74.77 ||
dvādaśaṃ kīrtitaṃ mantraṃ śeṣatastraipuraṃ mahaḥ|
tanmahastraipuraṃ mantraṃ śṛṇuṣvaikamanāstvidam|| 74.78 ||
prāntādistasya cāpyādirvahnirvāgbhavasandhitaḥ|
ādyaṃ tripurabhairavyā bījamādyaṃ prakīrtitam|| 74.79 ||
upāntaśca tadādiśca vahniśeṣasvarastathā|
caturthasvarabindvinduyutāścaitat dvitīyakam|| 74.80 ||
upāntaśca tadādiśca vahniśeṣasvarastathā|
samāptirbindusahitā sahitastu tṛtīyakaḥ|| 74.81 ||
etat tattvaṃ vijānāti yo naro bhuvi bhūmaṇiḥ|
siddavidyādharebhyastu so’dhikastatsamo bhavet|| 74.82 ||
ete trayodaśa proktā mantrā mantreṣu cojjvalāḥ|
viṃśatestu sahasrebhyaḥ parāścaite prakīrtitāḥ|| 74.83 ||
viṃśatestu sahasrāṇāmādyametat prakīrtitam|
tripurāyāstu bālāyā mantraṃ tacchṛṇu bhairava|| 74.84 ||
vāgbhavaṃ kāmarājastu upāntādiḥ sabindukaḥ|
śeṣasvarasamāptibhyāṃ mantrametat prakīrtitam|| 74.85 ||
eṣā tu tripurā bālā madhyā proktā puraiva hi|
śeṣā tejasvinī proktā yeyaṃ tripurabhairavī|| 74.86 ||
madhyāyāḥ pūjanaṃ proktaṃ bālāyāḥ śṛṇu sāmpratam|
tathā tripurabhairavyāḥ sarvasiddhipradāyakam|| 74.87 ||
vibhidya śaktyā śambhuṃ tu śaktiṃ cāpi vibhedayet|
śambhave varṇaṣaṭkoṇaṃ keśaraṃ tatra saṃlikhet|| 74.88 ||
madhyāyāstripurāyāstu yādṛśe dvāramaṇḍale|
tādṛśe’trāpi kartavyaṃ koṇeṣu likhitaṃ tathā|| 74.89 ||
pāpotsāraṇakarmāṇi bhūmyādīnāṃ viśodhanam|
pūrvamuttaratantroktaṃ tripurāpīṭhabhāṣitam|| 74.90 ||
kāmākhyāpūjane proktaṃ sarvaṃ kuryāt tu sādhakaḥ|
dahanaplavanādīni pratipattiṃ ca pātrake|| 74.91 ||
sarvaṃ tu pūrvavat kāryaṃ kāmākhayāpūjane yathā|
kṛtvā’tra dehanyāsaṃ tu mantravarṇaistathākṣaraiḥ|| 74.92 ||
sarvaiḥ svaraistathā kādyaistato rūpaṃ vicintayet|
caturbhujāṃ raktavarṇāṃ raktavastravibhūṣitām|| 74.93 ||
dakṣiṇordhve srajaṃ cādho bibhratīṃ pustakottamam|
abhayaṃ vāmahastābhyāṃ varaṃ ca dadhatīṃ tathā|| 74.94 ||
sahasrasūryasaṃkāśāṃ trinetrāṃ gajagāminīm|
pīnatuṅgastanayugaṃ sitapretāsanasthitām|| 74.95 ||
smitaprasannavadanāṃ sarvālaṃkārasaṃyutām|
tisṛbhirmuṇḍamālābhiḥ śirovakṣaḥ kaṭīṣu ce|| 74.96 ||
triguṇāṃ triguṇībhūtaiḥ pratyekama paribhūṣitām|
madirāghūrṇanayānāṃ raktadantatacchadadvayām|| 74.97 ||
cintayed varadāṃ devīmevaṃ tripurabhairavīm|
bālāyāstripurāyāstu rūpaṃ pūrvaṃ prapūjane|| 74.98 ||
uktaḥ kramaḥ pīṭhayoge tantrādi śṛṇu bhairava|
puṣpabāṇāṃstu pāśaṃ ca dhatte pauṣpaṃ śarāsanam|| 74.99 ||
pāśaṃ ca kuṇapārūḍhā sā bālā tripurā smṛtā|
manmathe tripure devīṃ vidyahe padamāditaḥ|| 74.100 ||
kāmeśvarīṃ dhīmahi tvāṃ tannaḥ klinne pracodayāt|
eṣā tripuragāyatrītyāvāhanaviśeṣataḥ|| 74.101 ||
snānādyaiḥ pūjayet samyag bālāmanyāṃ ca bhairavīm|
asyāḥ krame viśeṣo yo nyāse cottarakarmaṇi|| 74.102 ||
tatsarvaṃ saha mantraighaiḥ śṛṇu vetāla bhairava|
brāhme muhūrte utthāya cintayet paramaṃ gurum|| 74.103 ||
tato’nu svaguruṃ śuddhaṃ tatastripurabhairavīm|
caturbhujāṃ śuklavarṇāṃ varadābhayapustakām|| 74.104 ||
akṣamālāṃ ca kramato dhatte vāme ca dakṣiṇe|
suvarṇaratnakhacite saṃsthitāṃ pravarāsane|| 74.105 ||
sauvarṇamuttarīyaṃ tu dhatte sauvarṇakuṇle|
svaguruṃ varṇato dhyānāt tathaiva paricinyate|| 74.106 ||
bhairavīṃ cintayitvā tu tata utthāya cācaret|
maitramācamanaṃ caiva dantānāṃ śodhanaṃ tathā|| 74.107 ||
prātaḥsnānaṃ tataḥ kuryāt traipuraṃ yojayan kramam|
sarvatra devīmantreṣu vaidikeṣvapi bhairavīm|| 74.108 ||
tripurāṃ cintayennityaṃ devamantreṣu ca kramāt|
tribhistu tripurābījaistridhā majjanamācaret|| 74.109 ||
devānāmapi sarveṣu bhairaveṣu padaṃ sadā|
kuryād viśeṣaṇaṃ nityaṃ noccāryaṃ nirviśeṣaṇam|| 74.110 ||
āpaḥ punantu pṛthivīmuktvā tripurabhairavīm|
kuryādācamanaṃ vipro drupadāyāṃ tathācaret|| 74.111 ||
idaṃ viṣṇurbhairavastu vicakrama itīritam|
mṛdālambhanakṛtyeṣu nityamevāpyudīrayet|| 74.112 ||
gāyatrīṃ tripurādyāṃ tu bhairavīmāhvayecchivām|
mārtaṇḍabhairavāyeti sūryāyārghyaṃ nivedayet|| 74.113 ||
udtyaṃ jātavedasaṃ devaṃ vahanti ketavaḥ|
dṛśe viśvāya sūryaṃ śeṣe bhairavamīrayet|| 74.114 ||
tarpaṇādau prayuṃjīta tṛpyatāṃ brahmabhairavaḥ|
āvāhane svayaṃ pitṛn bhairavāniti kīrtayet|| 74.115 ||
tṛpyatāṃ bhairavīmātaḥ pitarbhairava tṛpyatām|
ādau ca tripurāpūrvaṃ tarpaṇe’pi prayojayet|| 74.116 ||
jyotiṣṭomāśvamedhādau yatra yaṃ yaṃ prapūjayet|
tatra bhairavarūpeṇa devīmapi ca bhairavīm|| 74.117 ||
madirāpātramālokya raktavastrāṃ striyaṃ tathā|
śiro narasya dṛṣṭvā tu bhairavīṃ cintayed dvijaḥ|| 74.118 ||
striyo dṛṣṭvā hyathaikatra yuvatīḥ sumanoharāḥ|
tābhyastripurabhairavyāḥ prītaye vandanādikam|| 74.119 ||
dadyād bhaktyā tu manasā cintayannatha bhairavīm|
bhairavīṃ pratigṛhṇāmi bhairavo’haṃ pratigrahī|| 74.120 ||
kanyāyāṃ bhāvayed dhīmāṃstripurāyāḥ prapūjakaḥ|
bhairavāya dadāmyadya devīṃ tripurabhairavīm|| 74.121 ||
itīrayet pradānaṃ tu kanyāyāstripurāṃ tataḥ|
tasyāḥ pūjopakaraṇapātrādi nānyapūjane|| 74.122 ||
āsavādyaṃ ca satataṃ nopayojyaṃ kadācana|
sakṛt tu dāpayedanyairmadirāṃ sādhako dvijaḥ|| 74.123 ||
śūdrādayastu satataṃ dadayurāsavamuttamam|
evaṃ tu vāmabhāvena yajet tripurabhairavīm|| 74.124 ||
bālāṃ tu vāmadākṣiṇyamārgābhyāmapi pūjayet|
śmaśānabhairavīṃ devīmugratārāṃ tathaiva ca|| 74.125 ||
ucciṣṭabhairavīṃ jaṇḍīṃ tathā tripurabhairavīm|
etāstu vāmabhāvena pūjyā dakṣiṇatāṃ vinā|| 74.126 ||
ṛṣīn devān pitṛścaiva manuṣyān bhutasañcayān|
yajet paṃcabhiryajñairṛṇāni pariśodhayet|| 74.127 ||
vidhivat snānadānābhyāṃ kurvan yadvidhipūjanam|
kriyate sarahasyaṃ tu taddākṣiṇyamihocyate|| 74.128 ||
sarve ca pitṛdevādau yasmād bhavati dakṣiṇaḥ|
devī ca dakṣiṇā yasmāt tasmād dakṣiṇa ucyate|| 74.129 ||
yā punaḥ pūjyamānā tu devādīnāṃ ca pūrvataḥ|
yajñabhāgaṃ svayaṃ dhatte sāvāmā tu prakīrtitā|| 74.130 ||
pūjako’pi bhaved vāmastatraiva satataṃ suta|
paṃcayajñān na vā kuryād yad vāmyaprapūjane|| 74.131 ||
anyasya pūjābhāgaṃ hi yato gṛhṇāti vāpikā|
yatpūjayedvāmabhāvairna tat syādṛṇaśodhanam|| 74.132 ||
pitṛdevanarādīnāṃ jāyate ca kadācana|
so’bhyasya tripurāyogaṃ tena yogena saṃyutaḥ|| 74.133 ||
jāyate yadi suprājñastadā mokṣamavāpnuyāt|
sa ca mokṣaścireṇaiva jāyate’tra punaḥ punaḥ|| 74.134 ||
ṛṇāśodhanajaiḥ pāpairākrāntaścaiva bhairava|
iha loke sukhaśvairyayuktaḥ sarvatra vallabhaḥ|| 74.135 ||
madanomakāntena śarīrema virājatā|
sarāṣṭrakaṃ ca rājānaṃ vaśīkṛtya samantataḥ|| 74.136 ||
mohayan vanitāḥ sarvāḥ sarvāśca madavihvalāḥ|
siṃhān vyāghrān starakṣūṃśca bhatapretapiśācakān|| 74.137 ||
vaśīkurvan vicarati vāyuvegodyatastataḥ|
bālāṃ vā tripurāṃ devīṃ madhyāṃ vāpyatha bhairavīm|| 74.138 ||
yo yajet parayā bhaktyā yaśca bāṇopamākṛtiḥ|
kāmeśvarīṃ tu mākākyāṃ pūjayet tu yathecchayā|| 74.139 ||
dākṣiṇyād vāmabhāvād vā sarvathā siddhimāpnuyāt|
mahāmāyāṃ śāradāṃ ca śailaputrīṃ tathaiva ca|| 74.140 ||
yathā yathā prakārema dākṣiṇyādeva pūjayet|
yo dākṣiṇyaṃ vinā bhāvaṃ mahāmāyāṃ samarcati|| 74.141 ||
sa pāpaḥ svargalokebhyaścyuto bhavati rogadhṛk|
anyāstu śivadūtyādyā devyo yāḥ pūrvamīritāḥ|| 74.142 ||
tāstu vāṃ pāntu dākṣiṇyāt pūjitavyāstu sādhakaiḥ|
kintu yaḥ pajako vāmaḥ so’nyāsāṃ parivarjitaḥ|| 74.143 ||
sarvāsāṃ pūjakaḥ syāt tu dakṣiṇastena uttamaḥ|
atha tripurabhairavyā nyāsaṃ ca śṛṇu bhairava|| 74.144 ||
yena vai nyāsamātreṇa devavajjāyate naraḥ|
bhairavītantramantrasya ṛṣirdakṣima ucyate|| 74.145 ||
chandaḥ paṃktiḥ samākhyātā devī tripurabhairavī|
kāmārthayoḥ sādhane ca viniyogaḥ prakīrtitaḥ|| 74.146 ||
hakāraṃ vinyasennābhau sakāraṃ vastito nyaset|
vakāraṃ śephe vinyasya ekāraṃ ca gude tathā|| 74.147 ||
punarūrvostathaivādyaṃ jānuyugme dvitīyakam|
tṛtīyaṃ jaṅghayornyasya caturtha pādayornyaset|| 74.148 ||
trivudhaṃ vinyased devaṃ nābhyādeḥ pādasaṅgatam|
dvitīyasya tu bījasya ādyaṃ tṛtīyaṃ hyadyeva vinyaset|| 74.149 ||
vāme stane dvītīyaṃ tu tṛtīyaṃ dakṣima stane|
caturthamudare nyasya paṃcamaṃ pārśvayornyaset|| 74.150 ||
ṣaṣṭhaṃ nābhau parinyasya nyaseccāpi tridhā tridhā|
tṛtīyasya tu bījasya mūrdhni cādyaṃ tu vinyaset|| 74.151 ||
dvitīyaṃ nyasya keśānte tṛtīyaṃ vadane nyaset|
caturthaṃ hṛdaye nyasya yathā syāt tu tridhā tridhā|| 74.152 ||
ādyādyaṃ dakṣiṇāṅguṣṭhe dvitīyaṃ tarjanīṃ punaḥ|
tṛtīyaṃ ca madhyamāyāmanāmāyāṃ caturthakam|| 74.153 ||
dvitīyādyaṃ kaniṣṭhāyāṃ vāmāṅguṣṭhe dvitīyakam|
tṛtīyaṃ vāmatarjanyāṃ caturthaṃ madhyamātanau|| 74.154 ||
ānāmāyāṃ paṃcamaṃ tu ṣaṣṭhaṃ śeṣe tu vinyaset|
evaṃ tridhā tu vinyasya dvitīyamatha bījakam|| 74.155 ||
ubhayorhastayoḥ kṛtvā aṅguṣṭhādyaṃ yugaṃ yugam|
tṛtīyaṃ bījavarṇāṃstu vinyaset kramato budhaḥ|| 74.156 ||
piṇḍitaṃ sarvabījaṃ tu vinyaset tu kaniṣṭhayoḥ|
ādya tu talayornyasya pṛṣṭhayośca dvitīyakam|| 74.157 ||
tālatrayaṃ tato dattvā tṛtīyena tu veṣṭanam|
karṇayościbuke gaṇḍe mukhe dṛṅnāsayostathā|| 74.158 ||
skandhayośca kaphoṇau ca jaṭhare śiśnamūrdhani|
pādayoḥ pārśvayoścaiva hṛdaye stanayugmake|| 74.159 ||
kaṇṭhadeśe ca nyastavyā mantravarṇakramāt punaḥ|
liṅge ratyai nama iti vāgbhavādyena vinyaset|| 74.160 ||
oṃ klīṃ prītyai nama iti hṛdaye vinyaset tataḥ|
manobhavāyeti tato bhruvormadhye tṛtīyakam|| 74.161 ||
vinyaset tripurābījaṃ sadyo devatvasiddhaye|
oṃ īṃ īśānarūpāya tato manobhavāya vai|| 74.162 ||
nama ityantataḥ prokto mūrdhnīśānaṃ nyaset punaḥ|
vaktre tatpuruṣaṃ cāpi bījena makaradhvajam|| 74.163 ||
hṛdaye ghorakandarpamādyabījena vai nyaset|
śiśne vā vāmadevaṃ tu manmathaṃ cāpi vinyaset|| 74.164 ||
sadyojātaṃ pādadvaye kāmadevaṃ ca vinyaset|
oṃkāraṃ ca hakāraṃ ca rephamekatra sandhitam|| 74.165 ||
prāntasvaraṃ vāgbhavādyaṃ svarairhrasvaistu pañcabhiḥ|
ebhistu pañcabhirmantrairīśanādīni vinyaset|| 74.166 ||
vaktrāṇi pūrvamuktāni svamukhordhve tu pūrvataḥ|
dakṣiṇottarayoḥ paścāt paścime cāpi vinyaset|| 74.167 ||
hṛdayādiṣaḍaṅgāni dīrghairādyasvaraiḥ punaḥ|
nyaset tataḥ pñabāṇān mūrdhādiṣvatha vinyaset|| 74.168 ||
oṃ hrīṃ klīṃ sauṃ drāvaṇāya nyasenyamūrdhni tataḥ punaḥ|
oṃ hrīṃ kṣobhaṇabāṇāya padbhyāṃ nama itīrayet|| 74.169 ||
oṃ klīṃ klīṃ hrīṃ samāpyantu ṣaṭkārāntārdhacandrakaiḥ|
vaktrevaśīkṛtaṃ liṅge sammohanamatho nyaset|| 74.170 ||
ākarṣaṇaṃ tathā bāṇaṃ hṛdi mantraiḥ kramānnyaset|
vāgbhavādyantakārānto vaṣaṭkārasamanvitaḥ|| 74.171 ||
triḥśeṣasvara evātra candrārdho bindusaṃyutaḥ|
ebhistu pañcabhirmantrairaṣṭaśaktīḥ kramādimāḥ|| 74.172 ||
eteṣu cāṣṭasthāneṣu vinyasenmantravit punaḥ|
subhagāṃ ca bhagāṃ devīṃ tṛtīyāṃ bhagarūpiṇīm|| 74.173 ||
bhagamālāṃ caturthīṃ tu anaṅgakusumāṃ tataḥ|
anaṅgamekhalāṃ paścādanaṅgamadanāṃ tathā|| 74.174 ||
aṣṭamīṃ ca tathā devīṃ madavibhramamantharām|
rūpato dhyānataścaitā yathā tripurabhairavī|| 74.175 ||
lalāṭabhrūmadhyabhāgamukharṇāntakaṇṭhake|
hṛnnābhiliṃgeṣvevātra nyastavyā aṣṭaśaktayaḥ|| 74.176 ||
śirolalāṭabhrūyugmakarṇanetradvayeṣu ca|
gaṇḍayoratha nāsāyāṃ dantavīthyāṃ mukhe tathā|| 74.177 ||
caturdaśapadeṣveṣu nyaseccaturdaśasvarān|
cibuke tvatha grīvāyāṃ kaṇṭhedeśe tu pārśvayoḥ|| 74.178 ||
stanayoḥ kakṣayoścāpi kaphoṇyorhastayostathā|
tat pṛṣṭhayostathā nābhau liṅge corudvaye tathā|| 74.179 ||
aṣṭhīvatorjaṅghayostu sphicostu padamūlayoḥ|
caraṇāṅguṣṭhayoḥ kādimātrān varṇāstu vinyaset|| 74.180 ||
mekhalāyāṃ kaṇṭhadeśa bāhubhūṣaṇabhāgataḥ|
hāre sraji kuṇḍale ca keśabandhe tathaiva ca|| 74.181 ||
cūḍāmaṇau ca nyastavyā yakārādyāḥ kramātpunaḥ|
mantrākṣarāṇi trīṇyeva sandhitāni punastathā|| 74.182 ||
prātilomyena vinyasya mantraimūrdhni tridhā tridhā|
amṛtāṃ yoginīṃ viśvayoginīṃ cākṣarakramāt|| 74.183 ||
tato bījatryakṣarāṇi mūrdhni bāhau tathā hṛdi|
vinyasya pūrvavat pūjāmārabhenmantravid budhaḥ|| 74.184 ||
pūrvavat pūjayed devīṃ pīṭhadevavivarjitām|
viśeṣato hyaṣṭaśaktī kramāt tu suvabhagādikāḥ|| 74.185 ||
maṇḍalasyāṣṭadigbhāge pūrvādau paricintayet|
trikoṇāgre’mṛtādyāstu sampūjyāstu trinonayaḥ|| 74.186 ||
madhye’ṣṭabhūṣaṇānyeva pūjayet tu tataḥ punaḥ|
īśānādīni vaktrāṇi mama bhairava madhyataḥ|| 74.187 ||
pūjayet tu tathā tatra manobhavamukhānapi|
anyacca pūjane tatra kramaḥ pūrvoditaśca yaḥ|| 74.188 ||
sa eva satataṃ grāhyaḥ tripurāparipūjane|
nirmālyadhāriṇī devī caitasyāḥ śuṇu bhairavī|| 74.189 ||
visarjanaṃ cottarasyāṃ tyaktvā nirmālyamācaret|
trimūrti pūjayet tāṃ tu devīṃ tripurabhairavīm|| 74.190 ||
na japet triṃśatā nyūnaṃ sādhakastu kadācana|
aṅguṣṭhamadhyamānāmāṅgulībhistasṛbhiḥ punaḥ|
sadā puṣpādikaṃ dadyānmālāṃ tu triguṇāṃ caret|| 74.191 ||
carmāsanamadhiṣṭhāya paścāt kṛtvā padadvayam|| 74.192 ||
pūjayennirjane deśe sādhako’nanyamānasaḥ|
āsādayet tu puṣpādi naibedyādi ca yad bhavet|
tad vāmahastamukhyena satataṃ sādhako budhaḥ|| 74.193 ||
tricchidrā tripurā proktā na samyakpūjitā yadi|| 74.194 ||
śarīre nindito vyādhirjāyate’vaśyameva hi|
avaśyāḥ putradārāśca bhṛtyādyāśca bhavanti hi|| 74.195 ||
astrāghāto bhavet svasya prāṇatyāgo na saṃśayaḥ|
tricchidradāyinī caivamanyathā pūjitā yadi|| 74.196 ||
itaḥ prakārāṃ satataṃ samyag vetāla bhairava|
