eṣā ca tripurādevī yāścānyāḥ pūrvabhāṣitāḥ|| 74.197 ||
sarvāstu māyā bhairavyā yoganidrā jagatprasūḥ|
tasyāḥ prapaṃcarūpaistu bahubhiḥ saiva krīḍati|| 74.198 ||
mahāmāyā mūlabhūtā tatastu śāradā purā|
umā tataḥ śailaputrī matpriyāyāstatastvimāḥ|| 74.199 ||
ugracaṇḍā pracaṇḍādyāstriṣurādyāstathaiva ca|
tāsāṃ cāpi sadaivāhaṃ mahābhairavarūpadhṛk|| 74.200 ||
nāyakaḥ sutarāṃ tābhirnityaṃ nityaṃ vased budhaḥ|
mama bhairavarūpasya mantraḥ pūrvaṃ mayoditaḥ|| 74.201 ||
rūpaṃ coktaṃ pūjaneṣu tripurāyāḥ kramaḥ smṛtaḥ|
mahābhairavaṃ vidmahe kālarudrāya dhīmahi|| 74.202 ||
tannaḥ kāmo bhairavastu kledin nityaṃ pracodayāt|
eṣā bhairavarūpasya gāyatrī me pratiṣṭhitā|| 74.203 ||
yatheṣṭhamāṃsamadyādi bhojanārthaṃ mayā dhṛtaḥ|
mahābhairavakāyo’yaṃ tathā strīratisaṅgame|| 74.204 ||
ayaṃ tu vāmyabhāvena pūjyo madyādibhiḥ sadā|
vāmaḥ kāyo brahmaṇo’pi māṃsamadyādibhuktaye|| 74.205 ||
kṛto mahāmohanāmā cārvākādipravartakaḥ|
viṣṇorvāmātmikā mūrtirnarasiṃhāhvayā bhavet|| 74.206 ||
sā tu dākṣiṇyavāmābhyāṃ pūjanīyā sadā budhaiḥ|
tathaiva bālagopālamūrtirjarāyuveṣṭitā|| 74.207 ||
madyamāṃsāśano bhogī lolupaḥ strīṣu sarvadā|
vahvyastu caṇḍikādevyāḥ vāmikā mūrtayaḥ smṛtāḥ|| 74.208 ||
lakṣmyāstu vāmikāmūrtiruktā dahanabhairavī|
yāgnidāhaṃ puragrāmamandireṣvakarodiyam|
apūjitā mahālakṣmīrdehalyāṃ tāṃ tu pūjayet|| 74.209 ||
vāgbhairavī sarasvatyā vāmikāmūrtirīritā|| 74.210 ||
tasyā mantraṃ purā proktaṃ śuklavarṇā tu sā smṛtā|
madhyāyāstripurāyāstu rūpaṃ dhyānamihocyate|| 74.211 ||
pūjākramastathaivoktaḥ sarvatraiva tu bhairava|
mārtaṇḍabhairavo nāma mūrtiḥ sūryasya kīrtitā|| 74.212 ||
gaṇeśasyāgnivetālaḥ kathito vāmanāmakaḥ|
ete vāmyena bhāvena pūjanīyā viśeṣataḥ|| 74.213 ||
tridhādyastu yathāpūrvaṃ namayairralavaistathā|
vāntairdvirephaiḥ sarvatra yathā kṛtvā tathā tathā|| 74.214 ||
anusvāravisargābhyāṃ prākśeṣau parikīrtitau|
madhye tu kevalāḥ pūrvaṃ sānusvāravisṛṣṭibhiḥ|| 74.215 ||
paścād dvitrikramād yastu varṇairekena caiva hi|
vyastaiḥ samastairapi ca dakārādiṣu saṃyutaiḥ|| 74.216 ||
ādyāyāstripurāyāstu mantravad yojitaistathā|
tathā tripurabhairavyā mantravaccākṣarairapi|| 74.217 ||
triścaturdaśabhiḥ kṛtvā ḍādīṃstrīṃstu viśārayet|
dvitīyaṃ dviguṇaṃ kṛtvā śeṣe’trādau ca yojayet|| 74.218 ||
viṃśatistu sahasrāṇi śeṣe cāpi trayodaśa|
ādyamādyaṃ tataḥ proktaṃ vāgbhavādyaṃ tṛtīyakam|| 74.219 ||
evaṃ ca paramapyetanmantrāṇāṃ ca catuṣṭayam|
etajjñātvā naraḥ kāmānakhilān prāpya saṅgataḥ|| 74.220 ||
mṛte devīpuraṃ yāti kramādeva tu bhairava|
yaḥ sakṛt tu japedetat sakalaṃ mantrasañcayam|| 74.221 ||
prathamaṃ kāmato nyasya sādhakastu tribhirdinaiḥ|
cintayanmanasā devīṃ samyak tripurabhairavīm|| 74.222 ||
sa kāmānakhilān prāpya svarūpe madanopamaḥ|
dhārmiko nṛpatirbhūyād brāhmaṇo dvijarāḍ bhavet|| 74.223 ||
avādhitaśarīrastu piśācādyaiḥ sadaiva hi|
nīrogaśca cirāyuśca valavānapi jāyate|| 74.224 ||
evaṃ tripurabhairavyā mayā proktastvayaṃ kramaḥ|
vaiṣṇavyāstu mahādevyāḥ sahasrāmi tu ṣoḍaśa|| 74.225 ||
śṛṇu bhairava mantrāṇi śivaikāgramanāḥ punaḥ|
aṣṭottarasahasraṃ tu catuḥṣaṣṭistathā trayaḥ|| 74.226 ||
mantrāḥ proktā mahādevyā mūrtibhedena tāḥ punaḥ|
anusvāravisargābhyāṃ dviguṇāste punaḥ samāḥ|| 74.227 ||
kādivyañjanasaṃyogādūrdhvādho vyastabhāvataḥ|
dvābhyāṃ tribhiśca satatamuddharenmantravit punaḥ|| 74.228 ||
aṣṭāvaṣṭau tataḥ kṛtvā samastavyastasaṃyutaiḥ|
visvaraiḥ sasvaraiścāpi sānusvāravisargakaiḥ|| 74.229 ||
kevalairapi tatraiva dviyastairantaraistathā|
evamaṣṭottaraṃ yāvat saṃyogayogabhāvataḥ|| 74.230 ||
devyāstu ṣaṭsahasrāṇi sahasrāṇi tathā daśa|
mantrāstu saṃkhyayā khyātāḥ kramād vetāla bhairava|| 74.231 ||
samastavyastarūpema vaiṣṇavyā ye mayoditāḥ|
tāñ jñātvā mānavo yāti mamaiva sadanaṃ prati|| 74.232 ||
aṣṭamyāṃ ca navamyāṃ ca sahasrāṇi tu ṣoḍaśa|
yo japenmantrabījāni sakṛdeva tu bhairava|| 74.233 ||
dhyāyaṃstu vaiṣṇavīṃ mūrtiṃ tadekāgramanāḥ śṛṇu|
nararājo bhaved bhūmau paṇḍitaścātiharṣitaḥ|
cirāyuḥ sukhabhoghī syādudrikto balavāhanaiḥ|| 74.234 ||
tānyeva cāṣṭadhā japtvā sārvabhaumo nṛpo bhavet|
gaṇādhyakṣo mṛte sa syāt tato muktimavāpnuyāt|| 74.235 ||
iti sakalaguṇaughairastadoṣastu nityaṃ bhavati kaluṣahantā śrīvivṛddhyai sumantraḥ|
satatamakhilavettā yo bhavedetayostu sa ca bhavati jitārī rogaśokapramuktaḥ|| 74.236 ||
|| iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe tripurabhairavībālātridhākalpe catuḥsaptatitamo’dhyāyaḥ || 74 ||

<75>
|| pañcasaptatitamo’dhyāyaḥ ||
||śrībhagavānuvāca||
niṣpallavodvādaśabhirlakṣairmantrajapaistathā|
puraścaret sādhakastu kāmamiṣṭāptihetave|| 75.1 ||
jātīpuṣpaṃ ca bakulaṃ mālatīpuṣpameva ca|
nandyāvartaṃ pāṭalaṃ ca sitapadmamataḥ param|| 75.2 ||
ājyamannaṃ pāyasaṃ ca dadhikṣīraṃ tathā madhu|
lājāścāpi sakarpūrā amī eva caturdaśa|| 75.3 ||
puraścaraṇasambhūtā tripurāyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ|
dvādaśaṣveva lakṣeṣu japteṣvapi ca sādhakaḥ|| 75.4 ||
etāni sarvadravyāṇi juhuyādanalojjvale|
lakṣatrayaṃ tu yo japtvā puraścaraṇamācaret|| 75.5 ||
sa tu sājyaṃ sakarpūraṃ juhuyāt tu catuṣṭayam|
daśabhirnavalakṣeṣu dravyairmantrī puraścaret|| 75.6 ||
japteṣu cāṣṭabhiḥ ṣaṭsu sarvaiḥ sarvatra cācaret|
hastamātraṃ tu kuṇḍaṃ syāt ṣaṭkoṇaṃ tryaṅgulādhikam|| 75.7 ||
tripurāyāstu madhyāyā bālāyāśca sadaiva hi|
tathā tripurabhairavyāḥ kuṇḍamānaṃ prakīrtitam|| 75.8 ||
catuṣkoṇaṃ bhavet kumḍaṃ hastamātradvayeṣu ca|
aṣṭāṅgulādhikaṃ proktaṃ vaiṣṇavyāstu puraścare|| 75.9 ||
trikoṇaṃ hastamātraṃ tu kāmākhyāyāstu kuṇḍakam|
evaṃ sarvaprapañcānāmāsāmapi tathā tathā|| 75.10 ||
saṃskuryādanalaṃ vṛddhaṃ vidhivad vaiṣṇavīkṛtau|
kāmākhyāyāstathā kuryājjyotiṣṭomādi matsuta|| 75.11 ||
ādau tripurabhairavyāścaturbhidaṃśabhistathā|
juhuyādanale vṛddhe āhutīśca caturdaśa|| 75.12 ||
paścāt tu mūlamantreṇa aṣṭottaraśatatrayam|
homaṃ yanpan vā tena śatāni nava vā’thavā|| 75.13 ||
japānte tu baliṃ dadyād vaiṣṇavyā balidānataḥ|
ratnakarpūrakanakān yatraiva gurudakṣiṇāḥ|| 75.14 ||
alābhe dadhipuṣpājyalājairdevyāḥ puraścaret|
lābhe caturdaśadravyairjuhuyād vidhipūrvakam|| 75.15 ||
asyā yantraṃ rahasyena śṛṃṇu vetālabhairava|
yatkṛtvaivākhilān kāmām̐llabhate narasattamaḥ|| 75.16 ||
ṣaṭkoṇaṃ maṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā tat tu koṇatraye likhet|
mantraṃ tripurabhairavyāstrivarṇaṃ tu tatastvadhaḥ|| 75.17 ||
ādyāyāstripurāyāstu tribījāni likhedanu|
madhyabījatrayaṃ madhye likhitvā pīṭhayantrake|| 75.18 ||
sarvaistu mātṛkāvarṇaistridhā saṃveṣṭayedanu|
lākṣārasairlikhitvā tu trilohairveṣṭayet tataḥ|| 75.19 ||
tad dhāryaṃ mūrdhni satataṃ tena sarvajayī bhavet|
rūpavān balavān vāgmī dhanaratnayutaḥ sadā|| 75.20 ||
dīrghāyuḥ kāmabhogī ca suprajaḥ sa ca jāyate|
madhye bījaṃ likhitvaikaṃ mūrdhni cādhastathāparam|| 75.21 ||
ādyāyāstripurāyāstu bhairavyāstadvadeva hi|
imāni ṣaṭakmantrāṇi kramād vetālabhairava|| 75.22 ||
pūrvavat sam̐llikhitvaikaṃ saṃveṣṭyātha trilohakaiḥ|
vāme bāhau dakṣiṇe ca hṛdi kaṇṭhe kare tathā|| 75.23 ||
mūrdhni dhāryāṇi kramataḥ phalametacca tadbhavam|
sampatsaubhāgyasaṃstambhavaśīkaraṇamohanam|| 75.24 ||
kavitvamatha sarvatra bhavedetanna saṃśayaḥ|
yantramantrāṇi tantrāṇi traipurāṇi tu bhairava|| 75.25 ||
sa pañca ṣaṭ sahasrāṇi mantraughaistriguṇīkṛtaiḥ|
tajjñātvā pūjako dhīmān paratreha na sīdati|| 75.26 ||
mantraurghastantramantrairavicalitapadaṃ traipuraṃ yat pradhānaṃ yadviprādyairadeyaṃ vigatabhayapadaṃ yatkavitvapradātṛ|
traivargīyaṃ trirūpaṃ tridivamatha surā yatra santi trayo’pi tajjñānaudhaiḥ subhūtaṃ sakalaśubhaphalaṃ yanmahastraipurākhyām|| 75.27 ||
kavacaṃ tripurāyāstu śṛṇu vetāla bhairava|
yajjñatvā mantravit samyak phalamāpnoti pūjane|| 75.28 ||
upacārāḥ purā proktā yena evātra pūjane|
pratipattistu saivātra kīrtitā nityapūjane|| 75.29 ||
kavacasya ca māhātmyamahaṃ brahmā na keśavaḥ|
vaktuṃ kṣamastvananto’pi bahujihvaḥ kadācana|| 75.30 ||
kravyād bhayaṃ na labhate tathā toyapariplave|
kavacasmaraṇādeva sarvaṃ kalyāṇamāpnuyāt|| 75.31 ||
oṃ tripurākavacasyāsya ṛṣirdakṣima ucyate|
chandaścitrāhvayaṃ proktaṃ devī tripura bhairavī|| 75.32 ||
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ viniyogastu sādhane|
yathādyātripurākhyāyā bījāni kramataḥ suta|| 75.33 ||
nāmato vāgbhavādīni kīrtitāni mayā purā|
tathā tripurabhairavyā bījānāmapi nāmataḥ|| 75.34 ||
vāgbhavaḥ kāmarājaśca tathā trailokyamohanaḥ|| 75.35 ||
avatu sakalaśīrṣaṃ vāgbhave vācamugrāṃ nikhilaracitakāmān kāmarājo’vatānme|
sakalakaraṇavargamīśvaraḥ pātu nityaṃ tanugatabahutejo vardhayan buddhihetuḥ|| 75.36 ||
kuṭaistu pañcabhiridaṃ gaditaṃ hi yantram mantraṃ tato’nu satataṃ mama teja ugram|
tejomayaṃ mahati nityaparāyaṇasthaṃ tantro hṛdi pravitatāṃ tanutāṃ subuddhim|| 75.37 ||
ādhāre vāgbhavaḥ pātu kāmarājastathā hṛdi|| 75.38 ||
bhruvormadhye ca śīrṣe ca pātu trailokyamohanaḥ|| 75.39 ||
vitatakulakalājñā kāminī bhairavī yā tripurapuradahākhyā sarvalokasya mātā|
vitaratu mama nityaṃ nābhipadme sakukṣau gaṇapativanitā māṃ rogahāniṃ sukhaṃ ca|| 75.40 ||
yogairjaganti parimohayatīva nityaṃ jāgarti yā tripurabhairavabhāminīti|
sāyaṃ ca bhāvakalitā mama pañcabhāge nāsākṣikarṇarasanātvaci pātu nityam|| 75.41 ||
ādyā tu tripureyaṃ yā madhyā yā kāmadāyinī|| 75.42 ||
tridhā tu hyavatāṃ nityaṃ devī tripurabhairavī|| 75.43 ||
udayadiśi sadā māṃ pātu bālā tu mātā yamadiśi mama madhyābhadramugraṃ vidadhyāt|
varuṇapavanakāṣṭhāmadhyato bhairavī māmavatu sakalarakṣāṃ kurvatī sundarī me|| 75.44 ||
mahāmāyā mahāyonirviśvayoniḥ sadaiva tu|
sā pātu tripurā nityaṃ sundarīṃ bhairavī ca yā|| 75.45 ||
lalāṭe subhagā devī pūrvasyāṃ diśi kāmadā|
nityaṃ tiṣṭhatu rakṣantī sadā tripurasundarī|| 75.46 ||
bhruvormadhye tathāgneyyāṃ diśi māṃ tripurā ca yā|
vardhayantī bhagagaṇān pātu tripurabhairavī|| 75.47 ||
vadane dakṣiṇasyāṃ ca diśi māṃ bhagasarpiṇī|
tripurā yamadūtādīn vārayantī sadā’vatu|| 75.48 ||
karmayoḥ paścimāyāṃ ca diśi māṃ bhagamālinī|
ayonijā jagadyonirbālā māṃ tripurā’vatu|| 75.49 ||
anaṅgakusumākaṇṭhe pratīcyāṃ diśi sundarī|
tripurābhairavī mātā nityaṃ pātu maheśvarī|| 75.50 ||
hṛdi mārutakāṣṭhāyāṃ devī cānaṅgamekhalā|
nābhāvudīcyāṃ diśi māṃ mātaṅgī tripurāparā|| 75.51 ||
anaṅgamadanā devī pātu tripurabhairavī|
aiśānyāṃ diśi liṅge ca madavibhramamantharā|| 75.52 ||
vāgnavādinī rakṣatu māṃ sadā tripurabhairavī|
gudameḍhāntare pātu ratistripurabhairavī|| 75.53 ||
hṛdayābhyantare prītiḥ pātu tripurabhairavī|
bhrūnāsayormadhyadeśe nityaṃ pātu manobhavaḥ|| 75.54 ||
drāvaṇī māṃ grahat pātu vāṇī māṃ durgamūrdhani|
kṣobhaṇā māṃ sāda pātu kravyādbhyo’niṣṭabhītitaḥ|| 75.55 ||
vaśīkaraṇavāṇī māmagnitaḥ pātu rājataḥ|
ākarṣaṇāhvayā vāṇī māṃ pātu śastraghātataḥ|| 75.56 ||
mohanaḥ sarvabhūtebhyaḥ piśācebhyo jalāttathā|
nityaṃ pātu mahābāṇastanvānaḥ kāmamuttamam|| 75.57 ||
mālā māṃ śāstrabodhāya śāstravāde sadā’vatu|
pustakaṃ pātu manasi saṅkalpaṃ vardhayan mama|| 75.58 ||
varaḥ pātu sadā dhāmnidhāmatejo vivardhayan|
abhayaṃ hyabhayaṃ dhattāṃ sarvebhyo bhūtibhāvanam|| 75.59 ||
ūrdhvādhobhāvabhūtasthitatarakaraṇai raktakīrṇā sucakrā kālāgniprakhyarociḥ sakalasuragaṇairarcitā muṇḍamālā|
jñānadhyānaikatānaprabalabalakaraṃ tattvabhūtapratiṣṭhaṃ pātādūrdhvaṃ tathādhaḥ sakalabhayabhṛto bhogabhīrostu vidyā|| 75.60 ||
haḥ pātu hṛdi māṃ nityaṃ saḥ śīrṣe pātu nityaśaḥ|
raḥ pātuguhyadeśe māṃ sauḥ pātu kaṇṭhapārśvayoḥ|| 75.61 ||
rakāro mama nāḍīṣu śiraḥ sauḥ pātu sarvadā|
śakraḥ pātu sadākāśebrahmā rakṣatu sarvataḥ|| 75.62 ||
vidyā vidyābhāvinī kāmarūpā sthūlā sūkṣmā māyayā yādimāyā|
brahmendrādyairacitā bhūtidātrī rakṣāṃ kuryāt sarvato bhairavī mām|| 75.63 ||
ādyā madhyā bhāvinī nītiyuktā samyagjñānajñeyarūpāparā yā|
ādāvante madhyabhāge ca tārā pāyād devī traipurī bhairavī yā|| 75.64 ||
yanmantrabhāgatantrāṇāṃ yantrāṇāmapi keśavaḥ|
brahmā rudraśca jānāti tatvaṃ nānyo namo’stu tān|| 75.65 ||
tvaṃ brahmāṇī bhavāni viśvabhaviturlakṣmīratiryoginī|
tvaṃ vāgmī subhagā bhavāyutayugaṃ mantrākṣaraṃ niṣkalam|
varṇāste nikhilā stanāvacalitastvaṃ kāminīkāmadā tvaṃ devi tripure kavitvamamalaṃ saubhāgyamuccaiḥ kuru|| 75.66 ||
idaṃ tu kavacaṃ devyā yo jānāti sa mantravit|
nādhayo vyādhayastasta na bhayaṃ ca sadā kvacit|| 75.67 ||
iti te paramaṃ guhyamākhyātaṃ kavacaṃ param|
tadbhajasva mahābhāga tataḥ siddhimavāpsyasi|| 75.68 ||
idaṃ pavitraṃ paramaṃ puṇyaṃ kortivivardhanam|
tripurāyāstrimūrtestu kavacaṃ mayakoditam|| 75.69 ||
yaḥ paṭhet prātarutthāya sa prāpnoti manogatam|
likhitaṃ kavacaṃ yastu kaṇṭhe gahvāti mantravit|| 75.70 ||
na tasya gātraṃ kṛntanti raṇe śastrāṇi bhairava|
saṃgrāme śāstravāde ca vijayastasya jāyate|| 75.71 ||
idaṃ kavacamajñātvā yo japet tripurāṃ naraḥ|
sa śastraghātamāpnoti bhairavīṃ sundarīmapi|| 75.72 ||
bījamuccārayet svastho gatavāg doṣaniścitaḥ|
saṃyogabodhaḥ pratyekabhedaśravaṇagocaraḥ|| 75.73 ||
yathaiva jāyate samyagyajñādidiṣavarjitaḥ|
yasyoccāraṇaraṇakṛtye tu saṃyogo bodhadūṣaṇam|| 75.74 ||
pratyekabhinnatābodhaḥ sa kuṣṭhī jāyate naraḥ|
nyāsānāṃ pracuratve tu phalānāmapi bhūritā|| 75.75 ||
uktanyāso na hi tyājyo hyadhikaṃ tu samācaret|
mayoktanyāsamajñātvā na kṛtvā vā pramādataḥ|| 75.76 ||
yaḥ kuryāt pūjanaṃ devyā āpnuyāt sa mahāpadam|
mantrākṣarasya vinyāsaḥ sarvamantreṣu kīrtitaḥ|| 75.77 ||
vaiṣṇave cāthavā raudre mahābhāge’thavā punaḥ|
mantre kalevaragate mahāmāyāprapūjane|| 75.78 ||
mantranyāse na vā kuryāt kuryād vānyatra vācaret|
aṅgarāgeṣu sindūraṃ pāneṣu madirā tathā|| 75.79 ||
vastraṃ raktaṃ tu kauśeyaṃ tripurāprītidaṃ matam|
trayo dīpāḥ pradātavyāḥ pañca vā sapta bhairava|| 75.80 ||
ito nyūnān na pradadyāt tripurāyai kadācana|
mallikāmālatīkundaṃ vako droṇaḥ sitāmbujam|| 75.81 ||
śuklapuṣpāṇi tripurāprītidāni tu bhairava|
raktāmbujaṃ javā raktā karavīro’tha komalaḥ|| 75.82 ||
raktaṃ tripurabhairavyāḥ prītidā snehakāñcanaiḥ|
idaṃ te kathitaṃ putra saṃkṣepādeva bhairava|| 75.83 ||
avāpya siddhiṃ paramāṃ svayaṃ vistārayiṣyasi|
ārādhya tvaṃ mahāmāyāmavāpya ca gaṇeśatām|| 75.84 ||
kalpamantraudhamantrāṇāṃ bhaviṣyasi vitānaka|
asyāstripurabhairavyāḥ śuklarūpāṇi yāni tu|| 75.85 ||
tāni sārasvatākhyāni mantrāḥ samyagudīritāḥ|
sarasvatī tu yā devī vīṇāpustakadhāriṇī|| 75.86 ||
srak kamaṇḍaluhastā ca dakṣiṇe śuklaparṇikā|
mahācalasya pṛṣṭhasthā sitapadmoparisthitā|| 75.87 ||
śuklavarṇā śuklavastrā śuklalābharaṇabhūṣitā|
tasyāstu vāgbhavādyābhyāṃ netrabījaṃ dvitīyakam|| 75.88 ||
kṛtvānte viniyojyaiva mantraṃ prākpratipāditam|
varadābhayahastā ca mālāpustakadhāriṇī|| 75.89 ||
śuklapadmāsanagatā sā parā vāg sarasvatī|
bālābījādyakṣaraṃ tu dviruktaṃ cārdha candrakam|| 75.90 ||
mantramasyāḥ purā proktaṃ tantraṃ sāmānyamīritam|
eṣā tu yā raktavarṇā muṇḍamālāvibhūṣitā|| 75.91 ||
tasyāḥ proktaḥ purā mantraḥ sā tu vṛddhā sarasvatī|
ṣaṣṭhamantrastathaitasyāstrayodaśanirūpaṇe|| 75.92 ||
eṣā kavitvaśāstraidhatattvādaviniścaye|
sukhasampatkarā proktā nityameva tu bhairava|| 75.93 ||
asyā vyastasamastaiśca śukraktādibhedataḥ|
catuḥṣaṣṭimūrtayaśca traipurāduta vāgbhavam|| 75.94 ||
mahāmāyā yoganidrā mūlabhūta jagatprasūḥ|
jaganmātā jagaddhātrī vidyāvidyāparātmikā|| 75.95 ||
tasyā eva mahābhāga tripurādyā vibhūtayaḥ|
prastutāḥ kathitā nityaṃ tāḥ svayaṃgata eva hi|| 75.96 ||
iti te kathitaṃ putra mahādevyā manoharam|
rahasyaṃ vāmadākṣiṇyaṃ mantrasiddhiṃ śṛṇuṣva me|| 75.97 ||
|| iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe tripurākavacaṃ nāma pañcasaptatitamo’dhyāyaḥ|| 75 ||

<76>
|| ṣaṭsaptatitamo’dhyāyaḥ ||
||śrībhagavānuvāca||
mantraśuddhimavekṣyeva gṛhṇīyānmantramuttamam|| 76.1 ||
tatra siddhaṃ susiddhaṃ ca sādhyaṃ śātravameva ca|
mantraṃ caturvidhaṃ proktaṃ tadviddhyakṣarabhedataḥ|| 76.2 ||
varṇakramaḥ śāśvatastu yo mayā bhāṣitaḥ purā|
tatrādau bhairava jñātvā paścāccakraṃ śṛṇuṣva me|
varṇānāṃ tu mukhādīnāṃ vaiṣmavītantrasaṃjñaka|| 76.3 ||
yaḥ prokto’bhūnmahāmantrastasyāsannakṣarāṇi tu|
mūlabhūtāni tānyeva tato’nyānapi vardhayet|| 76.4 ||
akāraśca kakāraśca caṭakārau tathaiva ca|
tapakārau yakāraśca vargādyāḥ parikīrtitāḥ|| 76.5 ||
a ā i ī u ū ṛ ṝ lṛ lṛ ete’dīrghadīrghakāḥ|
e ai o au visargaśca bindivādiryājñikastathā|| 76.6 ||
dhvanerantarajāśceti kīrtitāstu svarā amī|
khakarāśca gakarāśca gha ṅo vargaḥ prakīrtitaḥ|| 76.7 ||
vyañjanakārādichajau bhairavaśabdādireva ca|| 76.8 ||
ṇakārāntastṛtīyo’yaṃ vargoṣṭhādiḥ prakīrtitaḥ|
thakāraśca dakāraśva dharmaśabdādireva ca|| 76.9 ||
navaśabdasya caivādiścaturtho varga ucyate|
phalaśabdasya yaścādirbahuśabdādireva ca|| 76.10 ||
bhakāro manaḥśabdādiḥ pañcamo varga ucyate|
yakāraśca rakāraśca lakāro vastathaiva ca|| 76.11 ||
ebhiścaturbhirvargā’yaṃ ṣaṣṭho bhairava ucyate|
śaṣasā haḥ kṣakāraśca saṃyogaḥ parivedakaḥ|| 76.12 ||
pañcabhiḥ śeṣavargo’yaṃ saptamaḥ parikīrtitaḥ|
saṃyogayogasaṃlomapratilomairime suta|| 76.13 ||
varṇāḥ syurmantranāmādau vāṅmātre’pi ca bhairava|
cartuvargapradā varṇāḥ sukhaduḥkhakarāstathā|| 76.14 ||
rogaṃ ca tejasampūjyapūjakāḥ karikortitāḥ|
ahaṃ viṣṇuśca brahmā ca gāyatrī brahmamātṛkāḥ|| 76.15 ||
aparaṃ brahmavarṇā ye parabrahmasukhapradam|
aparaṃ brahmakuśalaḥ parabrahmadhigacchati|| 76.16 ||
sisṛkṣurīśvaro varṇājja ganti svecchayā punaḥ|
sasarja mama vaktre tāṃ brahmavaktre ca vai nyadhāt|| 76.17 ||
ahaṃ tu sakalān varṇān nyasya bhairava tantrakam|
akāra bahulaṃ putra jñānamārgaṃ vivardhayan|| 76.18 ||
ya ime gaditā varṇā mayā varṇaviniścaye|
mantraśuddhivivekārthaṃ varṇacakraṃ tataḥ śṛṇu|| 76.19 ||
śaktiśambhusvarūpiṇyo rekhe dve prathamaṃ nyaset|
tanmadhyataḥ punārekhe viṣṇulakṣmītale tathā|| 76.20 ||
tayostu rekhayormaṃdhye dve rekhe samato nyaset|
tasya cakrasya cāreṣu rekhāstu parisaṃkhyayā|| 76.21 ||
catasrastu pradātavyāḥ svaramadhye tu bhairava|
bhinnānāṃ ca tathā varṇāḥ sandhayo’ṣṭau prakīrtitāḥ|| 76.22 ||
nemayastu catasro’sya sandhimadhyeṣu kīrtitāḥ|
aṣṭārasaṃyutaṃ cakraṃ caturnemisamanvitam|| 76.23 ||
bahirveṣṭanasaṃyuktaṃ varṇacakraṃ prakīrtitam|
meṣādīnāṃ ca rāśīnāmudayāstapratijñayā|| 76.24 ||
idameva bhaveccakraṃ jñānaśrīvṛddhikārakam|
idaṃ cakraṃ likhitvā tu samabhūmāvudaṅmukhaḥ|| 76.25 ||
prāṅmukho vā likhed varṇāṃcchuciriṣṭaṃ naman gurum|
pradakṣiṇaṃ likhet tasmin varṇāsteṣveva tu kramāt|| 76.26 ||
puro nemevakāraṃ tu rakāraṃ cāpi vai likhet|
akāraṃ varjayed dīrghamīkāraṃ ca svareṣu vai|| 76.27 ||
akarādikṣakārānta ḍha ṭa ña ṇa varjitam|
pradakṣiṇakramādeva likhitvā varṇasañcayam|| 76.28 ||
svanāmādyakṣaraṃ gṛhya kuryāt tu gaṇanakramam|
mantrasyādyakṣaraṃ yāvat siddhādyaṃ tatra yojayet|| 76.29 ||
navaikapañcake siddhaḥ sādhyaḥ ṣaḍyugmapaṅkiṣu|
trisaptakādaśeṣveva susuddhaḥ parikīrtitaḥ|| 76.30 ||
dvādaśāṣṭajaturtheṣu bhātravaḥ parikīrtitaḥ|
siddhenāvācirāt siddhiḥ sādhyaḥ kālena sidhyati|| 76.31 ||
kramānnāśayate śatruḥ susiddhaḥ siddhido’cirāt|
yo yo varṇakramaḥ prokto mantre dakṣiṇagocare|| 76.32 ||
vāmyārādhanamantreṣu kramaṃ śṛṇviha bhairava|
ṛlṛdvayaṃ ṅa ña ṇa nā varjyāśca varṇagocare|| 76.33 ||
likhed vāmakrameṇaiva tatra varṇāṃstu mantravit|
nṛsiṃhārkavarāhāṇāṃ prasādapraṇavasya ca|| 76.34 ||
ekākṣaradvyakṣarāṇāṃ na siddhādivicintanam|
bījetu cāpi sarveṣu dīkṣārtheṣu ca bhairava|| 76.35 ||
siddhādicintā no kāryā grāhyāstu daśa vaśyakam|
susiddhaṃ kāmadaṃ grāhyaṃ sādhyasiddhavicāraṇāt|| 76.36 ||
na grāhyaḥ śātravo dhīrairgṛhītvāpnoti cāpadam|
yo yasyaikākṣaro mantrastannāmnā sa nigadyate|| 76.37 ||
sahitaścandrabindubhyāṃ tadbījamiti gadyate|
tathā śakro sakāraḥ syāt sārdhacandraḥ sabindukaḥ|| 76.38 ||
sa eva śakrabījaṃ syāt tathānyatrāpi yojayet|
mantroddhāreṣu sarvatra parataḥ parataḥ puraḥ|| 76.39 ||
pūrvato’pi pare kāryamanuktaḥ pūrvapakṣakaḥ|
yadā ṣoḍaśasāhasraṃ vaiṣmavyā mantrasañcayam|| 76.40 ||
cakre nirīkṣyate tatra ṣoḍaśāraṃ tu cakrakam|
viṃśatistu sahasrāṇi tripurāyā yadīkṣate|| 76.41 ||
dvātriṃśāraṃ tatra cakraṃ lekhanīyaṃ sadā budhaiḥ|
idameva mahācakraṃ ṣoḍaśārādikaṃ kṛtī|| 76.42 ||
kuryādadhikarekhābhirmantraśuddhyantare suta|
iyaṃ te kathitā putra mantrasiddhirabhīṣṭadā|| 76.43 ||
jānāti samyak ya imāṃ sa jayī kāmamāpnuyāt|
rahasyaṃ paramaṃ putra prayogādiprakārataḥ|| 76.44 ||
vakṣyāmi tat samāsena śṛṇu vetāla bhairava|
dantaḥ pakṣaviḍālasya tattvacā pariveṣṭitaḥ|| 76.45 ||
nirmālyena tu vaiṣmavyā tat saṃveṣṭaya guṇatrayam|
tat tad vā vāmasūtrasya tattanmantreṇa mantritam|| 76.46 ||
gṛhītvā dakṣiṇe pāṇau mantrāṇāṃ śatamāditaḥ|
sañcayedatha vaiṣṇavyā aṣṭamyāṃ niyatendriyaḥ|| 76.47 ||
tatastu dakṣiṇe bāhau dhāryaṃ yantrottamaṃ budhaiḥ|
tato dvādaśasiddhiḥ syāddhartācennātritittilīm|| 76.48 ||
jayaḥ saṃgrāmavādeṣu śarīrasyāpyarogitā|
vaśakṛdrājaputrāṇāṃ rājñāmapi ca santatam|| 76.49 ||
bhūtapretapiśācāśca no yānti netragocaram|
yoṣitāṃ samadānāṃ tu vaśakṛccintanāt sakṛt|| 76.50 ||
rudhirāṇāṃ śleṣmaṇā ca dhātūnāṃ stambhanaṃ tathā|
tejasāṃ stambhakaṃ caiva cakṣustejaḥ pradaṃ tathā|| 76.51 ||
mūrdhni pakṣaviḍālasya hastaṃ dattvā śatatrayam|
vaiṣmavītantramantraṃ tu japtvā taṃ sthāpayed gṛhe|| 76.52 ||
taṃ viḍālaṃ tu yā paśyenmalinī vanitā suta|
nāputrā sā bhavitrī tu kadācidapi bhairava|| 76.53 ||
tādṛk pakṣaviḍālastu yasya tiṣṭhati mandire|
mṛtāpatyāpi tadgehe jīvatputrā prajāyate|| 76.54 ||
kokilo bhṛṅgarājo vā cakoro vā śuko’thavā|
vaiṣṇavītantramantreṇa mantrito yatra tiṣṭhati|| 76.55 ||
vighnaṃ na mandire tasya bhavitṛ suprajā bhavet|
na sarpāstatra gacchanti gatāḥ khādanti no narān|
nārī na bandhakī tasya mandire’pi prajāyate|| 76.56 ||
pañcamūrteścaṇḍikāyā nirmālyāni ca pañcamaḥ|| 76.57 ||
teṣām̐ valīnāṃ māṃsna sthālyāṃ paktvā dinatrayam|
aṣṭamyāṃ tatpunardevyai dattvā tanmantramantritaiḥ|| 76.58 ||
toyaurbhyukṣya bhuñjīyānmanasā cintayecchivām|
tasmin bhukte tu dīrghāyujaṃrā śokavivarjitaḥ|| 76.59 ||
tejasvī śatrudamanaḥ kavirvāgmo ca jāyate|
lalāṭe mūrdhni kaṇṭhe ca bāhvoḥ pāṇyostathā hṛdi|| 76.60 ||
vaiṣṇavītantramantrasya yāni cāṣṭākṣarāṇi ca|
likhitvā tāni caiteṣu sthāneṣu mantravid budhaḥ|| 76.61 ||
kuṅkumaṃ kṣīramalayajātapaṅkaḥ suyāvakaiḥ|
aṣṭamyāṃ saṃyato bhūtvā navamyāṃ prathamaṃ naraḥ|| 76.62 ||
pratiṣṭhāne nyasya karamaṣṭāvaṣṭau japed budhaḥ|
āvartanena mantrāṇāṃ tato’nu pūjayecchivām|| 76.63 ||
tatastasmin dine devyai vijātīyaṃ valitrayam|
dattvā sahasraṃ mantrasya saṃkhyayā japamārabhet|| 76.64 ||
japānte tu havirbhuktā saṃyato rajanīṃ nayet|
evaṃ sakṛtkṛte putra raṇe tasya parājayaḥ|| 76.65 ||
kadācidapi no bhūyānna ca vādeṣu śāstrataḥ|
vidhimevaṃ sakṛtkṛtvā raṇakāle yathā tathā|| 76.66 ||
sadā likhet kṣatriyastu vijayāya raṇeṣu ca|
aparaṃ tu raṇāṣṭāṅgaṃ guhyametat prakīrtitam|| 76.67 ||
anenaiva tu guhyena vijayī tvaṃ bhaviṣyasi|
iti nau kathitaṃ sarvaṃ guhyād guhyataraṃ śubham|| 76.68 ||
sukhasampatkaraṃ mantraṃ yantratantrasamanvitam|
yacchrotuṃ tridaśāḥ sarve nityaṃ vāñchanti cāmṛtam|| 76.69 ||
tadidante samākhyātaṃ putra vetāla bhairava|
etat sarvaṃ nado jñātvā tattvataḥ putra bhairava|| 76.70 ||
sa kāmānakhilān prāpya nityaṃ kaivalyamāpnuyāt|
śṛṇoti yaḥ sakṛdidaṃ kathyamāno dvijottamaiḥ|| 76.71 ||
na tasya vighnā jāyante nāputraḥ sa ca jāyate|
dīrghāyurbalayuktaśca nityaṃ pramuditaḥ kṛtī|
vāñcitārthamavāpnoti devīgṛhamavāpnuyāt|| 76.72 ||
gacchataṃ kāmarūpāntaḥpīṭhaṃ nīlācalāhvayam|| 76.73 ||
kāmākhyānilayaṃ guhyaṃ kubjikāpīṭhasaṃjñakam|
ākāśagaṅgā yatrāsti tajjalairabhiṣicya ca|| 76.74 ||
tatrārādhayataṃ putrau mahāmāyāṃ jaganmayīm|
sā prasannācirād devo varadā nau bhaviṣyati|| 76.75 ||
||aurvya uvāca||
ityuktvā vṛṣabhārūḍhastadā vetālabhairavau|
sa putrau tu parityajya tatraivāntaradhīyata|| 76.76 ||
tatastau nāṭaka śailaṃ parityajya tapasvinau|
āsedaturmahātmānaṃ vasiṣṭhaṃ brahmaṇaḥ sutam|| 76.77 ||
sa tu sandhyācalagatastau dṛṣṭvā samupasthitau|
sabhājayāmāsa muniḥ śiṣyavat tau harātmajau|| 76.78 ||
tatastasyopadeśena vasiṣṭhasya mahātmanaḥ|
jagmatustau mahāśailaṃ nīlaṃ kāmākhyayāgatam|| 76.79 ||
tatra gatvā mahātmānau vaiṣṇavītantragocaram|
ādāya jātāṃ tāṃ devīṃ mahāmāyāṃ jaganmayīm|| 76.80 ||
bhairavākhyasya liṅgasya nikaṭasthau śivātmanaḥ|
ākāśagaṅgāmāplāvya sthaṇḍile maṇḍalottamam|| 76.81 ||
vidhāya naraśārdūlau jepaturmantramuttamam|
tau japtvā vidhivanmantraṃ siddhamaṣṭākṣarātkam|| 76.82 ||
vetālasya tathāsādhyamaṣṭalakṣāṇi saṃkhyayā|
tribhirvarṣaistu lakṣāṇāṃ caturṇāmantatastataḥ|| 76.83 ||
tridhā puraścaraṇaṃ ca tau bhaktyā samakurvatām|
yadyaduttaratantroktaṃ kalpoktaṃ pūjane kṛtam|| 76.84 ||
tatsarvaṃ cakratustau tu taṃ trihāyaṇasaṃvṛtau|
kāmākhyā tripurādīnāmanyāsāmapi pūjanam|| 76.85 ||
sakṛtkṛtvā pīṭhayātrāṃ ceraturvidhivat tadā|
evaṃ tau baddhakavacau kṛtanyāsau harātmajau|| 76.86 ||
suprītā cānujagrāha mahāmāyā’tha tau tadā|
dhyānasthayostu japatoryajatośca jaganmayo|| 76.87 ||
śivaliṅgaṃ vinirbhidya tadā pratyakṣatāṃ gatā|
tasyāṃ vinirgatāyāṃ tu śivaliṅgaṃ tridhā’bhavat|| 76.88 ||
bhairavo bhairavī ceti herukaśca tathā trayaḥ|
tāṃ dadarśa tadā devīṃ vetālo bhairavastadā|
yathā dhyānagatā dṛṣṭā bahiścāpi tathā tathā|| 76.89 ||
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā cārusarvāṅgī pīnonnatapayodharām|| 76.90 ||
baradābhayahastāṃ ca siddhasūtrāsidhāriṇīm|
raktapadmapratīkāśāṃ sitapretāsanasthitām|| 76.91 ||
nimīlya nayanadvandvaṃ tadā vetālabhairavau|
trāhi trāhi mahāmāye ūcatustau muhurmuhuḥ|| 76.92 ||
tatastayā mahādevyā tejasāpyāyi tu tau|
pasparśa varahastasya cāgrabhāgena vaiṣṇavī|| 76.93 ||
āpyāyitau tatastau tu spṛṣṭāvapi tathā punaḥ|
āsedatuśca devatvaṃ manuṣyatvaṃ vihāya ca|| 76.94 ||
devabhūtau tadā tau tu mahāmāyāṃ jaganmayīm|
stutibhirnatibhiśceti tadā tuṣṭuvatuḥ śivām|| 76.95 ||
||vetālabhairavāvūcatuḥ||
jaya jaya devi suragaṇārcitapaṅkaje 
viśvasya bhūtibhāvinī śaśimaulikelibhāvini girije|
netratrayanirjitavivasvadvidhuvahlikāntitulitakamalaje 
madhyanetranatabhrū bhaṅgabhaktaraktamaticayajvāyakavimalaje|| 76.96 ||
ājñācakrāntaśāntanavakoṭikaroṭitulyakānta śāntaśaśadhare|
bahumāyakāyabhogayogataraṅgasārasya padmavasucare|| 76.97 ||
trināḍinītamadhyabaddhaviṣkiravallabhaśubhasuṣumnasamādhārapare|
vibudharatnavimodiviśvamūrtimahomayānavasi ṣaṭcakradhare|| 76.98 ||
ādiṣoḍaśacakracumbitacārudehapīnatuṅgakucācalāligitabhūmimadhyanāgaśākagate
paṅkajātakamūlamaṇicaturbāhuyute|
jñānatālakamantratantrayogiyoganibaddhasārasūtabhaṅga vinodakṛte|
ātmatattvaparaikaśāraratnahārakamuktisūktivivekasitapretarate|| 76.99 ||
ratnasārasamastasaṅgataraṃgarāgaviyogimantraśāntapuraviśeṣakṛte|
yoginīgaṇanaॉtyabhṛtyabhāvananibaddhanaddhahārakaṅkaṇamukhyabhūṣaṇapate|
sāṭṭahāsavinodamoditamuktakeśasureśanibaddhadehapuṭe|
dehi devi śokaśocanabandhamocanapāpaśātanaśuddhamate|| 76.100 ||
sarvavidyātmikāṃ guhyāṃ mantrayantramayīṃ śivām|
praṇamāmi mahāmāyāṃ loke vede ca kīrtitām|| 76.101 ||
parāparātmikāṃ nityāṃ sādhyādhāraikasaṃsthitām|
kāmāhlādakarīṃ kāntāṃ tvāṃ namāmi jaganmayom|| 76.102 ||
prapañcaparamavyaktaṃ jagadekavivardhini|
prabhāvanenārdharaktāṃgi devi tubhyaṃ namo’stu te|| 76.103 ||
kāmākhyā nityarūpākhyā mahāmāyā sarasvatī|
yā lakṣmīrviṣṇuvakṣaḥsthā nāmāvo hyacyutāṃ śivām|| 76.104 ||
mantrāṇi yasyāstantrāṇi sahasrāṇi ca ṣoḍaśa|
mantrayantrātmake tubhyaṃ nam’stu mama pārvati|| 76.105 ||
||aurvauvāca||
iti stutā tatastābhyāṃ mahāmāyā jagatprasūḥ|
uvāca muditā ceti varaṃ varayataṃ yuvām|| 76.106 ||
pratyakṣato mahāmāyāṃ pūrvavad dhyānagocarām|
tau dṛṣṭvā bhargatanayau prāhatuścedamuttamam|| 76.107 ||
||vaitālabhairavāvūcatuḥ||
devyanena śarīrema bhavatyāḥ śaṅkarasya ca|
prārthaye śāśvatīṃ sevāṃ nityaṃ yāvadraviḥ śaśī|| 76.108 ||
nānyaṃ varaṃ sodhayāvo māye tvatto jaganmayi|
anyathā tava bhaktyaiva sthāsyāvo girikandare|| 76.109 ||
||aurvauvāca||
evamuktā tatastābhyāṃ mahāmāyā jaganmayī|
evamastviti covāca bhavatyevaṃ muhurmuhuḥ|| 76.110 ||
evaṃ siddhirjagaddhātrī proktā svasyātha cūcuke|
niṣpīḍya kārayāmāsa kṣīradhārādvayaṃ śivā|| 76.111 ||
tatastu niḥsṛtaṃ kṣīraṃ pāyayāmāsa bhairavam|
vetālaṃ ca mahārāja pibatastau ca tat tadā|| 76.112 ||
pītvā tau ca tadā kṣīraṃ devatvaṃ prāpya śāśvatam|
ajarau cāmarau bhūtī mahātejasvinau śubhau|| 76.113 ||
tasyāstu kṣīramamṛtaṃ tat pītvā tau mahābalau|
pīyūṣapānāt sajātau tatastau prāha vaiṣṇavī|| 76.114 ||
gaṇānāṃ devadevasya bhavatascādhipau yuvām|
dvāḥsthau ca nityamāsannau nandivad bhavataṃ sutau|| 76.115 ||
ityuktvā harasammatyā mahāmāyā jaganmayī|
yoginīgaṇasaṃyuktā tatraivāntaradhīyata|| 76.116 ||
antarhitāyāṃ tasyāṃ tu tadā vetālabhairavau|
muditau paramaprītau kṛtakṛtyau babhūvatuḥ|| 76.117 ||
athāgacchad devagaṇaiḥ sārdhaṃ sapramatho haraḥ|
sabhājayitumatyarthaṃ putrau vetālabhairavau|| 76.118 ||
tāvāsādya mahādevastadā nīlāhvayaṃ girim|
sakalaṃ darśayāmāsa pīṭhaṃ tu sthānabhedataḥ|| 76.119 ||
kāmākhyāyā guhāṃ tatra darśayitvā manobhavām|
tataḥ svīyāṃ kāmaguhāṃ cāyācchatraṃ svamālayam|| 76.120 ||
svakīyaṃ pañcamūrtīnāṃ saṃsthānaṃ cāpyadarśayat|
kāmarūpasya sakalaṃ pīṭhaṃ devamayaṃ tathā|| 76.121 ||
pratyekaṃ darśayāmāsa kramatastripurāntakaḥ|
prathamaṃ karatoyākhyāṃ satyagaṅgā sadāśivām|
puṇyatoyamayīṃ śuddhāṃ dakṣiṇābdhyekagaminīm|| 76.122 ||
|| iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe vetālabhairavayoḥ siddhilābho nāma ṣaṭsaptatitamo’dhyāyaḥ|| 76 ||

<77>
|| saptasaptatitamo’dhyāyaḥ ||
||aurvva uvāca||
tatastu kāmarūpasya vāyavyāṃ tripurāntakaḥ|
ātmano liṅgamatulaṃ jalpīśākhyaṃ vyadarśayat|| 77.1 ||
yatra nandī samārādhya mahādevaṃ jagatpatim|
abhinnena śarīreṇa gāṇapatyamavāpnuyāt|| 77.2 ||
nandikuṇḍaṃ mahākuṇḍaṃ yatra nandī purā’karot|
abhiṣekaṃ labdhavaraṃ pītaṃ toyamanutamam|| 77.3 ||
tatra snātvā ca pītvā ca kṛtakṛtyo narottamaḥ|
harasya sadanaṃ yāti nandino’pi mahāśriyaḥ|| 77.4 ||
tasyāsanne mahādevīṃ nātidūre vyavasthitām|
siddheśvarīṃ yonirūpāṃ mahāmāyāṃ jaganmayīm|| 77.5 ||
tryambako darśayāmāsa bhairavāya mahātmane|
yatra nandī mahāmāyāmājñayā śaśidhāriṇaḥ|| 77.6 ||
stutibhirnatibhiḥ pūjya gāṇapatyamavāpnuyāt|
suvarṇamānasastatra nadamukhyo manoharaḥ|| 77.7 ||
nandino’nugrahāyāśu mānasākhyaṃ sarastu tat|
āgataṃ cājñayā śambhoḥ pūrvameva tapasyataḥ|| 77.8 ||
jaṭodbhavā tatra nadī himavatprabhavā śubhā|
yasyāṃ snātvā naraḥpuṇyamāpnoti jāhnavīsamam|| 77.9 ||
gaurīvivāhasamaye sarvairmātṛgaṇaiḥ kṛtaḥ|
jalābhiṣeko bhargasya jaṭājūṭeṣu yaḥ purā|| 77.10 ||
taistoyairabhavadyasmājjaṭodākhyā nadī tataḥ|
caitre māsi sitāṣṭamyāṃ snātvā yasyāṃ naro vrajet|| 77.11 ||
pūrṇāyurvai naraśreṣṭha śivasya sadanaṃ prati|
dvāparasya tu yā gaṅgā triḥsrotākhyā saridvarā|| 77.12 ||
himavatprabhavā śuddhacandravimbād vinirgatā|
yasyāṃ snātvā mahāmādhyāṃ mātṛyonau na jāyate|| 77.13 ||
candrasūryagrahe snātvā kaivalyaṃ prāpnuyānnaraḥ|
sitaprabhā nāma nadī mahādevātāritā|| 77.14 ||
himavatprabhavā sāpi sitā dakṣa samudragā|
tasyāṃ daśaharāyāṃ tu daśamyāṃ śuklapakṣake|| 77.15 ||
snātvā viṣṇugṛhe yāti naro vai muktapātakaḥ|
navatoyā nāma nadī tataḥ pūrvasthitā purā|| 77.16 ||
navaṃ navaṃ navaṃ nityaṃ kurvantī sā punāti hi|
navatoyā tataḥ proktā himavatprabhavaiva sā|| 77.17 ||
tasyāṃ snātvā mahāmāghyāṃ naro gacchati devatām|
sampūrṇaṃ māghamāsaṃ tu snātvā viṣṇugṛhaṃ vrajet|| 77.18 ||
tāsāṃ nadīnāṃ tu patiragado nāma vai nadaḥ|
pīṭhapūrvo sthitaḥ puṇyo brahmapādasamudbhavaḥ|| 77.19 ||
himavatprabhavaḥ so’pi devagandharvasevitaḥ|
tatra snātvā ca pītvā ca naro brahmagṛhaṃ vrajet|| 77.20 ||
kārtikaṃ sakalaṃ māsaṃ yo’gadākhye mahānade|
pīṭhapūrve stitaḥ puṇeye brahmapādasamudbhavaḥ||
himavatprabhavaḥ so’pi devagandharvasevitaḥ|
tatra snātvā ca pītvā ca naro brahmagṛhaṃ vrajet||
snānaṃ karoti manujastasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu|| 77.21 ||
iha loke tvarogaḥ sa prāpya caivottamaṃ sukham|
śeṣe brahmagṛhaṃ prāpya tato mokṣamavāpnuyāt|| 77.22 ||
nandikuṇḍe naraḥ snātvā bhaktaṃ kuryāt tadā niśi|
tataḥ parasmin divase gacchejjalpīśamandiram|| 77.23 ||
tatra snātvā mahānadyāṃ jalpīśaṃ pratipūjya ca|
tasyāṃ niśi haviṣyāśī saṃyatastāṃ niśāṃ nayet|| 77.24 ||
tato’nudivase prāpte gacchet siddheśvarīṃ śivām|
tāṃ pūjayet tathāṣṭamyāmuvāsaṃ tathācaret|| 77.25 ||
caturbhujā tu sā devī pīnonnatapayodharā|
sindurapuñjasaṅkāśā dhatte kartrī ca kharparam|| 77.26 ||
dakṣiṇe bāmabāhubhyāmabhītivaradāyinī|
jaṭāmaṇḍitaśīrṣā ca raktapadmoparisthitā|| 77.27 ||
paṃcākṣarajapāntādirmantresyāḥ parikīrtitaḥ|
kāmakhyātantramevāsyāḥ pūjane tantramīritam|| 77.28 ||
evaṃ kṛtvā naro dhīraḥ punaryonau na jāyate|
jāmadagnyabhayād bhītāḥ kṣatriyāḥ pūrvameva ye|| 77.29 ||
mlecchacchadmāṇyupādāya jalpīśaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ|
te mlecchavācaḥ satatamāryavācaśca sarvadā|| 77.30 ||
jalpīśaṃ sevamānāste gopāyanti ca taṃ haram|
ta eva tu gaṇāstasya mahārājamanoharāḥ|| 77.31 ||
toṣayitvā tathā sarvān jalpīśaṃ pūjayennaraḥ|
varadābhayahasto’yaṃ dvibhujaḥ kundasannibhaḥ|| 77.32 ||
tatpuruṣasya tu mantreṇa pūjayed devamuttamam|
evaṃ puṇyakaraḥ pīṭho jalpīśasya mahātmanaḥ|
evaṃ jñātvā naro yāti śaṃkarasya puraṃ prati|| 77.33 ||
|| iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe saptasaptatitamo’dhyāyaḥ|| 77 ||

<78>
|| aṣṭasaptatitamo’dhyāyaḥ ||
||mārkaṇḍeya uvāca||
etacchrutvā tu saṃvādamuttamaṃ śaṃkarasya ca|
bhairavasya tu vetālasahitasya mahātmanaḥ|| 78.1 ||
bhūyaśca sagaro rājā munimaurvaṃ mahāmatim|
papraccha modasaṃhṛṣṭaḥ sūnṛtaṃ cedamuttamam|| 78.2 ||
||sagara uvāca||
vicitramidamākhyātaṃ bhagavanmunisattama|
kāmarūpasya pīṭhasya saṃsthānaṃ nirṇayaṃ tathā|| 78.3 ||
bhūyaśca śrotumicchāmi vistareṇa mahāmate|
vāyavyasyātha madhyasya pūrvabhāgasya nirṇayam|| 78.4 ||
yathā yasmin niṣṭhito’sti mahādevo’mbikā tathā|
tatsarvaṃ muniśārdūla kathaya śrotumutsahe|| 78.5 ||
||aurva uvāca||
ukto vāyavyabhāgasya nirṇayo nṛpasattama|
nairṛtyottaramadhyādreḥ śṛṇvidānīṃ vinirṇayam|| 78.6 ||
bahurokā nāma nanī karatoyā pradakṣiṇe|
uttaraśrāvaṇī cāste tat pūrvaṃ kāmarūpakam|| 78.7 ||
suraso nāma jīmūtaḥ kāmarūpaṃ tataḥ sthitaḥ|
niḥsṛtā bahuroketi nadī tasmād vṛṣapradā|| 78.8 ||
āsanne surasākhyasya śivaliṅgo mahāvṛṣaḥ|
māheśvarī tatra devī yonimaṇḍalarūpiṇī|| 78.9 ||
snātvā tu bahurokāyāmāruhya surasācalam|
mahāvṛṣaṃ pūjayitvā mahādevīṃ maheśvarīm|| 78.10 ||
dhūtapāpo jitadvandvaḥ punaryonau na jāyate|
caturbhūjo vṛṣārūḍho varadābhayaśūladhṛk|| 78.11 ||
śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśau jaṭāvāna sa mahāvṛṣaḥ|
aghorasya tu mantreṇa pūjā’sya parikīrtitā|| 78.12 ||
kāmeśvaryāḥ svarūpaṃ tu māheśvaryāḥ prakīrtitam|
pūjāpi yadvadevāsyāstadvatphalapradāyikā|| 78.13 ||
tatra vasiṣṭhakuṇḍaṃ tu vasiṣṭhamunisevitam|
yatra sthito vasiṣṭhastu narakeṇa nivāritaḥ|| 78.14 ||
aprāpya gantuṃ jīmūtaṃ nīlākhyaṃ vāśapattu tam
svasnānārthaṃ kṛtaṃ tatra kuṇḍaṃ devagaṇārcitam|| 78.15 ||
tatra snātvā naro yāti nākapṛṣṭhaṃ yathecchayā|
surasasya ca pūrvasyāṃ kṛttivāsāhvayo giriḥ|| 78.16 ||
kṛttivāsāḥ svayaṃ tatra satyā sahāvasat purā|
candrikākhyā nadī yatra tasyāṃ snātvā divaṃ vrajet|| 78.17 ||
candrikāyāṃ naraḥ snātvā sampūjya kṛttivāsasam|
bhādraśuklacaturthyāṃ tu niṣkalaṅko bhavennaraḥ|| 78.18 ||
pūrṇabhādrapadaṃ māsaṃ candrikāyāṃ narottamaḥ|
snātvā gacchati bhūteśaṃ dṛṣṭvai kṛttivāsasam|| 78.19 ||
uttarasrāviṇī nityaṃ candrikākhyā saridvarā|
nātidūre candrikāyāḥ pūrvasyāṃ diśi phenilā|| 78.20 ||
saṃjñayā ca saricchreṣṭhā śatānandāvatāritā|
brahmaṇo duhitā sā tu gaṅgā parvatasambhavā|| 78.21 ||
phenilāyāṃ naraḥ snātvā brahmotthānadine punaḥ|
phālgune māsi narakaṃ jitvā svargamavāpnuyāt|| 78.22 ||
tataḥ sitāhvayā pūrvaṃ sariduttaragāminī|
tasyāṃ snātvā mahācaitryāṃ gaṅgāsnānaphalaṃ labhet|| 78.23 ||
tataḥ pūrvaṃ sumadanā yojanadvatayāntare|
nadī janakarājena samārādhya vṛṣadhvajam|| 78.24 ||
hitāya bhairavākhyasya sutīkṣṇādavatāritā|
sutīkṣṇaṃ girimāruhya snātvā sumadanājale|| 78.25 ||
māghaśuklacaturthyāṃ tu pūjayitvā maheśvaram|
saṃprāpya sakalān kāmān śivalokāya gacchati|| 78.26 ||
etā nadyaḥ kāmarūpe nairṛtyāmuttarasravāḥ|
pīṭhasya pūrvatastatra tripurā yatra pūjyate|| 78.27 ||
evaṃ te kathitaṃ rājan mahāpuṇyadamuttamam|
kāmarūpasya nairṛtyāṃ yatra śambhuḥ sadāmbikā|| 78.28 ||
punareva mahārāja yā nadyo dakṣiṇasravāḥ|
himavatprabhavā yātāḥ kramaśaḥ śṛṇu bhūpate|| 78.29 ||
agadasya nadasyordhvaṃ bhadrākhyā tu mahānadī|
bhādre kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ yasyāṃ snātvā divaṃ vrajet|| 78.30 ||
tataḥ pūrvaṃ subhadrākhyā nadī puṇyatamā sadā|
vaiśākhasya tṛtīyāyāṃ yasyāṃ snātvā divaṃ vrajet|| 78.31 ||
tatastu mānasā nāma nadī puṇyatamā matā|
saraso mānasākhyāt tu tṛṇabindvavatāritā|| 78.32 ||
vaiśākhaṃ sakalaṃ māsaṃ tasyāṃ snātvā narottamaḥ|
viṣṇulokamavāpyaiva tato mokṣamavāpnuyāt|| 78.33 ||
himavannikaṭe śailo vibhrāṭaḥ sa mahādyutiḥ|
yasmin vasati bhūteśaḥ sadā bhairavarūpadhṛk|| 78.34 ||
tasmāt tu bhairavī nāma nadī puṇyodakā śubhā|
praṅ mānasādyā sravati gaṅgeva phaladāyinī|| 78.35 ||
yasyāṃ vasantasamaye snātvā gacchati ve divam|
yasyāṃ sampūjya kāmākhyāmiṣṭaṃ jñānamavāpnuyāt|| 78.36 ||
sampūjyātha mahāmāyāṃ dviguṇaṃ prāpnuyāt phalam|
ūrdhvaṃ tato devagaṅgā varṇāsākhyā saridvarā|| 78.37 ||
himavatprabhavā nityaṃ phaladā mānasopamā|
subhadrādyāstu yāḥ proktā varṇāsāntāḥ saridvarāḥ|| 78.38 ||
himavatprabhavāstāstu sarvā evottaraplavāḥ|
pūrve tu madanāyāstu brahmakṣetrasya paścime|| 78.39 ||
ravikṣetraṃ yatra deva ādityaḥ satataṃ sthitaḥ|
bhairavasya hitārthāya yatra sarveśvarāḥ sthitāḥ|| 78.40 ||
kāmarūpe mahāpīṭhe brahmendravaruṇādayaḥ|
tadā tattvāhvaye śaile śrī sūryo’pi vyavasthitaḥ|| 78.41 ||
trisrotā nāma yasyāsti nadī pūrvadiśi sthitā|
kapotakaraṇaṃ paścādasya kuṇḍadvayaṃ sthitam|| 78.42 ||
kapotakuṇḍe vidhivat snātvā karaṇakuṇḍake|
tattvācalaṃ samāruhya sampūjya ca divākaram|| 78.43 ||
sakṛdeva naro yāti bhāskarasya gṛhaṃ prati|
sūryaraśmisamudbhūtaṃ kapotakaraṇāmṛtam|| 78.44 ||
puṇyatoyasamākhyātaṃ pāpaṃ kapota me hara|
ityanena tu mantreṇa snātvā kapotapuṣkare|| 78.45 ||
karaṇaṃ samupaspṛśya tattvaśaile raviṃ yajet|
travidhaṃ brahmabījaṃ tu sahasrapadamantataḥ|| 78.46 ||
raśmaye’pi caturthyāṃ tu devo jāyā tu ceṣṭataḥ|
aṅgabījamidaṃ proktamādityasyātikāmadam|| 78.47 ||
padmāsanaḥ padmakaraḥ padmagarbhasamadyutiḥ|
saptāśvaḥ saptarajjuśca dvibhujo bhāskaraḥ sadā|| 78.48 ||
vartulaṃ maṇḍalaṃ cāsya aṣṭapatrasamanvitam|
aṅguṣṭhāgrāṅgulīnāṃ ca hṛdādīnāṃ tathā ca ṣaṭ|| 78.49 ||
aṅgamantreṇa sahita upānte vahnisaṃyutaḥ|
sarvanyāse samuddiṣṭo mantraḥ sarvaphalapradaḥ|| 78.50 ||
hṛcchirastu śikhāvarmanetrāsyodarapṛṣṭhataḥ|
bāhvoḥ pāṇyorjaṅghayostu pādayoścāpi vinyaset|| 78.51 ||
jaghane ca samastāni kramānmantrākṣarāṇi ca|
kramāccottarataḥ proktaḥ pūjane parikīrtitaḥ|| 78.52 ||
visarjanaṃ tathaiśānyāṃ vidyādyā dalaśaktayaḥ|
nirmālyadhṛk tattvacaṇḍo māṭharādyāstu pārśvayoḥ|| 2.8.53 ||
bījamuttaratantrasya pūrvataḥ pratipāditam|
anena vidhinā tattve pūjayitvā narottamaḥ|| 78.54 ||
sa kāmānakhilān prāpya ihaloke pramodate|
sukhī śeṣe tathā gacched bhāskarasyālayaṃ prati|| 78.55 ||
nātidūre bhāskarasya dakṣiṇasyāṃ śubhāhvayaḥ|
tasyordhvasānau vasati liṅgaṃ śāṃkaramuttamam|| 78.56 ||
parivārya sadā yānti mahākāyāstu vānarāḥ|
parivāryātiṣṭhante sevamānāśca śaṅkaram|| 78.57 ||
trisrotāyāṃ naraḥ snātvā yaḥ paśyet tu śubhācale|
mahātmānaṃ mahādevaṃ kāmamiṣṭaṃ labhennaraḥ|| 78.58 ||
tataḥ pūrvaṃ suranadī nāmnā kusumamālinī|
kṣīrodākhyāparā tasmāt te gate dakṣiṇasrave|| 78.59 ||
ete api mahārāja puṇyatoye’mṛtasrave|
tayoḥ snātvā naro yāti śaṅkarasyālayaṃ prati|| 78.60 ||
tato’pi pūrvato devī lolākhyā cāparā nadī|
yasyāṃ snātvā mahānadyāṃ śivalokāya gacchati|| 78.61 ||
tataḥ pūrvaṃ śivā caṇḍī caṇḍikākhyā mahānadī|
niryāti dhavalākhyāt tu parvatāt sumanoharāt|| 78.62 ||
śivaliṅgadvayaṃ tatra nātidūre vyavasthitam|
golokaṃ cātha śṛṅgaṃ ca krośamātrāntare sthitam|| 78.63 ||
caṇḍikāyāṃ naraḥ snātvā āruhya dhavaleśvaram|
dakṣiṇaṃ sāgaraṃ vīkṣya pṛṣṭvā golokasaṃjñakam|| 78.64 ||
tato’vatīrya ca punaḥ śṛṅgiṇaṃ bhūmipīṭhakam|
śivapūjāvidhānena pūjayitvā maheśvaram|| 78.65 ||
aśvamedhasya yajñasya phalaṃ samprāpya mānavaḥ|
sarvān kāmānavāpyeha dehānte śivatāṃ vrajet|| 78.66 ||
etā yāḥ kathitā nadyaḥ sarvā vai dakṣiṇasravāḥ|
tasmādīśānakāṣṭhāyāṃ parvato gandhamādanaḥ|| 78.67 ||
yatra bhṛṅgāhvayaṃ liṅgaṃ śivasyāste mahattaram|
sa eva parvataśreṣṭhaḥ prāptaḥ kṣetrasya paścime|| 78.68 ||
dhṛtvā brahmaśilāṃ devīṃ sāvitraṃ pratigāminī|
gandhamādanakasyānte bhṛṅgeśasya padadvayam|| 78.69 ||
sravadgaṅgājalaṃ cāste kuṇḍaṃ tatrāntarālakam|
antarālakakuṇḍe tu snātvā pītvā ca tajjalam|| 78.70 ||
bhṛṅgeśasya tato dṛṣṭvā śilāsaṃsthaṃ padadvayam|
pūjayitvā mahābhṛṅgaṃ gāṇapatyamavāpnuyāt|| 78.71 ||
śambhūpādasamudbhūtamantarāladṛśākaram|
vṛṣadhvajapadānāṃ tvaṃ saṃyojaya mahāvṛṣa|| 78.72 ||
ityanena tu mantreṇa snānaṃ kṛtvāntarājale|
bhṛṅgadevaṃ tataḥ paśyet kubjapīṭhāntavāsinam|| 78.73 ||
maṇikūṭasyātha girergandhamādanakasya ca|
madhye sravati lauhityo brahmaṇāgnisamutthitaḥ|| 78.74 ||
varṇāśāyā dakṣiṇasyāṃ lauhityo nāma sāgaraḥ|
maṇikūṭaḥ sthitaḥ pūrve hayagrīvo hariryataḥ|| 78.75 ||
sa hayagrīvarūpeṇa viṣṇurhatvā jvarāsuram|
nihatya sa hayagrīvaḥ krīḍāyai yatra saṃsthitaḥ|| 78.76 ||
hatvā jvaraṃ tathā viṣṇustatra vāsamathākarot|
naradevāsurādīnāṃ yathā bhavati vai hitam|| 78.77 ||
jvareṇāpīḍita tanurjvaraṃ hatvā mahāsuram|
sarvalokahitārthāya so’gadasnā namacaret|| 78.78 ||
agadasnānasambhūtaṃ saṃjātaṃ ca mahāsaraḥ|
tasya svayaṃ hayagrīvo nāma cakre’punarbhavam|| 78.79 ||
na punarjāyate yasmāt tatra snātvā narottamaḥ|
apunarbhavasaṃjñaṃ tat sarastu parikīrtitam|| 78.80 ||
maṇikaṭācale viṣṇurhayagrīvasvarūpadhṛk|
śatavyāmapramāṇena vistareṇaiva śobhitam|| 78.81 ||
tasmāt pūrve bhadrakāmaḥ parvatastu trikoṇakaḥ|
yatra kālahayo nāma śivaliṅgo vyavasthitaḥ|| 78.82 ||
tasyāsanne dakṣiṇasyāmapunarbhavakuṇḍakam|
apunarbhūḥsarastīre parvate bhadrakāmade|| 78.83 ||
haravothīti vikhyāta śilā brahmasvarūpiṇī|
tatra yogī mahādevo yogajño dhyānatatparaḥ|| 78.84 ||
yaṃ dṛṣṭvā yogavān martyo mṛto mokṣamavāpnuyāt|
tasyāmeva śilāyāṃ tu gokarṇo nāma śaṅkaraḥ|| 78.85 ||
gokarṇo nihato yena andhakasya sakhā purā|
gokarṇasya tathaiśānyāṃ kedāraḥ śambhurantataḥ|| 78.86 ||
tato’ndhakasamaḥ proktaḥ kamalākarabhogadhṛk|
yatrāsti śambhuḥ kedāraḥ sa girirmadanāhvayaḥ|| 78.87 ||
tatraiva kamalaḥ proktaḥ sa mahātmālayapradaḥ|
snātvā’punarbhavajale dṛṣṭvā gokarṇayoginau|| 78.88 ||
kedārakamalau dṛṣṭvā muktirmādhavadarśane|
dṛṣṭvā tu mādhavaṃ devaṃ tataḥ kāmaṃ vilokayet|| 78.89 ||
kāmaṃ vilokya tatrastho nirīkṣedapunarbhavam|
evaṃ kṛtvā pīṭhayātrāmanena kramayogataḥ|| 78.90 ||
sapta pūrvān sapta parānātmānaṃ daśa pañca ca|
pitṛnuddhṛtya tridivaṃ nayet sa puruṣottamaḥ|| 78.91 ||
viṣṇusthānasamudbhūtā punarbhavaharīśvara|
pāpaṃ hara svargahetorjitasaṅgamahodadhe|| 78.92 ||
anenaiva tu mantreṇa snāyād vīro’punarbhave|
hayagrīvasya tantraṃ tu puraiva pratipāditam|| 78.93 ||
rūpaṃ śṛṇu mahārāja cintayet tasya yādṛśam|
karpūrakundadhavalaḥ sitapadmoparisthitaḥ|| 78.94 ||
caturbhujaḥ kumḍalādinānālaṅkārabhūṣitaḥ|
varadābhayahastastu vāmahastadvayena tu|| 78.95 ||
pustakaṃ sitapadmaṃ ca dhatte hastadvaye’pare|
śrīvatsakaustubhoraskaḥ kvacicca garuḍāsanaḥ|| 78.96 ||
sarva uttaratantroktaḥ kramo grāhyaḥ prapūjane|
viṣvakseno hayārestu nirmālyadhṛgvisarjane|| 78.97 ||
śilārūpapraticchannaḥ sadāste garuḍadhvajaḥ|
krīḍamāno’tha gandharvaiḥ sthito lokahitāya ca|| 78.98 ||
hayagrīvasya mantrasya siddhirlakṣadvayena tu|
yāvakaiḥ pāyasairājyairhomaṃ kurvan puraścaret|| 78.99 ||
ekenaiva tu rājendra puraścaraṇakarmaṇā|
iṣṭasiddhimavāpyeha viṣṇulokamavāpnuyāt|| 78.100 ||
mantraistu pañcavaktrāṇāṃ pañcamūrtīḥ sadārcayet|
pūrve tatpuruṣādīnāṃ kāmādīn pūjako dvijaḥ|| 78.101 ||
kāmastatpuruṣo jñeyo yogīśānaḥ prakīrtitaḥ|
adhoro hyatha gokarṇaḥ kedāro vāmadevakaḥ|| 78.102 ||
sadyojātastu kamalāmantraistaistaiḥ prapūjayet|
parvataśceva kedāraḥ śivagaṅgā tu kālikā|| 78.103 ||
hayagrīvasya pūrvasyāṃ kedārasya tu paścime|
chāyābhogāhvayasthānaṃ purī bhogavatī tathā|| 78.104 ||
yo gacchenmaṇikūṭākhyāt kautukācca punarbhavam|
sa sarvatīrthayātrāṇāṃ phalamāpnoti mānavaḥ|| 78.105 ||
jyaiṣṭhe māsi site pakṣe pañcadaśyaṣṭamīṣu ca|
snātvā’punarbhavajale yaḥ paśyed vidhivaddharim|
sa sarvaṃ kulamuddhṛtya viṣṇusāyujyamāpnuyāt|| 78.106 ||
jyeṣṭha tu sakalaṃ māsaṃ nityaṃ paśyet tu yo harim|
harau vilīnatāṃ yāti sa sarvaiḥ sahitaḥ kulaiḥ|| 78.107 ||
etat te kathitaṃ puṇyaṃ maṇikūṭāhvayaṃ param|
vārāṇasīto hyadhikaṃ siddhavidyādharārcitam|| 78.108 ||
yaḥ paṭhecchṛṇuyādvipro maṇikūṭasya nirṇayam|
sa sarvavedasya phalaṃ prāpnotyeva na saṃśayaḥ|| 78.109 ||
|| iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe aṣṭasaptatitamo’dhyāyaḥ|| 78 ||

<79>
|| ekonāśītitamo’dhyāyaḥ ||
||aurvva uvāca||
tataḥ pūrva mahārāja darpaṇo nāma parvataḥ|
kubero yatra vasati dhanapālaiḥ samaṃ sadā|| 79.1 ||
yasminnāste madhyabhāge rohito rohitākṛtiḥ|
yasmiṃllohādikaṃ spṛṣṭaṃ svarmatāṃ yāti tatkṣaṇāt|| 79.2 ||
yatrātidūre sravati darpaṇo nāma vai nadaḥ|
himādriprabhavo nityaṃ lauhityasadṛśaḥ phalaiḥ|| 79.3 ||
samutpannaṃ hi lauhityaṃ sarvairdaivagaṇairhariḥ|
sarvatīrthodakaiḥ samyak snāpayāmāsa taṃ sutam|| 79.4 ||
tasya snānasamudbhūtaḥ pāpadarpasya pāṭanaḥ|
tenāyaṃ darpaṇo nāma purā devagaṇaiḥ kṛtaḥ|| 79.5 ||
tasmin snātvā nadavare yo’rcayed darpaṇācale|
kuberaṃ pratipattithyāṃ kārtike śuklapakṣake|| 79.6 ||
sa yāti brahmasadanamiha bhūtiśatairyutaḥ|
darpaṇād diśiṃ pūrvasyāmagnimālāhvayau giriḥ|| 79.7 ||
sarpākāraḥ saptaśatavyāmadīrdhodardhaṃ vistṛtaḥ|
tatra tiṣṭhati vai vahnirūrdhvabhāge’gnimaṇḍale|| 79.8 ||
sindūrapuñjasaṅkāśe cārudāruśilātale|
tasminnirindhano vahrirnityamadyāpi kaśate|| 79.9 ||
bhairavasya hitārthāya kāmākhyāparisevane|
pūrvameva sthitastatra sākṣād vahnirgaṇaiḥ saha|| 79.10 ||
lauhityapāthasi snātvā tvagnimālāhvayaṃ girim|
āruhya vahniṃ sampūjya modate viṣṇumandire|| 79.11 ||
purastādagnimālasya kuṇḍakaṃ vāruṇāhvayam|
tasya tīre giriśreṣṭho nāmnā kaṃsakaraḥ smṛtaḥ|| 79.12 ||
varumastatra vasati nityameva jalādhipaḥ|
tasmin kaṃsakare samyak pūjayitvā pracetasam|| 79.13 ||
snātvā ca vāruṇe kuṇḍe vāruṇaṃ lokamāpnuyāt|
ādyaṃ vyañjanamevātra pañcamasvarasaṃyutam|| 79.14 ||
śambhucūḍāśikāyuktaṃ kauveraṃ bījamucyate|
saptamo yaḥ pakārasya binduścandrardhasaṃyutaḥ|| 79.15 ||
makārapañmaḥ khyātaṃ tena vahni prapūjayet|
makārapañcamaḥ somabindunā vārumaḥ smṛtaḥ|| 79.16 ||
ebhirmantrairimān devān nityameva prapūjayet|
vāyukūṭo nāma giriḥ pūrvasyāṃ varumācalāt|| 79.17 ||
dvikhaṇḍo vāyubījena maṇḍalena samanvitaḥ|
vāyulokasthitaścandro yasmānniḥsṛtya mārutaḥ|| 79.18 ||
ūrdhvādhobhāgamāsādya nityaṃ vahati bhupate|
tatra vāyuṃ samabhyarcya vāyulokamavāpnuyāt|| 79.19 ||
pūrvaṃ vāyugireḥ śailaścandrakūṭa iti smṛtaḥ|
trikoṇaścandrasaṅkāśastadūrdhve candramaṇlam|| 79.20 ||
dvitīyavargasyādyaṃ tu bindunā samalaṅkṛtam|
candrabījamiti proktaṃ tena candraṃ prapūjayet|| 79.21 ||
adyāpi pratidarśe tu parvataṃ taṃ niśāpatiḥ|
pradakṣiṇīkarotyeva daśabhiścāpi khecaraiḥ|| 79.22 ||
tasyaiva pūrvabhāge tu somakuṇḍāhvayaṃ saraḥ|
tatra snātvā ca pītvā ca naraḥ kaivalyamaśnute|| 79.23 ||
svargādavataraccandraḥ kāmākhyāsevane yadā|
tadā tadraśmisaṅghātānniḥ sṛtāstoyarāśayaḥ|| 79.24 ||
taistoryarvāsavaḥ kuṇḍamakarodindracandrayoḥ|
madhye puṇyatame sthāne svayaṃ brahmaśilopari|| 79.25 ||
candraśmisamudbhūta candrakuṇḍamahedadhe|
yaṃ yaṃ bhāvaṃ samāsādya tvaṃ candra kaluṣa hara|| 79.26 ||
sudhāsravaṇamāhlāda tvaṃ candra kaluṣaṃ hara|
ityanena tu mantreṇa yaḥ snātvā candrapāthasi|| 79.27 ||
candrakūṭaṃ samāruhya pūjayet yastu taṃ naraḥ|
avicchinnā santatistu sukāntā tasya jāyate|| 79.28 ||
paratra candrabhavanaṃ bhittvā yāti paraṃ padam|
tīre tu candrakūṭasya nandano nāma vai giriḥ|| 79.29 ||
tasmin vasati śakrastu kāmākhyāsevane rataḥ|
pañcabhāvaṃ sāmasādya sarvadeveśvaro hariḥ|
sevituṃ tridaśeśānīṃ satataṃ vartate naraḥ|| 79.30 ||
candrakūṭagireryāmyabhāge girijanārdanaḥ|
tasya yāmye tvadhobhāge aśvakrāntāhvayaṃ saraḥ|| a ||
na tasya sadṛśaṃ tīrthamasti brahmāṇḍagocare|
jale sthale mṛtā ye’tra yānti brahma sanātanam|| ā ||
janārdanagirau viṣṇuḥ kūrmarūpasvarūpadhṛk|
śilāṃ bhittvā sthitastatra devagandharvasevitaḥ|| i ||
aśvakrāntajale snātvā pūjayitvā janārdhanam|
vaṃśakoṭiṃ samuddhṛtya svayaṃ syāt puruṣottamaḥ|| ī ||
candrakūṭasya tu girernandanasya tathā gireḥ|
pratidarśaṃ tathā candraḥ pradakṣiṇayati tridhā|| 79.31 ||
candrakūṭajale snātvā samāruhyātha nandanam|| 79.32 ||
ārādhya śakraṃ lokeśaṃ mahāphalamavāpnuyāt|
nandanāt pūrvabhāge tu bhasmakūṭo mahāgiriḥ|| 79.33 ||
yaḥ svayaṃ bhargarūpaḥ sa sadā cecchāntamuttamam|
dakṣiṇe bhasmakūṭasya devī pīyūṣadhāriṇī|| 79.34 ||
urvaśī nāma vikhyātā śakraprītikarī sadā|
devairyat sthāpitaṃ pūrvamabhṛtaṃ bhojanāya vai|| 79.35 ||
kāmākhyāyāstadādāya svayaṃ tiṣṭhati corvaśī|
śilārūpo harastāṃ tu samāvṛtyaiva tiṣṭhati|| 79.36 ||
sā caivāmṛtarāśiṃ tu kṛtvā kiṃcana kiṃcana|
upasthāpayate nityaṃ kāmākhyāyonimaṇḍale|| 79.37 ||
sudhāśilāntarasthā tu urvaśīkuṇḍavāsinī|
urvaśībhasmakūṭasya madhye kuṇḍaṃ sādavṛtam|| 79.38 ||
dvātriṃśaddhanurākīrṇaṃ pañcāśaddhanurāyatam|
tatra snātvā ca pītvā ca narā mokṣamavāpnuyāt|| 79.39 ||
kāmākhyāyoniraiśānīṃ diśaṃ yāti sadaiva hi|
bhasmakūṭe praviśati urvaśīmapi yogino|| 79.40 ||
āpyāyitā cāmṛtena nityaṃ devī pramodate|
modayuktā mahādevī kāmena modate sadā|| 79.41 ||
bhasmakūṭasya caiśānyāṃ maṇikūṭo mahāgiraḥ|
maṇikarṇo nāma harastatra tiṣṭhati liṅgakam|| 79.42 ||
sa sadyojātarūpastu maṇikarṇa itīritaḥ|
sadyojātasya mantreṇa pūjitavyaḥ sadāśivaḥ|| 79.43 ||
candratīrthajale snātvā dṛṣṭvā candraṃ savāsavam|
maṇikarṇeśvaraṃ dṛṣṭvā muktirbhasmācalaṃ gate|| 79.44 ||
śvetaḥ śvetāmbaradharo daśāśvo hemabhūṣitaḥ|
gadāpāṇirdvibāhuśca kartavyo varadaḥ śaśī|| 79.45 ||
sahasranetro gaurāṅgo dvibhujo vāmastagam|
vajraṃ gadāṃkuśaṃ dhatte dakṣiṇenāpi pāṇinā|| 79.46 ||
airāvatagajasthastu bāṇatūṇīrabandhanaḥ|
dhanuśca kakṣe gṛhṇāti sevamāno maheśvarīm|| 79.47 ||
vakārānantaro varṇaścandrabindusamanvitaḥ|
śakrabījamiti proktaṃ śakraṃ tena prapūjayet|| 79.48 ||
nadī sumaṅgalā nāma himaparvatanirgatā|
pūrvasyāṃ maṇikūṭasya sadā sravati śobhanā|| 79.49 ||
maṇikūṭaṃ samārūhya yastāṃ paśyati vai nadīm|
sa gaṅgāsnānajaṃ puṇyamavāpya tridivaṃ vrajet|| 79.50 ||
maṇikūṭācalāt pūrvaṃ matsyadhvajakulācalaḥ|
nirdagdho yatra madano haranetrāgninā punaḥ|| 79.51 ||
śarīraṃ prāpa tapasā samārādhya vṛṣadhvajam|
tatra matsyasvarūpastu kāmadeva samaṃsthita|| 79.52 ||
adhityakāyāṃ pṛthivīṃ bīkṣamāmaḥ samantataḥ|
nadī tu śāśvatī nāma tatrāste dakṣimasravā|| 79.53 ||
saraḥ kāmasaro nāma tatra śaile vyavasthitam|
śāśvatyāṃ vidhivatsnātvā pītvā kāmasaro’mbhasi|| 79.54 ||
gandhamādanapūrvasyāṃ sukrānto nāma parvataḥ|| 79.55 ||
tatprānte vāsavaṃ kuṇḍaṃ vāsavāmṛtabhojanam|
yatra sthitvā dakṣiṇasyāṃ purā śakraḥ śacīpatiḥ|| 79.56 ||
amṛtaṃ śrāntadehastu kāmarūpāntare papau|
snātvā tu vāsave kuṇḍe samāruhya sukāntakam|| 79.57 ||
vāsavasya priyo bhūtvā śakralokamavāpnuyāt|
pūrvasyāṃ tu sukāntasya rakṣaḥkūṭāhvayo giriḥ|| 79.58 ||
yatrāste satataṃ devo nirṛtī rākṣaseśvaraḥ|
khaḍgahasto mahākāyo vāme carmadharastathā|| 79.59 ||
jaṭājūṭasamāyuktaḥ prāṃśuḥ kṛṣṇācalopamaḥ|
dvibhujaḥ kṛṣṇavāsāstu gardabhoparisaṃsthitaḥ|| 79.60 ||
prāntopāntau binducandrasahitāvādireva ca|
nairṛtyaṃ kathitaṃ bījaṃ tena taṃ paripūjayet|| 79.61 ||
rakṣaḥ kūṭaṃ samāruhya niṛtiṃ rākṣaseśvaram|
yaḥ pūjayet vidhānena caṇḍikāṃ rākṣaseśvarīm|| 79.62 ||
na tasya rākṣasebhyo’sti bhayaṃ nṛpa kadācana|
rākṣasāśca piśācāśca vetālā gaṇanāyakāḥ|| 79.63 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā puruṣaṃ rājan sarvadaiva prabibhyati|
rakṣaḥkūṭāt pūrvadiśi bhairava nāma mādhavaḥ|| 79.64 ||
pāṇḍunātha iti khyāto grāvarūpeṇa saṃsthitaḥ|
taṃ pāṇḍunāthaṃ satatamaṣṭākṣarabhavottaram|| 79.65 ||
tenaiva pūjayed devaṃ pāṇḍunāthāhvayaṃ harim|
varṇena raktagaurāṅgaṃ gadāpadmadharaṃ kare|| 79.66 ||
dakṣiṇe cakraśktī ca bāhubhyāmapi bibhratam|
caturbhujaṃ raktapadmasaṃsthitaṃ mukuṭojjvalam|| 79.67 ||
kuṇḍale bibhrataṃ śuddhe śrīvatsoraskamuttamam|
namo nārāyaṇāyeti mūlabījena vā hareḥ|| 79.68 ||
evaṃ sampūjayed bhūpa caturvargasya siddhaye|
pāṇḍunāthasyottarasyāṃ brahmakūṭāhvayaṃ saraḥ|| 79.69 ||
brahmaṇā nirmitaṃ pūrvaṃ snānārthaṃ svargavāsinām|
āyāmena śatavyāmaṃ vistīrṇaṃ tvatadardhakam|| 79.70 ||
sarvapāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃ devalokāt samāgatam|
kamaṇḍalusamudbhūta brahmakuṇḍāmṛkasrava|| 79.71 ||
hara me sarvapāpāni puṇyaṃ svargaṃ ca sādhaya|
ityanena tu mantrema snātvā tasmin sarojale|| 79.72 ||
pāṇḍunāthaṃ ca sampūjya viṣṇusāyujyamāpnuyāt|
brahmakuṇḍajale snātvā pūjayitvā umāpatim|| 79.73 ||
vāyukūṭaṃ samāruhya muktimevāpnuyānnaraḥ|
pāṇḍunāthāt pūrvadiśi giriścitraharo hariḥ|| 79.74 ||
satataṃ yatra ramate viṣṇurvārāharūpadhṛk|
tatastu nīlakūṭākhyaṃ kāmākhyānilayaṃ param|| 79.75 ||
tatpūrvabhāge vasati brahmā brahmagiriḥ punaḥ|
brahmaśailasya pūrvasyāṃ bhūmipīṭhe vyavasthitam|| 79.76 ||
cārunimnaśubhāvartaṃ kāmākhyānābhimaṇḍalam|
tatrogratārārūpeṇa ramate parameśvarī|| 79.77 ||
tatra tenaiva rūpeṇa pūjitavyā śubhātmikā|
tasyāstu bījaṃ pūrvasminnuttare pratipāditam|| 79.78 ||
rūpaṃ śṛṇu naraśreṣṭha yena dhyeyā sadā śivā|
kṛṣṇā lambodarī dīrghā viralā raktadantikā|| 79.79 ||
caturbhujā kṛśāṅgī tu dakṣiṇe kartṛkharparau|
khaḍgaṃ candīvaraṃ vāme śīrṣe caikajaṭā punaḥ|| 79.80 ||
vāmapādaṃ śavasyorvornidhāyāṅghriṃ tu dakṣiṇām|
śavasya hṛdaye nyasya sāṭṭahāsaṃ prakurvatī|| 79.81 ||
nāgahāraśiromālābhūṣitā kāmadā parā|
trikoṇaṃ maṇḍalaṃ cāsyā huṅkāraṃ madhyabījakām|| 79.82 ||
dvāreśānāṃ yoginonāṃ nāmānyasyāstu tantrake|
jñeyāni naraśārdūla yat proktaṃ vāmyagocare|| 79.83 ||
urvaśyāṃ vidhivat snātvā spṛṣṭvā pāṇḍuśilāṃ tathā|
nīlakūṭaṃ samāruhya punaryonau na jāyate|| 79.84 ||
purandarapurāyāte vārāṇasyāḥ |
sudhāsaṃkīrṇatoyaudhaiḥ pāpa hara mamorvaśi|| 79.85 ||
amṛtasrāviṇīṃ devī sudhaughaparipūraṇī|
amṛtenāmṛtaṃ me’dya dehi devi mamorvaśi|| 79.86 ||
purandarapriye devi vārāmasyāḥ sadāmdhike|
lohityahradasaṃkīrṇe pāpaṃ hara manorvaśi|| 79.87 ||
ityebhiḥ stitibhirmantraiḥ snātvā puṇyorvaśījale|
sarvapāpavinirmukto viṣṇuloke viceṣṭate|| 79.88 ||
urvaśī dvibhujā proktā svarṇakaṅkaṇadhāriṇī|
sauvarṇapātramamṛtasrāvaṇāya bibharti ca|| 79.89 ||
śuklavastrā gauravarṇā pīnonnatapayodharā|
sarvāṅgasundarī śuddhā sarvābharaṇabhūṣitā|| 79.90 ||
etannāmādyakṣaraṃ tu mantramasyāḥ prakīrtitam|
umātantre tu gaditaṃ mantramasyāḥ prakīrtitam|| 79.91 ||
gaṇeśaḥ pūrvadvārasthaḥ kāmākhyāparvatasya tu|
tatraiva cāgnivetālaḥ sthito dvāri manoharaḥ|| 79.92 ||
tayo rūpaṃ ca mantraṃ ca yathoktaṃ śambhunā purā|
tadahaṃ prativakṣyāmi mahārāja śṛṇuṣva me|| 79.93 ||
oṃ nama ulkāmukhāyeti mūlabījādisaṅgatam|
mantraṃ siddhagaṇeśasya dvārasthasya prakīrtitam|| 79.94 ||
rūpaṃ tasya pravakṣyāmi gajavaktraṃ trilocanam|
lambodaraṃ caturbāhuṃ vyālayajñopavītinam|| 79.95 ||
śūrpakarṇa bṛhadgaṇḍamekadantaṃ pṛthūdaram|
dakṣiṇe tu kare daṇḍamutpalaṃ ca tathāpare|| 79.96 ||
laḍḍukaṃ paraśuṃ caiva vāmataḥ parikīrtitam|
bṛhattvākṣiptagaganaṃ pīnaskandhāṅghripāṇinam|| 79.97 ||
yuktaṃ buddhikubuddhibhyāmadhastānmūṣakānvitam|
tantrastu yādṛśaḥ proktaḥ pañcavaktrasya pujane|| 79.98 ||
sa eva tantro grāhyastu tādṛgvidhiniṣedhanam|
dvibhujaḥ pīnavadano raktanetro bhayaṅkaraḥ|| 79.99 ||
churikāṃ dakṣiṇe pāṇau vāme rudhirapātrakam|
daṃṣṭrākarālavadanaṃ kṛśo dhamanisantataḥ|| 79.100 ||
jaṭāṃ dīrghāṃ mūrdhni bibhradghorarāvayutastathā|
pacaturtho’gnibījena ṣaṣṭhasvaravibhūṣitaḥ|| 79.101 ||
agnivavetālabījo’yaṃ sarvatra bhayanāśakaḥ|
pūjayedagnivetālaṃ sarvatra bhayavāraṇam|| 79.102 ||
yaḥ pūjayet tasya pūnarbhūtādibhyo bhayaṃ nahi|
aṣṭānāmatha mantrāṇāṃ yoginīnāṃ kramānnṛpa|| 79.103 ||
śailaputrīpramukhyāṇāṃ mantrāṇyaṣṭākṣarāṇi tu|
vaiṣmavītantrasaṃstāni pūrvaproktāni tāni tu|| 79.104 ||
śailaputryāstathā cāṅgamantraṃ prāk pratipāditam|
rūpaṃ tu naraśārdūla yoginīnāṃ viśeṣataḥ|| 79.105 ||
pratyakṣarem bījena durgātantreṇa vā tvimāḥ|
netrabījenaiva pūjyā yoginyo nṛpasattama|| 79.106 ||
kātyāyānīṃ pādadurgāṃ durgātantreṇa pūjayet|
tadeva pūjanaṃ rūpaṃ tatpūrvaṃ pratipāditam|| 79.107 ||
kālarātryāstu mantreṇa kālarātriṃ prapūjayet|
kālarātryā rūpamantrau pūraiva pratipāditau|| 79.108 ||
mahāmāyātantramantraiḥ pūjayed bhuvaneśvarīm|
etāḥ sarvāstu yoginyaḥ kāmākhyāvat phalapradāḥ|| 79.109 ||
viśeṣo yatra naivokto rūpe tantre ca pūjane|
durgātantreṇa mantreṇa tatra pūjāṃ samācaret|| 79.110 ||
pratyekaṃ yoginīṃ yastu pūjayennarasattamaḥ|
sa sarvayajñasya phalaṃ prāpnoti narasattama|| 79.111 ||
nīlaśailasya pūrvasmin svarūpaṃ pratipāditam|
nābhimaṇḍalapūrvasyāṃ bhasmakṛṭasya dakṣiṇe|| 79.112 ||
pūrvasyāṃ karpaṭo nāma parvato yamarūpadhṛk|
tatra yāmyaśilā kṛṣṇā nīlāñjanasamaprabhā|| 79.113 ||
adhityakāyāṃ rājendra vyāmapañcasuvistṛtā|
pūyajet tatra śanaṃ pāṇau daṇḍaṃ sadaiva yaḥ|| 79.114 ||
dhatte tu pāṇinā nityaṃ prāṇidaṇḍasya sādhanam|
kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ tu dvibhujaṃ kirīṭamukuṭojjvalam|| 79.115 ||
dadhataṃ cāsiputrīṃ ca vāmapāṇau sadaiva hi|
kṛṣṇavastraṃ sthūlapādaṃ bahirniḥsṛtadantakam|| 79.116 ||
bhayābhayapradaṃ nityaṃ nṛṇāṃ mahiṣavāhanam|
pūjayet parayā bhaktyā yāmyabījena sādhakaḥ|| 79.117 ||
upāntavargasyādiryo varṇo bindvindusaṃyutaḥ|
yamabījamiti khyātaṃ yamasya prītidāyakam|| 79.118 ||
anenaiva tu mantreṇa śamanaṃ pūjayet tu yaḥ|
karpaṭākhye’calavare nāpamṛtyumavāpnuyāt|| 79.119 ||
pūrvasyāṃ karpaṭākhyāt tu śailaccitra iti smṛtaḥ|
yaḥ pūrvabhāgaprānte’būddiśyāgneyyāmavasthitaḥ|| 79.120 ||
pīṭhastu brahmagrāvastu sa prāk parvata ucyate|
tasmin vasanti satataṃ grahā nava yathecchayā|| 79.121 ||
tatra tān pūjayed yastu sa nāpnotyāpadaṃ kvacit|
rūpaṃ mantraṃ ca sūryasya candrasya pratipāditam|| 79.122 ||
saptānāmitareṣāṃ tu mantraṃ rūpaṃ śṛṇuṣva me|
raktāmbaradharaḥ śūlī śaktimāṃśca gadādharaḥ|| 79.123 ||
caturbhujo meṣaratho varado maṅgalo mataḥ|
pītāmbaradharaḥ śūlī pītamālyānulepanaḥ|| 79.124 ||
khaḍgacarmagadāpaṇiḥ siṃhastho varado budhaḥ|
svarṇagauraḥ pītavāsāḥ svarṇaparyaṃkasaṃsthitaḥ|| 79.125 ||
mālāṃ kamaṇḍaluṃ daṇḍaṃ vāmena varadāyakam|
caturbhujaṃ ca sarvajñaṃ cintayed devatīrthakam|| 79.126 ||
sarvairdevagaṇairnityaṃ namyamānaṃ manoharam|
śuklavastraṃ śuklavarṇaṃ śaṅkhanāgoparisthitam|| 79.127 ||
caturbhujaṃ pāśamālāṃ pustakaṃ ca varābhaye|
kramād dakṣimavāmāyāṃ dhatte daityaguruḥ sadā|| 79.128 ||
indranīlanibhaḥ śūlī varado gṛdhravāhanaḥ|
pāśabāṇāsanadharo dhyātavyo’rkasutaḥ sadā|| 79.129 ||
kāmadevasyabījaṃ tu mantraṃ bhaumasya kīrtitam|
durgāyā netrabījasya yattu madhyāvaraṃ śubham|| 79.130 ||
tanmantraṃ śaśiputrasya sarvakāmaphalapradam|
taṃkārapañcamādistu catuḥṣaṭsvarasaṃyutam|| 79.131 ||
gaṇeśabījāntamidaṃ gurormantra prakīrtitam|
bindvindusaṃyutaṃ cāpi pūrvavarmadvayaṃ punaḥ|| 79.132 ||
saptamasvarasaṃyukto makārastvādirantaram|
prāntavargādyakṣaraṃ tu bindvindubhyāṃ samanvitam|| 79.133 ||
bhavecchukrasya bījaṃ tu sarvakāmasamṛddhidam|
prāntavargādyakṣaraṃ tu candrabindusamanvitam|| 79.134 ||
ādyamantrasvaropetaṃ tadevetyādisaṃyutam|
śanaiścarasya mantro’yaṃ sarvadoṣavināśanaḥ|| 79.135 ||
binducandrasamāyuktaṃ nāmādyakṣarameva vā|
teṣāṃ sarvagrahāṇāṃ vai mantramaṅga prakīrtitam|| 79.136 ||
śāntike pauṣṭike kṛtye ebhirmantrairgrahānimān|
pūjayet sarvadā dhīro bhūtikāmo mahāmatiḥ|| 79.137 ||
varadābhayahastaśca khaḍgacarmadharastathā|
siṃhāsanagataḥ kṛṣṇā rāhurdhīraḥ pracakṣyate|| 79.138 ||
dhūmravarṇo viśālākṣaḥ puccharūpī caturbhujaḥ|
khaḍgacarmagadābāṇapāṇiḥ ketuḥ śavāsanaḥ|| 79.139 ||
upāntādirdvādaśena svarema sahitaḥ punaḥ|
upāntaḥ pañcamenendubindubhyāṃ sahitāvubhau|| 79.140 ||
mantro’yamanulomena rāhoḥ ketorvilomataḥ|
ādyakṣaraṃ pūrvavad vā mantrayuktamataitayoḥ|| 79.141 ||
evaṃ citre śailavare pūjayitvā navagrahān|
abhīṣṭāllam̐bhate kāmānnaraḥ śāntiṃ tathottamām|| 79.142 ||
citrakūṭāt tu pūrvasyāṃ kajjalācala uttamaḥ|
sarvavidyādharādyāstu santyasmin devayonayaḥ|| 79.143 ||
taṃ parvataṃ samāruhya praṇamya sakalān surān|
svargaṃ yānti naraśreṣṭha iha cāpyatulāṃ śriyam|| 79.144 ||
kajjalācalaśailāt tu pūrvasmiñchubhaparvataḥ|
śacyā sārdhaṃ purā reme yatra śakraḥ sureśvaraḥ|| 79.145 ||
tatpūrvasyāṃ mahādevī nadī kapilagaṅgikā|
tasyāṃ snātvā naro gaṅgāsnānajaṃ phalamāpnuyāt|| 79.146 ||
kāmākhyānilayāt pūrvaṃ dakṣiṇasyāṃ tathā diśi|
vidyate mahadāvartaṃ bhuvi brahmabilaṃ mahat|| 79.147 ||
paṃcaviṃśatimānena yojanānāṃ nareśvara|
tasmādāyāti sunadī sitāmbho’pama toyabhāk|| 79.148 ||
ko brahmā kīrtito devairyasmāt tasya bilāt sṛtā|
gaṃgeva phaladā yasmāt tasmāt kapilagaṃgikā|| 79.149 ||
snātvā kapilagaṅgāyāṃ sarvamanvantareṣu ca|
naraḥ svargamavāpyādau brahmalokaṃ tato vrajet|| 79.150 ||
atītya tāṃ nadīṃ pūrvabhāge damanikāhvayā|
nadī mahākṛṣṇatoyā pāpasya damanī tathā|| 79.151 ||
tato vṛddhāhvayā cābhūdaparā sariduttamā|
tasyā nadyāḥ pūrvabhāge gaṅgāvat phaladāyinī|| 79.152 ||
māghaṃ tu sakalaṃ māsaṃ snātvā muktimavāpnuyāt|
tathā damanikāyāṃ ca paraṃ nirvāṇamāpnuyāt|| 79.153 ||
tataḥ pūrve parā devī nāmnā sā sariduttamā|
mahatī divyayamunā yamunāvat phalapradā|| 79.154 ||
dakṣiṇādrisamudbhūtā dakṣiṇodadhigāminī|
tasyāṃ tu kārtikaṃ māsaṃ snātvā muktimavāpnuyāt|| 79.155 ||
iha caivottamān bhogān bhāgadheyāt pratiṣṭhitān|
tanmadhye bhairavo devo bhargasambhogasambhavaḥ|| 79.156 ||
durjayākhye varagirāvastyupatyakabhūtigaḥ|
yo’sau śarabharūpasya madhyakhaṇḍo’tibhairavaḥ|| 79.157 ||
sa eva bhairavākhyo’yaṃ pañcavaktrasya mantrakaiḥ|
sampūjya tatra matimān sa yāti śivalokatām|| 79.158 ||
kāmeśvarasya yā pūjā kathitā nīlanirṇaye|
sampūjya parvataśreṣṭhe durjaye cācalottame|| 79.159 ||
tatra bhairavaghaṅgāsti saro vai bhairavāhvayam|
tayoḥ snātvā naro yāti śivalokaṃ sanātanam|| 79.160 ||
durjayākhyasya pūrvasyāṃ pura nāma varāsanam|
taddakṣiṇe mahāśailaḥ kṣobhako nāma nāmataḥ|| 79.161 ||
tasmin girau śilāpṛṣṭhe raktadevī vyavasthitā|
pañcapuṣkariṇī nāmnā pañcayonisvarūpiṇī|| 79.162 ||
pañcabhirdurgāyonibhiḥ pūjayet paṃcavaktrakam|
sthitā ramayituṃ tatra nityameva himādrijā|| 79.163 ||
tacchailapūrvabhāge tu kāntā nāma mahānadī|
dakṣiṇaṃ sāgaraṃ yāti prathamaṃ cottarasravā|| 79.164 ||
divyaṃ kuṇḍaṃ mahākuṇḍaṃ tacchailopatyakākṣitau|
saṃstitaṃ tatra snātvā tu tāṃ devīṃ paripūjayet|| 79.165 ||
divyakuṇḍe naraḥ snātvā pañcapuṣkaraṇīṃ śivām|
yaḥ pūjayenmahābhāgaḥ sa yonau na hi jāyate|| 79.166 ||
pañcayonyaḥ puṣkariṇīḥ paṃcaiva parisaṃsthitāḥ|
yatastataḥ pañcarūpā pañcapuṣkariṇī matā|| 79.167 ||
yathābakalupuṣpāṇi tathaitāḥ pañcayonayaḥ|
pañcapuṣkariṇīdevyaḥ pracaṇḍāḥ sarvakāmadāḥ|| 79.168 ||
tripurāyāstu tantreṇa tāḥ pūjyāḥ sādhakottamaiḥ|
kāmeśvarītantramantrairathavā pūjayecchivām|| 79.169 ||
bālāyāstripurāyāstu mantramasyāḥ prakīrtitam|
kāmeśvaryāstu vā mantraṃ pūjane’syāḥ prakīrtitam|| 79.170 ||
ugracaṇḍā pracaṇḍā ca caṇḍogrā caṇḍanāyikā|
caṇḍā ceti ca yoginyaḥ pañcāsyāḥ parikīrtitāḥ|| 79.171 ||
śivaliṅgaṃ ca tatrāsti śilāyāṃ herukāhvayam|
devīdakṣiṇapūrvasyāṃ nāyakaṃ taṃ tu pūjayet|| 79.172 ||
bhairavasya tu mantreṇa pūjayitvā divaṃ vrajet|
nirmālyadhāriṇo devī caṇḍagaurīti kīrtitā|| 79.173 ||
etasyāṃ naraśārdūla purā bhargema bhāṣitā|
kāntāyāṃ salile snātvā vasante mānavottamaḥ|| 79.174 ||
rūpavān guṇavān bhūtvā śivalokāya gacchati|
kṣobhakākhyād mahāśailadaiśānyāṃ parvatottamaḥ|| 79.175 ||
tuṃgasandhyācalo nāma vasiṣṭho yatra śaptavān|
nimināmnastu rājarṣeḥ śāpād brahmasutaḥ purā|| 79.176 ||
vasiṣṭho hyaśarīro’bhūt tacchāpācca nimistathā|
tato brahmopadeśena nirjane kāmarūpake|| 79.177 ||
sandhyācale tapastepe tasya viṣṇurabhūt tadā|
pratyakṣastasya devasya varadānānmahāmuniḥ|| 79.178 ||
amṛtānyavatāryāśu kuṇḍaṃ kṛtvā girestaṭe|
tatra snātvā ca pītvā ca śarīraṃ prāpa pūritam|| 79.179 ||
tasmādamṛtakuṇḍācca sandhyā nāma nadīvarā|
niḥsṛtā tatra cāplutya cirāyuragado bhavet|| 79.180 ||
tasmāt pūrvaṃ tu lalitā lalitākhyā saridvarā|
sāgarād dakṣiṇāt pūrvaṃ mahādevāvatāritā|| 79.181 ||
vaiśākhaśuklapakṣasya tṛtīyāyāṃ narastu yaḥ|
kuryād vai lalitāsnānaṃ sa śambhusadanaṃ vrajet|| 79.182 ||
lalitāyāḥ pūrvatīre bhagavānannāma parvataḥ|
svayaṃ viṣṇurliṅgarūpi tatrāste bhagavān hariḥ|| 79.183 ||
lalitāyāṃ naraḥ snātvā dvādaśyāṃ śuklapakṣake|
bhagavantaṃ samāruhya yo yajet parameśvaram|| 79.184 ||
sa yāti viṣṇusadanaṃ śarīreṇa virājatā|
etāḥ pūrvoditā nadyaḥ sarvāścaivottarasravāḥ|| 79.185 ||
kramāt tu dakṣiṇaṃ yānti sāgaraṃ jāhnavīsamāḥ|
kāmākhyāṃ prathamaṃ dṛṣṭvā snātvā caivorvaśījale|
ya etāsu caret snānaṃ sa tu muktimavāpnuyāt|| 79.186 ||
|| iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe ekonāśītitamo’dhyāyaḥ|| 79 ||

<80>
|| aśītitamo’dhyāyaḥ ||
||śāśvatī kathitā yā tu nadī matsyadhvajāsitā|
tasyāḥ pūrve samākhyātā nadī dīpavatī matā|| 80.1 ||
eṣā ca himavajjātā chindantī dīpavattamaḥ|
tena devamanuṣyeṣu nadī dīpavatī smṛtā|| 80.2 ||
dīpavatyāḥ pūrvatastu śṛṅgāṭo nāma parvataḥ|
tatra devasya bhargasya liṅgamakaṃ pratiṣṭhitam|| 80.3 ||
sarit tu siddhā triḥsrotā dakṣiṇodadhigāminī|
śṛṅgāṭakasya satataṃ sravantī sā tu pādataḥ|| 80.4 ||
dakṣiṇaṃ sāgaraṃ yāti bhargasya priyakāriṇī|
salile yo naraḥ snātvā triḥsrotāyā narottamaḥ|| 80.5 ||
śṛṅgāṭakaṃ samāruhya pūjayelliṅgaśaṅkaram|
sa dīptakāyaḥ śuddhātmā prāpya kāmānihātulān|| 80.6 ||
ante bhargagṛhaṃ yāti tato mokṣamavāpnuyāt|
harastu dvibhujastasmin sadā vṛṣabhavāhanaḥ|| 80.7 ||
umayā ramate sārdhaṃ vāmadevasya mantrakaiḥ|
tantraiśca pūjayed devamumāmantreṇa caṇḍikām|| 80.8 ||
tatpūrvato nimnaga tu nāmnā tu vṛddhavedikā|
tasyāṃ snātvā phalaṃ martyo vedikāsnānajaṃ labhet|| 80.9 ||
tato bhaṭṭārikā nāma himaśailasamudbhavā|
mahānadī devagaṇairyā sadopāsyate sukham|| 80.10 ||
tasyāṃ yaḥ kurute snānaṃ yugādiṣu caturṣvapi|
sa yāti paramaṃ sthānaṃ tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam|| 80.11 ||
asti nāṭakaśaile tu saro mānasasannibham|
yatra sārdhaṃ śailaputryā jalakrīḍāṃ sadā haraḥ|| 80.12 ||
kurute naraśārdūla svarmapaṅkajaśobhite|
tasya paścānmadhyapūrvabhāgebhyastu sarittrayam|| 80.13 ||
avatīrṇaṃ prayātyeva dakṣiṇaṃ sāgaraṃ prati|
tasya paścimabhāge tu nadī dikkarikāhrayā|| 80.14 ||
diggajakṣatasaṃjātā tena dikkarikāhvayā|
madhyabhāgāt sṛtā yā tu śaṅkareṇāvatāritā|| 80.15 ||
vṛddhagaṃgāhvayā sā tu gaṃgeva phaladāyinī|
yā niḥsṛtā purvabhāgāt tasmād girivarānnadī|| 80.16 ||
suvarṇaśrīriti vikhyātā sā gaṅgāsadṛśīphale|
kurvatyāḥ sarasi snānaṃ pārvatyāśca śarīrataḥ|| 80.17 ||
niḥsṛtāḥ svarṇakaṇikāstā vahanti jalairimāḥ|
krīḍārthaṃ śambhunā gātre kaṇikābhiḥ samācitāḥ|| 80.18 ||
svasthānāt tatra saṃlagnāstataścandanabindavaḥ|
tā umāyāḥ śarīrāt tu saṃsravanti jalaiḥ saha|| 80.19 ||
tataḥ svarṇavahā nāma svarṇaśrīḥ sarvato’dhikā|
etāsu caitramāsaṃ tu snātvā martyo nararṣabhaḥ|| 80.20 ||
kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśyāṃ trikālaṃ yatra manāvaḥ|
ciraṃ devīgṛhe sthitvā śeṣe brahmagṛhaṃ vrajet|| 80.21 ||
bhūmāvavagataḥ paścātsārvabhaumo nṛpo bhavet|
vṛddhagaṅgājalasyāntastīre brahmasutasya vai|| 80.22 ||
viśvānāthāhvayo devaḥ śivaliṅgasamanvitaḥ|
viśvadevī mahādevī yonimaṇḍalarūpiṇī|| 80.23 ||
hayagrīveṇa yuyudhe tatra devo jagadpatiḥ|
hayagrīvaṃ yatra hatvā maṇikūṭaṃ purāgatam|| 80.24 ||
tatra yaḥ pūjayed durgāṃ śāradāṃ tantramantrakaiḥ|
hayagrīvasya mantreṇa tantreṇa garuḍadhvajam|| 80.25 ||
kāmeśvarasya tantreṇa mantreṇāpi ca śaṅkaram|
yo yajet parayā bhaktyā dvādaśyāṃ samupoṣitaḥ|| 80.26 |
aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu|
kalpakoṭitrayaṃ sthitvā śivagehe gṛhe hareḥ|| 80.27 ||
tāvantaṃ saṃsthitaḥ kālaṃ tāvantaṃ ca śivāgṛhe|
śeṣe bhuvaṃ samāsādya vedavid brāhmaṇo bhavet|| 80.28 ||
nadyāḥ svarṇaśriyaḥ pūrvaṃ nadī kāmāhvayā śubhā|
kāmāyāḥ pūrvabhāge tu nadī somāśanāhvayā|| 80.29 ||
somāśanāyāḥ pūrvasyāṃ nado nāmnā vṛṣodakā|
tataḥ pūrve kāmarūpaṃ pīṭhaṃ te jagatāṃ prasūḥ|| 80.30 ||
jaganmayo mahāmāyā devī dikkaravāsinī|
etā yāḥ kathitā nadyaḥ sakalā dakṣiṇasravāḥ|| 80.31 ||
tāsu snātvā ca pītvā ca svargalokamavāpnuyāt|
prānte dikkaravāsinyāḥ sadā vahati svarṇadī|| 80.32 ||
sitagaṅgāhvayā loke sākṣād gaṅgāphalapradā|
sā bhūmipīṭhasaṃsthā ca devī dikkaravāsinī|| 80.33 ||
antarjale plāvayantī yāti pratyakṣatāṃ suraiḥ|
sitagaṅgājale snātvā dṛṣṭvā śambhuṃ hariṃ vidhim|| 80.34 ||
iṣṭvā lalitakāntākhyāṃ punaryonau na jāyate|
liṅgasvarūpī bhagavācchambhustatra svayaṃ sthitaḥ|| 80.35 ||
viṣṇuḥ śilāsvarūpeṇa brahmaliṅgasvarūpadhṛk|
pīṭhe dikkaravāsinyā dvirīpā ramate śivā|| 80.36 ||
tīkṣṇakāntāhvayā tvekā yogratārā prakīrtitā|
parā lalitakāntākhyā yā śrīmaṅgalacaṇḍikā|| 80.37 ||
tasyāstu satataṃ rūpaṃ tīkṣṇakāntāhvayaṃ nṛpa|
kṛṣṇā lambodarī yā tu sā syādekajaṭā śivā|| 80.38 ||
tena rūpeṇa tāṃ dīvaṃ satataṃ paripūjayet|
aṅgamantraṃ ca rūpaṃ ca tasyāḥ prākpratipāditam|| 80.39 ||
trikoṇaṃ maṇḍalaṃ cāsyāḥ kartavyaṃ mantrapūrvakam|
ādau rekhe tataḥ paścāt surekheti padaṃ tataḥ|| 80.40 ||
tathā padaṃ cādhigamya tiṣṭhantivati padaṃ tataḥ|
maṇḍalasyāsya mantro’yaṃ tīkṣṇāyāḥ parikīrtitaḥ|| 80.41 ||
naratripuradevādiyamavetāladurdharāḥ|
gaṇaśrametyantakāntā dvārapālāḥ prakīrtitāḥ|| 80.42 ||
etāṃstu pūjayet samyaṅmaṇḍalasyāṣṭadikṣu vai|
ādau sambodhanaṃ kṛtvā vajrapuṣpaṃ tataḥ param|| 80.43 ||
vahnijāyaṃ tataḥ paścānmantrameṣāṃ prakīrtitam|
pātropakaraṇādīnāṃ sthānasyānyasya sarvataḥ|| 80.44 ||
sarvamuttaratantroktaṃ bhīṣaṇā bhagā|| 80.45 ||
vikaṭeti ca yoginyaḥ proktā yasyāstavaiva ṣaṭ|
he bhagavatyekajaṭe vidmahe padamantataḥ|| 80.46 ||
vikaṭadaṃṣṭre dhīmahi tannastāre pracodayāt|
eṣā tu tīkṣṇagāyatrī pīṭhadevyāḥ prakīrtitā|| 80.47 ||
nirmālyadhāriṇī cāsyā devī vikaṭacaṇḍikā|
mālā tu mṛnmayī proktā rudrākṣasambhavāpi vā|| 80.48 ||
viśeṣa eṣa devyāstu pūjane parikīrtitaḥ|
upacārādikaṃ kṛtyaṃ balidānaṃ japādikam|| 80.49 ||
sarvaṃ tu parvavad grāhyaṃ kāmākhyāpūjane yathā|
pāneṣu madirā śastā naro valiṣu pārthiva|| 80.50 ||
modako nārikelaṃ ca māṃsavyañjanamaikṣavam|
naivedyeṣu priyakarāstīkṣṇāyāḥ parikīrtitāḥ|| 80.51 ||
yaiṣā lalitakāntākhyā devī maṅgalacaṇḍikā|
varadābhyahastā sā dvibhujā gauradehikā|| 80.52 ||
raktapadmāsanasthā ca mṛkuṭojjvalamaṇḍitā|
raktakauśeyavasānā smitavaktrā śubhānanā|| 80.53 ||
navayauvanasampannā cārvaṅgī lalitaprabhā|
umāyā bhāṣitaṃ mantraṃ yat pūrvaṃ tvekamakṣaram|| 80.54 ||
mantramasyāstu tajjñeyaṃ tena devīṃ prapūjayet|
nārāyaṇyai vidmahe tvāṃ caṇḍikāyai tu dhīmahi|| 80.55 ||
tanno lalitakāṃnteti tataḥ paścāt pracodayāt|
eṣā lalitagāyatrī devyā iṣṭyai prakīrtitā|| 80.56 ||
lohitāṃgasya divasaḥ priyo’syāḥ parikīrtitaḥ|
kālo vasantakālaśca svaraścāpi tu pañcamaḥ|| 80.57 ||
aṣṭamyām̐ ca navamyāṃ tra pūjā kāryā vibhūtaye|
nirmālyadhāriṇī cāsyā devī lalitacaṇḍikā|| 80.58 ||
dūrvāṅkuraiḥ samāyuktamakṣataṃ prītida param|
ayamasyā viśeṣastu pūjane parikīrtitaḥ|| 80.59 ||
vaiṣṇavītantramantrasyatantraṃ grāhyaṃ tu pūjane|
upacāro baliścāsyā vihito yaḥ kramaḥ purā|| 80.60 ||
mahāmāyāmahādevyāstadgrāhyaṃ paripūjane|
svagātrarudhiraṃ dadyādātmanaśca hitāya vai|| 80.61 ||
paṭeṣu pratimāyāṃ vā ghaṭe maṅgalacaṇḍikām|
yaḥ pūjayed bhaumadine śubairdūrvāṅkuraiḥ śivām|| 80.62 ||
satataṃ sādhakaḥ so’pi kāmamiṣṭamavāpnuyāt|
evaṃ dikkaravāsinyāḥ kathitaḥ pūjanakramaḥ|| 80.63 ||
yacchrutvā nāśubhaṃ kiñcidāpnoti śravaṇe rataḥ|
dikkarastvaruṇaḥ proktastathā śambhuśca dikkaraḥ|| 80.64 ||
tasminnadhyuṣitā devī tasmād dikkaravāsinī|
jagattraye’pi yasyāstu sadṛśī kvāpi sandarī|| 80.65 ||
nānyāsti lalitā tana devī lalitakāntikā|
śaṅkarasya purā prokto grāhyo vai pūjanakramaḥ|| 80.66 ||
śṛṇu rājannavahito brahmaṇaḥ pūjanakramam|
brahmabojaṃ purā prāktaṃ tanmtraṃ sarvataścaret|| 80.67 ||
tenaiva taṃ tu sampūjya paraṃ nirvāṇamāpnuyāt|
etasya cāṅgamantra tu yathā bhargeṇa bhāṣitam|| 80.68 ||
vetālabhairavābhyāṃ tu rūpaṃ ca śṛṇu bhūmipa|
pastṛtīyaśca vahniśca śeṣasvarasamanvitaḥ|| 80.69 ||
candrabindusamāyukto brahmamantraṃ prakīrtitam|
anenaiva tu mantreṇa brāhmaṇaṃ yaḥ prapūjayet|| 80.70 ||
sa kāmamiṣṭaṃ saṃprāpya brahmalokeṣu modate|
brahmā kamaṇḍaludharaścaturvaktraścaturbhujaḥ|| 80.71 ||
kadācidraktakamale haṃsārūḍhaḥ kadācana|
varṇena raktagaurāṅgaḥ prāṃśustuṅgāṅga unnataḥ|| 80.72 ||
kamaṇḍaluṃ vāmakare sruca haste ca dakṣiṇe|
dakṣiṇādhastathā mālāṃ vāmādhaśca tathā sruvam|| 80.73 ||
ājyasthālī vāmapārśve devāḥ sarve’grataḥ sthitāḥ|
sāvitrī vāmapārśvasthā dakṣimasthā sarasvatī|| 80.74 ||
sarve ca ṛṣayo hyagre kuryādevaṃ vicintanam|
catuṣkoṇaṃ caturdvāramaṣṭapatrasamanvitam|| 80.75 ||
catuṣkoṇeṣvaṅkitaṃ tu srakkamaṇḍaluksruvaiḥ|
sammārjanādikaṃ sarvaṃ yāścānyāḥ pratipattayaḥ|| 80.76 ||
dṛṣṭvāścottaratantroktā yogapīṭhe’ṅgikādikāḥ|
ādhāraśaktipramukhāṃstathā sarvāṃstu pūjayet|| 80.77 ||
aṣṭapatreṣu padmasya dikpālāṃśca prapūjayet|
padmāsanāya vidyamahe haṃsarūḍhāya dhīmahi|| 80.78 ||
tanno brahmanniti padaṃ tataḥ paścāt pracodayāt|
eṣā tu brahmagāyatrī pūjayedanayā vidhim|| 80.79 ||
nirmālyadhārī caitasya sanatkumāra ucyate|
upacārāḥ pūrvavat tu netrāñjanavivarjitāḥ|| 80.80 ||
raktakauśeyavastraṃ tu brahmaprītikaraṃ param|
annaṃ sapāyasaṃ sarpapistilayuktaṃ ca bhojanam|| 80.81 ||
sitaraktasamāyuktaṃ candanaṃ parikīrtitam|
pārśvayoḥ śaṃkaraṃ viṣṇuṃ pūjane pūjayet puraḥ|| 80.82 ||
sruvādīn karasaṃsthāṃstu maṇḍale praripūjayet|
sarasvatīṃ ca sāvitriṃ haṃsa padmaṃ tathaiva ca|| 80.83 ||
ayaṃ viśeṣaḥ kathitaḥ praṇāmaścāsya daṇḍavat|
padmabījabhavā mālā japakarmaṇi kīrtitā|| 80.84 ||
pūrṇādarśau tithī grāhyī pūjākarmaṇi sarvadā|
kṣīreṇārghyaṃ pradadyāt tu sarvadā brahmaṇe nṛpa|| 80.85 ||
ayaṃ te kathito bhūpa yathā bhargeṇa bhāṣitaḥ|
darśayatā svaputrābhyāṃ kāmarūpāhvayaṃ śubham|| 80.86 ||
yatra tatra vidhiñcaiva sādhakaḥ paripūjayet|
pīṭhe samyak pūjayitvā paraṃ nirvāṇamāpnuyāt|| 80.87 ||
kathitā brahmaṇaḥ pūjā pūjanaṃ śṛṇu vaiṣṇavam|
bījaṃ tu vāsudevasya puraiva pratipāditam|| 80.88 ||
tadaṅgamantraṃ rājendra dvādvaśākṣaramucyate|
namo bhagavate pūrvaṃ vāsudevāya vai param|| 80.89 ||
aṅgamantramidaṃ caiva vāsudevasya kīrtitam|
asya pratyaṅgarūpaṃ tu dadhivāmanasaṃjñakam|| 80.90 ||
tasya mantraṃ naraśreṣṭha śambhunā bhāṣitaṃ śṛṇuḥ|
oṃ namo viṣṇave pūrvaṃ padaṃ tasya prakīrtitam|| 80.91 ||
padaṃ ca surapataye caturthyantaṃ mahābalam|
svāhāntaṃ hṛdayāsannaṃ pratyaṅgavaiṣṇavaṃ matam|| 80.92 ||
mantratrayaṃ tu yo veda bījaṃ pratyaṅgasaṃjñakam|
sa pumān devakāyastu na sa bhūyo’bhijāyate|| 80.93 ||
sarvaṃ uttaratantroktaḥ kramo grāhyaḥ prapūjane|
triṣu mantreṣu ca sadā viśeṣaṃ śṛṇu bhūpate|| 80.94 ||
rūpaṃ tu bījamantrasya prathamaṃ śṛṇū bhūpate|
pūrvacandropamaḥ śuklaḥ pakṣirājoparisthitaḥ|| 80.95 ||
caturbhujaḥ pītavastraistribhiḥ saṃvītadehabhṛt|
dakṣiṇordhve gadāṃ dhatte tadadho vikacāmbujam|| 80.96 ||
vāmordhve cakramatyugraṃ dhatte’dhaḥ śaṅkhameva ca|
śrīvatsavakṣāḥ satataṃ kaustubhaṃ hṛdi cāṃśumat|| 80.97 ||
datte kakṣe hyadhovāme tūṇīraṃ bāṇapūritam|
dakṣiṇe koṣagaṃ khaḍgaṃ nandakaṃ caśarāsanam|| 80.98 ||
śīrṣe kirīṭaṃ sūdyotaṃ karṇayoḥ kuṇḍaladvayam|
ājānulambinīṃ citrāṃ vanamālāṃ gale sthitām|| 80.99 ||
dadhānaṃ dakṣiṇe devīṃ śriyaṃ pārśve tu bibhratam|
sarasvatīṃ vāmapārśve cintayed varadaṃ harim|| 80.100 ||
bījamantrasya rūpaṃ ca kathitaṃ tava pārthiva|
dvādaśākṣaramantrasya rūpametacchṛṇuṣva me|| 80.101 ||
nīlotpaladalaśyāmaṃ tathaiva ca caturbhujam|
dakṣiṇordhvastitaṃ padmaṃ gadāṃ cātha prayojayet|| 80.102 ||
vāme’dhaścakramatulamūrdhve śaṃkhaṃ ca bibhratam|
cintayed varadaṃ devaṃ sarvamanyacca pūrvavat|| 80.103 ||
aṣṭādasākṣarasyāsya pratyaṅgasya ca cintanam|
śṛṇu rājannavahito dāridryabhayanāśanam|| 80.104 ||
pūrṇendusadṛśaṃ kāntyā śuklavarṇaṃ vicintayet|
kare vicintayed vāme poyūṣāpūritaṃ ghaṭam|| 80.105 ||
dadhyannakhaṇḍasaṃyuktaṃ dakṣiṇe svarṇabhājanam|
padmāsanagataṃ devaṃ candramaṇḍalamadhyagam|| 80.106 ||
śuklavastradharaṃ devaṃ pramāṇād vāmanaṃ sadā|
īṣaddhāsasamāyuktaṃ trilokeśaṃ trivikramam|| 80.107 ||
cintayed varadaṃ devaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam|
dahanaplavanādau ca pūrvatantroditā yathā|| 80.108 ||
tathā mantrāḥ parigrāhyāstathā cottaratantragāḥ|
maṇḍalasya kramaṃ tasya śṛṇu bhargeṇa bhāṣitam|| 80.109 ||
rakhayā nityapūjāsu rajobhiḥ paṃcabhistathā|
naimittike yathā kāryaṃ bhedābhedena sāmpratam|| 80.110 ||
hastamātraṃ caturddhāraṃ vatulāmbujasannibham|
catuṣkoṇa caturbhistu śaṅkhairyuktaṃ manoharam|| 80.111 ||
baddhadvāraṃ dikpatīnāmāyudhaiḥ karaṇaistathā|
aṣṭāsu dikṣu nihitaṃ sabahirveṣṭapadmakam|| 80.112 ||
evaṃ yathā rajobhistu kāryaṃ tacchṛṇu pārthiva|
sitaiḥ pītaistathā raktaiḥ śyāmaiśca haritaiḥ kramāt|| 80.113 ||
rajobhirmaṇḍalaṃ kuryādanyatā na samācaret
caturhastaṃ trihastaṃ ca dvihastaṃ hastamātrakam|| 80.114 ||
sarvatra maṇḍalaṃ karyād yathoktaṃ vādhikaṃ punaḥ|
rājasūyāśvamedhādī caturhastādhikaṃ matam|| 80.115 ||
kalpānatikramād bhūpa yathoktaṃ yatra yatra ca|
dikpālāyudhapadmānāṃ pūrvavallikhanakramaḥ|| 80.116 ||
sitai rajobhiḥ kartavyaṃ madhye padmaṃ suvartulam|
karṇikā pītavarṇāsya keśarāgraṃ tathāruṇam|| 80.117 ||
raktaiḥ pītaiḥ pūrayet tu bahiḥ padmasya sarvataḥ|
vajraṃ śaktaṃ lohadaṇḍaṃ khaḍgaṃ pāśāṅkuśaṃ gadām|| 80.118 ||
śūlamaṣṭadigīśānāmāyudhāni kramāt punaḥ|
śambhurgaurī tathā brahmā rāmaḥ kṛṣṇastathaiva ca|| 80.119 ||
etāstu satataṃ pūjyāḥ saṃsthitāḥ pañcadevatāḥ|
na kadācidabudhaḥ kuryācchambhūgauryo varyojanam|| 80.120||
viyoge tu kṛtā pūjā niṣphalā tasya jāyate|
vicchinnaṃ mūrdhni bhūtaṃ tu pūjitaṃ śaktameva ca|| 80.121 ||
nyāse tu maṇḍalasyāsya rajodoṣaṃ vivarjayet|
sarvatra maṇḍalaṃ kāryaṃ vāsudevasya pūjane|| 80.122 ||
evameva nṛpaśreṣṭha niṣphalaṃ canyathetaram|
balabhadraśca kāmaśca hyaniruddhastadudbhavaḥ|| 80.123 ||
nārāyaṇastathā brahmā viṣṇuḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ prakīrtitaḥ|
narasiṃho varāhaśca yoginno’ṣṭau prakīrtitāḥ|| 80.124 ||
pūrvādyaṣṭadale śvetānrūpato mantrataḥ pṛthak|
pūjayet karṇikāmadhye vāsudevaṃ tu nāyakam|| 80.125 ||
vimalā nāyikā tasya vāsudevasya kīrtitā|
balabhadramukhānāṃ tu yoginīḥ śṛṇu pārthava|| 80.126 ||
ādāvutkarṣiṇī jñeyā jñānā paścāt kriyāparā|
yogā prahvī tathaiśānī anugrāhī tathāṣṭamī|| 80.127 ||
sarva caturbhujāḥ proktāḥ śaṅkhacakragadādharāḥ|
yogino balabhadratu kāmaṃ vidhimṛte tathā|| 80.128 ||
vidhe rūpaṃ tu pūrvoktaṃ halaṃ ca muṣalaṃ balaḥ|
khaḍgaṃ cakraṃ ca dhatte yo gadāṃ pārśve sthitāṃ sadā|| 80.129 ||
kāmastu puṣpakodaṇḍaṃ dhatte vāmena pāṇinā|
gadāṃ cakraṃ ca puṣpaṃ ca dhatte’nyaiḥ pāṇibhiḥ punaḥ|| 80.130 ||
pārśve padmaṃ tathā dhatte sarvamanyacca pūrvavat|
cakraṃ śaṅkho varāhasya dakṣiṇe parikīrtitau|| 80.131 ||
nṛsiṃhasya punaścaśaṅkhau dakṣimavāmayoḥ|
śaṅkhaṃ padmaṃ tathā viṣmoḥ pāṇyordakṣiṇayoḥ sthitam|| 80.132 ||
śaṃkho gadā vāmatastu nārāyaṇakarasthitau|
dakṣiṇādho gadāṃ dhatte hyaniruddho narottama|| 80.133 ||
sitaraktastathā pīto bhinnāñjananibhastathā|
nīlotpaladalaśyāmastathā ktaghanaprabhaḥ|| 80.134 ||
bhramaraśyāmalaḥ piṅgaḥ svarṇagauraḥ kramādime|
varṇato yoginaḥ proktā vāsudevasya pārthiva|| 80.135 ||
yādagvarṇaśca dhyānaṃ ca yasya yasya ca yoginaḥ|
tādṛśīṃyoginīṃtasya cintayet tatsamīpagām|| 80.136 ||
ādhāraśaktipramukhāḥ sarvā āsanadevatāḥ|
grahāśca sarve dikpālā dhyānato mantratastathā|| 80.137 ||
pūjanīyā yathoddeśe maṇḍalasya kramānnṛpa|
devasya cintitaṃ yadyaccharīre kāmalādikam|| 80.138 ||
dhṛtāstraṃ vajraśaktyādigaruḍārdīśca pūjayet|
varṇamālāṃ śambhumatāmāsādya kramayogataḥ|| 80.139 ||
ādyadvitīyakramato gadādīnāṃ tu mantrakam|
pañcarātrodite bhāge nāradena yathoditāḥ|| 80.140 ||
mantrāścakragadādīnāṃ grāhyāḥ sarvatra pūjane|
garutmān sūryasaṅkāśo gadā kṛṣaamāyasī punaḥ|| 80.141 ||
sarasvatī śuklavarṇā lakṣmīrhemaprabhā sadā|
madhyāhnasūryapratimaṃ cakraṃ tu parikīrtitam|| 80.142 ||
sampūrṇacandrapratimaḥ śaṅkhastu parikīrtitaḥ|
kaustubho hyaruṇaḥ proktaḥ śrīvatso hyaruṇadyutiḥ|| 80.143 ||
āraktakaustubho jñeyo mālā citrā prakīrtitam|
vidyutprabhā sarvabāṇāḥ śakracāpaprabhaṃ dhanuḥ|| 80.144 ||
svarmacūrṇaprakāśaṃ tu vastramasya prakīrtitam|
bālasūyaṃpratīkāśe kuṇḍale dve śravogate|| 80.145 ||
sūryasya sadṛśaṃ śīrṣe kirīṭaṃ parikīrtitam|
śṛṇu nyāsaṃ tato bhūpa yairnyāsairviṣṇurūpadhṛk|| 80.146 ||
sādhako hi bhavennityaṃ svargamokṣapradāyakam|
nyāsaṃ tu prathamaṃ kuryānmantravid dvādaśākṣaraiḥ|| 80.147 ||
vāsudevasya bījena bījaṃ caivātha yoginām|
tarto nyasenmahāmantrai stataścāṣṭādasākṣaraiḥ|| 80.148 ||
tatastu hṛdayādīnāṃ ṣaḍbhirmantrardvidhā punaḥ|
evaṃ caturbhirnyāsaistu pūjāmekāṃ samācaret|| 80.149 ||
prathamaṃ dakṣiṇāṅguṣṭhe nyasedādyakṣaraṃ budhaḥ|
dvādaśākṣaramantrasya śeṣabījāni tu kramāt|| 80.150 ||
tarjanyādau dakṣiṇasya vāmāṅguṣṭhāntameva ca|
śeṣākṣaradvayaṃ paścād nyaset pāṇitaladvaye|| 80.151 ||
hṛdi śīrṣe śikhāyāṃ ca skandhayordṛkpicaṇḍayoḥ|
pṛṣṭhe tu bhujayoḥ pāṇyorjaghayoḥ pādayoḥ kramāt|| 80.152 ||
dvādaśakṣaramantrasya bījāni ca tato nyaset|
aṅguṣṭhayostu prathamaṃ vāsudevasya tattvakam|| 80.153 ||
tarjanyādau yogināṃ tu bījānyaṣṭau dvayornyaset|
śirodṛgā syakaṇṭhoronābhiguhyeṣu jānunoḥ|| 80.154 ||
pādayorvāsudevasya yogibījāni vinyaset|
mantrāṇi hṛdayādīnāṃ yānyuktāni purā nṛpa|| 80.155 ||
tāni nyasyāṅguṣṭhamūle’ṅgulījāte dvaye dvaye|
vāmadakṣiṇapāṇyostu śeṣaṃ tu talayonyaset|| 80.156 ||
hṛdayādyastraparyantaṃ punastāni kramānnyaseset|
aṣṭādaśākṣarasyādinavavarṇān nyased budhaḥ|| 80.157 ||
śironetrādipūrvokte navabījasya gocare|
śeṣān varṇān saṅkīrṇapārśvastiṣu śephasi|| 80.158 ||
kaṭyāmūrvorjaṅghayośca nyaset pādāṅgulīṣu ca|
yasya mantrasya yā pūjā tantraistu yatra coditā|| 80.159 ||
tasya tantrasya tatraiva nyāsaṃ mantrī samācaret|
atha caikatra sarveṣāṃ nyāsaṃ kuryāda vicakṣaṇaḥ|| 80.160 ||
caturvidhaiḥ kṛtairnyāsaiḥ pūtātmā dhūtakalmaṣaḥ|
sākṣād viṣṇurbhavenmantrī samyak pūjāphalaṃ labhet|| 80.161 ||
