vināpi pūjanaṃ yastu nyāsaṃ kuryāccaturvidham|
sa dhīro viṣṇusāyujyamāpnoti paramaṃ padam|| 80.162 ||
yogapīṭhaṃ tato dhyātvā garuḍaṃ cakraṃśaṅkhaṃ ca|
gadāṃ lakṣmīṃ tathā padmaṃ kramādeteṣu vinyaset|| 80.163 ||
pūrvadakṣiṇakauberapaścāt koṇeṣu vai kramāt|
dakṣiṇe cottare vāpi vinyasenmantravid budhaḥ|| 80.164 ||
vanamālāṃ padmamadhye śrīvatsaṃ kaustubhaṃ maṇim|
vinyasya dakṣiṇe tasya nyesecchārṅga śarāsanam|| 80.165 ||
taṇīrayugalaṃ vāme khaḍgaṃ dakṣiṇato nyaset|
vāme carma nidhāyāśa tatra kuryāt sarasvatīm|| 80.166 ||
pūjayitvā ca sarvāṇi tato murdrāṃ pradarśayet|
mudrāḥ puṭādyā yāḥ proktā viṣṇuryāścāpi yoginām|| 80.167 ||
grahāṇāṃ dikpatīnāṃ ca mudrāstā darśayet pṛthak|
śeṣamantrāḥ purā proktā acchidrasyāvadhāraṇe|| 80.168 ||
tanmantrān saṃpaṭhitvaiva sūryāyārdhyaṃ nivedayet|| 80.169 ||
nirmālyadhārī viṣṇostu viṣvaksenascaturbhujaḥ|
śaṅkhacakragadāpāṇirdīrghaśmaśrujaṭādharaḥ|
raktapiṅgalavarṇastu sitapadmoparisthitaḥ|| 80.170 ||
yat tṛtīyasvarāntena sayukto bindunendunā|
kīrtitastasya mantro’yaṃ tena taṃ paripūjayet|| 80.171 ||
visarjanaṃ tathā viṣṇoraiśānyāṃ parikīrtitam|
anyeṣāṃ manasā kuryād balādīnāṃ visarjanam|| 80.172 ||
evaṃ yaḥ kurute pūjāṃ viṣṇoḥ śambhorvidheḥ kvacit|
pīṭhe dikkaravāsinyāḥ sa yāti paramaṃ padam|| 80.173 ||
yatra yatra bhaved viṣṇoḥ pūjanaṃ nṛpasattam|
tatra tatraiva tantro’yaṃ grāhyo vai vaiṣṇavairbudhaiḥ|| 80.174 ||
saṅkṣepeṇaiva tatraiva pūjayed dadhivāmanam|
hṛdayādyaṅgapūjā tu na kartavyā’sya pūjane|| 80.175 ||
saṃkṣepairvistarairvāpi vāsudevaṃ prapūjayet|
raktaṃ kośeyavastraṃ ca pītaṃ śuklaṃ tathaiva ca|| 80.176 ||
prītidaṃ vāsudevasya vastrametat prakīrtitam|
ghṛtapradīpo dīpeṣu gandheṣu malayodbhavaḥ|| 80.177 ||
pānārdhyabhojyapātreṣu tāmraṃ prītikaraṃ matam|
kirīṭaṃ kuṇḍala hāro bhūṣaṇaṃ viṣṇutuṣṭidam||80.178 ||
śaṅkhaḥ snānīyapātreṣu dhūpeṣvagurureva ca|
prītido vāsudevasya satataṃ parikīrtitaḥ|| 80.179 ||
kadambaṃ kubjakaṃ jātī mallikā mālato tathā|
paṅkajaṃ ceti puṣpāṇi tad viṣṇoḥ prītidānyuta|| 80.180 ||
nirjanaṃ sthaṇḍilaṃ sthānaṃ tīrthaṃ toyamathāpi vā|
tad viṣṇoriti mantrastu stutiḥ puruṣasūktakam|| 80.181 ||
putrañjīvodbhavā mālā praśastā viṣṇupūjane|
tithiśca dvādaśī proktā vasantaḥ kāla uttamaḥ|| 80.182 ||
śālyodanaṃ haviṣyānnaṃ yāvakaṃ pāyasaṃ ghṛtam|
kṛśarānnaṃ tathānneṣu pāneṣu kṣīramiṣyate|| 80.183 ||
daleṣu tulasīpatraṃ bailvamāmalameva ca|
hareḥ prītikarāṇi syuretāni nṛpasattama|| 80.184 ||
sarvāṇi parakīyāṇi yāni tāni ca varjayet|
evaṃ yaḥ pūjayed viṣṇuṃ satataṃ narasattamaḥ|| 80.185 ||
kulakoṭiṃ samuddhṛtya sa svayaṃ syājjanārdanaḥ|
idaṃ te kathitaṃ bhūpa vāsudevasya yantrakam|| 80.186 ||
pīṭhasya kāmarūpasya saṅkṣepānnirṇayaṃ tathā|
iti sarvaṃ kāmarūpapīṭhaṃ śambhuradaśayat|| 80.187 ||
putrābhyāṃ sa punastābhyāṃ kailāsaṃ prayayau girim|
tatra gatvā yatāyogaṃ nidhāya tanayau svakau|| 80.188 ||
vimuktaśāpāste jātāḥ śambhurgirisutā tathā|
vetālo bhairavaśceti nṛpasattama nirjarāḥ|| 80.189 ||
idaṃ yo mahadākhyānaṃ śṛṇotyekāgramānasaḥ|
śāpabhītirna tasyāsti vyādhayastasya nādhayaḥ|| 80.190 ||
putrapautradhanaiśvaryayuktaḥ sarvatra vallabhaḥ|
sarvakalyāṇasaṃyukto dīrghakālaṃ sa jīvati|| 80.191 ||
kāmarūpaṃ mahāpīṭhaṃ yo jānāti narottamaḥ|
sa divyajñānasampannaḥ paraṃ nirvāṇamāpnuyāt|| 80.192 ||
yaḥ kāmarūpe sakale pīṭhayātrāṃ samācaret|
āsādya sakalān pīṭhān pūjayet sarvadevatāḥ|| 80.193 ||
daśapūrvān daśa parānātmānaṃ caikaviṃśatim|
divye jñāne vidhāyāśu sarvaṃ muktimiyāt saha|| 80.194 ||
|| iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe’śītitamo’dhyāyaḥ|| 80 ||

<81>
|| ekāśītitamo’dhyāyaḥ ||
||aurvva uvāca||
kamārūpe mahāpīṭhe snātvā pītvā ca devatāḥ|
pūjayitvā ca vipulā lokāḥ svargaṃ purā yayuḥ|| 81.1 ||
kecid bhejuśca nirvāṇaṃ kecid yānti sma śambhutām|
na yamastān vārayituṃ netuṃ ca nijamandiram|| 81.2 ||
kṣamo’bhūnnaraśārdūla śivāyājātasādhvasaḥ|
yamadūtaṃ tatra yāntaṃ bādhante śaṃkarā gaṇāḥ|| 81.3 ||
na tadbhiyā tatra yānti yamadūtāḥ pracoditāḥ|
tathā dṛṣṭvātha śamanaḥ svakriyāparivarjitaḥ|| 81.4 ||
vidhātāraṃ samāsādya vacanaṃ cedamabravīt|
vidhātaḥ kāmarūpe’smin snātvā pītvā ca mānavaḥ|| 81.5 ||
kāmākhyāgaṇatāṃ yāti tathā śabhbhugaṇeśatām|
tatra me nādhikāro’sti na tān vārayituṃ kṣamaḥ|| 81.6 ||
vidhatsvātrocitāṃ nītiṃ yujyate yadi gocare|
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ|| 81.7 ||
jagāma viṣṇubhavanaṃ sahaiva samavartinā|
tamāsādya tathā prāha viṣṇuṃvai yamabhāṣitam|| 81.8 ||
yathāvat sarvalokeśaḥ sa ca tadvākyamagrahīt|
saha brahmayamābhyāṃ tu viṣṇuḥ śambhuṃ yayau tataḥ|
satkṛtastatena pṛṣṭaśca prāhedaṃ yamabhāṣitam|| 81.9 ||
||śrībhagavānuvāca||
sarvadevaiḥ sarvatīrthaiḥ sarvakṣetraistathaiva ca|
etad vyāptaṃkāmarūpaṃ nāto’nyad vidyate param|| 81.10 ||
idaṃ pīṭhaṃ samāsādya devatvaṃ yānti mānavāḥ|
amṛtatvaṃ gaṇatvaṃ ca tatra śakto yamo nahi|| 81.11 ||
tathā kuru mahādeva yathā tatra kṣamo yamaḥ|
yamo nirasto yatrāsti maryādā na pradṛśyate|| 81.12 ||
||aurvva uvāca||
etad viṣṇuvacaḥ śrutvāḥ vidhinā sahitasya tu|
aṅgīcakāra hṛdaye tadvacaḥ sādhyasādhane|| 81.13 ||
visṛjya tān brahmaviṣṇuyamān vṛṣabhavāhanaḥ|
ādāya svagaṇān sarvān kāmarūpāntaraṃ yamau|| 81.14 ||
ugratārāṃ tato devīṃ gaṇaṃ ca prāha śaṅkaraḥ|
utsārayantu sakalānnimām̐llokān gaṇā drutam|| 81.15 ||
ugratāre mahādevi tvaṃ cāpyutsāraya drutam|
tato gaṇāḥ kāmarūpāddevī cāpyaparājitā|| 81.16 ||
lokānutsārayāmāsuḥ pīṭhaṃ kartuṃ harasyakam|
utsāryamāṇe loke tu caturvarṇadvijātiṣu|| 81.17 ||
sandhyācalagato vipro vasiṣṭhaḥ kupito muniḥ|
so’pyugratārayā devyā utsārayitumīśayā|| 81.18 ||
gaṇaiḥ saha dhṛtaḥ prāha śāpaṃ kurvan sudāruṇam|
yasmādahaṃ dhṛto vāme tvayotsārayituṃ muniḥ|| 81.19 ||
tasmāt tvaṃ vāmyabhāvena pūjyā bhava samantrikā|
bhramanti mlecchavad yasmād gaṇānāṃ mandabuddhayaḥ|| 81.20 ||
bhavantu mlecchāstasmād vai bhavantaḥ kāmarūpake|
mahādevo’pi yasmānmāṃ niḥsārayitumudyataḥ|| 81.21 ||
tapodhanaṃ muniṃ dāntaṃ mlecchavad vedapāragam|
tasmād mlecchapriyo bhūyācchaṅkaraścāsthibhasmadhṛk|| 81.22 ||
etat tu kāmarūpākhyaṃ mlecchairguptaṃ madatvaram|
svayaṃ viṣṇurna cāyāti yāvat sthānamidaṃ punaḥ|| 81.23 ||
viralāścāgamāḥ santu ya etatpratipādakāḥ|
viralaṃ yastu jānāti kāmarūpāgamaṃ budhaḥ|| 81.24 ||
sa eva prāpte kāle’pi sampūrṇaṃ phalabhāpsyati|
evamuktvā vasiṣṭhastu tatraivāntaradhīyata|| 81.25 ||
te gaṇā mlecchatāṃ yātāḥ kāmarūpe surālaye|
vāmā’bhūdrugratārāpi śambhurmleccharato’bhavat|| 81.26 ||
āgamā viralāścāsan ye ca matpratipādakāḥ|
vedamantravihīnaṃ tu caturvarṇavivarjitam|| 81.27 ||
kāmarūpaṃ kṣaṇājjātaṃ yad yamenānusāśtim|
āgate’pi harau mukte śāpāt pīṭhe phalaprade|| 81.28 ||
yathā na samyak sthāsyanti tatpīṭhe devamānuṣāḥ|
guptaye sarvakuṇḍānāṃ brahmopāyaṃ tathā’karot|| 81.29 ||
apunarbhavakuṇḍasya somakuṇḍasya cobhayoḥ|
brahmorvaśīkuṇḍasya nadīnāmapi bhūriśaḥ|| 81.30 ||
nadīnāṃ pūrvamuktānāmanuktānāṃ ca guptaye|
sarvasyaikaphalajñāne brahmopāyaṃ tathā’karot|| 81.31 ||
amoghāyāṃ śāntanostu bhāryāyāṃ tanayaṃ svakam|
jalarūpaṃ samutpādya jāmadagnyena dhīmatā|| 81.32 ||
avatārayadavyagraṃ plavayan kāmarūpakam|
sa tu brahmasuto dhīraḥ plāvayan kuṇḍasañcayān|| 81.33 ||
ācchādya sarvatīrthāni bhuvi guptāni cākarot|
lauhityamātraṃ ye kecijjānanti tatra vai narāḥ|| 81.34 ||
te lauhityasnānaphalaṃ prāpnuvanti suniścitam|
na jānanti ca kuṇḍāni nāpi tīrthāni cānyataḥ|| 81.35 ||
vasiṣṭhaśāpādetat tu pravṛttaṃ tīrthagopanam|
yaḥ kaścit tatra jānāti tīrthānāṃ ca viśeṣatām|| 81.36 ||
samavāpnoti tat snānaphalaṃ samyag narottama|
sarvā nadīḥ samāplāvya sarvatīrthāni sarvataḥ|
lauhityo brahmaṇaḥ putro yāti dakṣiṇasāgaram|| 81.37 ||
evaṃ te kathitaṃ rājan kāmarūpasya kīrtanam|
yadanyadrocate tubhyaṃ tat pṛccha nigadāmi te|| 81.38 ||
|| iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe ekāśītitamo’dhyāyaḥ|| 81 ||

<82>
|| dvyaśītitamo’dhyāyaḥ ||
||mārkaṇḍeya uvāca||
aurvasya vacanaṃ śrutvā sagarastaṃ muniṃ punaḥ|
papracchedaṃ dvijaśreṣṭhā harṣasaṃplutamānasaḥ|| 82.1 ||
||sāgara uvāca||
amoghāyāṃ kathaṃ yajñe lauhityo brahmaṇaḥ sutaḥ|
kathaṃ śāntanujāyāyāṃ rataḥ sa kamalāsanaḥ|| 82.2 ||
pārastraiṇeyaputro vā kathaṃ jajñe pitāmahāt|
tat sarvaṃ śrotumicchāmi kathayasva dvijottama|| 82.3 ||
||aurva uvāca||
śṛṇu tvaṃ rājaśārdūla kathayāmi mahattaram|
ākhyānaṃ brahmaputrasya lauhityatya mahātmanaḥ|| 82.4 ||
harivarṣe mahāvarṣe śāntanurnāma nāmataḥ|
munirāsīnmahābhāgo jñānavān sa taporataḥ|| 82.5 ||
tasya bhāryā mahābhāgā amoghākhyā mahāsatī|
hiravyamagarbhasya munestṛṇabindvāśramodbhavā|| 82.6 ||
tayā sārdhaṃ sa kailāsaṃ maryādāparvate vasan|
lohitākhyasya sarasastīre vai gandhamādane|| 82.7 ||
ekadā sa taponiṣṭho nijapuṣpādigocaram|
jagāma vanamadhyaṃ tu cinvan bahuphalāni ca|| 82.8 ||
tasminnavasare brahmā sarvalokapitāmahaḥ|
tatrājagāma yatrāsti amoghā śāntanoḥ priyā|| 82.9 ||
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā devagarbhāyāṃ yuvatīmatisundarīm|
mohito madanenāśu tadā’bhūd dūṣitendriyaḥ|| 82.10 ||
udīritendriyo bhūtvā jighṛśrustām̐ mahāsatīm|
athādhāvat tato brahmā sammukho madanārditaḥ|| 82.11 ||
dhāvamānaṃ vidhātāraṃ dṛṣṭvā’moghā mahāsatī|
naivaṃ naivamiti proktvā parṇaśālāṃ vyalīyata|| 82.12 ||
idaṃ covāca dhātāramamoghā kupitā tadā|
parṇaśālāntaraṃ gatvā dvāramāvṛtya tatkṣaṇāt|| 82.13 ||
akāryaṃ na mayā kāryaṃ munipatnyā vigarhitam|
balāt pramathyā cāhaṃ cet tvayā tvāṃ ca śapāmyaham|| 82.14 ||
amoghayā cauvamukte vidhātuśca tadā nṛpa|
retaścaskanda tatraiva āśrame śāntanormuneḥ|| 82.15 ||
cyute retasi dhātāpi haṃsayānaṃ samutthitaḥ|
lajjayā’tiparītātmā drutaṃ vai svāśramaṃ yayau|| 82.16 ||
gate vedhasi śāntanuśca nijamāśramamāgataḥ|
āgatya dṛṣṭvā haṃsānāṃ padakṣobhaṃ tadā bhuvi|| 82.17 ||
tejaśca patitaṃ bhūmau vidhāturjvalanopamam|
amoghāṃ paripapraccha parṇaśālāntarasthitām|| 82.18 ||
kimetadatra subhage pravṛttaṃ dṛśyate tu yat|
pakṣiṇāṃ ca padakṣobhaṃ tejaścedaṃ ca kīdṛśam|| 82.19 ||
sā tasya vacanaṃ śrutvā śāntanu munisattamam|
amarṣitaiva nyagadadākulā vikalānanāṃ|| 82.20 ||
haṃsayuktasyandanena ko’pyāgatya caturmukhaḥ|
kamaṇḍalu karo’tīva ratiṃ māṃ samayācataṃ|| 82.21 ||
tato mayā tarjitaḥ sa uṭajāntaralīnayā|
pracyāvya tejaḥ saṃyāto mama śāpabhayārditaḥ|| 82.22 ||
kuru tatra pratīkāraṃ yadi śaknoṣi śāntano|
na hīmāṃ dharṣaṇāṃ soḍhuṃ kaścicchaknoti jīvabhṛt|| 82.23 ||
sa tasyā vacanaṃ śrutvā svayaṃ brahmā samāgataḥ|
iti niścitya manasā tadā dhyānaparo’bhavat|| 82.24 ||
divyajñānena sa jñātvā devakāryamupasthitam|
tīrthāvataraṇaṃ cāpi hitāya jagatāṃ muniḥ|| 82.25 ||
jñātvodarkaṃ cintayitvā svabhāryāmidamabravīt|
idaṃ tejāṃ brahmaṇastvaṃ pibāmodhaṃ mamājñayā|| 82.26 ||
hitāya sarvajagatāṃ devakāryārthasiddhaye|
bhavatyā nikaṭaṃ brahmā svayameva samāgataḥ|| 82.27 ||
tvāmaprāpya mahat kṛtyamāvayoḥ sa samarpya ca|
gato nijāspadaṃ tat tvaṃ kartumarhasi tad vacaḥ|| 82.28 ||
tacchrutvā śāntanorvākyamamoghātīva lajjitā|
sāntvayantīva taṃ prāha patiṃ natvā mahāsatī|| 82.29 ||
nānyasya tejo dhāsyāmi na ca te vimanaskatā|
avaśyaṃ yadi kartavyaṃ pītvā tvaṃ mayi cotsṛja|| 82.30 ||
tatastasyā vacaḥ śrutvā yuktaṃ tathyaṃ ca śāntanuḥ|
svayaṃ pītvā tu tat tejaḥ svabhāryāyāṃ nyaṣecayat|| 82.31 ||
saṃkrāmitaiḥ śāntanunā tejobhirbrahmaṇaḥ satī|
garbhaṃ dadhāramoghākhyā hitāya jagatāṃ tataḥ|| 82.32 ||
tasyāḥ kāle tu samprāpte nāsāto jalasañcayaḥ|
tanmadhye tanayaścāpi nīlavāsāḥ kīrīṭadhṛk|| 82.33 ||
ratnamālāsamāyukto raktagauraśca brahmavat|
caturbhujaḥ padmavidyādhvajasāvatadharastathā|| 82.34 ||
śiśumāraśirasthaśca tulyakāyo jalotkaraiḥ|
tajjātaṃ ca tathābhūtaṃ śāntanurlokaśāntanuḥ|| 82.35 ||
caturṇāṃ parvatānāṃ ca madhyadeśe nyavīviśat|
kailāsaścāttare pārśve dakṣiṇe gandhamādanaḥ|| 82.36 ||
jārudhiḥ paścime śailaḥ pūrve saṃvartakādvayaḥ|
teṣāṃ madhye svaryaṃ kuṇḍaṃ parvatānāṃ vidheḥ sutaḥ|| 82.37 ||
kṛtvā’tivavṛdhe nitya śaradīva niśākaraḥ|
taṃ toyamadhyagaṃ putramāsādya druhiṇaḥ sutam|| 82.38 ||
kramatastasya saṃskārānakarod dehaśuddhaye|
atha kāle bahutithe vyatīte brahmaṇaḥ sutaḥ|| 82.39 ||
toyarāśisvarūpeṇa vavṛdhe pañcayojanāt|
tasmin devāḥ papuḥ sasnurdvitīya iva sāgare|| 82.40 ||
sitāmalajale hyadye divyaiścāpsarasāṃ gaṇaiḥ|
tasminnavasare rāmo jāmadagnyaḥ pratāpavān|| 82.41 ||
cakre mātṛvadhaṃ ghoramayuktaṃ piturājñayā|
tasya pāpasya mokṣāya svapituścopadeśataḥ|| 82.42 ||
sa jagāma mahākuṇḍaṃ brahmākhyaṃ snātumicchayā|
tatra snātvā ca pītvā ca mātṛhatyāmapānayan|
vīthīṃ paraśunā kṛtvāṃ taṃ mahyāmavatārayavat|| 82.43 ||
||sagara uvāca||
jamadagneḥ suto rāmaḥ kimarthaṃ nijamātaram|
jaghāna tasya mātā ca kinnāmno kasya cātmajā|| 82.44 ||
muneḥ putraḥ kathaṃ jātastathā krūro mahābalaḥ|
yo yaddhakuśalo voro rājanyān samapothayat|| 82.45 ||
tadahaṃ śrotumicchāmi tattvato munisattama|
kathayasya mahābhāga yadi guhyaṃ tathāpi me|| 82.46 ||
||aurva uvāca||
śṛṇu rājannavahito jamadagneḥ sutasya vai|
caritaṃ sa yathā jaghne prasūṃ krūrataraśca saḥ|| 82.47 ||
brahmaputro bhṛgurnāma ṛcīkastatsuto’bhavat|
sa bhāryārthī caran bhūmau kānyakubjaṃ gataḥ purā|| 82.48 ||
dadarśa cāraṇyagataṃ jahnorvaṃśasamudbhavam|
kuśikasya sutaṃ gādhiṃ tapaḥsthaṃ nṛpasattama|| 82.49 ||
araṇyasthasya tasyātha putrakāmasya bhūbhṛtaḥ|
sabhāryasya sutā jajñe devakanyāsamā guṇaiḥ|| 82.50 ||
ṛcīko bhṛguputrastāṃ bhāryārthaṃ samayācata|
dātūṃ gādhiṃ nṛpatiśārdūlaṃ ca covāca nṛpo munim|
dātuṃ bhogyāṃ sutāṃ rājan yadīcchā te dadāmyahaham||
yogyā sutā mo’dya tadvidhāya mahāmune|| 82.51 ||
kiṃ tvekaḥ kuladharmo me vidyate śulkasaṃgrahe|
ekatra kṛṣṇavarṇānāmaśvānāṃ candravarcasām|
sahasramekaṃ yo dadyāt tasmai putrī prīyate|| 82.52 ||
||ṛcoka uvāca||
dāsyāmyaśvasahasraṃ vai tava rājaṃstathāvidham|
kiṃcit kālaṃ pratīkṣasva yāvat tadahamānaye|| 82.53 ||
evamastviti ta gādhiruvāca bhṛgusūnave|
gaṅgātīraṃ kānyakubjaṃ so’gacchaddhayasādhane|| 82.54 ||
tatrārādhya bhṛgoḥ putro varuṇaṃ yādasāṃ patim|
tena dattaṃ tadā lebhe sahasraṃ vājināṃ muniḥ|| 82.55 ||
tena yatra tadā labdhā aśvān nṛpatisattama|
tadaśvatīrthaṃ vikhyātaṃ mahāphalakaraṃ param|| 82.56 ||
gaṅgājalādutthitaṃ tu dattaṃ samyak pracetasā|
ādāyāśvasahasraṃ tu manurgādhimathāmyayāt|| 82.57 ||
tānaśvān gādhirādāya putrīṃ satyavatīṃ sutām|
ṛcīkāya dadau lakṣmīṃ keśavāyeva sāgaraḥ|| 82.58 ||
ṛcīko gādhitanayāṃ labdhvā bhāryāmaninditām|
muditaḥ sa tayā reme yathākāmaṃ svakāśrame|| 82.59 ||
kṛtadāraṃ sutaṃ śrutvā draṣṭuṃ putraṃ snuṣāṃ bhṛguḥ|
athājagāma matimān sanuṣāṃ dṛṣṭvā nananda ca|| 82.60 ||
dampatī taṃ samāsīnaṃ bhṛguṃ devagaṇārcitam|
pūjayitvā yathānyāyaṃ tasthatustau kṛtāñjalī|| 82.61 ||
tato bhṛguḥ snuṣāṃ svīyāṃ suprīta idamabravīt|
varaṃ vṛṇīṣva dāsyāmi vāñchitaṃ varavarṇini|| 82.62 ||
adeyaṃ duṣkaraṃ vāpi yatra te vidyate spṛhā|
tataḥ satyavatīṃ putraṃ tapa āmnāyapāragam|| 82.63 ||
mātuśca vīramatulaṃ putraṃ varamayācata|
sa caivamastvityuktvaiva bhūtvā dhyānaparastadā|| 82.64 ||
viśvamādhṛtya manasā yatnācchvāsaṃ sasarja saḥ|
tasya niḥśvāsavātāt tu niḥsṛtaṃ vai carudvayam|| 82.65 ||
tasyai taddvitayaṃ dattvā bhṛgustāmidamabravīt|
carudvayaṃ gṛhāṇedaṃ snuṣe satyavati svayam|| 82.66 ||
snātvā ṛtau ṛtau mātā snuṣe tvaṃ ca kariṣyathaḥ|
āliṃgyāśvatthavṛkṣaṃ te mātā puṃsavanāya vai|| 82.67 ||
carumāraktakaṃ cemaṃ sā bhokṣyati sutastataḥ|
tvaṃ codumbaravṛkṣaṃ tu samāliṃgyāsitaṃ carum|| 82.68 ||
bhokṣyase tava putrastu bhaviṣyati sanātanaḥ|
evamuktvā bhṛguryāto yathecchaṃ sāpi saṃmudam|| 82.69 ||
avāpa mātrā sahitā bhartrā pitrā ca bhāminī|
atha snānadine’śvatthamāliṃgyāraktakaṃ carum|| 82.70 ||
ādāt satyavatī tasyā mātā phalguṃ sitaṃ carum|
parivartaṃ tu taj jñātvā divyajñāno bhṛgurmuniḥ|| 82.71 ||
athāgatya snuṣāṃ tāṃ tu vacanaṃ cedamabravīt|
viparyayastvayā bhadre vṛkṣāliṅganakarmaṇi|| 82.72 ||
tathā caruprāśane tutatredaṃ te bhaviṣyati|
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyācārastava putro bhaviṣyati|| 82.73 ||
kṣatriyo brāhmaṇācāro mātuste bhavitā sutaḥ|
ityuktvā bhṛguṇā sādhvo tadā satyavatī bhṛgum|| 82.74 ||
punaḥ prasādayāmāsa pautro me’stviti tādṛśaḥ|
evamastviti sa procya tatraivāntardadheḥ bhṛguḥ|| 82.75 ||
atha kāle sutaṃ dīptaṃ jamadagniṃ ca gādhijā|
suṣuve jananī tasyā viśvāmitreṃ taponidhim|| 82.76 ||
jamadagnistato vedāṃścaturaḥ prāpa mā ciram|
prādurāsīd dhanurvedaḥ svayaṃ tasmin mahātmani|| 82.77 ||
viśvāmitro’pi sakalān vedānāpa tathā’cirāt|
dhanurvedaṃ tathā kṛtsnaṃ vipraścābhūt tapobalāt|| 82.78 ||
jājvalyamānastejasvī jamadagnirmahātapāḥ|
vedaistapobhiḥ sa munīnatyakrāmacca sūryavat|| 82.79 ||
|| iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe dvyaśītitamo’dhyāyaḥ|| 82 ||

<83>
|| tryaśītitamo’dhyāyaḥ ||
||aurvva uvāca||
atha kāle vyatīte tu jamadagnirmahātapāḥ|
vidarbharājasya sutāṃ prayatnena jitāṃ svayam|| 83.1 ||
bhāryārthaṃ pratijagrāha reṇukāṃ lakṣaṇānvitām|
sā tasmāt suṣuve ṣatrāṃścaturo vedasammitān|| 83.2 ||
ruṣaṇvantaṃ suṣeṇāṃ ca vasuṃ viśvāvasuṃ tathā|
paścāt tasyāṃ svayaṃ jajñe bhagavān madhusūdanaḥ|| 83.3 ||
kārtavīryavadhāyāśu śakrādyaiḥ sakalaiḥ suraiḥ|
yācitaḥ paṃcamaḥ so’bhūt rāmāhvayastu saḥ|| 83.4 ||
bhārāvataraṇārthāya jātaḥ paraśunā saha|
sahajaṃ paraśuṃ tasya na jahāti kadācana|| 83.5 ||
ayaṃ nijapitāmahyāścarubhuktiviparyayāt|
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyācāro rāmo’bhūta krūrakarmakṛt|| 83.6 ||
sa vedānakhilān jñātvā dhanurvedaṃ ca sarvaśaḥ|
satataṃ kṛtakṛtyo’bhūd vedavidyāviśāradaḥ|| 83.7 ||
ekadā tasya jananī snānārthaṃ reṇukā gatā|
gaṅgātoye hyathāpaśyannāmnā citrarathaṃ nṛpam|| 83.8 ||
bhāryābhiḥ sadṛśībhiśca talakrīḍārataṃ śubham|
sumālinaṃ suvastraṃ taṃ taruṇaṃ candramālinam|| 83.9 ||
tathāvidhaṃ nṛpaṃ dṛṣṭvā sañjātamadanā bhṛśam|
reṇukā spṛhayāmāsa tasmai rājñe suvarcase|| 83.10 ||
spṛhāyutāyāstasyāstu saṃkledaḥ samajāyata|
vicetanāmbhasā klinnā trastā sā svāśramaṃ yayau|| 83.11 ||
abodhi jamadagnistāṃ reṇukāṃ vikṛtāṃ tathā|
dhig dhikkāraratetyevaṃ nininda ca samantataḥ|| 83.12 ||
tataḥ sa tanayān prāha caturaḥ prathamaṃ muniḥ|
ruṣaṇvatpramukhān sarvānekaikaṃ kramato drutam|| 83.13 ||
chindhīmāṃ pāpaniratāṃ reṇukāṃ vyabhicāriṇīm|
te tadvaco naiva cakrurmūkāścāsan jaḍā iva|| 83.14 ||
kupito jamadagnistāñchaśāpeti vicetasaḥ|
gādhiṃ nṛpatiśārdūlaṃ sa covāca nṛpo munim|| 83.15 ||
bhavadhvaṃ yūyamācirājjaḍā gobuddhigardhitāḥ|
athājagāma caramo jāmadagnye’tivīryavān|| 83.16 ||
taṃ ca rāmaṃ pitā prāha pāpiṣṭhāṃ chindhi mātaram|
sa bhrātṛṃśca tathābhūtān dṛṣṭvā jñānavivarjitān|| 83.17 ||
pitrā śaptān mahātejāḥ prasūṃ paraśunācchinat|
rāmeṇa reṇukāṃ chinnāṃ dṛṣṭvā vikrodhano’bhavat|| 83.18 ||
jamadagniḥ prasannaḥ sanniti vācamuvāca ha|
prīto’smi putra bhadra te yat tvayā madvacaḥ kṛtam|| 83.19 ||
tasmādiṣṭān varān kāmāṃstvaṃ vai varaya sāmpratam|
sa tu rāmo varān vavre māturutthānamāditaḥ|| 83.20 ||
vadhasyāsmaraṇaṃ tasyā bhrātṛṇāṃ śapamocanam|
mātṛhatyāvyapanayaṃ yuddhe sarvatra vai jayam|| 83.21 ||
āyuḥ kalpāntaparyantaṃ kramād vai nṛpasattama|
sarvān varān sa pradadau jamadagnirmahātapāḥ|| 83.22 ||
suptotyiteva jananī reṇukā ca tadābhavat|
vadhaṃ na cāpi sasmāra sahajā prakṛtisthitā|| 83.23 ||
yuddhe jayaṃ cirāyuṣyaṃ lebhe rāmastadaiva hi|
mātṛhatyāvyapohāya pitā taṃ vākyamavravīt|| 83.24 ||
na putra varadānena mātṛhatyāpagacchati|
tasmāt tvaṃ brahmakuṇḍāya gaccha snātuṃ ca tajjale|| 83.25 ||
tatra snātvā muktapāpo nacirāt punareṣyasi|
jagaddhitāya putra tvaṃ brahmakuṇḍaṃ vraja drutam|| 83.26 ||
sa tasya vacana śrutvā rāmaḥ paraśudhṛk tadā|
upadeśāt piturghāto brahmakuṇḍaṃ vṛṣodakam|| 83.27 ||
tatra snānaṃ ca vidhivat kṛtvā dhautaparaśvadhaḥ|
śarīrānniḥsṛtāṃ mātṛhatyāṃ samyag vyalokayat|| 83.28 ||
jātasaṃpratyayaḥ so’tha tīrthamāsādya tadvaram|
vīthīṃ paraśunā kṛtvā brahmaputramavāhayat|| 83.29 ||
brahmakuṇḍāt sṛtaḥ so’tha kāsāre lohitāhvaye|
kailāsopatyakāyāṃ tu nyapatad brahmaṇaḥ sutaḥ|| 83.30 ||
tasyāpi sarasastīre samutthāya mahābalaḥ|
kuṭhāreṇa diśaṃ pūrvāmanayad brahmaṇaḥ sutam|| 83.31 ||
tataḥ paratrāpi giriṃ hemaśṛṅgaṃ vibhidya ca|
kāmarūpāntaraṃ pīṭhamāvahadyadamuṃ hariḥ|| 83.32 ||
tasya nāma svayaṃ cakre vidhirlohitagaṅgakam|
lohitāt saraso jāto lohitākhyastato’bhavat|| 83.33 ||
sa kāmarūpamakhilaṃ pīṭhamāplāvya vāriṇā|
gopayan sarvatīrthāni dakṣiṇaṃ yāti sāgaram|| 83.34 ||
prāgeva divyayamunāṃ sa tyaktvā brahmaṇaḥ sutaḥ|
punaḥ patati lauhitye gatvā dvādaśayojanam|| 83.35 ||
caitre māsi sitāṣṭamyāṃ yo naro niyatendriyaḥ|
caitraṃ tu sakalaṃ māsaṃ śuciḥ prayatamānasaḥ|| 83.36 ||
snāti lauhityatoye tu sa yāti brahmaṇaḥ padam|
lauhityatoye yaḥ snāti sa kaivalyamavāpnuyāt|| 83.37 ||
iti te kathitaṃ rājan yadarthaṃ mātaraṃ purā|
ahan vīro jāmadagnyo yasmād vā krūrakarmakṛt|| 83.38 ||
idaṃ tu mahadākhyānaṃ yaḥ śṛṇoti dine dine|
sa dīrghāyuḥ pramudito balavānabhijāyate|| 83.39 ||
iti te kathitaṃ rājañcharīrārdhaṃ yathādrijā|
śambhorjahāra vetālabhairavau ca yathāhvayau|| 83.40 ||
yasya vā tanayau jātau yathā yātau gaṇeśatām|
kimanyat kathaye tubhyaṃ tadvadasva nṛpottama|| 83.41 ||
||mārkaṇḍeya uvāca||
ityaurvvasya ca saṃvādaḥ sagarema mahātmanā|
yo’sau kāyārdhaharaṇaṃ śambhorgirijayā kṛtaḥ|| 83.42 ||
sarvo’dya kathito viprāḥ pṛṣṭaṃ yaccānyaduttamam|
siddhasya bhairavākhyasya pīṭhānāṃ ca vinirṇayam|| 83.43 ||
bhṛṅgiṇaśca yathotpattirmahākālasya caiva hi|
uktaṃ hi vaḥ kimanyat tu pṛcchantu dvijasattamāḥ|| 83.44 ||
iti sakalasutantraṃ tantramantrāvadātaṃ bahutaraphalakāri prājñaviśrāmakalpam|
upaniṣadamavetya jñānamārgekatānaṃ sravati sa iha nityaṃ yaḥ paṭhet tantrametat|| 83.45 ||
|| iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe tryaśītitamo’dhyāyaḥ|| 83 ||

<84>
|| caturaśītitamo’dhyāyaḥ ||
||ṛṣaya ūcuḥ||
kathito bhavatā sargaḥ saṃśayaścāpi śātitāḥ|
tvatprasādānmahābhāga kṛtakṛtyā vayaṃ guro|| 84.1 ||
bhūyaśca śrotumicchāmo vayametad dvijottama|
ko’nyo bhṛṅgī mahākālo jātau vetālabhairavau|| 84.2 ||
vetālaṃ ca mahākālaṃ bhairavaṃ bhṛṅgiṇaṃ tathā|
śṛṇumo dvijaśārdūla kathameṣāṃ catuṣṭayam|| 84.3 ||
||mārkaṇḍeya uvāca||
bhuvaṃ gate mahākāle mānuṣyasthe ca bhṛṅgiṇi|
vetālabhairavākhye ca tayorbhūte dvijottamāḥ|| 84.4 ||
varalabdhe ca vetāle bhairave tena saṅgate|
andhakaṃ tapasā yuktaṃ bhṛṅgiṇaṃ cākaroddharaḥ|| 84.5 ||
andhakastu haraṃ pūrvaṃ virudhyāpadamāgataḥ|
paścāddharaṃ samārādhya putro’bhūt tasya so’suraḥ|| 84.6 ||
bhṛṅgisnehād bhṛṅgiṇaṃ taṃ saṃjñayā cākaroddharaḥ|
snehena tu mahākāle bāṇaṃ balisutaṃ haraḥ|
viṣṇunā chinnabāhuṃ tu mahākālamathākarot|| 84.7 ||
evaṃ munivarasteṣāṃ saṃyataṃ ca catuṣṭayam|
vetālabhairavau bhṛṅgimahākālau hyanukramāt|| 84.8 ||
||ṛṣayaṃ uvāca||
yat pṛṣṭaṃ sagareṇaiva munimaurvvaṃ mahādhiyam|
nītyā yaujyā yayā bhāryā suta ātmā’thavā guro|| 84.9 ||
rājanītau satāṃ nītau sadācāre ca ye sthitāḥ|
viśeṣāstena ye proktā aurveṇa sumahātmanā|| 84.10 ||
viśeṣeṇa dvijaśreṣṭha śrotuṃ samyak tapodhana|
icchāmastān mahābhāga kathayasva jagadguro|| 84.11 ||
||mārkaṇḍeya uvāca||
ye ye viśeṣāḥ kathitā aurveṇa sumahātmanā|
tad vaḥ sarvaṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇvantu munisattamāḥ|| 84.12 ||
śrutvaivaṃ sagaro rājā mantrakalpādikaṃ punaḥ|
viśeṣaṃ paripapraccha nītyādīnāṃ mahāmunim|| 84.13 ||
||sagara uvāca||
yayā nītyā prayoktavyaḥ suta ātmā priyā tathā|
teṣām̐ viśeṣaiḥ sahitaṃ sadācāraṃ vadasva me|| 84.14 ||
||aurvva uvāca||
krameṇa śṛṇu rājendra yayā nītyā niyojitāḥ|
ātmā suto vā bhāryā vā tadviśeṣaṃ śṛṇuṣva me|| 84.15 ||
jñānavidyātapovṛddhān vayovṛddhān sudakṣiṇān|
seveta prathamaṃ viprānasūyāparivarjitān|| 84.16 ||
tebhyāśca śṛṇuyāṃnnityaṃ vedaśāstraviniścayam|
yadūcuste ca tat kāryaṃ prājñaṃ caiva nṛpaścaret|| 84.17 ||
pañcendriyāṇi pañcāśvāḥ śarīraṃ ratha ucyate|
ātmā rathī kaśā jñānaṃ sārathirmana ucyate|| 84.18 ||
aśvān sudāntān kurvīta sārathiṃ cātmano vaśam|
kaśā dṛḍhā sadā kāryā śarīrasthiratā tathā|| 84.19 ||
adāntāṃstu samāruhya saindhavān syandanī yathā|
aśvānāmicchayā gacchannutpathaṃ pratipadyate|| 84.20 ||
tatrāvaśaḥ sārathistu svecchayā prerayan hayān|
nayet paravaśaṃ samyag prathitaṃ vīramapyuta|| 84.21 ||
tathendriyāṇi nṛpatirviṣayāṇāṃ parigrahe|
svavaśyāni prakurvīta mano jñānaṃ dṛḍhaṃ tathā|| 84.22 ||
jñāne dṛḍhe kaśāyāṃ ca dṛḍhāyāṃ nṛpasattama|
sārathiḥ svavaśo dāntānīśaḥ prerayituṃ hayān|| 84.23 ||
ato nṛpaḥ svendriyāṇi vaśe kṛtvā manastathā|
jñānamārgamadhiṣṭhāya prakurvītātmano hitam|| 84.24 ||
bhoktavyaṃ svecchayā bhūyo na kuryāllobhamāsave|
draṣṭavyamiti draṣṭavyaṃ na draṣṭavyaṃ ca svecchayā|| 84.25 ||
śrotavyamiti śrotavyaṃ nādhikaṃ śravaṇe caret|
śāstratattva mṛte dhīraḥ śrutivaśyo bhavenna hi|| 84.26 ||
evaṃ ghrāṇaṃ tvacaṃ cāpi vaśīkṛtyecchayā nṛpaḥ|
svecchayā nopabhuñjīta noddāmaṃ viṣayaṃ vrajet|| 84.27 ||
evaṃ yadi bhavedrājā tadā sa syājjitendriyaḥ|
jitendriyatve hetuśca śāstravṛddhopasevanam|| 84.28 ||
avṛddhasevyaśāstrajño nṛpaḥ śatruvaśo bhavet|
tasmācchāstramadhiṣṭhāya bhavedrājā jitendriyaḥ|| 84.29 ||
dhṛtiḥ prāgalbhyasutsāho vākpaṭutvaṃ vivecanam|
dakṣatvaṃ dhārayiṣṇutvaṃ dānamaitrīkṛtajñatā|| 84.30 ||
dṛḍhaśāsanatā satyaṃ śaucaṃ mativiniścayam|
parābhiprāyaveditvaṃ caritraṃ dhairyamāpadi|| 84.31 ||
kleśadhāraṇaśaktiśca gurudevadvijārcanam|
anasūyā hyakopitvaṃ guṇānetānnṛpo’bhyaset|| 84.32 ||
kāryākāryavibhāgaśca dharmārtho kāma eva ca|
satataṃ pratibudhyeta kuryādavasare’pi tat|| 84.33 ||
sāmadānaṃ ca bhedaśca daṇḍaśceti catuṣṭayam|
jñātvopāyāṃstu tatkāle tadupāyān prayojayet|| 84.34 ||
sāmnastu viṣaye bhedo madhyamaḥ parikīrtitaḥ|
dānasya viṣaye sāma yogyamevopalakṣyate|| 84.35 ||
dānasya viṣaye daṇḍo hyadhanaḥ parikīrtitaḥ|
daṇḍasya viṣaye dānaṃ tadapyadhamamucyate|| 84.36 ||
sāmnastu gocare daṇḍo hyadhamādadhamaḥ smṛtaḥ|
saujanyaṃ satataṃ jñeyaṃ bhūbhṛto bhedadaṇḍayoḥ|| 84.37 ||
sāmno dānasya ca tathā saujanyaṃ yāti gocare|| 84.38 ||
kāmaḥ krodhaśca lobhaśca harṣo māno madastathā|
etānatiśayān rājā śatrūniva viśātayet|
sevyāḥ kāle saṃyuktau te lobhagarvau vivarjayet|| 84.39 ||
teja eva nṛpāṇāṃ tu tīvraṃ sūryasya vai yathā|
tatra garvaṃ rogayuktaṃ kāyavāṃstaṃ tu satyajet|| 84.40 ||
ākheṭakākṣau strīsevā pānaṃ caivārthadūṣaṇam|
vāgdaṇḍayośca pāruṣyaṃ saptaitāni vivarjayet|| 84.41 ||
parastrīṣu viraktāsu sevāmekāntatastyajet|
satīṣu nijanārīṣu yuktaṃ kuryānniveśanam|| 84.42 ||
ratiputraphalā dārāstāṃstu naikāntatastyajet|
tayoḥ siddhayai striyaḥ sevyā varjayitvātisaktatām|| 84.43 ||
mṛgāyāṃ tu pramādānāṃ sthānaṃ nityaṃ vivarjayet|
akṣāṃstathā na kurvīti satkāryāsaktināśanam|| 84.44 ||
anyaiḥ kṛtaṃ kadācit tu seveta nātmanācaret|
akāryakaraṇe bījaṃ kṛtyānāṃ ca vivarjane|| 84.45 ||
akālabhentrabhede ca kalahe satkṛtikṣaye|
varjayet satataṃ pānaṃ śaucamāṅgalyanāśanam|| 84.46 ||
arthakṣayakaraṃ nityaṃ tyajeccaivātmadūṣaṇam|
abhiśasteṣu coreṣu ghātakeṣvātatāyiṣu|| 84.47 ||
satataṃ pṛthivīpālo daṇḍapāruṣyamācaret|
nānyatra daṇḍapāruṣyaṃ kuryānnṛpatisattamaḥ|| 84.48 ||
vākpāruṣyaṃ ca sarvatra naiva kuryāt kadācana|
rakṣaṇīyaṃ sadā satyaṃ satyamekaṃ parāyaṇam|| 84.49 ||
kṣamāṃ tejasvitāṃ caiva prastāvānnṛpa ācaret|
yānāsanāśrayadvaidhasandhayo vigrahastathā|| 84.50 ||
abhyaset ṣaḍguṇānetāṃsteṣāṃ sthānaṃ ca śāśvatam|
yaḥ pramāṇaṃ na jānāti sthāne vṛddhau tathā kṣaye|| 84.51 ||
koṣe janapade daṇḍe na sa rājye’vatiṣṭhate|
koṣe janapade daṇḍe caikaikatra trayaṃ trayam|| 84.52 ||
prastāvādviniyuñjīta rakṣennaikāṃstatastvimān|
mitre śatrāvudāsīne prabhāvaṃ triṣvapīrayet|| 84.53 ||
utsāho vijigīṣāyāṃ dharmakṛtye’ṣṭavargake|
śarīrayātrānirvāhe kriyeta satataṃ nṛpaiḥ|| 84.54 ||
mantraniścayasambhūtāṃ buddhiṃ sarvatra yojayet|
amātye śātrave rājye putreṣvantaḥpureṣu ca|| 84.55 ||
kṛṣiṃ durgaṃ ca vāṇijyaṃ khaḍgānāṃ karasādhanam|
ādānaṃ sainyakarayorbandhanaṃ gajavājinoḥ|| 84.56 ||
śūnye sadmamukhānāṃ ca yojanaṃ satataṃ janaiḥ|
trayāṇāṃ sārasetūnāṃ bandhanaṃ ceti cāṣṭamam|| 84.57 ||
etadaṣṭasu vargeṣu cārān samyak prayojayet|
kāryākāryavibhāgāya cāṣṭavargādhikāriṇām|| 84.58 ||
aṣṭau cārānniyuñjīyādaṣṭavargeṣu pārthivaḥ|
daśa śūnyeṣu yuñjīta kramataḥ śṛṇu tāni me|| 84.59 ||
svāmī sacivarāṣṭrāṇi mitraṃ kośo balaṃ tathā|
durgaṃ tu saptamaṃ jñeyaṃ rājyāṅgaṃ gurubhāṣitam|| 84.60 ||
durgamuktaṃ cāṣṭavarge cārānnātmani yojayet|
tasmādimāni śeṣāṇi paṃca cārapadāni ca|| 84.61 ||
śuddhānteṣu ca putreṣu sasūyyādau mahānase|
śatradāsīnayoścāpi balābalaviniścaye|| 84.62 ||
aṣṭādaśasu caiteṣu cārān rājā prayojayet|
na yatprakāśaṃ jānīyāt tat taccārairnirūpayet|| 84.63 ||
nirūpya tatpratīkāramavaśyaṃ chidrataścaret|
yathāniyogameteṣāṃ yo yo yatrānyathācaret|| 84.64 ||
jñātvā tatra nṛpaścārairdaṇḍayed vā viyojayet|
cārāṃstu mantriṇā sārdhaṃ rahasye saṃsthito nṛpaḥ|| 84.65 ||
pradoṣasamaye pṛcchet tadānīmeva sādhayet|
svaputre cātha śuddhānte ye tu cārā mahānase|| 84.66 ||
niyuktāstānmadhyarātre pṛcchet svo’pi ca mantriṇi|
etāṃścārān svayaṃ paśyennṛpatirmantriṇā vinā|| 84.67 ||
anyāṃstu mantriṇā sārdhaṃ nirūpya pradiśet phalam|
naikaveśadharaścāro naiko notsāhavarjitaḥ|| 84.68 ||
saṃstuto nahi sarvatra nātidīrgho na vāmanaḥ|
satataṃ na divācārī na rogī nāpyabuddhimān|| 84.69 ||
na vittavibhavairhīno na bhāryāputravarjitaḥ|
kāryaścāro nṛpatinā tattvaguhyavinirṇaye|| 84.70 ||
anekaveśagrahaṇakṣamaṃ bhāryāsutairyutam|
bahudeśavaco’bhijñaṃ parābhiprāyavedakam|| 84.71 ||
dṛḍhabhaktaṃ prakurvīta cāraṃ śaktamasādhyasam|
abhitiṣṭhet svayaṃ rājā kṛṣimātmasamaistathā|| 84.72 ||
vaṇikpathe tu durgādo teṣu śaktānniyojayet|
antaḥpure pitustulyān dhīrān vṛddhānniyojayet|| 84.73 ||
ṣaṇḍhān paṇḍāṃstathā vṛddhāṃ striyau vā buddhitatparāḥ|
śuddhānte dvāri yuñjīyāt striyo vṛddhā manīṣiṇīḥ|| 84.74 ||
naikaḥ svapet kadācit tu naiko bhuñjīta pārthivaḥ|
naikākinīṃ tu mahiṣīṃ brajenmaitrāya naikakaḥ|| 84.75 ||
amātyānupadhāśuddhān bhāryāḥ putrāṃstathaiva ca|
prakuryāt satataṃ bhūpaḥ saprasādaṃ samācaran|| 84.76 ||
dharmārthakāmamokṣaiśca pratyekaṃ pariśodhanaiḥ|
upetya dhīyate yasmādupadhā sā prakīrtitā|| 84.77 ||
arthakāmopadhābhyāṃ tu bhāryāputrāṃśca śodhayet|
dharmopadhābhirviprāṃstu sarvāṃbhiḥ sacivān punaḥ|| 84.78 ||
ebhiryajñaitasthā dānairihaiva nṛpatirbhavet|
tasmād bhavāṃstu rājyārthī dharmamevaṃ samācaret|| 84.79 ||
anenaivābhicāreṇa yajñairvāṃ pārthivo hyayam|
prāṇāṃstyajati rājā tvaṃ bhaviṣyasi na saśayaḥ|| 84.80 ||
iti dharmo nṛpasyaiva aśvamedhādikaśca yaḥ|
svayaṃ na kurute bhūpastasmāt tvaṃ kuru sattama|| 84.81 ||
evaṃ mantrairmantrayitvā nṛpaḥ kāryāntikād dvijāt|
tairajñātān svayaṃ jñātvā gṛhṇīyāt tasya tairmanaḥ|| 84.82 ||
yadi rājyābhilāṣeṇa sacivo’dharmamācaret|
nṛpatau bādhakaṃ kuryād dharmaṃ taṃ honatāṃ nayet|| 84.83 ||
ābhicārikamartyarthaṃ kurvāṇaṃ tu vighāyayet|
pravāsayed brāhmaṇaṃ tu pārthivaścābhicārikam|| 84.84 ||
eṣā gharmopadhā jñeyā tairamātyātan satāñjayet|
tādṛśīṃ tathaivānyāmupadhāṃ dharmataścaret|| 84.85 ||
kośādhyakṣān samāmantrya rājāmātyān pratārayet|
putrānanyān prati tathā mantrasaṃvaraṇākṣamān|| 84.86 ||
ayaṃ hi pracuraḥ koṣo madāyatto narottama|
ānaye tava saṃmatyā tad yadi tvaṃ pratīkṣasi|| 84.87 ||
tavārthalagnādasmākaṃ jīvanaṃ ca bhaviṣyati|
tvaṃ cāpi pracuraiḥ koṣaiḥ kiṃ kiṃ vā na kariṣyasi|| 84.88 ||
evamanyaiḥ koṣagatairupāyairnṛpasattamaḥ|
putrāmātyādikān sarvān satataṃ pariśodhayet|| 84.89 ||
koṣadoṣakarān hanyāt kartumicchan vivāsayet|
dvaidhacittān vimanyeta kuryād vai kośarakṣaṇam|| 84.90 ||
dāsīśca śilpinīrvṛddhā medhādhṛtimatīḥ striyaḥ|
antarbahiśca yā yānti viditāḥ sacivādibhiḥ|| 84.91 ||
tā rājā rahasi sthitvā bhāryādibhiralakṣitaḥ|
abhimantryātha saṃmantrya preṣayet sacivān prati|| 84.92 ||
tā gatvā hṛdayaṃ buddhā striyo vijñānatatparāḥ|
mahiṣīpramukhā rājñastvāṃ vai kāmayate śubhā|| 84.93 ||
tatrāhaṃ yojayiṣyāmi yadi te vidyate spṛhā|
sacivastvāṃ kāmayate tvadyogyo varavarṇini|| 84.94 ||
taṃ saṃgamayituṃ śaktā yadi śraddhā tavāstyaham|
ityanena prakāreṇa nānopāyaistathottaraiḥ|| 84.95 ||
bhāryāḥ putraduhitrīśca snuṣāśca prasnuṣāstathā|
śodhayet sacivān putrān pautrādīn sevakāṃstathā|| 84.96 ||
kāmopadhā’viśuddhāṃstu ghātayedavicārayan|
striyastu yojyā daṇḍena brāhmaṇāṃstu pravāsayet|| 84.97 ||
mokṣamārgāvasaktaṃ tu hiṃsāpaiśunyavarjitam|
kṣamaikasāraṃ nṛpatiḥ sacivaṃ parivarjayet|| 84.98 ||
mokṣamārgaviraktāṃstu daṇḍayānapi na daṇḍayet|
samabuddhistu sarvatra tasmāt taṃ parivarjayet|| 84.99 ||
iti sūtraṃ copadhānāmupadhā bahudhā punaḥ|
vivecitā cośanasā tacchāstre tatra bodhayet|| 84.100 ||
vigrahaṃ satataṃ rājā parairnna samyagācaret|
bhūvittamitralābheṣu niściteṣveva vigrahāḥ|| 84.101 ||
saptāṅgeṣu prasādaśca sadā kāryo nṛpottamaiḥ|
koṣasya sañcayaṃ rakṣāṃ satataṃ samyagācaret|| 84.102 ||
mantriṇastu nṛpaḥ kuryād viprān vidyāviśāradān|
vinayājñān kulīnāṃśca dharmārthakuśalānṛjūn|| 84.103 ||
mantrayet taiḥ samaṃ jñānaṃ nātyarthaṃ bahubhiścaret|
ekaikenaiva kartavyaṃ mantrasya ca viniścayam|| 84.104 ||
vyastaiḥ samastaiścānyasya vyapadeśaiḥ samantataḥ|
susaṃvṛtaṃ mantragṛhaṃ sthalaṃ vāruhya mantrayet|| 84.105 ||
araṇye niḥśalāke vā na yāminyāṃ kadācana|
śiśūñchākhāmṛgān paṇḍāñchukān vai sārikāstathā|| 84.106 ||
varjayenmantragehe tu manuṣyān vikṛtāṃstathā|
dūṣaṇaṃ mantrabhedeṣaṃ nṛpāṇāṃ yat tu jāyate|| 84.107 ||
na tacchakyaṃ samādhātuṃ dakṣairnṛpaśatairapi|
daṇḍyāṃstu daṇḍayed daṇḍairadaṇḍyān daṇḍayennahi|| 84.108 ||
adaṇḍayan nṛpo daṇḍyānnadaṇḍyāṃścāpi daṇḍayan|
nṛpatirvācyatāṃ prāpya caurakilviṣamāpnuyāt|| 84.109 ||
durge tu samatāṃ kuryād prākārāṭṭalatoraṇaiḥ|
bhūṣitānnagarādrājā dūre durgāśrayaṃ caret|| 84.110 ||
durgaṃ balaṃ nṛpāṇāṃ tu nityaṃ durgaṃ praśasyate|
śatameko yodhayati durgastho yo dhanurddharaḥ|| 84.111 ||
śataṃ daśasahasrāṇi tasmād durgaṃ praśasyate|
jaladurgaṃ bhūmidurgaṃ vṛkṣadurgaṃ tathaiva ca|| 84.112 ||
āraṇyaṃmarudurgaṃ ca śailajaṃ parikhodbhavam|
durgaṃ kāryaṃ nṛpatinā yathā durgaṃ svadeśataḥ|| 84.113 ||
durgaṃ kurvan puraṃ kuryāt trikoṇaṃ dhanurākṛti|
vartulaṃ ca catuṣkoṇaṃ nānyathā nagaraṃ caret|| 84.114 ||
mṛdaṅgākṛtidurgaṃ tu satataṃ kulanāśanam|
yathā rākṣasarājyasya laṅkā durgānvitā purā|| 84.115 ||
valeḥ puraṃ śoṇitākhyaṃ tejo durgaiḥ pratiṣṭhitam|
tad yasmād vyajanākāraṃ manobhraṣṭaḥ sivāvaliḥ|| 84.116 ||
saubhāgyaṃ śālvarājasya nagaraṃ paṃcakoṇakam|
divi yad vartate rājyaṃ tacca bhraṣṭaṃ bhaviṣyati|| 84.117 ||
yaccāyodhyāhvayaṃ bhūpa puramikṣvākubhūbhṛtām|
dhanurākṛti taccāpi tato’bhūd vijayapradam|| 84.118 ||
durgabhūbhau yajed durgāṃ dikpālāṃścaiva dvārataḥ|
pūjayitvā vidhānena jayaṃ bhūpaḥ samāpnuyāt|| 84.119 ||
ato durgaṃ nṛpaḥ kuryāt satataṃ jayavṛddhaye|
na brāhmaṇān sadā rājā kenāpyavamanīkṛtān|| 84.120 ||
avamanya nṛpo viprān pretyeha duḥkhabhāg bhavet|
na virodhastu tai kāryaḥ svāni teṣāṃ na cādadet|| 84.121 ||
kṛtyakāleṣu satataṃ tāneva paripūjayet|
naiṣāṃ nindāṃ prakurvīta nābhyasūyāṃ tathācaret|| 84.122 ||
evaṃ nṛpo mahābuddhistattvamaṇḍalasaṃyutaḥ|
aprabhādī cāracakṣurguṃṇavān supriyaṃvadaḥ|| 84.123 ||
pretyeha mahatīṃ siddhiṃ prāpnoti sukhabhogavān|
yairguṇairyojitaścātmā taiḥ putrānapi yojayeta|| 84.124 ||
nṛpasya ca svatantratvaṃ satataṃ svaṃ vināśayet|
svatantro bhūpatanayo vikāraṃ yāti niścitam|| 84.125 ||
nirvikārāya satataṃ vṛddhāṃśca pariyojayet|
bhojane śayane yāne puruṣāṇāṃ ca vīkṣaṇe|| 84.126 ||
viyojayet sadā dārān bhūpaḥ kāmaviceṣṭane|
asvatantrāḥ striyaḥ kāryāḥ satataṃ pārthivena tu|| 84.127 ||
tāḥ svatantrāḥ striyo nityaṃ hānaye sambhavanti hi|
tasmāt kumāraṃ mahiṣīmupadhābhirmanoharaiḥ|| 84.128 ||
śodhayitvā niyuñjīta yauvarājyāvarodhayoḥ|
antaḥpurapraveśe tu svatantratvaṃ niṣedhayet|| 84.129 ||
bhūpaputrasya bhāryāyā bahiḥsāre tathaiva ca|
ayaṃ viśeṣaḥ saṃkṣepānnṛpadharmo mayoditaḥ|| 84.130 ||
putrāṇāṃ guṇavinyāse bhāryāṇāmapi bhūpate|
uśanā rājanītīnāṃ tantrāṇi tu bṛhaspatiḥ|| 84.131 ||
cakārānyān viśeṣāṃstu tayostantreṣu bodhayet|
evaṃ rājā mahābhāge rājanītau viśeṣatām|
kurvanna sīdati sadā bhūyasīṃ śriyamaśnute|| 84.132 ||
|| iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe nṛpadharmakathane caturaśītitamo’dhyāyaḥ|| 84 ||

<85>
|| pañcāśītitamodhyāyaḥ ||
||aurvva uvāca||
sadācāreṣu rājendra viśeṣāñ śṛṇu samprati|
yānavaśyaṃ nṛpaḥ kuryāt tānmattaḥ sakalāñ śṛṇu|| 85.1 ||
sādhavaḥ kṣīṇadoṣāśca sacchabdaḥ sādhuvācakaḥ|
teṣāmācaraṇaṃ yat tat sadācāraḥ sa ucyate|| 85.2 ||
āgameṣu purāṇeṣu saṃhitāsu yathoditān|
samuddiṣṭasadācārān gṛhṇīyāt tān gṛhasthavat|| 85.3 ||
ṛṣīn yajed vedapāṭhairdevān homaiḥ prapūjayet|
śrāddhaiḥ pitṛṃ starpayet tu bhūtāni valibhistathā|| 85.4 ||
maitraṃ prasādhanaṃ snānaṃ dantadhāvanamañjanam|
sarvaṃ gṛhasthavat kuryānniṣekādyaṃ vidhiṃ tathā|| 85.5 ||
ṣaṭkarmasu niyuñjīta rājā viprān samantataḥ|
tathaiva kṣatriyādīṃśca sve sve dharme niyojayet|| 85.6 ||
yaḥ svadharmaṃ parityajya paradharmaṃ samācaret|
taṃ śatena nṛpo daṇḍaṃ punastasmin niyojayet|| 85.7 ||
sāṃvatsareṣu kṛtyeṣu viśiṣyaitān samācaret|
avaśyaṃ pārthivo rājan tān viśeṣāñ śṛṇuṣva me|| 85.8 ||
śaratkāle mahāṣṭamyāṃ durgāyāḥ paripūjanam|
nīrājanaṃ daśamyāṃ tu kuryād vai balavṛddhaye|| 85.9 ||
pauṣe māsi tṛtīyāyāṃ kuryāt puṣyābhiṣecanam|
pūjayitvā śriyaṃ devīṃ pañcamyām̐ nṛpatiścaret|| 85.10 ||
śrīyajñaṃ dhanadhānyasya vṛddhaye nṛpasattama|
jyaiṣṭhe daśaharāyāṃ tu viṣṇoriṣṭiṃ tathācaret|| 85.11 ||
ravau haristhe dvādaśyāṃ śakrapūjāṃ samācaret|
viśiṣyaitāṃstu nṛpatiḥ kuryād yajñān bahuvyayaiḥ|| 85.12 ||
ebhiḥ kṛtairbalaṃ rājyaṃ koṣaścāpi vivardhate|
akṛteṣveṣu yajñeṣu durbhikṣaṃ maraṇaṃ tathā|| 85.13 ||
jāyante cetayaḥ sarvā viśiṣyaitāṃstataścaret|
śaratkāle mahāṣṭamyāṃ durgāyāḥ pūjane vidhiḥ|| 85.14 ||
purā proktastu vidhinā tena kāryaṃ tu pūjanam|
vidhiṃ nīrājanasya tvaṃ śṛṇu pārthivasattama|| 85.15 ||
kṛtena yena cāśvānāṃ gajānāmapi vardhanam|
āśvine śuklapakṣai tu tṛtīyā svātīyoginī|| 85.16 ||
aiśānyāṃ svapurasyaiva gṛhṇīyāt sthānamuttamam|
nīrājanaṃ tataḥ kuryāt saṃprāpte divase’ṣṭame|| 85.17 ||
nīrājanasya kālastu pūrvamukto mayā tava|
vidhānamātraṃ śṛṇu me kṛtakṛtyo bhaviṣyasi|| 85.18 ||
ekaṃ hayaṃ mahāsattvaṃ sumanoharameva ca|
pūjayet saptadivasān gandhapuṣpāṃśukādibhiḥ|| 85.19 ||
tṛtīyādau pūjayitvā nayeta yajñamaṇḍalam|
ceṣṭāṃ nirūpayaṃstasya jānīyāt tu śubhāśubham|| 85.20 ||
pararāṣṭrāvamardaḥ syādaśvo yadi palāyate|
mriyate rājaputrastu yadi cāśrūṇi muñcati|| 85.21 ||
nīyamāno na gacchet tu mahiṣīmaraṇaṃ tataḥ|
tathaiva mukhanāsākṣi śabdaṃ kuryāddhayo yadi|| 85.22 ||
yaḥ kāṣṭhābhimukhaḥ kuryāt tat kāṣṭhāyāṃ jayedripūn|
utkṣipya dakṣiṇāgraṃ tu padamaśvo bhavet puraḥ|| 85.23 ||
tadā yadi samastāṃśca nṛpatirvijayedripūn|
prātarnārājanaṃ kuryād daśamyāṃ nṛpasattama|| 85.24 ||
tadaprāptau ca dvādaśyāṃ tasyāmeva samācaret|
kārtike paṃcadaśyāṃ vā tatrābhāve tu pārthiva|| 85.25 ||
aiśānyāṃ svapurasyoccairhastamānena ṣoḍaśa|
daśahastaṃ tu vipulāṃ kuryād vai tatra toraṇam|| 85.26 ||
dvātriṃśaddhastamātraṃ tu hastaṣoḍaśavistṛtam|
yajñārthaṃ maṇḍalaṃ kuryānmadhye vediṃ vanirdiśet|| 85.27 ||
vedyāścottarataścāśvavediṃ kuryādanuttamām|
yatra saṃsthāpya cāśvaśca pūjitavyaḥ purohitaiḥ|| 85.28 ||
sarjodumbaraśākhānāmarjunasyāthavā nṛpa|
matsyaśaṅkhāṅkitaicakraijaiścāpyabhibhūṣayet|| 85.29 ||
toraṇaṃ kanakaratnastathā nānārvidhaiḥ phalaiḥ|
bhallātakaṃ śālikuṣṭhaṃ siddhyarthaṃ saindhavasya tu|| 85.30 ||
kaṇṭhadeśe nibadhnīyāt puṣṭiśāntyarthameva ca|
vaiṣṇavaṃ maṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā dikpālāṃśca navagrahān|| 85.31 ||
viśvedevāṃstu mantreṇa viṣṇumukhyān prapūjayet|
ājyaistilaiśca puṣpaiśca miśrīkṛtya purohitaḥ|| 85.32 ||
ravestu varuṇasyaiva prajeśasya tathaiva ca|
puruhutasya viṣṇośca homaṃ saptāhamācaret|| 85.33 ||
ekaikasya sahasraṃ vā aṣṭottaraśataṃ ca vā|
kuryāt tu pratyahaṃ homaṃ caturvargasya siddhaye|| 85.34 ||
samadhaścāpi hotavyāḥ pālāśāḥ khādirāstathā|
audumbaryaśca kāśmaryā āśvatthāśca purodhasā|| 85.35 ||
sauvarṇān rājatān vāpi mārtikān vā yathecchayā|
kuryāt tu kalaśānaṣṭau phalāmrāmvarayojitān|| 85.36 ||
kṣipet teṣu ghaṭeṣveva samaṅgaharitālakam|
candanaṃ ca tathā kuṣṭhaṃ priyaṅguṃ ca manaḥśilām|| 85.37 ||
añjanaṃ ca haridrāṃ ca śvetāṃ dantīṃ tathaiva ca|
bhallātakaṃ pūrṇakośaṃ sahadevīṃ śatāvarīm|| 85.38 ||
vacāṃ sanāgakusumāṃ somarājīṃ suguptikām|
tutthaṃ ca karavīraṃ ca tulasīdalameva ca|| 85.39 ||
etāni nikṣipenmadhye kalaśānāṃ purohitaḥ|
kanakarambujairyajñadārubhiḥ sruksruvau tathā|| 85.40 ||
kartavye śāntikāmena nīrājanavidhau nṛpa|
evaṃ saptāhaparyantaṃ pūjābhirhavanaistathā|| 85.41 ||
pūrvoktān pūjayitvā tu nṛpaḥ saptāhamācaret|
yāvannīrājanaṃ kuryāt tāvadrājā vased gṛhe|| 85.42 ||
rātrau na yajñabhūmau tu nivasecchāntimicchrukaḥ|
nārohayet turaṅgaṃ taṃ gajaṃ vā tatra pārthivaḥ|| 85.43 ||
yāvat saptāhaparyantaṃ yānenānyena vai vrajet|
bhakṣyairnānāvidhaiścaiva madhupāyasayāvakaiḥ|| 85.44 ||
modakairvā baliṃ kuryādannavyañjanasambhavaiḥ|
pūrvoktānāṃ tu devānāṃ saptāhaṃ yāvaduttamam|| 85.45 ||
saptame’hni tu rebhantaṃ pūjayet toraṇāntare|
sūryaputraṃ mahābāhuṃ dvibhujaṃ kavacojjvalam|| 85.46 ||
jvalantaṃ śuklavastreṇa keśānudgrathya vāsavā|
kaśāṃ vāmakare bibhrad dakṣiṇaṃ tu karaṃ punaḥ|| 85.47 ||
sa khaḍgaṃ nyasya vāmāyāṃ sitasaindhavasaṃsthitam|
evaṃvidhe tu rebhantaṃ pratimāyāṃ ghaṭe’pi vā|| 85.48 ||
sūryapūjāvidhānena pūjayeta toraṇāntare|
pūjayitvā tu rebhanta dviradaṃ turagaṃ tathā|| 85.49 ||
ahatāmbarasaṃvītaṃ srakcandanasamanvitam|
suvarṇaviddhanistriṃśaṃ vicitraṃ kavacādibhiḥ|| 85.50 ||
yuktaṃ tu homakuṇḍasya aiśānyāmaśvavedikām|
pūrvaṃ kṛtāṃ nayedaśvagajapālau pṛthak pṛthak|| 85.51 ||
nīyamāne gaje cāśve pūrvoktaṃ tu nimittakam|
yatnād vīkṣeta nṛpatiḥ phalaṃ caivāvadhārayet|| 85.52 ||
homakuṇḍasyottarasyāṃ vaiyāghre carmaṇi sthitaḥ|
vedavidā cāśvavidā sahito vīkṣya saindhavam|| 85.53 ||
nītāya turagāyāśu bhaktapiṇḍīṃ sugandhinīm|
dadyāt purohitastatra saṃmantrya śāntimantrakaiḥ|| 85.54 ||
bhakṣaṇād yadi jighret tadaśnīyād vā hayaḥ saca|
tadā syāt sarvakalyāṇaṃ viparītamato’nyathā|| 85.55 ||
śākhāmaudumbarīmāmrīṃ sakuśāṃ ca ghaṭodake|
āplāvyāplāvya turagān gajān bhūpaṃ ca sainikān|| 85.56 ||
rathāṃśca saṃspṛśenmantraiḥ śāntikaiḥ pauṣṭikaistathā|
secayet sahitairvipraiścaturaṅgaṃ purohitaḥ|| 85.57 ||
dikpālānāṃ grahāṇāṃ ca mantraiśca vaiṣṇavaistathā|
bahudhā cābhiṣicyātha tataḥ sauvarṇa darpaṇam|| 85.58 ||
vīkṣayitvā nṛpaṃ cartvik tato mantriṇameva ca|
rājaputraṃ tathāmātyānanyānapi ca sainikān|| 85.59 ||
kampayan dvijaśārdūlaḥ sarvāneva tu darśayet|
caturaṃgasya svasyāpi kṛtvaivaṃ śāntipauṣṭike|| 85.60 ||
mṛnmayaṃ śātravaṃ kṛtvā cābhicārikamantrakaiḥ|
hṛdi śūlena vidhvā taṃ śiraṃ khaḍgena chedayet|| 85.61 ||
ācāryaḥ kavikāṃ paścādabhimantrya hayāya vai|
aindraiḥ prābhākarairmandatrairdadyād vaktre svayaṃ punaḥ|| 85.62 ||
tamanena tu mantreṇa samāruhya nṛpastadā|
gaccheduttarapūrvāṃ tu diśaṃ sarvairbalairyutaḥ|| 85.63 ||
ṛtvik purohitācāryāḥ sarva eva nṛpaṃ tadā|
anugaccheyuranyāni nimittāni vilokitum|| 85.64 ||
vāditraghoṣaistumulairātapatrairvṛtastathā|
gacchennīrājane rājā dārayanniva medīnīm|| 85.65 ||
maṇividrumamuktādisvarṇaratnairalaṅkṛtaḥ|
krośamātraṃ tato gatvā pūrvadvāreṇa pārthivaḥ|| 85.66 ||
svapuraṃ praviśed viprairyajñaṃ yāyāt purohitaḥ|
tatra gatvā dakṣiṇāṃ tu hiraṇyaṃ gāṃ tathā tilam|| 85.67 ||
dattvā paścād dvijebhyastu dadyād dānāni śaktitaḥ|
evaṃ nīrājanaṃ kṛtvā bālānāṃ ca mahīkṣitaḥ|| 85.68 ||
pretyeha susthirāṃ lakṣmīṃ nṛpatiḥ prāpnuyāt tathā|
tvamaśvāmṛtasañjāta sāgarodbhava saindhava|| 85.69 ||
yena satyena bahase śaktaṃ teneha māṃ vaha|
yena satyena rebhantaṃ yena satyena bhāskaram|| 85.70 ||
vahase tena satyena vijayāya vahasva mām|
ābhyāṃ tu bhūpa mantrābhyāmaśvārohaṇamācaret|| 85.71 ||
āruhyāgre mahiṣyāstu śuddhānte lambayet tataḥ|
mahiṣī ca tato bhūpaṃ paryaṅkopari saṃsthitam|| 85.72 ||
dūrvākṣataiḥ sasiddhārthaḥ strībhiḥ saha tamarcayet|
kṛte tu bhūmigrahaṇe tṛtīyāyāṃ nirājane|| 85.73 ||
sūtakaṃ yadi jāyeta tatra duṣyati kevalam|
sūtakī mṛtakī vāpi pārthivastu yathā tathā|| 85.74 ||
balanīrājanaṃ kuryāt tanmātraṃ ca viśeṣataḥ|
sadyaḥ śaucaṃ bhavedrājño vyavahāravilokane|| 85.75 ||
tathādhivāsane yajñe pararāṣṭravimardane|
ayaṃ te kathito rājannīrājanakramo mayā|
puṣyasnānavighānaṃ tu pārthiva śṛṇu sāmpratam|| 85.76 ||
|| iti śrīkālikāpurāṇepañcāśītitamo’dhyāyaḥ|| 85 ||

<86>
||aurvva uvāca||
śṛṇu rājan pravakṣyāmi puṣyasnānavidhikramam|
yena vijñānamātreṇa vighnā naśyanti santatam|| 86.1 ||
pauṣe puṣyarkṣage candre puṣyasnānaṃ nṛpaścaret|
saubhāgyakalyāṇakaraṃ durbhikṣamaraṇāpaham|| 86.2 ||
viṣṭyādiduṣṭakaraṇe vyatīpāte ca vaidhṛtau|
vajre śūle harṣaṇādau yoge tu yadi labhyate|| 86.3 ||
tṛtīyāyuktapuṣyarkṣaṃ raviśaurikuje’hani|
tadā samastadaṣāṇāṃ tat snānaṃ hānikārakam|| 86.4 ||
grahadoṣāśca jāyante yadi rājyeṣu cetayaḥ|
tadā puṣye tu nakṣatre kuryānmāsāntare’pi ca|| 86.5 ||
iyaṃ tu brahmaṇā śāntiruddiṣṭā gurave purā|
śakrādisarvadevānāṃ śāntyarthaṃ ca jagatpatiḥ|| 86.6 ||
tuṣakeśāsthivalmīkakīṭadeśādivarjite|
śarkarākṛmikuṣmāṇḍabahukṛṣṭavivarjite|| 86.7 ||
kākolūkaiśca kaṅkaiśca kākālairgṛghraśaunakaiḥ|
varjite kaṇṭakivane vibhītakavivarjite|| 86.8 ||
śigruśleṣmātakābhyāṃ tu jalaukādyairvivarjite|
svasthāne campakāśokavakulādivirājite|| 86.9 ||
haṃsakāraṇḍavākīrṇe sarastīrethavā śucau|
puṣyasnānāya nṛpatirgṛhṇīyāt sthānamuttamam|| 86.10 ||
tataḥ purohito rājā nānā vāditraniḥsvanaiḥ|
pradoṣasamaye gacchet tat sthānaṃ pūrvavāsare|| 86.11 ||
tasya sthānasya kauveryāṃ diśi sthitvā purohitaḥ|
sugandhacandanaiḥ pānaiḥ karpūrādyadhivāsitaiḥ|| 86.12 ||
gaurocanābhiḥ siddhārthairakṣataiḥ saphalādibhiḥ|
gandhadvāretyādibhirmantraiḥ sarvādhisiktakaiḥ|| 86.13 ||
adhivāsya tu tatsthānaṃ pūjayet tatra devatāḥ|
gaṇeśaṃ keśavaṃ śakraṃ brahmāṇaṃ cāpi śaṅkaram|| 86.14 ||
umayā sahitaṃ devaṃ sarvāśca gaṇadevatāḥ|
mātṛśca pūjayet tatra nṛpatiḥ sapurohitaḥ|| 86.15 ||
maṅgalān kalaśān kṛtvā nānānaivedyasañcayān|
pradadyāt pāyasaṃ svāduphalaṃ modakayāvakau|| 86.16 ||
adhivāsya ca tat sthānaṃ dūrvāsiddhārthakākṣataiḥ|
tatsthānāccāpi bhūtāni sārayenmantramīrayan|| 86.17 ||
apasarpantu te bhūtā ye bhūtā bhūmipālakāḥ|
bhūtānāmavirodhena snānakarma karomyaham|| 86.18 ||
tataḥ karau puṭīkṛtya mantreṇānena pārthivaḥ|
āvāhayedimān devān pūjyān puṣyābhiṣekataḥ|| 86.19 ||
āgacchantu surāḥ sarve ye’tra pūjābhilāṣiṇaḥ|
diśo hi pālakāḥ sarve ye cānye’pyaṃśabhāginaḥ|| 86.20 ||
tataḥ puṣpāñjaliṃ dattvā punarmantraṃ paṭhedimam|
adya tiṣṭhantu vibudhāḥ sthānamāsādya māmakam|| 86.21 ||
svapūjāṃ prāpya pātāro dattvā śāntiṃ mahībhuje|
tatastāṃ nṛpatī rātriṃ nayet tu sapurohitaḥ|| 86.22 ||
svapne śubhāśubhaṃ vidyānnṛpastu sapurohitaḥ|
kṛtvā pūjāṃ tu devānāṃ rātrau sthāne nṛpaḥ svapet|| 86.23 ||
śubhāśubhaphalaṃ svapne jñeyaṃ doṣajñasammate|
duḥsvapnadarśanaṃ cet syāt tadā puṣyābhiṣecane|| 86.24 ||
homaṃ caturguṇaṃ kuryād dattvā cāpi gavāṃ śatam|
govājikuṃjarāṇāṃ tu prāsādasya girestaroḥ|| 86.25 ||
ārohaṇaṃ śubhakaraṃ rājyaśrīvṛddhikārakam|
dadhidevasuvarṇānāṃ brahmaṇasya pradarśanam|| 86.26 ||
vīṇādūrvākṣataphalaṃ puṣpacchatravilepanam|
śītāṃśu cakraśaṃkhānāṃ padmasya suhṛdastathā|| 86.27 ||
lābhāḥ kṣayakarāḥ śatrau ratnākarasya bhūbhṛtaḥ|
darśanaṃ coparāgasya nigaḍena ca bandhanam|| 86.28 ||
māṃsasya bhojanaṃ caiva parvatasya vivartanam|
nābhimadhye tarūtpattirmṛtaṃ pratyanurodanam|| 86.29 ||
agamyāgamanaṃ kūpa paṅkagarbhāvatīrṇatā|
parvatasya tathā nadyāḥ srotasāṃ laṅghanaṃ tathā|| 86.30 ||
svaputramaraṇaṃ caiva pānaṃ rudhiramadyayoḥ|
bhojanaṃ pāyasasyāpi manuṣyārohaṇaṃ tathā|| 86.31 ||
kalyāṇasukhasaubhāgyarājyaśatrukṣayaṃ tathā|
ete svapnāḥ prakurvanti nṛsya nṛpasattama|| 86.32 ||
kha roṣṭramahiṣāṇāṃ ca āroho rājyanāśanaḥ|
nṛtyaṃ gītaṃ tathā hāsyaṃ pāṭhaścāpyaśubhapradaḥ|| 86.33 ||
raktavastraparidhānaṃ raktamālānulepanam|
raktāṃ kṛṣṇāṃ striyaṃ caiva kāmayat mṛtyumāpnuyāt|| 86.34 ||
kūpāntare praveśaḥ svād dakṣiṇāśāgatistathā|
paṅke nimajjanaṃ snānaṃ bhāryāputravināśanam|| 86.35 ||
lābhastasya bhavet svapne’pyarutpattirnṛpabhya ca|
ādāya garbhanāṅīṃ tu śakuno yāti khañjanaḥ|| 86.36 ||
sa tu rājyāntaraṃ prāpya mahākalyāṇamāpnuyāt|
dīrghaṃ viṃśatihastaṃ tu hastaṣoḍaśavistṛtam|| 86.37 ||
kuryāt tu lakṣaṇopetaṃ yajñamaṇḍalamuttamam|
tato’pare’hni pūrvāhṇemātṛṇāṃ pūjanaṃ caret|| 86.38 ||
kuḍyalagnāṃ vasordhārāṃ vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ tathaiva ca|
candanāgurukastūrīdhūmakarpūracūrṇakaiḥ|| 86.39 ||
sampūjya maṇḍalasthānaṃ tasmin hrauṃ śambhave namaḥ|
astrāya huṃ phaḍityevaṃ likhenmantradvayaṃ budhaḥ|| 86.40 ||
mantravinmaṇḍalajñaśca sūtraiḥ kambalasambhavaiḥ|
kauśeyairvā svastikākhyaṃ prathamaṃ maṇḍalaṃ likhet|| 86.41 ||
caturhastapramāṇaṃ tu maṇḍalaṃ vilikhet tataḥ|
hastapramāṇaṃ padmaṃ tu maṇḍalasya prakīrtitam|| 86.42 ||
dvārāṇi sārdhahastāni karṇikākeśarojjvalam|
sitaṃ raktaṃ ca pītaṃ ca kṛṣṇaṃ haritameva ca|| 86.43 ||
śālicūrṇaiśca kausumbhairhāridrairharidudbhavaiḥ|
kuryāt tathāñjanaiścūrṇai rājā maṇḍavṛddhaye|| 86.44 ||
padmāntataḥ samārabhya tālaṃ paścimagāminam|
paścimadvāramadhye caśatapatraṃ vinirdiśet|| 86.45 ||
pratyekaṃ dvāramadhye tu padmaṃ caivāṣṭapatrakam|
kuryānmaṇḍalabhāgajñaścūrṇaireva pṛthak pṛthak|| 86.46 ||
cūrṇaistu maṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā sūtrāṇyutsārayet tataḥ|
utsārya sūtraṃ prathamaṃ maṇḍalaṃ pūjayet tataḥ|| 86.47 ||
bhavanāya nama iti tato hastaṃ viyojayet|
savyāvalambahastaṃ tu rajaḥpātraṃ samācaret|| 86.48 ||
madhyamānāmikāṅguṣṭhairupariṣṭād yathecchayā|
adhomukhāṅgulīḥ kṛtvā pātayecca vicakṣaṇaḥ|| 86.49 ||
samārekhā tu kartavyā vicchitrā puṣparañjitā|
aṅguṣṭhaparvanaipuṇyāt samā kāryā vijānatā|| 86.50 ||
saṃsaktaviṣamaṃ sthūlaṃ vicchinnaṃ kṛsarakṛtim|
paryantamarpitaṃ hrasvamālikhenna kadācana|| 86.51 ||
saṃsakte kalahaṃ vidyādūrdhva rekhe tu vigraham|
atisthūle bhaved vyādhirnityaṃ pīḍāvimiśrite|| 86.52 ||
bindubhirbhayamāpnoti śatrupakṣānna saṃśayaḥ|
kṛśāyāṃ cārthahāniḥ syācchinnāyāṃ maraṇaṃ dhruvam|| 86.53 ||
viyogo vā bhavet tasya iṣṭadravyasutasya vā|
aviditvā likhed yastu maṇḍalaṃ tu yathecchayā|| 86.54 ||
sarvadoṣānavāpnoti ye doṣāḥ pūrvamīritāḥ|
sitasarṣapadūrvāyā rekhāḥ kāryā vijānatā|| 86.55 ||
vimalaṃ vijayaṃ bhadraṃ vimānaṃ śubhadaṃ śivam|
vardhamānaṃ ca devaṃ ca śatākṣaṃ kāmadāyakam|| 86.56 ||
rucikaṃ svastikaṃ caiva dvādaśaite tu maṇḍalāḥ|
yathāsthānaṃ yathāyajñaṃ yojanīyā vicakṣaṇaiḥ|| 86.57 ||
sāgare madhyamāne tu pīyūṣārthaṃ surotkaraiḥ|
pīyūṣadhāraṇārthāya nirmitā viśvakarmaṇā|| 86.58 ||
kalāṃ kalāṃ tu devānāmasitvā te pṛthak pṛthak|
yataḥ kṛtāstu kalasāstataste parikīrtitāḥ|| 86.59 ||
navaiva kalasāḥ proktā nāmatastānnibodhata|
gohyopagohyo maruto mayūkhaśca tathāparaḥ|| 86.60 ||
manohācāryabhadraśca vijayastanu dūṣakaḥ|
indriyaghno’tha vijayo navamaḥ parikīrtitaḥ|| 86.61 ||
teṣāmeva kramād bhūpa nava nāmāni yāni tu|
śṛṇu tānyaparāṇyeva śāntidāni sadaiva hi|| 86.62 ||
kṣitīndraḥ prathamaḥ prokto dvitīyo jalasambhavaḥ|
pavanāgnī tato dve tu yajamānastataḥ paraḥ|| 86.63 ||
koṣasambhavāmnā tu ṣaṣṭhaḥ sa parikīrtitaḥ|
somastu saptamaḥ prokta ādityastu tathāṣṭamaḥ|| 86.64 ||
vijayo nāma kalaso yo’sau navama ucyate|
sa tu paṃcamukhaḥ prokto mahādevasvarūpadhṛk|| 86.65 ||
ghaṭasya pañcavaktreṣu pañcavaktraḥ svayaṃ tathā|
yathākāṣṭhāṃ sthitaḥ samyagvāmadevādināmataḥ|| 86.66 ||
maṇḍalasya tu padmānte pañcavaktraṃ ghaṭaṃ nyaset|
kṣitīndraṃ pūrvato nyasya paścime jalasambhavam|| 86.67 ||
vāyavye vāyavaṃ nyasya āgneye hyagnisambhavam|
nairṛtye yajamānaṃ tu aiśānyāṃ koṣasambhavam|| 86.68 ||
somamuttarato yojyaṃ sauraṃ dakṣiṇato nyaset|
nyasyaivaṃ kalasāṃścaiva teṣu caitān vicintayet|| 86.69 ||
kalasānāṃ mukhe brahmā grīvāyāṃ śaṅkaraḥ sthitaḥ|
mūle tu saṃsthito viṣṇurmadhye mātṛgaṇāḥ sthitāḥ|| 86.70 ||
dikpālā devatāḥ sarvā veṣṭayanti diśo daśa|
kukṣau tu sāgarāḥ sapta saptadvīpāśca saṃsthitāḥ|| 86.71 ||
nakṣatrāṇi grahāḥ sarve tathaiva kulaparvatāḥ|
gaṅgādyāḥ saritaḥ sarvā vedāścatvāra eva ca|| 86.72 ||
kalase saṃsthitāḥ sarve teṣu tāni vicintayet|
ratnāni sarvabījāni puṣpāṇi ca phalāni ca|| 86.73 ||
vajramauktikavaidūryamahāpadmendrasphāṭikaiḥ|
sarvadhāmamayaṃ bilvaṃ nāgarodumbara tathā|| 86.74 ||
bījapūrakajambīrakāśmīrāmrātadāḍimam|
yavaṃ śāliṃ ca nīvāraṃ godhūmaṃ sitasarṣapam|| 86.75 ||
kuṅkumāgurukarpūramadanaṃ rocanaṃ tathā|
candanaṃ ca tathā māṃsīmelāṃ kuṣṭhaṃ tathaiva ca|| 86.76 ||
kastūrīpatracūrṇaṃ ca jalaniryāsakāmbudam|
śaileyaṃ vadaraṃ jātīpatrapuṣpe tathaiva ca|| 86.77 ||
kālaśākaṃ tathā pṛkkā devīparṇakameva ca|
vacāṃ dhātrīṃ samañjiṣṭhāṃ turuṣkaṃ maṅgalāṣṭakam|| 86.78 ||
dūrvāṃ mohanikāṃ bhadrāṃ śatamūlīṃ śatāvarīm|
varṇānāṃ saralāṃ kṣudrāṃ sahadevīṃ gajāhvayām|| 86.79 ||
pūrṇakoṣāṃ sitāṃ pīṭhāṃ guñjāṃ śirasikānalau|
vyāmakaṃ gajadantaṃ ca śatapuṣpaṃ punarnavām|| 86.80 ||
brāhmīṃ devīṃ śivāṃ rudrāṃ sarvasandhānikāṃ tathā|
samāhṛdya śubhānetān rudrāṃ kalaseṣu nidhārpayet|| 86.81 ||
kalasasya yathādeśaṃ vidhiṃ śambhuṃ gadādharam|
yathākramaṃ pūjayitvā śambhu mukhyatayā yajet|| 86.82 ||
prāsādena tu mantreṇa śambhuṃ tantreṇa śaṅkaram|
prathamaṃ pūjayenmadhye nānā naivedyavedanaiḥ|| 86.83 ||
dikpālānāṃ ghaṭeṣveva dikpālānapi pūjayet|
pūrvaṃ bahiḥ sthāpiteṣu grahāṇāṃ kalaseṣu ca|| 86.84 ||
navagrahān pūjayet tu mātṛrmātṛghaṭeṣu ca|
sarve devā ghaṭe pūjyā ghaṭāsteṣāṃ pṛthak pṛthak|| 86.85 ||
navaiva tatra pūrvoktāḥ smṛtā mukhyatayā nṛpa|
bhakṣyairbhojyaiśca peyaiśca puṣpairnānāvidhaiḥ phalaiḥ|| 86.86 ||
yāvakaiḥ pāyasaiścaiva yathāsambhavayojitaiḥ|
puṣyasnānāya nṛpatiḥ pūjayet sakalān surān|| 86.87 ||
dakṣiṇe maṇḍalasyātha kuṇḍaṃ nirmāṃya pāyasaiḥ|
samidbhiḥ śālisiddhārthairghṛtairdūrvākṣataistathā|| 86.88 ||
kevalaiśca tathaivājyaiḥ pūjitān sakalān surān|
homena toṣayed vṛddhyai nṛpaḥ sartvikpurohitaḥ|| 86.89 ||
homānte maṇḍalodīcyāṃ vedikāyāṃ sapaṭṭakam|
rocanākhyamarlakārāṃstathā sarvān niyojayet|| 86.90 ||
vṛddhāvaṅgulamaṅgulyā ṣaḍviṃśaṅgulikāvadhi|
vṛttaṃ vā caturasraṃ vā padmaṃ trikoṇasaṃjñakam|| 86.91 ||
ratneśān padmamadhye tu gomuṣṭikavināyakaiḥ|
śrīśrīvṛkṣavarārohāmumādevīṃ śubhānvitām|| 86.92 ||
ratnaiḥ sarvairalaṅkāraiḥ paṭṭaṃ kāryaṃ dvihastakam|
hastavistāramucchrāyaṃ navahastaṃ daśāṅgulam|| 86.93 ||
snānārthaṃ sārdhahastaṃ ca paṭṭaṃ vṛttaṃ guṇānvitam|
śayyā caturguṇā dīrghā dhanurmānaṃ tu pīṭhakam|| 86.94 ||
gajasiṃhakṛtāṭopaṃ hemaratnavibhūṣitam|
siṃhākhyaṃ sārdhavistārād daṇḍāsanamathāpi vā|| 86.95 ||
vyāghracitrakapaṭṭairvā upadhānāni kārayet|
anyairvā nirmitāṃ carmamṛdutūlakapūritā|| 86.96 ||
śayyā dīrghārghavistīrṇā caturhastā sulakṣaṇā|
vitastyadhikamicchanti nṛpasya guruvidyayā|| 86.97 ||
ardhacandrasamaṃ kuryādāsanaṃ caturasrakam|
upadhānāni śayyāyāḥ karṇādimūlabhedataḥ|| 86.98 ||
ṣoḍaśaivātra kāryāṇi varṇacitrayutāni ca|
yānaṃ siṃhāsana paṭṭaṃ śayyopakaraṇādikam|| 86.99 ||
rājño nūtanayogyaṃ tad vedyā uttarato nyaset|
teṣāṃ tu paścime svarṇaratnaughakhacite vare|| 86.100 ||
paryaṅke yajñadārvaughanirmite mahadāstare|
ardhācchādanasaṃyukte carmāvṛtacatuṣṭaye|| 86.101 ||
vṛṣabhasya tathorṇāyāḥ siṃhaśārdulayorapi|
pādapīṭhe ratnayute pādāvāropya pārthivaḥ|| 86.102 ||
tasmin paryaṅkapīṭhasthe carmāvṛtacatuṣṭaye|
nānālaṅkārabhūṣāḍhyaṃ nṛpatiṃ ratnaśālinam|| 86.103 ||
snāpayed brāhmaṇaiḥ sārdhaṃ rājānaṃ sukhasaṅgatam|
saṃvītakambalaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ bahuvastraiśca śaubhitam|| 86.104 ||
kalasevalipuṣpādyaiḥ śālicūrṇaiśca snāpayet|
aṣṭau ṣauḍaśa viṃśāṣṭaśatamadhikaṃ ca vā|| 86.105 ||
kalasānāṃ samākhyātā adhikasyottarottaram|
jayakalyāṇadairmantrairmaṅgalotthaiśca śāmbhavaiḥ|| 86.106 ||
vaiṣṇavairatha dikpālairgrahamantraiśca mātṛkaiḥ|
ājyaṃ tejaḥ samuddiṣṭamājyaṃ pāpaharaṃ param|| 86.107 ||
ājyaṃ surāṇāmāhāraprājye lokāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ|
bhaumāntarikṣaṃ divyaṃ vā yat te kalmamaṣamāgatam|| 86.108 ||
sarvaṃ tadājyasaṃsparśāt praṇāśamupagacchatu|
tato’panīyagātrāt tu kambalaṃ vastrameva ca|| 86.109 ||
kalasaiḥ snāpayed bhūpaṃ puṣpasnānīyapūritaiḥ|
ebhirmantrairnaraśreṣṭha tanutattvārthaṃsādhakaiḥ|| 86.110 ||
surāstvāmabhiṣiñcantu ye ca siddhāḥ purātanāḥ|
brahmā viṣṇuśca rudrāśca sādhyāśca samarudgaṇāḥ|| 86.111 ||
ādityā vasavo rudrā aśvinau yau bhiṣagvarau|
aditirdevamātā ca svāhā lakṣmīḥ sarasvatī|| 86.112 ||
kīrtirlakṣmīrghṛtiḥ śrīśca sinīvālī kuhūstathā|
ditiśca surasā caiva vinatā kadrureva ca|| 86.113 ||
devapatnayaśca yāḥ proktā devamātara eva ca|
sarvāstvāmaṣiñcantu siddhāścāpsarasāṃ gaṇāḥ|| 86.114 ||
nakṣatrāṇi muhūrtāśca pakṣāhorātrasandhayaḥ|
saṃvatsara nimeṣāśca kalāḥ kāṣṭhāḥ kṣaṇā lavāḥ|| 86.115 ||
sarve tvāmabhiṣiñcantu kālasyāvayavastathā|
vaimānikāḥ suragaṇā manavaḥ sāgaraiḥ saha|| 86.116 ||
saritaśca mahānāgā nāgāḥ kiṃpuruṣāstathā|
vaikhānasā mahābhāgā dvijā vaihāyasāśca ye|| 86.117 ||
saptarṣayaḥ sadārāśca dhruvasthānāni yāni tu|
marīciratriḥ pulahaḥ pulastyaḥ kraturaṅgirāḥ|| 86.118 ||
bhṛguḥ sanatkumāraśca sanakaśca sanandanaḥ|
sanātanaśca dakṣaśca jaigīṣavyo’bhinandanaḥ|| 86.119 ||
ekataśca dvitaścaiva trito jāvālikāśyapau|
durvāsā durvinītaśca kaṇvaḥ kātyāyanastathā|| 86.120 ||
mārkaṇḍeyo dīrghatamāḥ śunaḥśepho vidūrathaḥ|
aurvaḥ saṃvartakaścaiva cyavano’triḥ parāśaraḥ|| 86.121 ||
dvaipāyano yavakrīto devarātaḥ sahātmajaḥ|
ete cānye ca bahavo vedavrataparāyaṇāḥ|| 86.122 ||
saśiṣyāste’bhiṣiñcantu sadārāśca tapodhanāḥ|
parvatāstaravo nadyaḥ puṇyānyatatanāni ca|| 86.123 ||
prajāpatiḥ kṣitiścaiva gāvo viśvasya mātaraḥ|
vāhanāni ca divyāni sarve lokāścarācarāḥ|| 86.124 ||
agnayaḥ pitārastārā jīmūtāḥ khaṃ diśo jalam|
ete cānye ca bahavaḥ puṇyasaṃkīrtanāḥ śubhāḥ|| 86.125 ||
tauyaistvāmabhiṣiñcantu sarvotpātanibarhaṇaiḥ|
ityevaṃ śubhadairetairdivyairmantraistathāparaiḥ|| 86.126 ||
sorairnārāyaṇai raudrairbrahmaśakrasamudbhavaiḥ|
āpohiṣṭhā hiraṇyeti sambhaveti sureti ca|| 86.127 ||
mānastoketi mantreṇa gandhadvāretyanena ca|
sarvamaṃgalamāṃgalye śrīśca te grahayogibhiḥ|| 86.128 ||
ityevaṃ snānamāsādya gātramāvṛtya kambalaiḥ|
sarvamaṃgalamantreṇa vastraṃ kārpāsakaṃ dhriyāt|| 86.129 ||
ācamya ca tato devān guruṃ viprāṃśca pūjayet|
dhvajacchatraṃ cāmaraṃ ca ghaṇṭāṃ cāśvān gajāṃstathā|| 86.130 ||
mantraṃ japtvā dhārayet tu tato gaccheddhutāśanam|
tatra gatvā vahnimadhye vahneḥ śrīrvīkṣya pārthivaḥ|| 86.131 ||
nimittānyanimittāni lakṣayet tatra bindubhiḥ|
daivajñakañcukyamātyavandipaurajanairvṛtaḥ|| 86.132 ||
vāditraghoṣaistumalaistathā tauryatrikaiḥ śubhaiḥ|
kṛtvā śeṣe punaḥ śāntimāśīrvācya ca vai dvijān|| 86.133 ||
pūrṇāṃ vidhāya vidhivad dakṣiṇāṃ kanakānvitām|
dhānyāni cātha vāsāṃsi dattvā kuryād visarjanam|| 86.134 ||
tataḥ śeṣajalaiḥ sarvānamātyādīn purohitaḥ|
secayeccaturaṅgaṃ ca balaṃ cāpi sarāṣṭrakam|| 86.135 ||
evaṃ kṛtvā nṛpaḥ paścāt trirātraṃ saṃyatoṃ bhavet|
māṃsamaithunahīnaśca kuryānmāṅgalyasevanam|| 86.136 ||
puṣyanakṣatrayuktā tu tṛtīyā yadi labhyate|
tasyāṃ pūjyā sadā devī caṇḍikā śaṃkareṇa ha|| 86.137 ||
pāñcālikāvihārādyaiḥ śiśūnāṃ kautukaistathā|
vaivāhikena vidhinā mohayeccaṇḍikāṃ śivām|| 86.138 ||
catuṣpatheṣu sarveṣu devadevīgaheṣu ca|
patākābhiralaṃ kuryādevaṃ kurvanna sīdati|| 86.139 ||
evaṃ kṛtvā śāntiyāgaṃ tathā puṣyābhiṣecanam|
caturaṅgaiḥ samaṃ rājā bhāryābhistu naraiḥ saha|| 86.140 ||
rājyamaṇḍalasaṃyuktaḥ paratreha na sīdati|
nātaḥ parataro yajño nātaḥ paratarotsavaḥ|| 86.141 ||
nātaḥ paratarā śāntirnātaḥ parataraṃ śivam|
anenaiva vidhānena nṛpaterabhiṣecanam|| 86.142 ||
yuvarājyābhiṣekaṃ ca kuryādrājapurohitaḥ|
nṛpābhiṣekakaraṇamādau yadi samācaret|| 86.143 ||
anenaiva vidhānena sthiraḥ syānnṛpatistadā|
ayaṃ yajñaḥ samuddiṣṭaḥ śakrārthaṃ brahmaṇā purā|
evaṃ yajñaṃ nṛpaḥ kṛtvā paratreha na sīdati|| 86.144 ||
|| iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe ṣaḍaśautitamo’dhyāyaḥ|| 86 ||

<87>
||aurva uvāca||
athātaḥ śṛṇu rājendra śakotthānaṃ dhvajotsavam|
yat kṛtvā nṛpatiryāti na kadācit parābhavam|| 87.1 ||
ravau haristhe dvādaśyāṃ śravaṇena viḍaujasam|
arādhayennapaḥ samyak sarvavighnopaśāntaye|| 87.2 ||
rājoparircaro nāma vasunāmāparastu yaḥ|
nṛpastenāyamatulo yajñaḥ prāvartitaḥ purā|| 87.3 ||
pravṛṭkāle ca nabhasi dvādaśyāmasitetare|
purāhito bahuvidhairvādyaistūryaiḥ samanvitaḥ|| 87.4 ||
prathamaṃ śakaketvartha vṛkṣamāmantrya vardhayet|
sāṃvatsaro vārdhakiśca kṛtamaṅgalakautukaḥ|| 87.5 ||
udyāne devātagāre śmaśāne mārgamadhyataḥ|
ye jātāstaravastāṃstu varjayed vāsavadhvaje|| 87.6 ||
bahuvallīyutaṃ śuṣkaṃ bahukaṇṭakasaṃyutam|
kubjaṃ vṛkṣādanīyuktaṃ latācchannataruṃ tyajet|| 87.7 ||
pakṣivāsasamākīrṇaṃ koṭarairbahubhiryutam|
pavanānalavidhvastaṃ taruṃ yatnena varjayet|| 87.8 ||
nārīsaṃjñāśca ye vṛkṣā atihrasvā atikṛśāḥ|
tān sadā varjayed dhīraḥ sarvadā śakrapūjane|| 87.9 ||
arjuno’pyaśvakarṇaśca vaṭaḥ priyakoṣakaśca|
audumbaraśca paṃcaite ketvarthe hyuttamāḥ smṛtāḥ|| 87.10 ||
anye ca devadārvādyāḥ śālādyāstaravastathā|
praśastāstu parigrāhyā nāpraśastāḥ kadācana|| 87.11 ||
dhṛtvā vṛkṣaṃ tato rātrau spṛṣṭvā mantramimaṃ paṭhet|
yāni vṛkṣeṣu bhūtāni tebhyaḥ svasti namo’stu vaḥ|| 87.12 ||
upahāraṃ gṛhītvemaṃ kriyatāṃ vāsavadhvajam|
pārthivastvāṃ varayate svasti te’stu nagottama|| 87.13 ||
dhvajārthaṃ devarājasya pūjeyaṃ pratigṛhyatām|
tato’pare’hni taṃ chittvā mūlamaṣṭāṃgulaṃ punaḥ|| 87.14 ||
jale kṣipet tathāgrasya chitvaiva caturaṅgulam|
tato nītvā puradvāraṃ ketuṃ nirmāya tatra vai|| 87.15 ||
śuklāṣṭamyāṃ bhādrapade ketuṃ vedīṃ praveśayet|
dvāviṃśaddhastamānastu adhamaḥ keturucyate|| 87.16 ||
dvātriṃśat tu tato jyāyān dvācatvāriṃśadeva ca|
tato’dhikaḥ samākhyāto dvāpañcāśat tathottamaḥ|| 87.17 ||
kumāryaḥ pañca kartavyāḥ śaktasya nṛpasattama|
śālamayyastu tāḥ sarvā aparāḥ śakramātṛkāḥ|| 87.18 ||
ketoḥ pādapramāṇena kāryāḥ śakrakumārikāḥ|
mātṛkārdhapramāṇāstu yantraṃhastadvayaṃ tathā|| 87.19 ||
evaṃ kṛtvā kumārīśca mātṛkāḥ ketumeva ca|
ekādaśyāṃ site pakṣe yaṣṭiṃ tāmadhivāsayet|| 87.20 ||
adhivāsya tato yaṣṭiṃ gandhadvārādimantrakaiḥ|
dvādaśyāṃ maṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā vāsavaṃ vistṛtātmakam|| 87.21 ||
acyutaṃ pūjayitvā tu śakraṃ paścāt prapūjayet|
śakrasya pratimāṃ kuryāt kāñcanīṃ dākhīṃ ca vā|| 87.22 ||
anyataijasasambhūtāṃ sarvābhāve tu mṛnmayīm|
tāṃ maṇḍalasya madhye tu pūjayitvā viśeṣataḥ|| 87.23 ||
tataḥ śubhe muhūrte tu ketumutthāpayennṛpaḥ|
vajrahasta surārighna bahunetra purandara|
kṣemārthaṃ sarvalokānāṃ pūjeyaṃ pratigṛhyatām|| 87.24 ||
ehyehi sarvāmarasiddhasaṅghairabhiṣṭuto vajradharāmareśa|
samutthitastvaṃ śravaṇādyapāde gṛhāṇa pūjāṃ bhagavannamaste|| 87.25 ||
evamuttaratantroktairdahanaplavanādibhiḥ|
iti mantreṇa tantreṇa nānānaivedyavedanaiḥ|| 87.26 ||
apapaiḥ pāyasaiḥ pānairguḍairdhānābhireva ca|
bhakṣyairbhojyaiśca vividhaiḥ pūjayecchrīvivṛddhaye|| 87.27 ||
ghaṭe tu daśadikpālān grahāṃśca paripūjayet|
sādhyādīn sakalān devān mātṛḥ sarvā anukramāt|| 87.28 ||
tataḥ śubhe muhūrte tu jñānivardhakisaṃyutaḥ|
ketūtthāpanabhūmiṃ tu yajñavedyāstu paścime|| 87.29 ||
vipraiḥ purohitaiḥ sārdhaṃ gacchedrājā sumaṃgalaiḥ|
rajjubhiḥ paṃcabhirbaddhaṃ yantraśliṣṭaṃ samātṛkam|| 87.30 ||
kumārībhistu saṃyuktaṃ dikpālānāṃ ca paṭṭakaiḥ|
bṛhadbhiratikāntaiśca nānādravyaiḥ supūritaiḥ|| 87.31 ||
yathāvarṇairyathādeśe yojitairvastraveṣṭitaiḥ|
yuktaṃ taṃ kiṅkiṇījālairbṛhadghaṇṭaughacāmarai|| 87.32 ||
bhūṣitaṃ mukurairuccairmālyairbahuvidhaistathā|
bahupuṣpaiḥ sugandhaiśca bhūṣitaṃ ratnamālayā|| 87.33 ||
citramālyāmbaraiścaiva caturbhirapi toraṇaiḥ|
utthāpayenmahāketuṃ rājakīyai śanaiḥ śanaiḥ|| 87.34 ||
tamutthāya mahāketuṃ pūjitaṃ maṇḍalāntare|
pratimāṃ tāṃ nayenmūlaṃ ketoḥ śakraṃ vicintayan|| 87.35 ||
yajet taṃ pūrvavat tatra śacīṃ mātalimeva ca|
jayantaṃ tanayaṃ tasya vajramairāvataṃ tathā|| 87.36 ||
grahāṃścāpyatha dikapālān sarvāśca gaṇadevatāḥ|
apūpādyaiḥ pūtayet tu valibhiḥ pāyasādibhiḥ|| 87.37 ||
pūjitānāṃ ca devānāṃ śaśvaddhomaṃ samācaret|
homānte tu baliṃ dadyād vāsavāya mahātmane|| 87.38 ||
tilaṃ ghṛtaṃ cākṣataṃ ca pūṣpaṃ dūrvāṃ tathaiva ca|
etaistu juhuyād devān svaiḥ svairmantrairnarottama|| 87.39 ||
tato homāvasāne tu bhojayed brāhmaṇānapi|
evaṃ sampūjayennityaṃ saptarātraṃ dine dine|| 87.40 ||
brāhmaṇaiḥ sahitā rājā vedavedāṃgapāragaiḥ|
sarvatra śakrapūjāsu yajñeṣu parikīrtitaḥ|| 87.41 ||
trātāramiti mantro’yaṃ vāsavasya priyaḥ paraḥ|
evaṃ kṛtvā divābhāge śakrotthāpanamāditaḥ|| 87.42 ||
śravaṇarkṣayutāyāṃ tu dvādaśyāṃ pārthivaḥ svayam|
antyapāde bharaṇyāṃ tu niśi śakraṃ visarjayet|| 87.43 ||
supteṣu sarvalokeṣu yathā rājā na paśyati|
ṣaṇmāsānmṛtyumāpnoti rājā dṛṣṭvā visarjanam|| 87.44 ||
śakrasya nṛpaśārdūla tasmānnekṣeta tannapaḥ|
visarjanasya mantro’yaṃ purāvidbhirudīritaḥ|| 87.45 ||
sārdhaṃ surāsuragaṇaiḥ purandara śatakrato|
upahāraṃ gṛhītvema mahendradhvaja gamyatām|| 87.46 ||
sūtake tu samutpanne vāre bhaumasya vā śanaiḥ|
bhūmikampādikotpāte vāsavaṃ na visarjayet|| 87.47 ||
utpāte saptarātraṃ tu tathopaplavadarśane|
vyatītya śanibhaumau ca hyanyarkṣe’pi visarjayet|| 87.48 ||
sūtake tvatha saṃprāpte vyatīte sūtake punaḥ|
yasmin tasmin caiva sūtakānte visarjayet|| 87.49 ||
tathā ketuṃ nṛpo rakṣet patanti śakunā yathā|
na ketau nṛpaśārdūla yāvannahi visarjanam|| 87.50 ||
śanaiḥ śanaiḥ pātayet tu yathotthāpanamāditaḥ|
kṛtaṃ tathā yathā bhagne ketau mṛtyumavāpnuyāt|| 87.51 ||
visṛṣṭaṃ śakraketuṃ tu sālaṅkāraṃ tathā niśi|
kṣipedanena mantreṇa tvagādhe salile nṛpa|| 87.52 ||
tiṣṭha keto mahābhāga yāvat saṃvatsaraṃ jale|
bhavāya sarvalokānāmantarāya vināśaka|| 87.53 ||
utthāpayet tūryaravaiḥ sarvalokasya vai puraḥ|
raho visarjayet ketuṃ viśeṣo yaḥ prapūjane|| 87.54 ||
evaṃ yaḥ kurute pūjāṃ vāsavasya mahātmanaḥ|
sa ciraṃ pṛthivīṃ bhuktvā vāsavaṃ lokamāpnuyāt|| 87.55 ||
na tasya rājye durbhikṣaṃ nādhayo vyādhayaḥ kvacit|
sthāsyanti mṛtyurnākāle janānāṃ tatra jāyate|| 87.56 ||
tattulyaḥ ko’pi nānyo’sti priyaḥ śakrasya pārthiva|
tasya pūjā sarvapūjā keśavādyāśca tatragāḥ|| 87.57 ||
sakalakaluṣahāri vyādhidurbhikṣanāśaṃ sakalabhavaniveśaṃ sarvasaubhāgyakāri|
surapatigṛhagābhirvārcanaṃ śakraketoḥ pratiśaradamanekaiḥ pūjayecchrīvivṛddhayai|| 87.58 ||
|| iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe saptāśītitamo’dhyāyaḥ|| 87 ||

<88>
||aurva uvāca||
jyeṣṭha daśaharāyāṃ tu viṣṇoriṣṭiṃ nṛpa śṛṇu|
yena vā vidhinā kuryādiṣṭiṃ viṣṇornṛpaḥ sadā|| 88.1 ||
pratyabdaṃ pārthivaḥ kuryāt pratimāṃ kāñcanīṃ hareḥ|
anyatejīmayīṃ vāpi dāravīṃ vā śilāmayīm|| 88.2 ||
tāṃ pratiṣṭhāpya vidhinā mānonmānaistu silpibhiḥ|
pratiṣṭhāṃ vidhivat tasyāḥ kuryād vipraiḥ purohitaiḥ|| 88.3 ||
tāṃ saṃsthāpya surāgāre svayaṃ vā yatnataḥ kṛte|
vāsudevasya bījena pūrvoktavidhinā tathā|| 88.4 ||
sarvopacārairbhaktyā nu vāsudevaṃ prapūjayet|
pūjānte saṃskṛte vahnau kuṇḍamadhye sthito dvijaḥ|| 88.5 ||
ājyaiḥ sahasraṃ juhuyādāhutīnāṃ hareḥ priyam|
saṃpūjya vāsudevaṃ tu homaṃ kṛtvā tato dvijaḥ|| 88.6 ||
nṛpasyānumate tāṃ tu pratimāṃ maṇḍalaṃ nayet|
pratimāyāḥ kapolau dvau spṛṣṭvā dakṣiṇapāṇinā|| 88.7 ||
prāṇapratiṣṭhāṃ kurvīta tasyāṃ devasya vai hareḥ|
kṛtāyāṃ tu pratiṣṭhāyāṃ prāṇānāṃ nṛpasattama|| 88.8 ||
viṣṇuprāṇāstāṃ pratimāmāyānti niyataṃ svayam|
prāṇeṣvathāgateṣvasyāṃ devatvaṃ niyataṃ bhavet|| 88.9 ||
akṛtāyāṃ pratiṣṭhāyāṃ prāṇānāṃ pratimāsu ca|
yathāpūrvaṃ tathābhāvaḥ svarṇādīnāṃ na viṣṇutā|| 88.10 ||
anyeṣāmapi devānāṃ pratimāsvapi pārthiva|
prāṇapratiṣṭhā kartavyā tasyā devatvasiddhaye|| 88.11 ||
suvarṇaṃ tu suvarṇaṃ syācchilā dāru tathā śilā|
anyacca svasvarūpaṃ syāt prāṇasthānamṛte sadā|| 88.12 ||
vāsudevasya bījena tad viṣṇorityanena ca|
tathaivāṅgāṅgimantrābhyāṃ pratiṣṭhāmācareddhareḥ|| 88.13 ||
tathaiva hṛdaye’ṅguṣṭhaṃ dattvā śaśvacca mantravit|
ebhirmantraiḥ pratiṣṭhāpya hṛdaye’pi samācaret|| 88.14 ||
asyai prāṇāḥ pratiṣṭhantu asyai prāṇāḥ kṣarantu yat|
asau devatvasaṃkhyāyai svāheti yajuruccaran|| 88.15 ||
aṅgamantrairaṅgimantrairvaidikairityanena ca|
prāṇapratiṣṭhāṃ sarvatra pratimāsu samācaret|| 88.16 ||
pratimāpūjane kuryādātmanyapi ca mantravit|
prāṇapratiṣṭhāṃ prathamaṃ pūjābhāgaviśuddhaye|| 88.17 ||
asmin prāṇapratiṣṭhāṃ tu pratimāpūjanādṛte|
na kaścit tu budhaḥ kuryāt kṛtvā mṛtyumavāpnuyāt|| 88.18 ||
viṣṇoriṣṭimimāṃ kṛtvā daśamyāṃ pārthivottamaḥ|
tasyāmeva tu pūrṇāyāṃ pratimāṃ sthāpayet tataḥ|| 88.19 ||
evaṃ daśaharāyāṃ tu kṛtveṣṭiṃ pārthivo hareḥ|
sarvān kāmānavāpnoti nirvighno’pi sa jāyate|| 88.20 ||
śrīpaṃcamyāṃ śriyaṃ devīṃ kundaiḥ saṃpūjayetsadā|
vāsavaṃ gajarājasthamupahāraistathottamaiḥ|| 88.21 ||
lakṣmyāstantraṃ mahāmantraṃ vāsavasya puroditam|
atrāpi pūjane grāhyaṃ maṇḍalādi yathākramam|| 88.22 ||
evaṃ kṛte pūjane tu śrīpaṃcamyāṃ viśeṣataḥ|
śrīyuto nṛpatirbhūyānna śrīhānimavāpnuyāt|| 88.23 ||
sadācāraviśeṣo’yaṃ kathitastava pārthiva|
niṣedhe tu viśeṣāṃśca śṛṇu yena śriyeṣyate|| 88.24 ||
asaṃpūjya tathā viṣṇum̐ śivamagniṃ purandaram|
adattvā ca tathā dānaṃ na bhuñjīta nṛpaḥ kvacit|| 88.25 ||
hāvayedagnihotraṃ tu nityameva purohitaiḥ|
akṛtvā cāgnihotraṃ tu bhuñjannarakamāpnuyāt|| 88.26 ||
nārakṣite gṛhe rājā ratnadīpavivarjite|
svapet tathā striyā sārdhaṃ na kadācan saṃviśet|| 88.27 ||
bhuktvānnaṃ śrīphalaṃ nādyāt tathā dhātrīphalaṃ nṛpaḥ|
buddhikṣayakarā hyotā māṣa āsavamṛttikāḥ|| 88.28 ||
nimbāṭarūṣacyutāśca buddhivṛddhikarā matāḥ|
buddhikṣayakarān nityaṃ tvajedrājā ca bhojane|| 88.29 ||
bhakṣayedanvahaṃ buddhivṛddhihetuṃ nṛpottamaḥ|
na paryāyavihīnaṃ tu prārohedāsanaṃ nṛpaḥ|| 88.30 ||
na yānaṃ na gajaṃ nāśvamāroheddhīnamāsanaiḥ|
naikastu vicaredrājā kadācidapi nirjane|| 88.31 ||
madahetuṃ na bhuṃjīyāt kadācidapi bhojane|
kadācinnāpi seveta hyaṣṭamyāṃ māṃsamaithune|| 88.32 ||
darśaśrāddhaṃ gayāśrāddhaṃ tilaistarpaṇameva ca|
na jīvatpitṛko bhūpa kuryāt kṛtvāghamāpnuyāt|| 88.33 ||
na kṣetrajādīṃstanayān rājye rājābhiṣecayet|
pitṛṇāṃ śūddhaye nityamaurase tanaye sati|| 88.34 ||
aurasaḥ kṣetrajaścaiva dattaḥ kṛtrima eva ca|
gūḍhotpanno’paviddhaśca bhāgārhāstanayā ime|| 88.35 ||
kānīnaśca sahoḍhaśca krītaḥ paunarbhavastathā|
svayaṃdattaśca dāsaśca ṣaḍate putrapāṃsulāḥ|| 88.36 ||
abhāve pūrvapūrvoṣāṃ parān samabhiṣecaye|
paunarbhavaṃ svayaṃdattaṃ dāsaṃ rājye na yojayet|| 88.37 ||
dattādyāścāpi tanayā nijagotreṇa saṃskṛtāḥ|
āyānti putratāṃ samyaganyabījasamudbhavāḥ|| 88.38 ||
piturgotreṇa yaḥ putraḥ saṃskṛtaḥ pṛthivīpateḥ|
ācūḍāntaṃ sa putraḥ na putratāṃ yāti cānyataḥ|| 88.39 ||
cūḍādyā yadi saṃskārā nijagotreṇa saṃsthitāḥ|
dattādyāstanayāste syuranyathā dāsa ucyate|| 88.40 ||
ūrdhvaṃ tu paṃcamād varṣād dattādyāṃśca sutānnṛpa|
gṛhītvā paṃcavarṣīyaṃ putreṣṭiṃ prathamaṃ caret|| 88.41 ||
paunarbhavaṃ tu tanayaṃ jātamātraṃ samānayet|
kṛtvā paunarbhavaṣṭomaṃ jātamātrasya tasya vai|| 88.42 ||
sarvāṃstu kuryāt saṃskārān jātakarmādikānnaraḥ|
kṛte paunarbhavaṣṭome sutaḥ paunarbhavaḥ smṛtaḥ|| 88.43 ||
ekoddiṣṭaṃ pituḥ kuryānna śrāddhaṃ pārvaṇādikam|
krītā yā vanitā mūlyaiḥ sā dāsīti nigadyate|| 88.44 ||
tasyāṃ yo jāyate putro dāsaḥ putrastu sa smṛtaḥ|
na rājño rājyabhāk sa syād viprāṇāṃ nāpi śrāddhakṛt|| 88.45 ||
adhamaḥ sarvaputrebhyastaṃ tasmāt parivarjayet|
purāṇaṃ dharmaśāstrāṇi saṃhitāśca munīritāḥ|| 88.46 ||
nādhyāpayennṛpaḥ śūdrairvihitāni yadṛcchayā|
yasya rājye sadā śūdrāḥ purāṇaṃ saṃhitāṃ tathā|| 88.47 ||
paṭhanti syāt sa hīnāyuḥ rājā rāṣṭreṇa sānvayaḥ|
mohād vā kāmataḥ śūdraḥ purāṇaṃ saṃhitāṃ smṛtim|| 88.48 ||
paṭhannarakamāpneti pitṛbhiḥ saha pāpakṛt|
śūdrebhyo vihitaṃ yat tu yaśca mantra urāhṛtaḥ|| 88.49 ||
tadvipravacanād grāhyaṃ dvayaṃ śūdraiḥ sadaiva hi|
ya yojayennṛpaḥ śūdraṃ vyavahārasya darśane|| 88.50 ||
niyojya tatra taṃ bhūpastāmisre tena pacyate|
hīnāyuśca bhavelloko rājā vāpi sahāyajaḥ|| 88.51 ||
kāṇaṃ vyaṅgamaputraṃ yā nābhijñamajitendriyam|
na hrasva vyādhitaṃ vāpi nṛpaḥ kuryāt purohitam|| 88.52 ||
kṛpaṇasya ghanaṃ rājā na gṛhṇīyāt kadācana|
na dvijānaṃ tathā dadyād dhanāni vipulānyapi|| 88.53 ||
nārohet kāmukonmattagajaṃ rājā kadācana|
āruhyā kāmukastaṃ tu paratreha viṣīdati|| 88.54 ||
anāyuṣyaṃ na kuryāt tu karma bhūpaḥ kadācana|
satataṃ cāyuṣo vṛddhyai yateta sakalairdhanaiḥ|| 88.55 ||
na krūravāre nāṣṭamyāṃ na ṣaṣṭhyāṃ ca nṛpottamaḥ|
añjanābhyañjane kuryāt tāmbūlasyāpi bhojanam|| 88.56 ||
atisūkṣmaṃ tathā pūrṇaṃ grahaṇaṃ candrasūryayoḥ|
nālokayet svayaṃ rājā raktaṃ sūryaṃ tathaiva ca|| 88.57 ||
utpātaṃ jāyate yattu divyaṃ bhaumaṃ ca nābhasam|
nekṣeta yatnānnṛpatirdṛṣṭvā nādyāt tryahaṃ punaḥ|| 88.58 ||
sarvadā maṅgalaṃ ratnaṃ dhārayet saha dūrvayā|
avastrācchāditaṃ gātraṃ na viprebhyaḥ pradarśayet|| 88.59 ||
na toyeṣu makhaṃ paśyennādyānmāṃsāni parvasu|
nārohayet kharaṃ coṣṭraṃ na vāmāmapi gurviṇīm|| 88.60 ||
evaṃ nayayuto rājā caturaṅgaṃ vivardhayan|
ātmānaṃ satataṃ rakṣan sadā vīryaṃ vivardhayet|| 88.61 ||
vījakṣayakarannityaṃ bhakṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ ca pānakam|
varjayet kṣāraśākādyān bahvamlaṃ bahutiktakam|| 88.62 ||
kāṃsyarājatapātrasthaṃ toyaṃ nadyāśca vardhanam|
mūtravṛddhikaraṃ vīryakṣayakāri vivarjayet|| 88.63 ||
tāmrāyaḥsvarṇaśīsānāṃ pātrasthaṃ phalacarmaṇoḥ|
śukravṛddhikaraṃ toyaṃ tadupāsīta yatnataḥ|| 88.64 ||
sarvamūleṣu kṛtyeṣu sadācāreṣu tiṣṭhataḥ|
bhuktveha vividhān bhogānaindraṃ sthānaṃ vrajet param|| 88.65 ||
||mārkaṇḍeya uvāca||
evamaurvastu sagaraṃ śaśāsa munipuṅgavaḥ|
śāstrāṇi caiva sarvāṇi sadācārāṃśca gṛhyakān|| 88.66 ||
bahuśaḥ kathayāmāsa sagarāya mahātmane|
tannāsti yat puraurveṇa kathitaṃ sagarāya na|| 88.67 ||
rājanitiḥ satāṃ nītiryaccānyacchāstrasambhavam|
saṃhitāsu purāṇeṣu yaccāgamacaye sthitam|| 88.68 ||
sarvaṃ śuśrāva sagaro mukhādaurvasya dhīmataḥ|
teṣāṃ tu kathitaṃ kiṃciduddhṛtya dvijasattamāḥ|| 88.69 ||
viṣṇudharmottare pūrvaṃ mayā rahasi bhāṣitam|
rājanīti sadācāraṃ vedavedāṅgasaṅgatam|| 88.70 ||
rahasyaṃ satataṃ viṣṇorvikṣdhvaṃ dvijasattamāḥ|
yaccānnaditamanyatra gaditaṃ vā sasaṃśayam|| 88.71 ||
saṃśayacchedanaṃ teṣu yuṣmabhyaṃ kathitaṃ dvijāḥ|
anuktasaṃśayacchedi purāṇaṃ kālikāhvayam|
yo’bhyaset satataṃ vipraḥ sa vedānāṃ phalaṃ labhet|| 88.72 ||
|| iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe aṣṭāśītitamo’dhyāyaḥ|| 88 ||

<89>
||ṛṣaya ūcuḥ||
saṃkṣepataḥ sadācāro viśeṣo rājanītiṣu|
śrutastvadvacanādaurvaḥ sagarāya yathoktavān|| 89.1 ||
viṣṇudharmottare tantre bāhulyaṃ sarvataḥ punaḥ|
draṣṭavyastu sarācāro draṣṭavyāste prasādataḥ|| 89.2 ||
bhūyo naḥ saṃśayo yo’sti tadanuktaṃ tvayā purā|
chindhi viprendra pṛcchāmaḥ paraṃ kautūhalaṃ hi naḥ|| 89.3 ||
aputrasya gatirnāsti śrūyate vedalokayoḥ|
vetālabhairavau yātau purā vai tapase girim|| 89.4 ||
pūrvastvakṛtadarau tau tayoḥ putrā na ca śrutāḥ|
na jātā athavā jātā yadi nānā dvijottama|
teṣāṃ tu samyagicchāmi śrotuṃ sa sthānamuttamam|| 89.5 ||
||mārkaṇḍeya uvāca||
aputrasya gatirnāsti niścitaṃ ceti sattamāḥ|
svaputrairbhrātṛputrairvā putravanto hi svargatāḥ|| 89.6 ||
jātāpatyau ca tau viprā dhīrau vetālabhairavau|
tayorvaṃśān pravakṣyāmi śrṛṇvantu ca maharṣayaḥ|| 89.7 ||
samyak siddhimavāpyaiva yadā vetālabhairavau|
harasya mandiraṃ prāptau kailāsaṃ pratiharṣitau|| 89.8 ||
tadā harasya vacanānnandī tau rahasi dvajāḥ|
prāhedaṃ vacanaṃ tathyaṃ sāntvayannivaṃ bodhakṛt|| 89.9 ||
||nandyuvāca||
aputrau pūtrajanane bhavantau śaṅkarātmajau|
yatatāṃ jātaputrasya sarvatra sulabhā gatiḥ|| 89.10 ||
punnāma narakaṃ putravihīnaḥ paripaśyati|
na tapobhirna dharmeṇa tanmocayitumīśvaraḥ|| 89.11 ||
kevalāt putrajananāt tasmānmokṣaḥ prajāyate|
tadutpādayatāṃ putraṃ bhavantau devayoniṣu|| 89.12 ||
amartyatā tu yuvayoḥ kṣorapānādajāyata|
kātyāyanyāstataḥ putrānamartyāḥ svasamā yataḥ|| 89.13 ||
tasmād yathā tathā putrānutpādya surayoniṣu|
priyau bhavantau śivayorbhavanaṃ na cirāditi|| 89.14 ||
||mārkaṇḍeya uvāca||
tasyeti vacanaṃ śrutvā nandinaḥ prītamānasau|
evameva kariṣyāvo nandinaṃ cetyabhāṣatām|| 89.15 ||
tatastau satataṃ kṛtvā nandinau vacanaṃ hṛdi|
aceṣṭatāṃ svaputrārthe vrajantau tāvitastataḥ|| 89.16 ||
athaikadā bhairavo’sau urvaśīmapsarovarām|
himavatparvataprasthe dadarśa sumanoharām|| 89.17 ||
atha tāṃ kāmuko bhūtvā yayāce suratotsavam|
veśyābhāvācca suprītā sā yathecchamuvāca tam|| 89.18 ||
tatastasyāṃ bhairavastu cakāra suratotsavam|
prītāyāmurvaśīdevyāṃ suprīto’bhūcca kelibhiḥ|| 89.19 ||
suprītāyāmathorvaśyāṃ tejoṃbhirbhairavasya tu|
sadyojāto’bhavat putro bālasūryasamaprabhaḥ|| 89.20 ||
taṃ tu putra parityajya yayau svasthānamurvaśī|
ādāya tanayaṃ paścād bhairavaḥ svapadaṃ yayau|| 89.21 ||
saṃskṛtya tanayaṃ taṃ tu bhairavo modasaṃyutaḥ|
suveśamiti tannāma cakāra sagaṇādhipaḥ|| 89.22 ||
atha taṃ jātavayasaṃ śakrasūryasamaprabham|
vidyādharādhipatye ku suveśamabhyaṣecayat|| 89.23 ||
sa tu vidyādharādhyakṣastanayāmatisundarīm|
yeme gandharvarājasya dhṛtarāṣṭrāhvayasya ca|| 89.24 ||
tasyāṃ tasya suto jajñe rururnnāma manoharaḥ|
rurostu tanayo bāhurmainākyāmabhyajāyata|| 89.25 ||
bāhostu putrāścatvārastapano’ṅgada īśvaraḥ|
kumudo’bhūt kanīyāṃstu cārvatyāṃ tu manoharaḥ|| 89.26 ||
kumadasya suto jajñe devaseno mahābalaḥ|
sa devasenaḥ pṛthivīmavatīrya manoharaḥ|| 89.27 ||
māndhāturyau vanāśvasya tanayāṃ keśinīṃ muhuḥ|
varayāmāsa bhāryārthe mṛdvaṅgīmapsaraḥ samām|| 89.28 ||
yauvanāśvo’pi māndhātā śakrasya vacanād dadau|
keśinīṃ tanayāṃ svīyāṃ devasenāya vāñchayā|| 89.29 ||
keśinīmupayamyā devasenastayā saha|
vārāṇasyāṃ śambhupuryāṃ haramārādhayacchivam|| 89.30 ||
ārādhito haraḥ prītastasyeṣṭaṃ pradadau varam|
so’pyādade harāt tasmādiṣṭameva varatrayam|| 89.31 ||
yāvacca sūryo bhavitā tāvat sthāsyati saṃtatiḥ|
asyāmeva nagayryāṃ ye madvaṃśasyāpi rājatā|| 89.32 ||
prasanno mama vaṃśe tvaṃ nityameva bhaviṣyasi|
ityādāya varaṃ so’pi devaseno mahākṛtī|| 89.33 ||
śaṅkarasya prasādena ciraṃ tāṃ bubhuje purīm|
devaseno’tha keśinyāṃ janayāmāsa putrakān|| 89.34 ||
yūyaṃ śṛṇuta saptaitānnāmataḥ kīrtitāṃstathā|
sumanā vasudānaśca ṛtughṛg yavanaḥ kṛtī|| 89.35 ||
nīlo vivekī hyete vai sarvaśāstraviśāradāḥ|
sarve vaṃśakarāḥ putrā devasenasya sattamāḥ|| 89.36 ||
atha kāle tu saṃprāpte devaseno’pi bhāryayā|
putreṣa rājyaṃ niḥkṣipya yāto vidyādharakṣayam|| 89.37 ||
tataste tasya tanayāḥ kṛtvā sutanasaṃ nṛpam|
vasudānādayaḥ sarve bubhujuścottamāṃ śriyam|| 89.38 ||
jātāḥ sumanasaḥ putrāstrayaḥ śūrā mahābalāḥ|
sumatiśca virūpaśca satyaḥ śāstrārthapāragāḥ|| 89.39 ||
sumaterabhavat kanyā sutaḥ satyasya ḍiṇḍimaḥ|
virūpasyābhavad gādhargādhermitro’bhavat sutaḥ|| 89.40 ||
teṣāṃ kalpo’bhavadrājā kalpātu tu vijayo’bhavat|
yo vijitya kṣitiṃ sarvāṃ pārthivān bhūritejasaḥ|| 89.41 ||
śakrasyānumate cakre khāṇḍavaṃ śatayojanam|
yat savyasācī hyadahat pāṇḍuputraḥ pratāpavān|
āvahat paramāṃ prītiṃ jvalanasya mahātmanaḥ|| 89.42 ||
||ṛṣaya ūcuḥ||
kathaṃ sa khāṇḍavaṃ cakre vijayaḥ śatayojanam|
tad vayaṃ śrotumicchāmaḥ kathayasya tapodhana|| 89.43 ||
||mārkaṇḍeya uvāca||
somavaṃśe’bhavadrājā mahābalaparākramaḥ|
dhīraḥ sudarśano nāma cārurūpaḥ pratāpavān|| 89.44 ||
sa vai himavato nātidūre bhaṅktvā mahāvanam|
siṃhān vyāghrān samutsārya kvaciccāpi tapodhanān|| 89.45 ||
khāṇḍavīṃ nāma nagarīmakarīt tatra śobhanām|
triṃśadyojana vistīrṇāmāyatāṃ śatayojanām|| 89.46 ||
uccaprākārasaṃyuktāṃ sāṭṭālambudatoraṇām|
nimnābhiratidīrghābhiḥ parikhābhiḥ samāvṛtām|| 89.47 ||
aghṛṣyāmaparairvīrairnānājanasamāvṛtām|
dīrghikābhiścopavanairbahubhiścāpsarogaṇaiḥ|| 89.48 ||
ākīrṇāṃ ca tathārāmairuttamairapi mānavaiḥ|
sotsavāḥ satataṃ yatra janā devān divi sthitān|| 89.49 ||
spardhante sma mudā yuktā ādyābhogasamanvitāḥ|
sa vai sudarśano rājā khātvā bhūmiṃ vidārya ca|| 89.50 ||
gaṅgāṃ kanakhalāṃ devīṃ vāhayāmāsa khāṇḍavīm|
saṃplāvyākhāṇḍavīmadhyaṃ tena khātaiśca vartmabhiḥ|| 89.51 ||
vakrānuvakragā bhūtvā yāti sītāṃ nadīṃ prati|
sa jitvā sakalān bhūpān vittānyāhṛdaya bhūriśaḥ|| 89.52 ||
vaśīcakāra khāṇḍavyāṃ madhye ratnairanekaśaḥ|
anyeṣāṃ nagarebhyastu janānānīya bhūpatiḥ|| 89.53 ||
khāṇḍavyāṃ vāsayāmāsa haṭhādapi sudarśanaḥ|
devadānavagandharvāñ jitvā jitvā yudhā kṛtī|| 89.54 ||
devavṛkṣaṃ devaratnaṃ devīṃ cāpi tathauṣadhim|
khāṇḍavyāṃ ropayāmāsa sa bhūpālaḥ sudarśanaḥ|| 89.55 ||
viṣṇusto’pi vai viṣṇurnṛpatiṃ taṃ sudarśanam|
kṛtāpakāraṃ ca bahudhā devānāṃ ca tathā nṛṇām|| 89.56 ||
vārāṇasīpatiṃ vīraṃ vijayaṃ jayaśālinam|
yuddhāya kṛtasācivyaṃ tadvaire samayojayat|| 89.57 ||
vijayo vivaraṃ prāpya mahābalaparākramaḥ|
sudarśanasya nṛpateravaskandamathākarot|| 89.58 ||
nāsahat sa hyavaskandaṃ vijayasya sudarśanaḥ|
caturaṅgabalenāśu yuddhāyābhimukho’bhavat|| 89.59 ||
vijayo rathamāruhya niyojya caturaṅgiṇīm|
tataḥ sudarśanaṃ yoddhuṃ sammukho’bhavadañjasā|| 89.60 ||
tadā mahāyuddhamāsīdvijayena mahātmanā|
sudarśanasya nṛpatervṛtravāsavayoryathā|| 89.61 ||
sudarśanasya senānāṃ rumaṇvānnāma vīryavān|
kāṃcanaṃ rathamāruhya vijayasaṃmukho’bhyayāt|| 89.62 ||
akṣauhiṇyastu saptāsya parivārya samantataḥ|
vyadhamattāṃ śatrisenāṃ yāvatīmudyatāyudhaḥ|| 89.63 ||
vijayasya ca senānīḥ sañjayaḥ sa ripuñjayaḥ|
nāgānīkena jagrāha rumaṇvantaṃ sasainikam|| 89.64 ||
tayormahadabhūd yuddhaṃ senānyorvīrayormahat|
vavarṣa śaravarṣeṇa rumaṇvānatha saṃjayam|| 89.65 ||
kurvaṃścāpi mahānādaṃ gajaṃ dṛṣṭvaiva keśarī|
rumaṇvānatha viśatyā bā ṇairvidhvātha sañjayam|| 89.66 ||
kṣurapreṇa dhanustasya ciccheda kṛtahastavata|
so’pi kārmukamādāya tadā’nyat saṃjayastribhiḥ|| 89.67 ||
bāṇairvivyādha bhallena dhanuściccheda tatkṣaṇāt|
śatānyaṣṭau ca nāgānāṃ sahasrāṇi ca paṃcaṣaṭ|| 89.68 ||
pattīnāṃ vājināṃ trīṇi sahasrāṇi samantataḥ|
saṃjayo nirjaghānāśu bāṇavarṣaiḥ sudāruṇaiḥ|| 89.69 ||
athānyad dhanurādāya rumaṇvān kupito bhṛśam|
bhallena sāratherasya śiraḥ kāyādapāharat|| 89.70 ||
hayāṃścāsya caturbhiṃstu bāṇairninye yamakṣayam|
caturaḥ paṃcabhirbāṇairavidhyaccāpi śañjayam|| 89.71 ||
saṃjayo’pyativegena gadāmādāya tat kṣaṇāt|
avatīrya rathopasthādrumaṇvantamadhāvata|| 89.72 ||
sa dhāvantaṃ sañjayaṃ taṃ rumaṇvān drutahastavat|
śaravarṣeṇa sañchādya vārayāmāsa saṃjayam|| 89.73 ||
