gadāyā bhrāmaṇenāsau nivārya śaravarṣaṇam|
āsasāda rumaṇvantaṃ kesarīva mahāgajam|| 89.74 ||
āsādya tāṃ gadāṃ gurvīmāvidhyātīva sañjayaḥ|
ekenaiva prahāreṇa sarathaṃ taṃ vyapothayat|| 89.75 ||
sa papāta mahāvīraḥ pṛthivyāṃ gadayā hataḥ|
vajrahato yathā śālaḥ praphullo vanamadhyagaḥ|| 89.76 ||
rumaṇvantaṃ nipatitaṃ dṛṣṭvā rājā sudarśanaḥ|
śokakopasamāviṣṭaḥ sadhūma iva pāvakaḥ|| 89.77 ||
jajvālākuladeho’pi krodhenātīva saṃyutaḥ|
āruhya javanairaśvairyuktaṃ vaiyāghrakṛttinā|| 89.78 ||
rathaṃ kāṃcanacitrāṃgaṃ siṃhadhvajavibhūṣitam|
āmukto dhanurādāya visphārya ca punaḥ punaḥ|| 89.79 ||
sasainyaḥ sañjayaṃ rājā samādravata vegavān|
athāsya niśitaiḥ śastraiḥ senāmagragatāṃ bhṛśam|| 89.80 ||
nyahanat sakalāṃ rājā mṛgāniva mṛgādhipaḥ|
ekāmakṣauhiṇīmagragāminīṃ vipulaujasām|| 89.81 ||
krośadvayena nyahanat tāmaṃsīva divākaraḥ|
hatvā cākṣohiṇīmekāmāsādya saṃjayaṃ nṛpaḥ|| 89.82 ||
bāṇaiḥ ṣaṣṭvā tu vivyādha dhvajamekena cicchide|
saṃjayo’pyatha viṃśatyā hṛdi viddhvā sudarśanam|| 89.83 ||
lalāṭe tvekabāṇena prāvidhyat kṛtahastavat|
kṣurapreṇāsya kodaṇḍaṃ chittvā rājñaḥ pratāpavān|| 89.84 ||
sārathiṃ daśabhirbāṇaiḥ punarvivyādha sañjayaḥ|
kodaṇḍamanyamādāya tadā rājā sudarśanaḥ|| 89.85 ||
śaravarṣeṇa tīvreṇa vavarṣātīva sañjayam|
tayormahadbhūd yuddhaṃ munivismayakārakam|| 89.86 ||
śastrairastrairbhṛśaṃ tīkṣṇairbalivāsavayoriva|
tataḥ sudarśano rājā bhallenāsya dṛḍhaṃ dhanuḥ|| 89.87 ||
ciccheda sārathiṃ cāsya jaghāna niśitaiḥ śaraiḥ|
svayaṃ saṃyamya vāhān sa sañjayaḥ paravīrahā|| 89.88 ||
dhanuranyat samādāya parivārya sudarśanam|
vivyādha daśabhirbāṇairdhanurapyacchinad dṛḍham|| 89.89 ||
śarāsanāntaraṃ rājā samādāya sudarśanaḥ|
sañjayasya caturvāhāñcharairninye yamakṣayam|| 89.90 ||
muṣṭau dhanuśca ciccheda taṃ ca vivyādha paṃcabhiḥ|
virathaśchinnavāhaśca sañjayaḥ khaḍgacarmaṇī|| 89.91 ||
ādāya sammukhaṃ rājño’bhyadravat kupito bhṛśam|
tasya cāpaṃ tataḥ khaḍgaṃ kṣurapreṇa sudarśanaḥ|| 89.92 ||
dvidhā ciccheda bhallena carma cāpyacchinattadā|
atha drutaṃ tadopetya saññayaḥ syandanottamam|| 89.93 ||
sudarśanasya sūtaṃ tu karābhyāṃ pātayat kṣitau|
rathābhyāśe gatasyāsya sañjayasya sudarśanaḥ|| 89.94 ||
śiraściccheda khaḍgena tato’sau nyapatad bhuvi|
sa papāta tadā tasya rathābhyāśe mahābalaḥ|| 89.95 ||
kṛttaḥ paraśunā’raṇye puṣpitaḥ śālavṛkṣavat|
sañjayaṃ patitaṃ dṛṣṭvā vijayaḥ krodhamūrcchitaḥ|| 89.96 ||
mahatā śaṅkhanādena nādayaṃstu nabhaḥsthalam|
rathena svarṇacitreṇa vyāghracarmavirājinā|| 89.97 ||
ketunā vṛṣabheṇātha yojanārdhocchritena ca|
nādayan kakubhaḥ sarvā rathaughapariveṣṭitaḥ|| 89.98 ||
vimuñcañcharavarṣāṇi sasāda ca sudarśanam|
āsādya taṃ nṛpaṃ bhūpo vijayaḥ paravīrahā|| 89.99 ||
hṛdi viddhvā tribhirbāṇaistiṣṭhatiṣṭheti cābravīt|
sudarśano’pi vijayaṃ nadantaṃ kuṃjaropamam|| 89.100 ||
daśabhirniśitairbāṇairviddhvā ciccheda taddhanuḥ|
athainaṃ chinnadhanvānaṃ jatrudeśe tribhiḥ śaraiḥ|| 89.101 ||
nirbhidyātha mahānādaṃ nanāda sa sudarśanaḥ|
so’nyaddhanuḥ samādāya kaṃkapatraistribhiḥ śaraiḥ|| 89.102 ||
vivyādha hṛdaye vīro vijayo’pi sudarśanam|
tatastannṛpamuddiśya mahāśaktiṃ sudīpitām|| 89.103 ||
nāgakanyāṃ kopayuktāṃ lelihānāmivātulām|
svarṇadaṇḍāṃ sutīkṣṇāgrāṃ tailadhautāṃ sunirmalām|| 89.104 ||
samudyamyāthācikṣepa vijayaḥ śātravaṃ prati|
sudarśanasya hṛdayaṃ sā śaktiḥ praviveśa ha|| 89.105 ||
sa vihvalo rathopasthe hyadhovaktra upāviśat|
tasmin mohasamāpanne nṛpatau ca sudarśane|| 89.106 ||
tasyāgratastathā pārśve ye sthitāstatra sainikāḥ|
tān sarvānahanadrājā kṣaṇamātrād dvijottamāḥ|| 89.107 ||
rathān daśasahasrāṇi tāvantyeva ca dantinām|
paṃcaviṃśahasrāṇi vājināṃ ca tarasvinām|| 89.108 ||
lakṣadvayaṃ tu pattīnāṃ kṣaṇamātrādapothayat|
sa tu labdhvā tataḥ saṃjñāṃ dhanurādāya vai dṛḍham|| 89.109 ||
śaravarṣeṇa vijayaṃ vavarṣa sa sudarśanaḥ|
nivārya śaravarṣeṇa vijayaṃ tu sudarśanaḥ|| 2.80.110 ||
bhallena kārmukaṃ sajyaṃ tasya ciccheda tatkṣaṇāt|
sārathestu śiraḥ kāyād bhallenāpāharat tataḥ|| 89.111 ||
hayāṃśca caturaścāsya preṣayāmāsa mṛtyave|
athaivaṃ virathaṃ bhūpaṃ daśabhiḥ kaṅkapatribhiḥ|| 89.112 ||
vivyādha hṛdaye bhūyo nanāda ca sudarśanaḥ|
sa cchinnadhanvā viratho gadāmādāya vegavān|| 89.113 ||
vijayo vijayākāṅkṣī sudarśanamadhāvata|
āpatantaṃ mahāvīraṃ bāṇavarṣaiḥ sudarśanaḥ|| 89.114 ||
vavarṣa varṣāsu yathā vāridaḥ pṛthivīdharam|
vijayaḥ śaravṛṣṭiṃ tāṃ prācchādya svaśareṇa vai|| 89.115 ||
gadayā taṃ rathārūḍhamāsasāda uta tatkṣaṇāt|
āsādya taṃ mahāvīryaṃ vijayo’tha sudarśanam|| 89.116 ||
śīrṣa prahṛtya gadayā pātayāmāsa bhūtale|
gireḥ śṛṅgaṃ yathā tuṅgaṃ vajrāśanividāritam|| 89.117 ||
tathā sudarśano rājā dārito gadayā’patat|
tasminnipatite vīre senābhistasya sainikāḥ|| 89.118 ||
bhayāt saṃprādravaṃstasmād diśaśca pradiśastathā|
naṣṭeṣu tasya sainyeṣu vijayaḥ khāṇḍavīṃ purīm|| 89.119 ||
praviśya dadṛśe tatra rāśībhūtān girīniva|
suvarṇānāṃ ca ratnānāṃ saṃcayān bahuśaḥ punaḥ|| 89.120 ||
dṛṣṭvā sarāṃsi tatraiṣa praphullakamalāni ca|
haṃsakāraṇḍavānādairnāditāni samantataḥ|| 89.121 ||
rāśīn suvarṇaratnānāṃ parvatāniva vistṛtān|
puṣpitān devavṛkṣāṃśca bhramadbhramarabhūṣitān|| 89.122 ||
prāsādān vipulāñchubhrān kailāsasadṛśān gajān|
prasphuṭāṃśca sugandhāḍhyān pratigehe vyavasthitān|| 89.123 ||
utphullanayano rājā vijayaḥ paravīrahā|
mene’marāvatīṃ tāṃ tu purīm kṣitigatāmiva|| 89.124 ||
taṃ vākṣantaṃ narapatiṃ nagarīṃ tāṃ sureśvaraḥ|
sametya vijayaṃ prāha sāntvayan ślakṣṇayā girā|| 89.125 ||
||indra uvāca||
rājan mahāvanamidamāsīd devagaṇāvṛtam|
ta cca gandharvayakṣāṇāṃ munīnāṃ ca manoharam|| 89.126 ||
sarvānutsārya devādīn mama cāpyapriye rataḥ|
bhaṅktvā vanamidaṃ guhyamutsādya ca tapodhanam|| 89.127 ||
khāṇḍavīṃ nagarīṃ cakre haṭhādrājā sudarśanaḥ|
tadidaṃ punareva tvaṃ vanaṃ kuru narottama|| 89.128 ||
tatrāhaṃ vihariṣyāmi takṣakeṇa samaṃ rahaḥ|
munīnāṃ ca tapaḥ sthānamatulaṃ te prasādataḥ|
bhaviṣyati ca yakṣāṇāṃ kinnarāṇāṃ ca pārthiva|| 89.129 ||
||mārkaṇḍeya uvāca||
etacchratvā vacastasya śakrasya vijayastadā|
vanamevākarot tāntaṃ khāṇḍavī śakragauravāt|| 89.130 ||
gacchantu bho yathāsthānaṃ prajāḥ sarvā yathacchayā|
yeṣāṃ vāñchāsti lokānāṃ madrājyagamane punaḥ|| 89.131 ||
vārāṇasīṃ te gacchantu mayaiva pratipālitām|
tatastasya vacaḥ śrutvā janāḥ kecinnijāspadam|| 89.132 ||
jagmurvārāṇasīṃ kecid vijayenābhipālitām|
tato dhanānāṃ tān rāśīn ratnānāṃ ca pṛthak pṛthak|| 89.133 ||
maṇīnāṃ kanakānāṃ ca kupyānāṃ vijayastathā|
vividhairvārayāmāsa purīṃ vāraṇasīṃ prati|| 89.134 ||
gandharvāṇāṃ ca devānāṃ yadānītaṃ haṭhāt purā|
ratnadārvādikaṃ yat tu vijayaṃ tat prasādya ca|| 89.135 ||
taistairnītaṃ ca khāṇḍavyāḥ svasthānaṃ pratiharṣitaiḥ|
triṃśadyojanavistīrṇāṃ śatayojanamāyatām|| 89.136 ||
tāṃ purīṃ vijayaścakre nacirādeva vai vanam|
tasmiñchakrasya sammatyā takṣakaḥ sahito gaṇaiḥ|| 89.137 ||
uvāsa suciraṃ tatra tato’bhūnnirjanaṃ vanam|
tatra devāḥ sagandharvāḥ krīḍante’psarasāṃ gaṇāḥ|| 89.138 ||
āśaṃsantaśca vijayaṃ raṇeṣu vijayāvaham|
prāpte’ṣṭāviṃśatitame yuge dvāparaśeṣataḥ|| 89.139 ||
vahnirbrāhmaṇarūpeṇa bhikṣāṃ jiṣṇumamācata|
dātumaṅgīkṛte bhikṣāṃ tadā pāṇḍusutena vai|| 89.140 ||
vahniḥ svarūpamāsthāya jiṣṇuṃ vacanamabravīt|
ahamagniḥ pāṇḍuputra yajñabhāgātibhojanāt|| 89.141 ||
vyādhito’haṃ tato vyādhiṃ mama tvaṃ nāśayādhunā|
khāṇḍavaṃ nāma vipinaṃ sapatrimṛgarākṣasam|| 89.142 ||
yadi tvaṃ māṃ bhojayituṃ śakroṣi śvetavāhana|
tadā mama hyasau vyādhirapayāsyati no cirāt|| 89.143 ||
purā tu vijayo rājā khāṇḍavīṃ nāma tāṃ purīm|
bhaṅktvā vana yataścakre tena tat khāṇḍavaṃ vanam|| 89.144 ||
madarthaṃ devavihitaṃ vanaṃ tu śvetavāhana|
virodhāt tat tu śakrasya na svayaṃ bhoktumutsahe|| 89.145 ||
tasmāt trāhi mahābhāga vane tasminniyojaya|
yathāhaṃ sakalaṃ bhoktuṃ śaknomi tatprasādataḥ|| 89.146 ||
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā savyasācī mahābalaḥ|
dāhayāmāsa vipinaṃ tatsarvaṃ prāṇisaṃyutam|| 89.147 ||
devakītanayenāsau vāsudevena pālitaḥ|
khāṇḍavaṃ dāhayāmāsa jvalanasya hite rataḥ|| 89.148 ||
suprītaḥ pradadau tasmādarjunāya mahātmane|
vahnirdhanuśca gāṇḍīvaṃ vāruṇaṃ devanirmitam|| 89.149 ||
akṣayye ceṣudhī divye rupāḍhyāṃścaturo hayān|
hanumatādhiṣṭhitaṃ tu mahāntaṃ vānaradhvajam|| 89.150 ||
khaḍgaṃ ca triśikhaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ dahanaḥ savyasācine|
nīrogaścābhavad vahnistathā jiṣṇuprasādataḥ|| 89.151 ||
tairbāṇaistana dhanuṣā tena khaḍgena ketunā|
tadaśvasyandanenāpi vijigye phālguno ripūn|| 89.152 ||
evaṃ bhairavavaṃśeṣu sañjāto vijayo nṛpaḥ|
khāṇḍavaṃ nāma vipinaṃ cakāra sumahākṛtī|| 89.153 ||
vijayasya sutā jātāstrayodaśa mahābalāḥ|
dyutimān saumyadarśī ca bhūriḥ pradyumna eva ca|| 89.154 ||
kratustuṇḍo virūpākṣo vikrānto’tha dhanaṃjayaḥ|
praharṣaḥ prabalaḥ ketustathoparicaro’paraḥ|| 89.155 ||
eṣāṃ rājā’bhavad vīraḥ śeṣoparicarastu yaḥ|
vārāṇasyāṃ nagaryāṃ yo yajñalakṣaṃ purā’karot|| 89.156 ||
lakṣayajñakaraḥ ko’pi nāsīnnāpi bhaviṣyati|
rājā kṣitau mahābhāgo yathoparicarastathā|| 89.157 ||
eṣāṃ sūtiprasūtaiśca vyāptaṃ sarvamidaṃ jagat|
cireṇa tān kaḥ saṃkhyātuṃ śaknoti bhuvi mānuṣaḥ|| 89.158 ||
kramād bhairavavaṃśena vyāptaṃ lokatrayaṃ tvidam|
etad vaḥ kathitaṃ viprāḥ santānaṃ bhairavasya tu|| 89.159 ||
yeṣāṃ śrutvā kathāmātraṃ nāputro jāyate naraḥ|
idaṃ yaḥ kīrtayet puṇyaṃ caritaṃ vijayasya tu|| 89.160 ||
satataṃ vijayastasya jāyate na parābhavaḥ|
ekāgramanasā yastu śrṛṇuyādidamuttamam|
tasya vaṃśasya vicchedo na kadācid bhaviṣyati|| 89.161 ||
|| iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe ekonanavatitamo’dhyāyaḥ|| 89 ||

<90>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca
vetālasya ca santānaṃ śṛṇvantu munisattamāḥ|
yacchrutvā sarvapāpebhyastatkṣaṇādeva hīyate|| 90.1 ||
dakṣasya tanayā cābhūt surabhirnāma nāmataḥ|
gavāṃ mātā mahābhāgā sarvalokopakāriṇī|| 90.2 ||
tasyāṃ tu tanayā jajñe kaśyapāt tu prajāpateḥ|
nāmnā sā rohiṇī śubhrā sarvakāmadughā nṛṇām|| 90.3 ||
tasyāṃ jajñe śunaḥśephānmuneratitapodhanāt|
kāmadhenuriti khyātā sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutā|| 90.4 ||
sā sitābhrapratīkāśā caturvedacatuṣpadā|
stanaiścaturbhirdharmārthakāmaprasavakāriṇī|| 90.5 ||
sā suvarṇaśarīrā tu kālena mahatā satī|
nirmalaṃ yauvanaṃ prāpa kāmadhenurmanoharam|| 90.6 ||
tāṃ carantīṃ merupṛṣṭhe cārurūpāṃ sulabhaṇām|
dadarśa sa tu vetālaḥ kāmukaścābhyapadyata|| 90.7 ||
taṃ kāmukaṃ ca vetālaṃ viditvā kāmadhenukā|
paśudharmāt svayaṃ bheje taṃ putraṃ śaśabhṛdbhṛtaḥ|| 90.8 ||
so’vāpa tasyāṃ paramamāmodaṃ śaṅkarātmajaḥ|
sā cāpi paramāṃ tasmin mudamāpātiharṣitā|| 90.9 ||
tayoḥ pravṛtte surate tasyāṃ garbho’bhavat tadā|
kāle prāpte tu suṣuve kāmadhenurmahāvṛṣam|| 90.10 ||
so’cireṇaiva kālena sumahān vṛṣabho’bhavat|
mahākakudasaṃyuktaścāruśṛṅgasamanvitaḥ|| 90.11 ||
utkṣipya vicalatkarṇayugalo dīrghabāladhiḥ|
kakudena ca śṛṅgābhyāṃ karṇābhyāṃ sa sitābhravat|| 90.12 ||
vicalan dadṛśe devaiḥ śṛṅgairiva sitācalaḥ|
vetālastvakarot tasya nāma śṛṅga iti dvijāḥ|| 90.13 ||
sa tu śṛṅgo jñānaśālī samārādhayadīśvaram|
so’pi tuṣṭo varaṃ tasmai dadāviṣṭaṃ haraḥ prabhuḥ|| 90.14 ||
tameva vāhanaṃ cakre kṛtvā devatanuṃ vṛṣam|
sucirāyuśca balavān pṛthivīdhāraṇe kṣamaḥ|| 90.15 ||
śṛṅgo nāma mahātejāḥ ketuḥ so’pyabhavat prabhoḥ|
śṛṅgo bhūtvā mato yasmācchaṅkarasya mahātmanaḥ|| 90.16 ||
ataḥ śṛṅga iti khyātimatha prāha maheśvaraḥ|
sa tu śṛṅgo mahādeve dhyānāsakte kvacit kvacid|| 90.17 ||
varuṇasya gṛhaṃ gatvā surabhestanayāstu yāḥ|
rūpayauvanasampannā bheje’laṃ suratena tāḥ|| 90.18 ||
varuṇasya gṛhe gāvaḥ sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutāḥ|
tiṣṭhanti satataṃ viprāstāsu tāsa sutāḥ punaḥ|| 90.19 ||
vahvyastu ca samutpannāsteṣāṃ sūtiprasūtibhiḥ|
sarvaṃ jagadidaṃ vyāptaṃ tebhyo yajñaṃ pravartate|| 90.20 ||
ājyena devāstuṣyanti yajñā ājye pratiṣṭhitāḥ|
yajñāghīnamidaṃ sarvaṃ jagat sthāvarajaṅgamam|| 90.21 ||
tadājyaṃ tu gavādhīnaṃ tataḥ sarvaṃ gavi sthitam|
tadidaṃ sakalaṃ viśvaṃ gavādhīnaṃ dvijottamāḥ|| 90.22 ||
vetālasya ca tā gāvo vaṃśyāḥ sarvapriyāḥ sadā|
ya idaṃ śṛṇuyānnityaṃ vetālasya mahātmanaḥ|| 90.23 ||
vaṃśānāṃ janma viprendrāḥ sa sukhī balavān bhavet|
na gāvo nāpi vibhavāstasyaṃ naśyanti vai kvacit|| 90.24 ||
na ca bhūtapiśācādyāstaṃ paśyanti kadācana|
vetālaḥ satataṃ tasya rakṣāmācarati svayam|| 90.25 ||
iti vaḥ kathitaṃ viprā yathā vetālabhairavau|
janayāmāsatuḥ putrān vicchinnāḥ saṃśayāśca vaḥ|| 90.26 ||
yathā ca kālikā devī mohayāmāsa śaṃkaram|
yathotpannā śarīrārdhaṃ kṛtaṃ śambhoryathā tathā|| 90.27 ||
kālikāyai namastubhyamiti yo bhāṣate svayam|
tasya haste sthitā muktistrivargastu vaśānugaḥ|| 90.28 ||
iti vaḥ kathitaṃ puṇyaṃ purāṇaṃ kālikāhvayam|
mantrayantramayaṃ śuddhaṃ jñānadaṃ kāmadaṃ param|| 90.29 ||
iti guhyatamaṃ loke vedeṣu ca tathā dvijāḥ|
devagandharvasiddhādyaiḥ spṛhaṇīyamidaṃ sadā|| 90.30 ||
adhītaṃ ca śrutaṃ matto vasiṣṭhena mahātmanā|
idaṃ purāṇamamṛtaṃ kālikāhvayamuttamam|| 90.31 ||
tena guptamidaṃ sarvaṃ kāmarūpe surālaye|
tamidānīṃ samākhyātaṃ vyaktīkatya maharṣayaḥ|| 90.32 ||
yusmābhirapi no deya gopyaṃ lokeṣu sarvadā|
śaṭhāya calaciṃttāya nāstikāyājitātmane|| 90.33 ||
bhaktiśraddhāvihīnāya na dātavyaṃ kadācana|
idaṃ sakṛt paṭhed yastu purāṇaṃ kālikāhvayam|| 90.34 ||
sa kāmānakhilān prāpya śeṣe’mṛtamavāpnuyāt|
mandire likhitaṃ yasya purāṇamidamuttamam|| 90.35 ||
sadā tiṣṭhati no tasya vighnaḥ saṃjāyate dvijāḥ|
yo’dhīte’hanyahanyetad guhyaṃ tantramidaṃ param|| 90.36 ||
adhītāḥ sakalā vedāsteneha dvijasattamāḥ|
tasmānnaivādhiko’nyo’sti kṛtakṛtyo vicakṣaṇaḥ|| 90.37 ||
sa sukhī ballavām̐lloke dīrghāyurapi jāyate|| 90.38 ||
yo lokamīśaḥsatataṃ bibharti yaḥ pālayatyantakara stathānte|
idaṃ samastaṃ bhramamabhramaṃ vāyadīyarūpaṃ ca namostu tasmai|| 90.39 ||
pradhānapuruṣo yasya prapañco yogināṃ hṛdi|
yaḥ purāṇādhipo viṣṇuḥ prasīdatu sa vaḥ śivaḥ|| 90.40 ||
yo heturugraḥ puruṣaḥ purāṇaḥ sanātanaḥ śāśvata īśvaraḥ paraḥ|
purāṇakṛd vedapurāṇavedyaḥ prastaumi tannaumi purāṇaśeṣe|| 90.41 ||
iti sakalajagad bibharti yāsāṃ madhuripumohakarī ramāsvarūpā|
ramayati ca haraṃ śivāsvarūpā vitaratu vo vibhavaṃ śubhāni māyā|| 90.42 ||
|| iti śrīkālikāpurāṇe navatitamo’dhyāya|| 90 ||
|| iti śrīkālikāpurāṇaṃ samāptam ||
